01可背补全重点

TU05 Cancer Cell Biology — 可背补全重点（中英对照）

9份课件整合 + Part 2 图题通用答题框架（适合直接背）

怎么用这份笔记

目标
不用看slide，也能覆盖常考概念 + 典型图题/实验题问法。Goal: Cover common concepts + typical figure/experiment questions without re-reading slides.
背诵顺序建议
先“Part 2 图题模板”→ 再按9个模块背“必背清单”。Suggested order: memorize the Part-2 figure template first, then the 9-module must-know lists.
记忆方式
每条=一个“可回答点”。考试写不出长段，先把点写全。Memory method: each bullet = one answerable point. In exam, prioritize complete points over long prose.

Part 2 图题万能答题框架（必背）

1) 先把图“说清楚”

图类型识别
通路图 / Western blot / FACS / IHC / 存活曲线 / 迁移实验 / 代谢读数 / 剂量-反应等。Identify the figure type: pathway / WB / FACS / IHC / survival curve / migration assay / metabolic readout / dose-response, etc.
先写
实验对象（细胞/小鼠/患者样本）、处理（药物/基因敲除/缺氧等）、读出指标（蛋白/荧光/细胞数）。First state: model (cells/mice/patient sample), perturbation (drug/KO/hypoxia), and readout (protein/fluorescence/cell counts).

2) 再写“对照组-变量-结论”三件套

对照组是什么？（vehicle / WT / untreated / isotype / normoxia）。What is the control? (vehicle / WT / untreated / isotype / normoxia).

变量改变了什么？（↑/↓ 某通路、某标志物、细胞周期分布、凋亡比例、迁移速度）。What changed? (↑/↓ pathway, marker, cell-cycle distribution, apoptosis fraction, migration speed).

一句话结论
处理 → 机制方向（激活/抑制）→ 表型（增殖/死亡/侵袭/免疫逃逸）。One-line conclusion: perturbation → mechanism direction (activate/inhibit) → phenotype (proliferation/death/invasion/immune escape).

3) 最后补“机制 + 临床/治疗意义”

机制
尽量用课件词（例：PD-1/PD-L1、RIPK1/3-MLKL、MCT1/4、APC/C、E-cad/N-cad switch）。Mechanism: use slide keywords (e.g., PD-1/PD-L1, RIPK1/3-MLKL, MCT1/4, APC/C, E-cad/N-cad switch).
治疗意义
对应靶点/耐药/生物标志物/联合治疗思路（例：诱导非凋亡性死亡作为“backup”）。Clinical meaning: link to target/resistance/biomarker/combination (e.g., trigger non-apoptotic death as a backup).

模块1：Introduction — how a cell becomes cancerous

癌症=多步骤演化（clonal expansion）+ 获得多个Hallmarks/Enabling characteristics。Cancer = multistep evolution (clonal expansion) + acquisition of hallmarks/enabling characteristics.

典型驱动通路
RAS-RAF-MEK-ERK（RAS突变常通过GTPase活性受损→持续GTP结合态）。Key driver: RAS-RAF-MEK-ERK (oncogenic RAS often impairs GTPase activity → persistent GTP-bound state).
抑癌核心
pRb 与 p53（DNA损伤/生长信号整合→停滞/修复/凋亡/衰老）。Core suppressors: pRb and p53 (integrate DNA damage and growth signals → arrest/repair/apoptosis/senescence).
复制永生
端粒酶（telomerase）过表达→端粒维持。Replicative immortality: telomerase overexpression maintains telomeres.
侵袭转移
E-cadherin下降是EMT关键特征；HGF/c-Met可促进侵袭转移。Invasion/metastasis: E-cadherin loss is a key EMT feature; HGF/c-Met promotes invasion/metastasis.
血管生成
VEGF家族/VEGFR为主要轴，缺氧可诱导其表达。Angiogenesis: VEGF family/VEGFR is the main axis; hypoxia can induce expression.
代谢重编程
Warburg效应=偏好糖酵解（更快供能+提供生物合成底物）。Metabolic rewiring: Warburg effect = preference for glycolysis (fast ATP + biosynthetic intermediates).
基因组稳定
PARP参与单链断裂修复；DNA修复缺陷→突变累积。Genome stability: PARP participates in single-strand break repair; repair defects accelerate mutation accumulation.

模块2：Epigenetics

三大表观层面
DNA甲基化 + 组蛋白乙酰化 + 组蛋白甲基化（共同决定染色质开放/沉默）。Three epigenetic layers: DNA methylation + histone acetylation + histone methylation (control chromatin openness/silencing).
Writers
DNMTs（DNMT1/3a/3b等）加5mC；HATs；HMTs（如SETD2/DOT1L）。Writers: DNMTs (DNMT1/3a/3b) add 5mC; HATs; HMTs (e.g., SETD2/DOT1L).
Erasers
DNA去甲基化相关酶（如TET1）；HDACs；KDMs/LSDs。Erasers: DNA demethylation enzymes (e.g., TET1); HDACs; KDMs/LSDs.
Readers
MBD蛋白（含UHRF1）识别methyl-CpG；溴结构域蛋白如BRD4识别乙酰化。Readers: MBD proteins (incl. UHRF1) read methyl-CpG; bromodomain proteins like BRD4 read acetylation.
癌症甲基化特征
全局5mC降低 + 在启动子/增强子出现异常“局灶高甲基化”→抑癌基因沉默/致癌基因上调。Cancer methylation pattern: global loss of 5mC + focal hypermethylation at promoters/enhancers → suppress TSGs / enable oncogenes.
检测/研究
ChIP-chip/ChIP-seq用于定位组蛋白修饰与结合蛋白。Methods: ChIP-chip/ChIP-seq map histone marks and binding proteins.

表观药物（例）：DNMTi（azacitidine/decitabine）；HDACi；BETi（iBET762/OTX-015）；EZH2i（GSK-126/EPZ-6438）；DOT1Li（EPZ-5676）。Epigenetic drugs (examples): DNMTi (azacitidine/decitabine); HDACi; BETi (iBET762/OTX-015); EZH2i (GSK-126/EPZ-6438); DOT1Li (EPZ-5676).

模块3：Metabolic reprogramming & Angiogenesis

肿瘤核心压力
血流下降→氧/营养不足；需求上升→持续促血管生成信号。Core stress: reduced blood flow → low O2/nutrients; demand increases → sustained pro-angiogenic signaling.
VEGF轴
癌细胞高表达VEGF-A；内皮细胞高表达VEGFR-1/2；结合→二聚/自磷酸化→多通路效应。VEGF axis: tumor cells express VEGF-A; endothelial cells express VEGFR-1/2; binding → dimerization/autophosphorylation → pleiotropic effects.
Warburg效应关键后果
乳酸生成与外排（LDH-A上调；MCT1/4上调）→pH调控与微环境重塑。Warburg consequences: lactate production/export (↑LDH-A; ↑MCT1/4) → pH control and TME remodeling.
LDH
A/B亚基组成不同同工酶；肿瘤倾向LDH-A表达与“偏乳酸生成”的谱型。LDH: isoenzymes from A/B subunits; tumors favor LDH-A expression and lactate-producing profiles.
乳酸是信号分子
可通过GPCR（GPR81）↓cAMP，并影响免疫/炎症与血管生成。Lactate as a messenger: signals via GPCR (GPR81) to ↓cAMP and modulate immunity/inflammation/angiogenesis.
“Reverse Warburg”
癌细胞诱导CAF变‘分解代谢’→产生乳酸/丙酮酸供肿瘤利用（与MCT/LDH相关）。Reverse Warburg: cancer cells instruct CAFs to be catabolic → produce lactate/pyruvate to feed tumors (linked to MCT/LDH).
治疗思路
抗血管生成治疗 + 代谢适应控制（两条都可限制肿瘤生长）。Therapeutic concept: anti-angiogenic therapy + targeting metabolic adaptations can limit tumor growth.

模块4：Cytoskeleton

三大骨架
Actin（~8nm）/ Microtubule（~25nm）/ Intermediate filaments（~10nm）+ Septins（~5nm）。Cytoskeleton: Actin (~8nm) / Microtubules (~25nm) / Intermediate filaments (~10nm) + Septins (~5nm).
Actin动力学：成核→延长→稳态；存在临界浓度　；可treadmilling（+端聚合、-端去聚合）。Actin dynamics: nucleation → elongation → steady state; critical concentration (CC); treadmilling (+ end polymerization, - end depolymerization).
答案 C

Intermediate filaments：应对机械/多种应激；核内lamin与胞质IF都影响细胞形态与DNA完整性。Intermediate filaments: stress-response; nuclear lamins and cytoplasmic IFs affect cell shape and DNA integrity.

Septins
GTPase蛋白，形成异源寡聚体（六聚体/八聚体）并组装成丝；与膜曲率、PIPs、连接蛋白（anillin/BORG）相关。Septins: GTPase proteins form hetero-oligomers (hexamers/octamers) into filaments; linked to curvature, PIPs, and linkers (anillin/BORG).
Septin功能
胞质分裂（与actomyosin、ESCRT）、迁移、线粒体分裂、感染防御等。Septin functions: cytokinesis (actomyosin/ESCRT), migration, mitochondrial fission, infection defense, etc.
与癌症/耐药
BORG/Cdc42调控septin在actin/MT上的分布；BORG过表达促转移；septin-微管互作可影响紫杉烷（paclitaxel）敏感性/耐药。Cancer/resistance: BORG/Cdc42 controls septin localization on actin/MT; BORG overexpression is pro-metastatic; septin-MT interactions can affect taxane (paclitaxel) sensitivity/resistance.
Septin作为标志物
Septin 9甲基化DNA可用于结直肠癌血液检测（例子）。Septin as biomarker: Septin 9 methylated DNA used in blood testing for colorectal cancer (example).

模块5：Cell cycle, quiescence, senescence

细胞周期系统特点
顺序事件 + 开关式（不可逆）+ 鲁棒可调；可在G0静息可逆退出，或进入分化/衰老/死亡的永久退出。Cell-cycle system: ordered events + switch-like irreversibility + robust/adaptable; reversible exit to G0, or permanent exit to differentiation/senescence/death.
核心调控
Cyclin-CDK；CDK受CAK磷酸化激活，也受磷酸酶/激酶与CKI抑制。Core control: Cyclin-CDKs; CDKs activated by CAK phosphorylation and regulated by kinases/phosphatases and CKIs.
CKI家族
INK（抑制CDK4/6）；CIP/KIP（如p21/p27/p57）广泛抑制多种Cyclin-CDK。CKIs: INK family inhibits CDK4/6; CIP/KIP (e.g., p21/p27/p57) broadly inhibit cyclin-CDKs.

G1限制点（restriction point）：通过Rb-E2F开关决定是否承诺进入S期；受营养/生长因子/细胞大小/DNA完整性控制。G1 restriction point: Rb-E2F switch commits to S phase; controlled by nutrients/growth factors/cell size/DNA integrity.

泛素-蛋白酶体调控
APC/C（Cdc20/Cdh1）和SCF（如Skp2）通过降解关键蛋白推动周期。Ubiquitin-proteasome control: APC/C (Cdc20/Cdh1) and SCF (e.g., Skp2) degrade key regulators to drive the cycle.

纺锤体组装检查点（SAC）：MCC抑制Cdc20，直到所有动粒都正确连接；否则不进入后期。Spindle assembly checkpoint (SAC): MCC inhibits Cdc20 until all kinetochores attach; blocks anaphase otherwise.

后期与分裂
APC/C-Cdc20激活separase切割cohesin→姐妹染色单体分离；随后细胞质分裂由actomyosin环完成。Anaphase/cytokinesis: APC/C-Cdc20 activates separase to cleave cohesin → chromatid separation; actomyosin ring drives cytokinesis.
DNA损伤检查点
ATM/ATR→p53等；DDR与修复途径（HR/NHEJ/BER/NER/MMR，PARP1等）连接到周期停滞。DNA damage checkpoints: ATM/ATR → p53; DDR and repair pathways (HR/NHEJ/BER/NER/MMR, PARP1) couple to arrest.

模块6：Migration, polarity, EMT, metastasis

EMT/MET
发育与修复中正常；肿瘤中异常再激活→侵袭、入血（intravasion）、出血（extravasation）与转移定植。EMT/MET: normal in development/repair; aberrantly reactivated in cancer → invasion, intravasation, extravasation, colonization.
上皮连接类型
tight junction、adherens junction（E-cad）、desmosome、gap junction；细胞-基质连接：focal adhesion、hemidesmosome。Epithelial junctions: tight, adherens (E-cad), desmosome, gap; cell-matrix: focal adhesions and hemidesmosomes.

整合素（integrins）：focal adhesion中与talin/vinculin等连接actin；黏附周转（turnover）对迁移很关键。Integrins: in focal adhesions connect to actin via talin/vinculin; adhesion turnover is key for migration.

EMT经典开关
E-cad→N-cad；角蛋白→波形蛋白（vimentin）；半桥粒→黏着斑（hemidesmosome→focal adhesion）。EMT switches: E-cad → N-cad; keratin → vimentin; hemidesmosome → focal adhesion.
两种迁移模式
Mesenchymal（强黏附+ECM蛋白水解+lamellipodia，前端Rac1）；Amoeboid（低黏附+blebbing+后端RhoA/肌球蛋白收缩）。Two migration modes: mesenchymal (strong adhesion + ECM proteolysis + lamellipodia, front Rac1); amoeboid (low adhesion + blebbing + rear RhoA/myosin contraction).
方向决定因素
趋化梯度、基质硬度、黏附位点、电场，以及接触抑制/接触刺激迁移。Direction cues: chemoattractant gradients, stiffness, adhesion sites, electric fields, plus contact inhibition/stimulation of migration.
转移链条关键词
CTCs在血流中通常圆且硬；DTCs可进入休眠并耐药，醒来后导致复发。Metastasis keywords: CTCs in blood are often round/stiff; DTCs can be dormant and resistant, causing relapse upon reactivation.
治疗概念
Migrastatics（抗侵袭/抗转移药）是潜在方向，但时间窗与靶点选择困难。Therapy concept: migrastatics (anti-invasion/anti-metastasis drugs) are a potential direction, but timing/targets are challenging.

模块7：Cancer cell death

外源性凋亡
死亡受体（FAS/TNFR等）→DISC→caspase-8→caspase-3。Extrinsic apoptosis: death receptors (FAS/TNFR) → DISC → caspase-8 → caspase-3.
内源性凋亡
线粒体外膜通透化（Bax/Bak）→细胞色素c→Apaf1-凋亡小体（apoptosome）→caspase-9→caspase-3/7。Intrinsic apoptosis: MOMP (Bax/Bak) → cytochrome c → Apaf1 apoptosome → caspase-9 → caspase-3/7.
两条通路连接
caspase-8切割Bid→tBid，放大线粒体通路信号。Crosstalk: caspase-8 cleaves Bid → tBid to amplify mitochondrial signaling.
癌细胞抗凋亡机制
↑Bcl2/Bcl-XL，↑IAP/BIRC（如Survivin），↑cFLIP（抑制caspase-8），↓Bax/死亡受体，p53通路受损。Anti-apoptosis in cancer: ↑Bcl2/Bcl-XL, ↑IAP/BIRCs (e.g., Survivin), ↑cFLIP (blocks caspase-8), ↓Bax/death receptors, impaired p53.
促凋亡治疗例子
BH3 mimetics（如Venetoclax）；MDM2抑制剂稳定p53；IAP抑制剂/SMAC mimetics；死亡受体激动剂。Pro-apoptotic therapy examples: BH3 mimetics (e.g., Venetoclax); MDM2 inhibitors stabilize p53; IAP inhibitors/SMAC mimetics; death receptor agonists.

坏死性凋亡（Necroptosis）：TNFα/TNFR1→TRADD/RIPK1（去泛素化后分岔）→caspase-8被抑制时→RIPK1-RIPK3（necrosome）→MLKL成孔→炎症/DAMPs。Necroptosis: TNFα/TNFR1 → TRADD/RIPK1 (deubiquitination branch point) → when caspase-8 is inhibited → RIPK1-RIPK3 necrosome → MLKL pores → inflammation/DAMPs.

经典抑制剂
Necrostatin（RIPK1）；GSK-872（RIPK3）；NSA（MLKL）；zVAD（抑制caspases使细胞偏向坏死性凋亡）。Classic inhibitors: Necrostatin (RIPK1); GSK-872 (RIPK3); NSA (MLKL); zVAD inhibits caspases and can bias toward necroptosis.
生理/肿瘤意义
可作为凋亡失效时的“第二道清除”；但炎症也可能促肿瘤/促转移。Meaning: can act as a backup when apoptosis fails; but inflammation can also be pro-tumor/pro-metastatic.
MPT驱动坏死
ROS/Ca2+等→MPTP开放→ATP快速耗竭+氧化损伤。MPT-driven necrosis: ROS/Ca2+ triggers MPTP opening → rapid ATP depletion + oxidative damage.

模块8：Tumor heterogeneity, microenvironment & immunoediting

肿瘤异质性来源
克隆选择（线性/分支演化）+ CSCs + TME（CAFs、血管、免疫浸润）。Sources of heterogeneity: clonal selection (linear/branched evolution) + CSCs + TME (CAFs, vessels, immune infiltrates).
免疫编辑三阶段
Elimination → Equilibrium → Escape。Immunoediting phases: Elimination → Equilibrium → Escape.
Elimination
肿瘤新抗原经MHC-I呈递→CD8+ T细胞识别→穿孔素/颗粒酶B或FasL/TRAIL诱导死亡。Elimination: neoantigens via MHC-I → CD8+ T recognition → perforin/granzyme B or FasL/TRAIL-mediated killing.
TMB
肿瘤突变负荷高→更可能产生免疫原性新抗原→更易被T细胞识别。TMB: higher mutational burden → more immunogenic neoantigens → more likely T-cell recognition.
Equilibrium
免疫压力下选择“更不免疫原”的克隆（抗原丢失、MHC-I下调、PD-L1上调）。Equilibrium: immune pressure selects less immunogenic clones (antigen loss, MHC-I down, PD-L1 up).
PD-1/PD-L1轴
PD-L1（肿瘤）结合PD-1（T细胞）→抑制T细胞活化与抗肿瘤功能；IFN-γ可诱导肿瘤PD-L1表达。PD-1/PD-L1 axis: PD-L1 on tumor binds PD-1 on T cells → inhibits activation/effector function; IFN-γ can induce tumor PD-L1.

Escape（肿瘤内在）：抗原丢失、MHC-I下降、calreticulin下降（ICD下降）、↑STAT3/↑Bcl-2、↓FAS/TRAILR、↑CD39/CD73/IDO、↑TGF-β/IL-10。Escape (tumor-intrinsic): antigen loss, ↓MHC-I, ↓calreticulin (↓ICD), ↑STAT3/↑Bcl-2, ↓FAS/TRAILR, ↑CD39/CD73/IDO, ↑TGF-β/IL-10.

Escape（免疫细胞介导）：Tregs、MDSCs、TAM（偏M2）募集与抑制效应T细胞。Escape (immune-mediated): recruitment of Tregs, MDSCs, and TAMs (M2-like) to suppress effector T cells.

Tregs五种抑制
消耗IL-2；颗粒酶/穿孔素杀伤；CD39/CD73把ATP→腺苷；分泌TGF-β/IL-10/IL-35；表达CTLA-4抑制APC激活。Five Treg suppressive mechanisms: consume IL-2; granzyme/perforin killing; CD39/CD73 convert ATP→adenosine; secrete TGF-β/IL-10/IL-35; CTLA-4 inhibits APC activation.
MDSCs
PMN-MDSC（中性粒样）与M-MDSC（单核样）；通过耗竭氨基酸（L-Arg/Trp等）、氧化应激、诱导Tregs等抑制免疫。MDSCs: PMN-MDSC (neutrophil-like) and M-MDSC (monocyte-like); suppress via amino-acid depletion (L-Arg/Trp), oxidative stress, Treg induction, etc.
炎症双刃剑
ROS/TNF-α/IL-6/IL-1β等可提升突变率并通过NFκB/STAT3/AP-1促进存活、增殖、血管生成、侵袭与干性。Inflammation is double-edged: ROS/TNF-α/IL-6/IL-1β can increase mutation and via NFκB/STAT3/AP-1 promote survival, proliferation, angiogenesis, invasion, stemness.

模块9：Therapy, targets, biomarkers, resistance

靶向治疗例子
BCR-ABL（费城染色体）驱动CML；Imatinib设计用于抑制其ATP结合位点。Targeted therapy example: BCR-ABL (Philadelphia chromosome) drives CML; imatinib designed to inhibit its ATP-binding site.
四类生物标志物
易感性（predisposition）/ 诊断（diagnostic）/ 预测（predictive）/ 预后（prognostic）。Four biomarker types: predisposition / diagnostic / predictive / prognostic.
血清肿瘤标志物
通常不用于诊断（健康人与疾病人群重叠大）；更适合随访与复发监测（需要动态曲线）。Serum tumor markers: usually not for diagnosis (high overlap); better for follow-up/recurrence monitoring (needs longitudinal curve).
Liquid biopsy
CTCs、ctDNA、游离miRNA/ncRNA、外泌体/微囊泡、肿瘤教育血小板等。Liquid biopsy: CTCs, ctDNA, cell-free miRNA/ncRNA, extracellular vesicles (exosomes/microvesicles), tumor-educated platelets, etc.
细胞/组织标志物评估
IHC、RT-qPCR、免疫分析；DNA含量/倍体可用流式细胞术（直方图/散点图）分析。Tissue/cellular markers: IHC, RT-qPCR, immunoassays; DNA content/ploidy assessed by flow cytometry (histograms/dot plots).
耐药总框架
内在耐药 vs 获得性耐药；来源：克隆选择、癌症干细胞、肿瘤异质性与微环境。Resistance framework: intrinsic vs acquired; driven by clonal selection, CSCs, heterogeneity and microenvironment.
耐药机制清单
↓药物进入（SLC改变）/ ↑外排（ABC转运体ABCB1=P-gp、ABCG2等）/ 隔离（compartmentalization）/ 靶点改变或扩增 / 代谢适应 / 药物失活或前药激活缺陷 / DNA修复增强 / 表观与ncRNA调控。Resistance mechanisms: ↓influx (SLC changes) / ↑efflux (ABC transporters ABCB1=P-gp, ABCG2) / compartmentalization / target alteration or amplification / metabolic adaptation / drug inactivation or defective prodrug activation / enhanced DNA repair / epigenetic and ncRNA control.
课件举例
AraC需磷酸化才活化；下调相关激酶→耐药；Topo II、β-tubulin、BCR-ABL二次突变都可致靶点耐药。Slide examples: AraC requires phosphorylation; kinase downregulation → resistance; secondary mutations in Topo II, β-tubulin, BCR-ABL drive target resistance.

02Part 1 选择 / 判断题库

Part 1 选择/判断题库（中英对照 + 超短讲解）

一、Introduction & hallmarks（总论+癌症基本概念）

Part1选择题

1) 中文：所有癌症都源于基因突变/基因改变。

EN
All cancers arise from a gene mutation.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：癌症核心是遗传物质改变与其累积。EN: Cancer fundamentally involves accumulated genetic alterations.

2) 中文：遗传性癌症一般比散发性癌症发病更晚。

EN
Hereditary cancers generally have a later onset than sporadic cancers.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：遗传易感通常更早发病。EN: Hereditary predisposition often leads to earlier onset.

3) 中文：“诱导血管生成”和“细胞能量代谢失调”是癌症特征之一。

EN
“Inducing angiogenesis” and “Deregulating cellular energetics” are two hallmarks of cancer.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：它们属于经典/更新版 hallmarks。EN: Both are recognized hallmarks in the hallmark framework.

4) 中文：癌细胞中 RAS 产物被锁定在 GDP 结合（失活）状态，且不需要受体信号也能促分裂。

EN
In cancer cells, the RAS gene product is locked into its GDP-binding shape and does not require a signal at the receptor to promote cell division.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：致癌 RAS 通常锁定在 GTP（活化） 状态。EN: Oncogenic RAS is typically locked in the GTP-bound active state.

5) 中文：Septin 通过加强肌动蛋白纤维，促进癌相关成纤维细胞迁移。

EN
By strengthening actin fibers, septins favor cell migration of cancer-associated fibroblasts.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：septin 可组织/稳定细胞骨架结构从而促迁移。EN: Septins help organize/stabilize cytoskeletal structures supporting migration.

6) 中文：BRCA 基因是最著名的乳腺癌风险相关基因。

EN
BRCA genes are the most well-known genes linked to breast cancer risk.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：BRCA1/2 是经典遗传易感基因。EN: BRCA1/2 are classic hereditary susceptibility genes.

7) 中文：许多肿瘤中 RAS 被锁定在 GDP 结合状态以维持增殖。

EN
In many human tumors, the RAS gene product is locked into its GDP-binding shape to sustain proliferation.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：维持增殖通常是 GTP 结合活化。EN: Sustained signaling usually comes from GTP-bound active RAS.

8) 中文：TNM 是最常用的恶性肿瘤分期/分型系统。

EN
The TNM system is the most widely used classification for describing the growth and spread of malignant tumours.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：T（原发）、N（淋巴结）、M（远处转移）。EN: T (primary), N (nodes), M (metastasis).

9) 中文：癌症多步骤过程中，“严重异型增生”发生在促进期（promotion）。

EN
Cancer is a multistep process where severe dysplasia occurs during the promotion phase.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：促进期多见增殖/早期异常；严重异型增生更接近进展/晚期。EN: Promotion is early expansion; severe dysplasia is typically later progression.

10) 中文：促进期（promotion）可观察到异型增生（dysplasia）。

EN
Dysplasia is observed during the promotion step of cancer.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：promotion 可出现并扩增异常克隆。EN: Promotion expands abnormal clones and can show dysplasia.

11) 中文：所有癌症都源于基因改变。

EN
All cancers arise from changes in genes.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：广义“基因改变”含突变、缺失、扩增等。EN: “Gene changes” broadly include mutations, deletions, amplifications, etc.

12) 中文：癌症是一个多步骤过程。

EN
Cancer is a multistep process.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：需要多次“打击”累积。EN: Multiple hits accumulate over time.

13) 中文：致癌发生是一个多步骤的突变获得过程。

EN
Carcinogenesis is a multistep mutation acquisition process.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：突变/选择/克隆扩增逐步推进。EN: Mutation + selection + clonal expansion drive progression.

14) 中文：癌细胞在血管内皮表面滚动需要选择素（selectins）。

EN
Cancer cells need specific cell adhesion molecules (CAMs), called selectins, to roll at the surface of endothelial cells in vessels before their extravasation.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：滚动黏附常与选择素相关。EN: Rolling adhesion is commonly selectin-mediated.

15) 中文：癌细胞产生的细胞外囊泡一定都来自多囊泡体（MVB）。

EN
Extracellular vesicles (EVs) produced by cancer cells are always derived from multivesicular bodies.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：外泌体来自 MVB，但微囊泡可直接出芽于质膜。EN: Exosomes come from MVBs; microvesicles can bud from the plasma membrane.

16) 中文：伤口愈合实验可用于分析癌细胞迁移。

EN
Cancer cell migration properties can be analyzed in wound healing experiments.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：划痕（scratch/wound healing）是经典迁移实验。EN: Scratch/wound-healing assays are standard migration tests.

17) 中文：蛋白聚糖从不位于细胞膜上。

EN
Proteoglycans are proteins carrying glycosaminoglycans (GAGs) and these proteins are never found at the plasma membrane.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：如 syndecan 是膜蛋白聚糖。EN: Some proteoglycans (e.g., syndecans) are membrane-associated.

18) 中文：EMT 对胚胎发育必需，也与转移形成相关。

EN
The epithelial-mesenchymal transition (EMT) is essential for normal embryonic development and for the formation of metastasis.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：EMT 是发育过程；肿瘤中常被“挪用”促进侵袭。EN: EMT is developmental and can be co-opted for invasion/metastasis.

19) 中文：癌细胞更容易发生细胞死亡。

EN
Cancer cells are more susceptible to cell death.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：癌细胞常获得抗凋亡/抗死亡能力。EN: Cancer cells often acquire resistance to cell death.

20) 中文：癌细胞更耐受细胞死亡。

EN
Cancer cells are more resistant to cell death.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：逃避凋亡是典型特征。EN: Evading apoptosis is a classic cancer capability.

21) 中文：癌细胞可释放 EV 来调控微环境。

EN
Cancer cells can release extracellular vesicles that modulate their microenvironment.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：EV 可携带蛋白/miRNA 等重塑 TME。EN: EVs carry cargo that can remodel the TME.

22) 中文：放疗主要攻击癌细胞 DNA。

EN
Radiation therapy mainly targets the DNA of cancer cells.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：电离辐射导致 DNA 断裂/损伤。EN: Ionizing radiation induces DNA damage (e.g., strand breaks).

23) 中文：化疗只杀癌细胞，不影响正常细胞。

EN
Chemotherapy specifically targets cancer cells without affecting normal cells.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：快速分裂正常细胞（毛囊、骨髓、肠上皮）也会受损。EN: Rapidly dividing normal tissues are also affected.

24) 中文：慢性炎症可促进癌症发生。

EN
Chronic inflammation can contribute to cancer development.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：炎症=ROS、细胞因子、促进增殖与突变。EN: Inflammation promotes ROS/cytokines, proliferation, and mutagenesis.

25) 中文：转移是癌细胞从原发灶扩散到远处部位。

EN
Metastasis is the spread of cancer cells from the primary tumor to distant sites.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：定义题，直接背。EN: This is the definition.

26) 中文：微转移灶是小且临床上难检测的转移病灶。

EN
Micrometastases are small, clinically undetectable metastatic lesions.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：体积小/数量少，常需高敏检测。EN: They’re tiny and may require sensitive detection.

27) 中文：原位癌（in situ）已经侵入周围组织。

EN
In situ tumors have already invaded surrounding tissues.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：in situ = 未突破基底膜/未侵袭。EN: In situ means no invasion beyond the basement membrane.

28) 中文：肿瘤异质性指同一肿瘤内存在遗传与表型不同的细胞群。

EN
Tumor heterogeneity refers to the presence of genetically and phenotypically diverse cancer cell populations within the same tumor.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：同肿瘤不同克隆/不同状态。EN: Multiple clones/states coexist within one tumor.

二、Epigenetics & chromatin（DNA 甲基化、组蛋白修饰、lncRNA）

Part1选择题

29) 中文：DNMT 催化在胞嘧啶第 5 位碳加甲基。

EN
DNMTs catalyze the addition of a methyl group to the fifth carbon position of cytosines.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：形成 5mC（5-甲基胞嘧啶）。EN: This produces 5mC (5-methylcytosine).

30) 中文：癌细胞常出现全基因组 5mC 总体下降。

EN
In cancer cells, there is a global loss of 5-methylcytosine.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：常见“全局低甲基化 + 局部高甲基化”。EN: Typical pattern: global hypomethylation + focal hypermethylation.

31) 中文：启动子高甲基化会导致基因沉默。

EN
Hypermethylation of promotor genes results in gene silencing.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：尤其沉默抑癌基因启动子。EN: Often silences tumor suppressor gene promoters.

32) 中文：DNA 低甲基化可导致致癌基因异常再激活。

EN
DNA hypomethylation can induce aberrant oncogene re-activation.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：低甲基化可解除抑制、激活转座元件/致癌通路。EN: Hypomethylation can de-repress oncogenic programs/transposons.

33) 中文：UHRF1 高表达可抑制抑癌基因。

EN
High expression of UHRF1 inhibits tumor-suppressor genes.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：UHRF1 维持甲基化/沉默状态。EN: UHRF1 helps maintain methylation-linked repression.

34) 中文：组蛋白 H3 尾部赖氨酸常发生甲基化或乙酰化。

EN
Histone H3 tail lysine residues are frequently subject to methylation or acetylation.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：H3K4/H3K9/H3K27 等是高频位点。EN: Common sites include H3K4, H3K9, H3K27.

35) 中文：组蛋白甲基化既可激活也可抑制转录。

EN
Histone methylation can either promote or inhibit gene expression.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：取决于位点与甲基化程度。EN: It depends on the residue and methylation state.

36) 中文：CBP 与 p300 是组蛋白乙酰化“写入酶”。

EN
CBP and p300 are histone acetylation writers.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：它们是 HAT（histone acetyltransferases）。EN: They are HATs (histone acetyltransferases).

37) 中文：多种 HDAC 抑制剂作为表观遗传药物正在研究。

EN
Various HDAC inhibitors are under investigation as epigenetic drugs.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：HDACi 可提高乙酰化、改变转录。EN: HDACi increase acetylation and alter transcription.

38) 中文：BET 蛋白是乙酰化赖氨酸“读取蛋白”。

EN
BET proteins are acetyl-lysine recognition proteins.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：bromodomain 识别乙酰化位点。EN: Bromodomains recognize acetyl-lysines.

39) 中文：DNA 高甲基化会激活致癌基因。

EN
DNA hypermethylation induces oncogene activation.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：启动子高甲基化多导致沉默；致癌基因激活更常见于低甲基化。EN: Promoter hypermethylation usually silences; oncogene activation is more linked to hypomethylation.

40) 中文：TET 酶参与 DNA 去甲基化过程。

EN
TET enzymes are DNA-demethylating enzymes.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：TET 通过逐步氧化 5mC 促进去甲基化。EN: TET oxidizes 5mC stepwise to promote demethylation.

41) 中文：组蛋白 N 端尾部可发生甲基化、乙酰化、磷酸化或泛素化。

EN
N-terminal histone tails can be methylated, acetylated, phosphorylated or ubiquitinated.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：典型“组蛋白密码”。EN: This is the classic “histone code”.

42) 中文：HAT（组蛋白乙酰转移酶）失调与肿瘤发生或差预后相关。

EN
Deregulation of histone acetyltransferases is associated with tumorigenesis or poor prognosis.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：乙酰化异常可重编程转录。EN: Aberrant acetylation can reprogram transcription.

43) 中文：某些 lncRNA 在肿瘤中过表达可预测转移。

EN
Overexpression of specific lncRNAs in tumors can be predictive of metastasis.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：lncRNA 可作为预后/预测标志物。EN: lncRNAs can serve as prognostic/predictive markers.

44) 中文：表观遗传调控不改变 DNA 序列，却能改变表达。

EN
Epigenetics regulates gene expression without altering the DNA sequence.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：改变“读法”而非“字母”。EN: It changes how DNA is read, not the sequence itself.

45) 中文：UHRF1 高表达会导致 DNA 高甲基化。

EN
High expression of UHRF1 results in DNA hypermethylation.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：促进维持甲基化与基因沉默。EN: It supports maintenance methylation and repression.

46) 中文：lncRNA NEAT1 过表达可预测转移发生。

EN
Overexpression of the lncRNA NEAT1 is predictive of metastasis occurrence.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：作为转移相关 lncRNA 的经典例子。EN: NEAT1 is a commonly cited metastasis-associated lncRNA.

47) 中文：表观遗传重编程最近被认为是癌症新“标志”之一。

EN
Epigenetic reprogramming has been recently recognized as a new hallmark of cancer.
答案/Answer
T ⚠️ — 中文讲解：多数课件/更新版 hallmarks 会强调这一点；以你老师口径为准。EN: Many updated hallmark views emphasize this; follow your course wording.

48) 中文：启动子甲基化通常会激活基因表达。

EN
DNA methylation at promoters usually results in gene expression activation.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：启动子甲基化通常抑制转录。EN: Promoter methylation usually represses transcription.

49) 中文：不同组蛋白甲基化位点决定激活或抑制。

EN
Different histone methylation sites determine gene activation or repression.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：例：H3K4me（激活）vs H3K27me（抑制）。EN: e.g., H3K4me (active) vs H3K27me (repressive).

50) 中文：一些 lncRNA 可作预测标志物用于个体化治疗。

EN
Some long noncoding RNAs can be predictive markers that could be used for personalized therapy.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：预测标志物=预测疗效/获益。EN: Predictive markers forecast treatment response/benefit.

51) 中文：TET 酶参与组蛋白去甲基化。

EN
TET enzymes are involved in histone demethylation.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：TET 主要作用于 DNA 去甲基化通路。EN: TET primarily functions in DNA demethylation pathways.

52) 中文：DNA 去甲基化涉及逐步氧化反应。

EN
DNA demethylation involves stepwise oxidation reactions.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：5mC→5hmC→5fC→5caC 等。EN: 5mC→5hmC→5fC→5caC, etc.

53) 中文：DNA 甲基转移酶属于“擦除酶”。

EN
DNA methyltransferases are “Erasers”.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：DNMT 是 writer（写入）。EN: DNMTs are writers, not erasers.

54) 中文：lncRNA 通常长度为几百个核苷酸以上。

EN
Long noncoding RNAs are over a few hundred nucleotides in length.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：常用阈值是 >200 nt。EN: A common cutoff is >200 nt.

55) 中文：某些 lncRNA 过表达可调控染色质结构与基因表达。

EN
Overexpression of specific long noncoding RNAs can modulate chromatin structure and gene expression in cancer.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：lncRNA 可招募表观遗传复合体。EN: lncRNAs can recruit epigenetic complexes to chromatin.

56) 中文：DNA 甲基化模式可在细胞分裂中被“遗传”。

EN
DNA methylation patterns can be heritable during cell division.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：复制后由维持型甲基化机制传递。EN: Maintenance methylation propagates patterns after replication.

57) 中文：HDAC 去除乙酰基，倾向抑制基因表达。

EN
Histone deacetylases (HDACs) remove acetyl groups and tend to repress gene expression.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：去乙酰→染色质更紧→转录下降。EN: Deacetylation tightens chromatin → reduced transcription.

三、Cytoskeleton & migration / ECM / EMT（细胞骨架+极性+基质+转移）

Part1选择题

58) 中文：Septin 丝可作为扩散屏障。

EN
Septin filaments can act as diffusion barriers.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：可限制膜蛋白/脂质侧向扩散。EN: They can restrict lateral diffusion in membranes.

59) 中文：肌动蛋白丝参与内吞。

EN
Actin filaments are involved in endocytosis.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：为内吞提供力与结构支撑。EN: Actin provides force/structure for endocytosis.

60) 中文：微管对细胞器分子运输很关键。

EN
Microtubules are essential for organelle molecular transport.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：依赖 kinesin/dynein 的轨道运输。EN: They serve as tracks for kinesin/dynein transport.

61) 中文：中间丝参与细胞抗应激。

EN
Intermediate filaments play a role in cell stress management.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：提供机械强度、抗拉伸。EN: They provide mechanical resilience.

62) 中文：四大细胞骨架之间存在动态串话。

EN
Dynamic crosstalks occur between the four cytoskeletons.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：actin/MT/IF/septin 互相调控。EN: Actin/MT/IF/septin networks coordinate.

63) 中文：Dynein 与 kinesin 是微管特异性马达蛋白。

EN
Dynein and kinesin are molecular motors specific to microtubules.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：微管运输“双马达”。EN: The two key microtubule motors.

64) 中文：Vinblastine 是长春花碱类微管去稳定剂。

EN
Vinblastin is a microtubule-destabilizing agent of the vinca-alkaloid family.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：抑制微管聚合。EN: It inhibits microtubule polymerization.

65) 中文：G-actin 的 “G” 指它受小 GTP 酶调控。

EN
In “G-actin”, the “G” means that actin is regulated by small GTPases in migrating cells.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：G=globular（球状单体）。EN: G stands for globular monomer.

66) 中文：神经丝（neurofilaments）与“精神类疾病”相关。

EN
Neurofilaments are intermediate filaments involved in neuropsychiatric diseases.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：更典型是神经退行/轴突损伤相关（此表述过于不准）。EN: More classically linked to neurodegeneration/axonal injury; wording is inaccurate.

67) 中文：Septin 丝直径约 25 nm。

EN
Septins form hetero-hexameric or -octameric filaments of 25 nm diameter.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：25 nm 是微管典型直径；septin 更细。EN: 25 nm is typical for microtubules; septins are thinner.

68) 中文：成核（nucleation）总是比聚合更容易。

EN
The nucleation step is always easier than polymerization of filaments.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：成核通常是限速步骤，更难。EN: Nucleation is typically rate-limiting and harder.

69) 中文：切割酶只在纤维末端起作用。

EN
Severing enzymes act specifically at the tip of filaments.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：多在丝的“内部”切割产生新端。EN: They often cut within filaments to create new ends.

70) 中文：中间丝是无极性的弹性结构。

EN
Intermediate filaments are non-polarized elastic structures.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：IF 无 +/− 端方向性。EN: IFs lack plus/minus polarity.

71) 中文：病原体感染时可利用宿主 F-actin 和/或 septin。

EN
Pathogens can use cellular F-actin and/or septin filaments during infection.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：如 actin “彗星尾”推动传播。EN: e.g., actin comet tails enable motility/spread.

72) 中文：c

EN
F-actin is a polarized double helix of about 8 nm wide.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：肌动蛋白丝有 +/− 端。EN: Actin filaments have plus/minus ends.

73) 中文：微管是中空管状结构，直径约 25 nm。

EN
Microtubules are hollow tubes with a diameter of about 25 nm.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：典型结构数据题。EN: Standard structural fact.

74) 中文：8 个四聚体形成中间丝的 ULF（单位长度片段）。

EN
Association of 8 tetramers forms the unpolarized unit length fragment (ULF) of an intermediate filament.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：IF 组装先形成 ULF。EN: IF assembly proceeds via ULF formation.

75) 中文：kinesin 通常向微管 + 端行走。

EN
Kinesins walk toward the + end of microtubules.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：常用于顺行运输。EN: Typically mediates anterograde transport.

76) 中文：dynein 用 GTP 供能在微管上运动。

EN
Dyneins use GTP as a source of energy to run on microtubules.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：dynein 用 ATP。EN: Dynein is ATP-powered.

77) 中文：有丝分裂时，极间微管与动粒相连。

EN
During mitosis, interpolar microtubules are associated with kinetochores.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：动粒连接的是 kinetochore microtubules，不是 interpolar。EN: Kinetochores bind kinetochore MTs, not interpolar MTs.

78) 中文：角蛋白（cytokeratins）这种中间丝有助于细胞迁移。

EN
Intermediate filaments called cytokeratins allow for cell migration.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：迁移/EMT 更常见 vimentin 上调；角蛋白更偏上皮稳定。EN: Migration/EMT often involves vimentin upregulation; keratins support epithelial stability.

79) 中文：紫杉烷（taxanes）属于微管稳定剂。

EN
Taxanes belong to the family of microtubule stabilizing agents.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：稳定微管、阻断有丝分裂。EN: They stabilize microtubules and block mitosis.

80) 中文：中间丝参与细胞抗应激。

EN
Intermediate filaments play a role in cell stress management.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：同 61，重复背即可。EN: Same idea as #61.

81) 中文：细胞-细胞连接只存在于上皮细胞。

EN
Cell-cell junctions only concern epithelial cells.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：多种细胞也有细胞连接（如内皮、心肌等）。EN: Many cell types form cell-cell junctions (endothelial, cardiac, etc.).

82) 中文：细胞-基质连接只存在于上皮细胞。

EN
Cell-matrix junctions only concern epithelial cells.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：几乎所有贴壁细胞都有整合素介导黏附。EN: Most adherent cells have integrin-based matrix adhesions.

83) 中文：癌细胞可以改造/重塑 ECM。

EN
Cancer cells can modify the extracellular matrix (ECM).
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：通过 MMP、分泌 ECM 组分等。EN: Via MMPs, ECM secretion/remodeling, etc.

84) 中文：基底板（basal lamina）就是上皮细胞的基底质膜。

EN
The basal lamina is the basal plasma membrane of epithelial cells.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：basal lamina 是特化 ECM，不是质膜。EN: The basal lamina is specialized ECM, not membrane.

85) 中文：内皮细胞属于上皮样细胞，在结缔组织中形成毛细血管/血管。

EN
Endothelial cells are epithelial cells that form capillaries in the connective tissue.
答案/Answer
T（按课件表述） — 中文讲解：更准确说“上皮样单层”，形成血管内衬。EN: More precisely: an epithelial-like monolayer lining blood vessels.

86) 中文：ECM 可由成纤维细胞等合成。

EN
The extracellular matrix is synthesized by cells like fibroblasts.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：fibroblast 是主要 ECM 生产者。EN: Fibroblasts are major ECM producers.

87) 中文：ECM 中可以含有细胞外囊泡。

EN
The extracellular matrix can contain extracellular vesicles.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：EV 可滞留于基质并发挥信号作用。EN: EVs can be retained in ECM and signal locally.

88) 中文：ECM 可调控癌细胞迁移。

EN
The extracellular matrix can regulate cancer cell migration.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：硬度、配体、纤维排列都会影响迁移。EN: Stiffness, ligands, and fiber alignment shape migration.

89) 中文：ECM 可被细胞内吞。

EN
The extracellular matrix can be internalized by cells.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：受体介导内吞/重塑时可发生。EN: Receptor-mediated uptake can occur during remodeling.

90) 中文：ECM 不含糖蛋白。

EN
The extracellular matrix is devoid of glycoproteins.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：ECM 富含糖蛋白（fibronectin、laminin 等）。EN: ECM contains many glycoproteins (fibronectin, laminin, etc.).

91) 中文：EMT 期间含 occludin/claudin 的紧密连接会解体。

EN
Tight junctions containing Occludins or Claudins disassemble during EMT.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：EMT 的核心变化之一是细胞连接丢失。EN: Loss of junctions is a key EMT feature.

92) 中文：黏着连接中 E-cadherin 被 N-cadherin 与 vimentin 取代。

EN
In adherence junctions, E-cadherins are replaced by N-cadherins and vimentin.
答案/Answer
T（按“cadherin switch + vimentin↑”理解） — 中文讲解：EMT 常见 E↓、N↑、vimentin↑。EN: EMT often shows E-cadherin↓, N-cadherin↑, vimentin↑.

93) 中文：桥粒的“integrin”会被黏着斑 integrin 取代以促进迁移。

EN
Desmosomal integrins are replaced by focal adhesion integrins to allow for cell migration.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：桥粒主要是 cadherins，不是 integrins。EN: Desmosomes are cadherin-based, not integrin-based.

94) 中文：EMT 中 TGF-β 被激活/参与诱导。

EN
TGF-β is activated during EMT.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：TGF-β 是经典 EMT 诱导通路。EN: TGF-β is a canonical EMT inducer.

95) 中文：集体迁移需要完全 EMT。

EN
Complete EMT is required for collective migration of cancer cells.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：集体迁移常见“部分 EMT/混合表型”。EN: Collective migration often involves partial EMT/hybrid states.

96) 中文：迁移细胞前缘富含丝状伪足与微管。

EN
The leading edge of migrating cells is enriched in filopodia and microtubules.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：前缘 actin 驱动突起，MT 支持定向运输/极性。EN: Actin drives protrusions; MTs support polarity/traffic.

97) 中文：CAF 沉积 ECM，有利于上皮下结缔组织内细胞迁移。

EN
Cancer-associated fibroblasts (CAFs) deposit extracellular matrix… favor cell migration in the connective tissue below the epithelium.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：ECM “轨道化/硬化”可促侵袭迁移。EN: ECM alignment/stiffening can promote invasion.

98) 中文：基底板是薄而特化的 ECM，癌细胞侵入结缔组织前需破坏它。

EN
The basal lamina is a thin specialized extracellular matrix… cancer cells must digest or break to invade the connective tissue.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：突破基底膜是侵袭关键一步。EN: Breaching the basement membrane is key for invasion.

99) 中文：侵入血管（intravasation）指癌细胞离开血流去远处定植。

EN
Intravasation is the process where cancer cells exit the bloodstream to colonize a distant metastatic site.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：intravasation=入血；出血管叫 extravasation。EN: Intravasation = entry into blood; extravasation = exit.

100) 中文：有丝分裂细胞中所有连接都被强烈增强。

EN
All cellular junctions are strongly reinforced in mitotic cells.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：分裂时细胞圆整，许多连接会重塑/减弱。EN: During mitosis cells round up and junctions are remodeled/weakened.

101) 中文：上皮细胞顶底极性由 PIP2/PIP3 的不对称分布建立与维持。

EN
PIP2 and PIP3… are asymmetrically distributed to initiate and maintain apico-basal polarity of epithelial cells.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：膜脂不对称是极性标志之一。EN: Lipid asymmetry is a hallmark of polarity.

102) 中文：基底板缺陷可导致交界性大疱性表皮松解症等遗传病。

EN
Basal lamina defects can be involved in genetic diseases such as junctional epidermolysis bullosa.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：基底膜/连接复合体缺陷→皮肤脆弱起疱。EN: Basement membrane/adhesion defects cause skin fragility and blistering.

四、Cell cycle, quiescence, senescence（细胞周期+静止+衰老）

Part1选择题

103) 中文：Rb 是致癌基因。

EN
Rb is an oncogene.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：Rb 是经典抑癌基因（控制 G1/S）。EN: Rb is a classic tumor suppressor controlling G1/S.

104) 中文：v-Src 和 c-Src 都是致癌基因。

EN
v-Src and c-Src are oncogenes.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：v-Src 是 oncogene；c-Src 是 proto-oncogene。EN: v-Src is an oncogene; c-Src is a proto-oncogene.

105) 中文：p53 是抑癌基因。

EN
p53 is a tumor suppressor.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：DNA 损伤响应“守门人”。EN: A key guardian of the DNA-damage response.

106) 中文：p16/INK4 是抑癌基因。

EN
p16/INK4 is a tumor suppressor.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：抑制 CDK4/6→维持 Rb 活性。EN: Inhibits CDK4/6 to keep Rb active.

107) 中文：致癌发生是多步骤突变获得过程。

EN
Carcinogenesis is a multistep mutation acquisition process.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：同 13。EN: Same as #13.

108) 中文：增殖细胞 Ki67 阳性；G0/静止或衰老细胞 Ki67 阴性。

EN
Cycling cells are Ki67 positive, in contrast to G0/quiescent or senescent cells.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：Ki67 是增殖标志物。EN: Ki67 is a proliferation marker.

109) 中文：p21/p16 这些 CDKI 会被促分裂信号抑制，从而允许 Rb 磷酸化与 E2F 转录。

EN
The CDKIs p21 and p16 are… allowing Rb phosphorylation and E2F-mediated transcription…
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：CDKI 的核心作用是阻断CDK→抑制 Rb 磷酸化/E2F。EN: CDKIs block CDKs, limiting Rb phosphorylation/E2F.

110) 中文：APC/C-Cdc20 早于 APC/C-Cdh1 工作，促 cyclin B 降解以进入后期。

EN
The ubiquitin ligase APC/C Cdc20 operates before APC/C Cdh1… allow for anaphase.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：Cdc20 触发中期到后期转换。EN: Cdc20 triggers the metaphase–anaphase transition.

111) 中文：体内所有细胞都对称分裂。

EN
In the body, all cells divide symmetrically.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：干细胞常非对称分裂。EN: Stem cells often divide asymmetrically.

112) 中文：TMB（肿瘤突变负荷）是遗传性癌症特有的。

EN
The tumor mutational burden (TMB) is specific of hereditary cancer.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：TMB 受多因素影响，不专属于遗传癌。EN: TMB varies widely and is not specific to hereditary cancers.

113) 中文：衰老细胞是仍在分裂且分泌 SASP 的细胞。

EN
Senescent cells are dividing cells that produce SASP.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：衰老细胞不再分裂，但可分泌 SASP。EN: Senescent cells do not divide but can secrete SASP.

114) 中文：初级纤毛是 G0/静止细胞中的感觉结构。

EN
The primary cilium is a sensory structure found in G0/quiescent cells.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：进入细胞周期时常解体。EN: It often disassembles upon cell-cycle re-entry.

115) 中文：有丝分裂时纺锤体取向对组织结构很关键。

EN
Spindle orientation during mitosis is crucial for tissue organization.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：决定细胞命运与层次排列。EN: It influences fate and tissue architecture.

116) 中文：人体体细胞是二倍体，只有 23 条染色体。

EN
Human somatic cells are diploid with 23 chromosomes.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：二倍体是 46 条（23 对）。EN: Diploid means 46 chromosomes (23 pairs).

117) 中文：间期中，DNA 复制与中心体复制都发生在 S 期。

EN
During the interphase, centrosome duplication and DNA replication both occur at the S phase.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：S 期复制 DNA，同时启动中心体复制。EN: S phase replicates DNA and initiates centrosome duplication.

118) 中文：47 条染色体的人细胞是非整倍体。

EN
A human cell with 47 chromosomes is aneuploid.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：染色体数目不为 46 即为非整倍体。EN: Any deviation from 46 is aneuploidy.

119) 中文：非整倍体在癌细胞中很常见。

EN
Aneuploidy is frequently observed in cancer cells.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：染色体不稳定性（CIN）常见。EN: Chromosomal instability is common in tumors.

120) 中文：中心体扩增可促进肿瘤染色体不稳定。

EN
Centrosome amplification can contribute to chromosomal instability in tumors.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：多极纺锤体→错分离。EN: Multipolar spindles lead to mis-segregation.

121) 中文：静止（quiescence）是可逆的 G0 阻滞。

EN
Quiescence is a reversible cell cycle arrest (G0 state).
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：可在刺激下重返细胞周期。EN: Cells can re-enter the cycle upon stimulation.

122) 中文：衰老细胞不可逆停滞但仍代谢活跃。

EN
Senescent cells are irreversibly arrested but metabolically active.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：仍可分泌 SASP。EN: They remain active and can secrete SASP.

123) 中文：检查点监测 DNA 损伤与纺锤体组装以维持基因组稳定。

EN
Cell cycle checkpoints monitor DNA damage and spindle assembly to preserve genomic integrity.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：G1/S、G2/M、SAC 等。EN: Includes G1/S, G2/M, and the spindle assembly checkpoint.

124) 中文：Cyclin 过表达可驱动失控增殖。

EN
Overexpression of cyclins can drive uncontrolled proliferation.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：cyclin-CDK 过强→Rb 失控。EN: Excess cyclin-CDK pushes cells past checkpoints.

125) 中文：p53 功能缺失会削弱 DNA 损伤响应，从而促进肿瘤进展。

EN
Loss of p53 function facilitates tumor progression by impairing DNA damage response.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：损伤不修复也继续分裂。EN: Cells proliferate despite damage when p53 is lost.

五、Metabolism & angiogenesis（代谢+血管生成）

Part1选择题

126) 中文：CAF 可进行有氧糖酵解。

EN
Cancer-associated fibroblasts perform aerobic glycolysis.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：可产生乳酸供肿瘤细胞利用（代谢耦联）。EN: They can produce lactate to fuel tumor cells (metabolic coupling).

127) 中文：Warburg 效应由 HIF-1α、c-Myc、p53 通路“激活”共同触发。

EN
Signals that trigger the Warburg effect result from activation of HIF-1α, c-Myc and p53 pathways.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：Warburg 常见 HIF-1α/c-Myc↑ + p53 失活。EN: Warburg often involves HIF-1α/c-Myc up + p53 loss.

128) 中文：肿瘤细胞与微环境细胞之间可通过 MCT 进行乳酸穿梭。

EN
…shuttling of lactate through monocarboxylate transporters.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：MCT1/MCT4 负责乳酸进出。EN: MCT1/MCT4 mediate lactate transport.

129) 中文：VEGF 是重要促血管生成因子。

EN
VEGF is a prominent pro-angiogenic factor.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：缺氧→HIF-1α→VEGF。EN: Hypoxia → HIF-1α → VEGF.

130) 中文：线粒体故障通过稳定 p53 导致 VEGF 过表达。

EN
Mitochondria malfunction causes VEGF overexpression through p53 stabilization.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：p53 稳定通常更偏抑制肿瘤与血管生成。EN: Stabilized p53 tends to oppose tumor progression/angiogenesis.

131) 中文：HIF-1α 信号可导致 VEGF 表达上调。

EN
Increased VEGF expression results from HIF-1α signaling.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：缺氧条件下经典轴。EN: Canonical hypoxia response pathway.

132) 中文：新生血管生成需要增殖、迁移与连接形成的协调。

EN
Neoangiogenesis requires coordinated cell proliferation, migration and junction formation.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：内皮芽生长+管腔形成+稳定化。EN: Sprouting, lumen formation, and stabilization are coordinated.

133) 中文：果糖-2,6-二磷酸会刺激血管生成。

EN
Angiogenesis is stimulated by fructose-2,6-bisphosphate.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：F2,6BP 主要调糖酵解，不是血管生成核心刺激物。EN: F2,6BP mainly regulates glycolysis, not angiogenesis per se.

134) 中文：缺氧会促进新生血管生成。

EN
Neo-angiogenesis is promoted by hypoxia.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：缺氧诱导 VEGF 等促血管因子。EN: Hypoxia induces VEGF and other pro-angiogenic factors.

135) 中文：VEGF 受体信号涉及受体胞质段酪氨酸磷酸化。

EN
VEGF signaling involves phosphorylation of tyrosines in the cytoplasmic domain of the receptor.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：VEGFR 是受体酪氨酸激酶。EN: VEGFR is a receptor tyrosine kinase.

136) 中文：CAF 主要是合成代谢（anabolic）。

EN
Cancer-associated fibroblasts are mostly anabolic.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：常被描述为更“分解代谢/产乳酸”。EN: Often described as more catabolic/lactate-producing.

137) 中文：在分解代谢型肿瘤细胞中，葡萄糖→丙酮酸→进入 TCA 循环。

EN
In catabolic tumor cells, glucose is transformed into pyruvate to feed the Krebs/TCA cycle.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：这类细胞更偏氧化磷酸化。EN: These cells rely more on oxidative metabolism.

138) 中文：乳酸外排会酸化肿瘤细胞外环境。

EN
Lactate excretion acidifies the tumoral extracellular medium.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：酸性微环境促侵袭与免疫抑制。EN: Acidic TME promotes invasion and immune suppression.

139) 中文：缺氧与营养剥夺会使癌细胞变得更合成代谢。

EN
Oxygen and nutrient deprivation cause cancer cells to become anabolic.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：缺氧常推动糖酵解与应激生存，并不等同“更合成”。EN: Hypoxia often shifts to glycolysis/stress survival, not necessarily anabolic growth.

140) 中文：Warburg 效应细胞的糖酵解主要产物是乳酸。

EN
In cells that display the Warburg effect, lactate is the main product of glycolysis.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：即使有氧也偏乳酸发酵。EN: Aerobic glycolysis favors lactate production.

141) 中文：HIF-1α 的降解会促进 Warburg 效应。

EN
Degradation of HIF-1 contributes to the Warburg effect.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：Warburg 更依赖 HIF-1α 稳定而非降解。EN: Warburg is promoted by HIF-1α stabilization, not degradation.

142) 中文：细胞内乳酸也可作为信号因子。

EN
Intracellular lactate functions as a signaling factor.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：乳酸不仅是废物，也能参与信号/表观调控。EN: Lactate can signal and affect gene regulation.

143) 中文：肿瘤血管常异常且渗漏。

EN
Tumor blood vessels are often abnormal and leaky.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：结构紊乱、通透性高。EN: Disorganized structure and high permeability are common.

144) 中文：抗血管生成治疗目标是阻断或“正常化”肿瘤血管。

EN
Angiogenesis inhibitors aim to normalize or block tumor vasculature.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：既可减少血供，也可短暂改善灌注。EN: They can reduce vessels or transiently normalize perfusion.

145) 中文：肿瘤缺氧可诱导促血管生成因子表达。

EN
Hypoxia in tumors can induce expression of pro-angiogenic factors.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：HIF-1α 上调 VEGF 等。EN: HIF-1α induces VEGF and others.

146) 中文：肿瘤细胞与基质细胞之间可发生代谢耦联。

EN
Metabolic coupling can occur between cancer cells and stromal cells.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：乳酸/丙酮酸等在细胞间“互供”。EN: Metabolites (lactate/pyruvate) can be exchanged.

147) 中文：谷氨酰胺可作为增殖肿瘤细胞的替代碳源。

EN
Glutamine can serve as an alternative carbon source for proliferating tumor cells.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：谷氨酰胺分解进入 TCA（补碳）。EN: Glutaminolysis feeds the TCA cycle (anaplerosis).

148) 中文：糖酵解通量升高可支持快速分裂细胞的生物合成。

EN
Increased glycolytic flux supports biomass production in rapidly dividing cancer cells.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：提供核苷酸/脂质/氨基酸前体。EN: Provides precursors for nucleotides, lipids, amino acids.

149) 中文：癌细胞的线粒体呼吸总是关闭的。

EN
Mitochondrial respiration is always shut down in cancer cells.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：许多癌细胞仍依赖 OXPHOS。EN: Many cancers still rely on oxidative phosphorylation.

150) 中文：脂肪酸氧化可帮助癌细胞在应激下存活。

EN
Fatty acid oxidation can contribute to cancer cell survival under stress.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：FAO 提供 ATP/NADPH 等支持。EN: FAO provides ATP and redox support.

151) 中文：抗血管生成治疗可短暂正常化血管并改善药物递送。

EN
Anti-angiogenic therapies can transiently “normalize” tumor vessels and improve drug delivery.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：存在“正常化窗口”。EN: There can be a “normalization window”.

152) 中文：乳酸可作为氧化型肿瘤细胞的燃料。

EN
Lactate can act as a fuel for oxidative tumor cells.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：乳酸→丙酮酸→TCA。EN: Lactate → pyruvate → TCA.

153) 中文：低氧下 HIF-1α 被稳定并促进血管生成。

EN
HIF-1α is stabilized under low oxygen conditions and promotes angiogenesis.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：核心轴：Hypoxia→HIF-1α→VEGF。EN: Core axis: Hypoxia → HIF-1α → VEGF.

六、Therapy, biomarkers & resistance（标志物+个体化治疗+耐药）

Part1选择题

154) 中文：预测性生物标志物 = 易感/倾向标志物。

EN
A predictive biomarker is the same as a predisposition marker.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：预测性=预测疗效；易感性=预测患病风险。EN: Predictive = response; predisposition = risk.

155) 中文：血液肿瘤标志物可用于确诊。

EN
A bloodstream tumor marker can be used for diagnosis.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：多用于辅助/监测，不足以单独确诊。EN: Often for monitoring/support, not standalone diagnosis.

156) 中文：CTCs 可用于检测微小残留病灶（MRD）。

EN
CTCs allow the detection of minimal residual disease.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：血中残留肿瘤细胞提示复发风险。EN: Residual circulating tumor cells can indicate relapse risk.

157) 中文：组织 ER 水平可指导乳腺癌个体化治疗。

EN
Tissular estrogen receptor levels allow personalized therapy of breast cancers.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：ER 阳性→内分泌/抗雌激素治疗。EN: ER+ guides endocrine/anti-estrogen therapy.

158) 中文：表观遗传改变可能导致耐药。

EN
Epigenetic alterations may contribute to drug resistance.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：改变靶点/转运/凋亡通路表达。EN: Alters expression of targets, transporters, apoptosis pathways.

159) 中文：诊断标志物可用于随访肿瘤进展。

EN
Diagnostic cancer biomarkers allow following cancer progression in individuals.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：随访进展更偏“监测/预后/预测”用途。EN: Progress monitoring is more prognostic/monitoring use.

160) 中文：通过识别 CTC 可随访残留病灶。

EN
The residual disease is followed by identifying circulating tumor cells.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：CTC/ctDNA 都可用于 MRD 监测。EN: CTCs (and ctDNA) can track MRD.

161) 中文：乳腺癌中抗雌激素治疗属于个体化治疗。

EN
Anti-estrogen treatment is a personalized therapy.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：依据 ER 状态选择。EN: Chosen based on ER status.

162) 中文：肿瘤活检的 DNA 含量（倍体）是诊断标志之一。

EN
The DNA content of a cancer biopsy is a diagnostic marker.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：可用流式测倍体/细胞周期分布。EN: Flow cytometry can assess ploidy/cell-cycle distribution.

163) 中文：精准肿瘤学来自多组学数据的解释整合。

EN
Precision oncology results from the interpretation of multiple “omics” data.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：基因组+转录组+蛋白组等。EN: Integrates genomics, transcriptomics, proteomics, etc.

164) 中文：组织 DNA 含量测定靠 PCR。

EN
Tissular DNA content measurement is achieved by PCR.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：DNA 含量/倍体更常用 流式细胞术。EN: DNA content/ploidy is typically measured by flow cytometry.

165) 中文：流式细胞术是白血病分型的重要工具。

EN
Flow cytometry is a major tool for phenotyping leukemias.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：用表面标志物分群定型。EN: Uses immunophenotyping markers.

166) 中文：残留病灶可通过血样确定。

EN
Residual cancer disease is determined from blood samples.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：如 CTC/ctDNA；血液肿瘤更典型。EN: Via CTCs/ctDNA; especially in hematologic settings.

167) 中文：肿瘤细胞线粒体 DNA 含量可作为肿瘤标志物。

EN
The mitochondrial DNA content of tumor cells is a cancer biomarker.
答案/Answer
T ⚠️ — 中文讲解：更多是研究/分型相关标志物，考试按课件口径背。EN: Often research-associated; follow your course framing.

168) 中文：耐药可能来自靶点突变。

EN
Resistance can involve mutation(s) in drug target(s).
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：靶点构象变化→药物结合下降。EN: Target mutations reduce drug binding.

169) 中文：靶向治疗旨在抑制特定致癌驱动因子。

EN
Targeted therapies inhibit specific oncogenic drivers.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：如 EGFR、BRAF、ALK 等。EN: e.g., EGFR, BRAF, ALK.

170) 中文：联合治疗可延缓或避免耐药。

EN
Combination therapies can help prevent or delay resistance.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：多通路同时打击降低逃逸概率。EN: Multi-target pressure reduces escape routes.

171) 中文：治疗后监测 MRD 可预测复发。

EN
MRD can be monitored after treatment to predict relapse.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：MRD 阳性常提示复发风险高。EN: MRD positivity often predicts higher relapse risk.

172) 中文：标志物可按用途分为预测、诊断、预后等。

EN
Biomarkers can be predictive, diagnostic, or prognostic depending on their use.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：同一分子在不同场景可扮演不同角色。EN: The same marker can serve different roles depending on context.

七、Cancer cell deaths（凋亡、坏死、自噬、程序性坏死等）

Part1选择题

173) 中文：癌细胞更容易死亡。

EN
Cancer cells are more susceptible to cell death.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：癌细胞常获得抗死亡机制。EN: Cancer cells often acquire death-resistance mechanisms.

174) 中文：癌细胞更耐受死亡。

EN
Cancer cells are more resistant to cell death.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：逃避凋亡是核心能力之一。EN: Evading apoptosis is a core cancer capability.

175) 中文：Bcl-2 家族是内源性凋亡通路关键因子。

EN
Bcl-2 family proteins are key players of the intrinsic pathway of apoptosis.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：调控线粒体外膜通透化（MOMP）。EN: They regulate mitochondrial outer membrane permeabilization.

176) 中文：Bcl-2 家族是外源性凋亡通路关键因子。

EN
Bcl-2 family proteins are key players of the extrinsic pathway of apoptosis.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：外源性主轴是 death receptor→caspase-8。EN: Extrinsic apoptosis centers on death receptors → caspase-8.

177) 中文：自噬形成的双膜囊泡叫自噬体（autophagosome）。

EN
Double membrane vesicles formed upon autophagy are called autophagosomes.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：自噬体随后与溶酶体融合。EN: Autophagosomes later fuse with lysosomes.

178) 中文：凋亡形成的双膜囊泡叫溶酶体。

EN
Double membrane vesicles formed upon apoptosis are called lysosomes.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：凋亡产生的是凋亡小体（apoptotic bodies），不是溶酶体。EN: Apoptosis forms apoptotic bodies, not lysosomes.

179) 中文：当凋亡被阻断时，death receptor 激活可触发坏死性凋亡（necroptosis）。

EN
Necroptosis can be activated following death receptor engagement when apoptosis is blocked.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：典型 RIPK1/RIPK3/MLKL 轴。EN: Canonical RIPK1/RIPK3/MLKL axis.

180) 中文：当凋亡被阻断时，death receptor 激活会触发坏死（necrosis）。

EN
Necrosis can be activated following death receptor engagement when apoptosis is blocked.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：这里特指程序性坏死应为 necroptosis。EN: In this context it’s programmed necrosis = necroptosis.

181) 中文：凋亡是一种受调控的细胞死亡。

EN
Apoptosis is a regulated cell death pathway.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：caspase 级联严格调控。EN: It’s tightly regulated via caspase cascades.

182) 中文：caspase 通过切在半胱氨酸之后而被激活。

EN
Caspases are activated by cleavage after a cysteine residue.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：caspase 识别并切割 Asp（天冬氨酸） 位点。EN: Caspases cleave after Asp residues.

183) 中文：凋亡会释放促炎分子。

EN
During apoptosis, cells release pro-inflammatory molecules.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：凋亡通常免疫“沉默”，促炎更像坏死/焦亡。EN: Apoptosis is usually immunologically silent; pyroptosis/necrosis are inflammatory.

184) 中文：RCD = 受调控细胞死亡。

EN
RCD stands for regulated cell death.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：缩写题直接背。EN: Straight abbreviation.

185) 中文：效应 caspase 是线粒体蛋白。

EN
Effector caspases are mitochondrial proteins.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：效应 caspase（3/7）是胞质蛋白酶。EN: Effector caspases (3/7) are cytosolic proteases.

186) 中文：宏自噬成核依赖溶酶体上的 LC3-II。

EN
Bulk autophagy nucleation relies on lysosomal LC3-II.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：LC3-II 在自噬体膜；成核更靠 ULK1/Beclin-1/Vps34 轴。EN: LC3-II is on autophagosomes; nucleation involves ULK1/Beclin-1/Vps34.

187) 中文：若表达 gasdermin，治疗后肿瘤细胞可发生焦亡（pyroptosis）。

EN
Upon therapy, cancer cells can die by pyroptosis if they express gasdermins.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：gasdermin 成孔→炎症性死亡。EN: Gasdermins form pores → inflammatory cell death.

188) 中文：癌细胞释放 NET 来杀死中性粒细胞以逃避免疫。

EN
Cancer cells release a NET to kill neutrophils to escape anti-tumor immunity.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：NET 是中性粒细胞释放的。EN: NETs are released by neutrophils.

189) 中文：细胞吞噬（entosis）确保对死细胞的消化。

EN
Entosis ensures the digestion of dead cells.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：entosis 多指活细胞“细胞中细胞”吞入，不等于清除死细胞。EN: Entosis is “cell-in-cell” engulfment of live cells, not dead-cell clearance.

190) 中文：自噬既可促存活也可促死亡，取决于情境。

EN
Autophagy can contribute to both survival and death depending on context.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：营养缺乏时促存活；过度/特定情境可促死亡。EN: Often pro-survival in starvation; can also contribute to death.

191) 中文：坏死通常与失控裂解与炎症相关。

EN
Necrosis is typically associated with uncontrolled cell lysis and inflammation.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：内容物外泄→炎症反应。EN: Cell contents leak → inflammation.

192) 中文：凋亡细胞暴露“eat-me”信号被吞噬细胞清除。

EN
Apoptotic cells expose “eat-me” signals to be cleared by phagocytes.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：如磷脂酰丝氨酸外翻。EN: e.g., phosphatidylserine exposure.

193) 中文：铁死亡是铁依赖、由脂质过氧化驱动的受调控死亡。

EN
Ferroptosis is iron-dependent RCD driven by lipid peroxidation.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：核心是脂质 ROS 累积。EN: Key feature is lipid ROS accumulation.

194) 中文：凋亡、坏死性凋亡、焦亡之间存在串话。

EN
Crosstalk exists between apoptosis, necroptosis and pyroptosis pathways.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：共享上游信号与分子开关。EN: They share upstream signals and molecular switches.

195) 中文：抗凋亡的 Bcl-2 家族成员常在肿瘤中过表达。

EN
Anti-apoptotic Bcl-2 family members are often overexpressed in tumors.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：提升生存、造成耐药。EN: Promotes survival and contributes to resistance.

八、Stem cells, heterogeneity, TME & antitumor immunity（CSC + 免疫检查点 + TME）

Part1选择题

196) 中文：癌症干细胞对治疗高度敏感。

EN
Cancer stem cells are highly sensitive to therapy.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：CSC 常更耐药、易复发。EN: CSCs are often therapy-resistant and linked to relapse.

197) 中文：逃避免疫清除是癌症特征之一。

EN
Avoiding immune destruction is a hallmark of cancer.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：免疫逃逸/免疫抑制属于关键能力。EN: Immune evasion is a key cancer capability.

198) 中文：肿瘤微环境中巨噬细胞通常比 NK 细胞更丰富。

EN
Macrophages are more abundant than NK cells in a tumor microenvironment.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：TAM 往往是主要免疫细胞成分之一。EN: TAMs are often a dominant immune component.

199) 中文：临床上会用抗体阻断 Fas/FasL 相互作用来治疗。

EN
Antibody-mediated inhibition of the Fas/FasL interaction is used in therapy.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：阻断 Fas/FasL 会削弱 T 细胞杀伤。EN: Blocking Fas/FasL would impair cytotoxic killing.

200) 中文：T 细胞上的 CAR 可增强对癌细胞杀伤。

EN
Chimeric antigen receptors borne by T cells enhance cancer cell killing.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：CAR-T 直接识别抗原并激活杀伤。EN: CAR-T directly recognizes antigen and triggers killing.

201) 中文：IFN-γ 会诱导肿瘤细胞表达 PD-1。

EN
IFN-γ induces PD-1 expression on tumor cells.
答案/Answer
F — 中文讲解：IFN-γ 更典型诱导肿瘤细胞 PD-L1（PD-1 主要在 T 细胞）。EN: IFN-γ typically induces PD-L1 on tumors; PD-1 is mainly on T cells.

202) 中文：肿瘤细胞表达 PD-L1 会抑制 T 细胞抗肿瘤活性。

EN
PD-L1 expression inhibits T cell anti-tumor activity.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：PD-L1–PD-1 抑制 TCR 信号。EN: PD-L1–PD-1 dampens TCR signaling.

203) 中文：Treg 会抑制抗肿瘤免疫反应。

EN
Regulatory T cells suppress anti-tumor immune responses.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：分泌抑制性细胞因子/竞争 IL-2 等。EN: They suppress via inhibitory cytokines, IL-2 competition, etc.

204) 中文：抗 PD-L1 抗体已用于免疫治疗。

EN
Anti-PD-L1 antibodies are currently used in immunotherapies.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：属于免疫检查点抑制剂。EN: These are immune checkpoint inhibitors.

205) 中文：抗 PD-1 抗体能恢复 TCR 信号以恢复 T 细胞功能。

EN
Antibodies to PD-1 stimulate TCR signaling to restore T cell function.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：解除“刹车”，增强抗肿瘤反应。EN: Releases the “brake” to reinvigorate T cells.

206) 中文：肿瘤浸润淋巴细胞（TIL）在某些癌种与更好预后相关。

EN
Tumor-infiltrating lymphocytes can correlate with better prognosis in some cancers.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：代表更强的抗肿瘤免疫。EN: Often reflects stronger anti-tumor immunity.

207) 中文：肿瘤相关巨噬细胞可促进肿瘤生长并造成免疫抑制。

EN
Tumor-associated macrophages can promote tumor growth and immunosuppression.
答案/Answer
T — 中文讲解：TAM 可促血管生成、促侵袭、抑制 T 细胞。EN: TAMs promote angiogenesis/invasion and suppress T-cell responses.

03Part 1 填空题

I. 癌症基础 / Hallmarks / 血管生成与代谢

中文触发
端粒酶过表达 → 防止端粒 ____EN trigger: Telomerase overexpression prevents telomere ____空/Blank：shortening（缩短）
中文触发
乳腺癌可遗传突变基因 → ____EN trigger: Inherited mutations in breast cancer → ____ genes空：BRCA1/2
中文触发
新增“新兴能力”2项 → ____ + ____EN trigger: two “emerging capabilities” → ____ and ____空：avoiding immune destruction；deregulating cellular energetics
中文触发
配体非依赖性信号 → ligand-____ signalingEN trigger: ligand-____ signaling空：independent
中文触发
抗血管生成抑制通路 → ____ signaling inhibitorsEN trigger: inhibitors of ____ signaling (anti-angiogenesis)空：VEGF
中文触发
癌细胞葡萄糖代谢改变 → ____ metabolism；____ effectEN trigger: altered ____ metabolism → ____ effect空：glucose；Warburg

II. 表观遗传学

中文触发
表观遗传不改变 → DNA ____EN trigger: without altering the DNA ____空：sequence
中文触发
启动子高甲基化 → 转录性 ____EN trigger: promoter hypermethylation → transcriptional ____空：silencing（or repression）
中文触发
lncRNA 长度阈值 → > ____ ntEN trigger: lncRNAs are over ____ nucleotides空：200

III. 细胞骨架

中文触发
微管马达 dynein + ____EN trigger: microtubule motors dynein + ____空：kinesin(s)
中文触发
Vinblastine 家族（去稳定剂）→ ____EN trigger: vinblastine family → ____空：vinca-alkaloid
中文触发
actin 微丝构型 → ____ protofilaments；____ structureEN trigger: actin microfilaments → ____ protofilaments in a ____空：two；double helix（twisted helix）
中文触发
profilin + actin 单体 → ____ +端聚合EN trigger: profilin-actin → ____ polymerization at + end空：promotes / accelerates
中文触发
filopodia 的 actin 束类型 → ____ bundlesEN trigger: filopodia → tight ____ bundles空：parallel
中文触发
filopodia 小GTPase 开关 → ____EN trigger: filopodia activated by small GTPase ____空：Cdc42
中文触发
细胞核内骨架丝 → ____EN trigger: cytoskeleton filament in nucleus → ____空：actin microfilaments（nuclear actin）

IV. 细胞周期 / 静止 / 衰老

中文触发
malignant neoplasms = ____EN trigger: malignant neoplasms are called ____空：cancers
中文触发
CDK 全称 → ____-dependent kinaseEN trigger: CDK stands for ____-dependent kinase空：cyclin
中文触发
APC/C-Cdh1 的类型 → ____ ligase（促蛋白酶体降解）EN trigger: APC/C-Cdh1 is a ____ ligase (proteasome degradation)空：ubiquitin（E3 ubiquitin）
中文触发

9
By strengthening actin fibers, ________ favor matrix remodeling and cell migration in cancer-associated fibroblasts.通过增强肌动蛋白纤维，_________ 促进基质重塑并促进癌相关成纤维细胞迁移。答案/Answer: septins（隔蛋白）
10
One way cancer cells evade immunity is by ___________ antigen presentation.癌细胞逃避免疫的一种方式是 ___________ 抗原呈递。答案/Answer: downregulating（下调/降低）
11
CAF/Septins（矩阵重塑+迁移）_________ 有利于 CAF 的基质重塑与迁移。By strengthening actin fibers, ________ favor matrix remodeling and cell migration in cancer-associated fibroblasts.答案/Answer: septins
12
