04Part 2 图题答题框架

Figure Playbook 通用图题背诵版（中英对照）
  • A12 句万能开场句

1）这张图旨在检验 X 在癌症中相对对照是否发生异常变化。

This figure examines whether X is dysregulated in cancer compared with controls.

2）与对照组相比，肿瘤组中的 X 显著升高/降低。

Compared with the control group, X is significantly increased/decreased in the tumor group.

3）这些数据提示 X 可能参与肿瘤进展。

These data suggest that X may contribute to tumor progression.

4）X 高表达的患者总体生存更差/更好。

Patients with high X expression show worse/better overall survival than those with low expression.

5）因此，X 可能是一个预后标志物（高 X 代表预后差/好）。

Therefore, X is a potential prognostic biomarker (high X indicates poor/good prognosis).

6）敲低 X 会降低增殖/迁移/侵袭，说明 X 促进恶性表型。

Knockdown of X reduces proliferation/migration/invasion, indicating that X promotes malignant phenotypes.

7）过表达 X 会增强增殖/迁移/侵袭，进一步支持其致癌作用。

Overexpression of X enhances proliferation/migration/invasion, further supporting its oncogenic role.

8）敲低 X 会增加凋亡/细胞死亡，说明 X 支持癌细胞存活。

X depletion increases apoptosis/cell death, suggesting that X supports cancer cell survival.

9）体外与体内模型结果一致，从而增强结论的可信度。

These results are consistent across in vitro and in vivo models, strengthening the conclusion.

10）补救实验显示：恢复 Y 后，可逆转敲低 X 导致的表型。

The rescue experiment shows that the phenotype caused by X knockdown is reversed by restoring Y.

11）这为“X 位于 Y 上游并调控该通路”提供了证据。

This provides evidence that X acts upstream of Y and regulates the pathway.

12）综上，数据支持：X 通过 Y/Z 驱动肿瘤进展。

Taken together, the data support a model in which X drives cancer progression through Y/Z.

B. 8 类常考图
看到什么图就套什么模板

1）表达图（qPCR / WB / IHC / dot plot）

读法
比较 tumor vs normal，或不同细胞系，或处理前后。
How to read
tumor vs normal, different cell lines, or before/after treatment.
模板
X 在肿瘤组织/癌细胞系中相对对照上调/下调。
Template
X is upregulated/downregulated in tumor tissues/cancer cell lines compared with controls.
模板
这表明 X 在癌症中表达失调。
Template
This indicates dysregulated expression of X in cancer.

2）Kaplan–Meier 生存曲线

读法
high vs low，看曲线分离与 p 值。
How to read
high vs low groups, curve separation, and p value.
模板
X 高表达与更差/更好的生存相关。
Template
High X expression is associated with worse/better survival.
模板
因此 X 是负向/正向预后因子。
Template
Thus, X is a negative/positive prognostic factor.

3）增殖实验（CCK8/MTT/EdU/colony）

读法
knockdown vs control，看曲线或克隆数。
How to read
knockdown vs control, growth curves or colony numbers.
模板
敲低 X 会降低增殖/克隆形成。
Template
Knockdown of X decreases proliferation/colony formation.
模板
因此 X 促进细胞生长。
Template
Therefore, X promotes cell growth.

4）迁移/侵袭（wound healing / Transwell / Matrigel invasion）

读法
伤口闭合慢/快；穿膜细胞多/少。
How to read
slower/faster wound closure; fewer/more cells crossing membranes.
模板
敲低 X 会抑制迁移/侵袭。
Template
X depletion impairs migration/invasion.
模板
因此 X 促进转移相关能力。
Template
Thus, X contributes to metastatic potential.

5）凋亡/死亡（Annexin V–PI / cleaved caspase-3 / cleaved PARP）

读法
凋亡比例↑；剪切型蛋白↑。
How to read
higher apoptotic population; increased cleaved markers.
模板
敲低 X 会增加凋亡，说明 X 具有抗凋亡作用。
Template
X knockdown increases apoptosis, indicating an anti-apoptotic role of X.
模板
因此 X 支持癌细胞存活。
Template
Hence, X supports cancer cell survival.

6）通路激活（核转位 / 磷酸化 / reporter）

读法
p-蛋白↑；核定位↑；reporter 活性↑。
How to read
increased phosphorylation; increased nuclear localization; increased reporter activity.
模板
X 激活/抑制通路 Y。
Template
X activates/inhibits pathway Y.
模板
证据为：磷酸化/核转位/report 活性增加或减少。
Template
This is supported by increased/decreased phosphorylation/nuclear localization/reporter activity.
模板
因此 X 调控 Y 信号通路。
Template
Therefore, X regulates Y signaling.

7）ceRNA 机制（lncRNA–miRNA–target）高频

你要记的固定链条
lncRNA ↑ → miRNA ↓ → 靶基因 ↑ → 表型 ↑。

Fixed chain to memorize: lncRNA ↑ → miRNA ↓ → target gene ↑ → phenotype ↑.

模板
X 作为 ceRNA 海绵 miRNA-…，解除对靶基因 Y 的抑制。
Template
X acts as a ceRNA and sponges miRNA-…, thereby relieving repression of target Y.
模板
结果是 Y 上调，促进增殖/EMT/迁移/侵袭。
Template
As a result, Y increases and promotes proliferation/EMT/migration/invasion.

8）动物实验（xenograft / metastasis model）

读法
肿瘤体积/重量；Ki67；转移结节数。
How to read
tumor volume/weight; Ki67; metastatic nodule counts.
模板
敲低 X 会降低体内肿瘤生长，并伴随 Ki67 下降。
Template
Silencing X reduces tumor growth in vivo, with decreased tumor volume/weight and reduced Ki67 staining.
模板
因此 X 是肿瘤生长/转移扩增所必需的。
Template
Thus, X is required for tumor growth/metastatic outgrowth.

C. 4 个必考“设计实验”模板（直接背）

1）补救实验：共转染 siX 与 Y 过表达（或 miRNA 抑制剂），观察表型是否被救回。

Rescue experiment
co-transfect siX with Y overexpression (or a miRNA inhibitor) to test whether the phenotype is rescued.

2）机制验证：用含 WT 与突变结合位点的 luciferase reporter 证明直接调控。

Mechanism validation: use a luciferase reporter with WT and mutant binding sites to validate direct regulation.

3）通路依赖性：用通路抑制剂/激动剂检验 X 的效应是否依赖通路 Y。

Pathway dependency
treat with a pathway inhibitor/activator to test whether X-dependent effects require pathway Y.

4）体内验证：在移植瘤/转移模型中验证关键结论，并检测 Ki67、凋亡标志物和通路 IHC。

In vivo confirmation: validate the key finding in xenograft/metastasis models and assess Ki67, apoptosis markers, and IHC for the pathway.

D. 最终收尾句（万能结论段）

综上，数据表明 X 作为致癌基因/抑癌基因，通过调控 Y 影响增殖、存活和转移相关表型。

Overall, these data indicate that X functions as an oncogene/tumor suppressor by regulating Y, thereby affecting proliferation, survival and metastatic traits.

临床上，X 可能作为预后/预测标志物，并且是潜在治疗靶点。

Clinically, X may serve as a prognostic/predictive biomarker and a potential therapeutic target.

012025-10-08（第 1 讲）

1
癌症的局部分型、分期与预后评估

Localization, tumour staging and prognosis evaluation

1) 肿瘤的分型——按起源器官分类

Tumors are classified according to their organ of origin (breast, liver, kidney, bones)

肿瘤首先根据其发源的器官进行分类，如乳腺、肝脏、肾脏、骨骼等。

（老师备注：明确肿瘤起源对于判断发展趋势、制定治疗方案具有基础性意义。）

Tumors are first classified by where they originate, e.g., breast, liver, kidney, bones.

(Teacher’s comment: Knowing the origin is fundamental for evaluating progression and treatment.)

2) 病理学检查用于确定肿瘤的类型、分级和分期

Pathological examination to establish the TYPE, GRADE and STAGE of the tumor

a. 类型（TYPE）

上皮组织来源——癌（Carcinoma）

结缔组织来源——肉瘤（Sarcoma）

血细胞来源——淋巴瘤和白血病（Lymphoma & Leukemia）

胚胎组织来源——神经母细胞瘤（Neuroblastoma）

Outline
Carcinoma (epithelial), Sarcoma (connective), Lymphoma/Leukemia (blood), Neuroblastoma (embryonic)

b. 分级（GRADES）——用阿拉伯数字标记

GRADES written in Arabic numerals

评估细胞分化程度、细胞核和细胞质改变、核分裂次数、坏死范围等。

例如
前列腺癌分级常用Gleason评分。评分越高，恶性程度越大。
Assess by
degree of differentiation, nuclear/cytoplasmic abnormalities, mitotic count, necrosis extent.

Gleason score is a common grading system for prostate cancer; higher score means greater malignancy.

（老师举例：1分代表细胞近乎正常，分数增高代表细胞越来越异常和无序。Gleason 6分为低风险，分数越高风险越大1。）

c. 分期（STAGES）——用罗马数字表示

STAGES represented as Roman numerals

通用TNM分期标准：

T（肿瘤大小）、N（区域淋巴结转移）、M（远处转移）。

按0（无癌）到IV分级，X表示无法评估。

TNM classification
T = tumor size, N = node (lymph node involvement), M = metastasis. 0 = no cancer, I-IV, X = not assessable.

（老师提示：分期与预后及治疗关系密切。）

3) 分子标志物筛查

Search for molecular markers

检测特定分子标志物以进一步明确预后（PRONOSTIC）和对治疗方案的预测（PREDICTIVE）。

Screening for molecular markers can provide prognostic information or predict treatment response.

录音补充要点

“分级就是评估肿瘤的恶性程度，通常用阿拉伯数字表示，从接近正常到极度异常。例如Gleason评分用于前列腺癌，分数越高，细胞异常越明显，恶性程度越大。”

“分期主要依据肿瘤大小、是否有淋巴结及远处转移，决定用TNM系统来表征。”

听课笔记整合（中英对照）

总结：

1
肿瘤分类首要看起源器官；分型依据组织种类。 Tumors are first classified by organ; types derive from tissue of origin.
2
分级（恶性度）用阿拉伯数字，常用如Gleason评分；分数越高，异常越多，恶性越高。 Grades (malignancy) are in Arabic numerals, e.g., Gleason score; higher means more abnormal.
3
分期使用TNM系统和罗马数字，评价范围从T、N至M。 Staging uses TNM system in Roman numerals, covering tumor size, node involvement, metastasis.
4
分子标志物帮助判断预后及制定精准治疗方案。 Molecular markers aid prognosis and personalized therapy.
参考录音原话
“分级就是评估肿瘤的恶性程度……前列腺癌Gleason评分6分还好，分高风险就高。”

肿瘤首先按照起源器官进行分类（如乳腺、肝脏、肾脏、骨骼）。

Tumors are first classified according to their organ of origin (such as breast, liver, kidney, bones)。

通过病理学检查确定肿瘤的类型、分级和分期。

Pathological examination is used to establish the type, grade, and stage of the tumor11。

类型包括上皮来源的癌、结缔组织来源的肉瘤、血细胞来源的淋巴瘤与白血病，以及胚胎组织来源的神经母细胞瘤。

Types include carcinoma (epithelial tissue), sarcoma (connective tissue), lymphoma and leukemia (blood cells), and neuroblastoma (embryonic tissue)。

分级用阿拉伯数字表示，依据细胞分化、核和胞质异常、核分裂数和坏死范围。

Grades are written in Arabic numerals based on differentiation, nuclear and cytoplasmic abnormalities, number of mitoses, and extent of necrosis115。

以前列腺癌为例，常用Gleason评分，分数越高恶性度越高。

For example, the Gleason score is used for prostate cancer; the higher the score, the more malignant the tumor。

Gleason 6分风险较低，7分要区分3+4（较好）和4+3（较差），8分及以上恶性度更高。

A Gleason score of 6 is low risk, 7 requires distinction between 3+4 (better) and 4+3 (worse), and 8 or above indicates high malignancy1220。

分期用罗马数字表示，采用TNM系统：T代表肿瘤大小，N代表淋巴结受侵，M代表远处转移。

Staging uses Roman numerals and the TNM system: T for tumor size, N for regional lymph node involvement, M for distant metastases1112。

0表示无癌，I至IV表示分期进展，X表示无法评估。

0 means no cancer, I to IV indicates cancer progression, X means impossible to evaluate1112。

通过筛查分子标志物，可以进一步判断预后或者预测对治疗的反应。

Searching for molecular markers can further clarify prognosis or predict response to treatment115。

老师强调，分型、分级和分期都需要通过病理活检来获得，早发现有助于更好预后。

The teacher emphasized that all typing, grading, and staging rely on pathological biopsy, and early detection leads to a better prognosis512。

Step-by-Step Process

1
Carcinogenesis in the primary site 癌变始于原发部位 Cells acquire genetic mutations, transforming into cancer cells at a specific tissue location.
2
Hyperproliferation 细胞过度增殖 Cancer cells divide rapidly, leading to tumor growth.
3
Hypoxic environment 缺氧微环境形成 The growing tumor outpaces its blood supply, causing low-oxygen regions.
4
Sustained angiogenesis 持续的血管新生 Tumor releases factors (e.g., VEGF) to stimulate new blood vessel formation, ensuring nutrients and oxygen.
5
Crosstalk with parenchymal, stromal, endothelial and inflammatory cells 与实质细胞、间质细胞、内皮细胞和炎症细胞互作 Cancer cells interact with various normal cells in their environment, which can support tumor growth and invasiveness.
6
Migration and invasiveness 细胞迁移和侵袭性增强 Tumor cells acquire ability to move and invade surrounding tissues.
7
Intravasation into the bloodstream 穿入血管（内渗） Cancer cells enter nearby blood or lymphatic vessels.
8
Cell survival in the blood and lymphatic vessels 在血液和淋巴管中生存 Cells must survive immune attack and mechanical stress during transport.
9
Extravasation from the circulation 从循环系统中穿出（外渗） Cancer cells exit the vessels at distant sites.
10
Metastatic niche in which cancer cells should adapt 适应新的转移部位微环境 Cells must adapt to the new “soil” (microenvironment) to survive and grow.
11
Growth of the invading cells in the new site 新部位的癌细胞生长形成新肿瘤 If successful, these cells proliferate to form secondary (metastatic) tumors.

Structured Table

Step (English)中文对应Key Point
Carcinogenesis in the primary site原发部位癌变Initial genetic changes, tumor initiation
Hyperproliferation细胞过度增殖Increased cell division
Hypoxic environment缺氧Rapid tumor growth causes low oxygen
Sustained angiogenesis血管新生New vessels support further growth
Crosstalk with other cell types与各类细胞互作Tumor microenvironment changes
Migration and invasiveness迁移和侵袭力Cells invade nearby tissue
Intravasation into bloodstream内渗入血液/淋巴Cells enter vessels
Cell survival in vessels血管/淋巴中生存Survive in circulation
Extravasation外渗至新部位Leave vessels at distant sites
Metastatic niche adaptation适应转移生态位Survive & adjust at new site
Growth at new site新部位继续增殖Form secondary tumor

In summary

Cancer metastasis is a complex, stepwise process. It starts with transformation and uncontrolled growth, proceeds through interaction with the microenvironment, and involves migration, circulation, adaptation, and growth at distant locations. Each step requires cells to overcome biological barriers, explaining why not all cancer cells succeed in forming metastases

Cancer
a Multistep Process癌症是一个多步骤过程
1
Carcinogenesis in the primary site原发部位癌变： 细胞因基因突变转化为癌细胞，形成原发肿瘤（Most cancers start with carcinogenesis at a primary site, where genetic alterations transform normal cells）。
2
Hyperproliferation过度增殖： 癌细胞大量增殖，导致肿瘤块体迅速增大（Tumor cells proliferate excessively, causing rapid tumor growth）。
3
Hypoxic environment缺氧环境形成： 肿瘤生长过快、供氧不足，内部区域出现缺氧（Tumor expansion outpaces blood supply, resulting in hypoxia within the tumor）。
4
Sustained angiogenesis持续血管新生： 癌细胞分泌信号（如VEGF）促进新血管生成，以获得更多养分和氧气（Tumor secretes angiogenic factors (e.g., VEGF) to stimulate the growth of new blood vessels）。
5
Crosstalk with surrounding cells与周围细胞的相互作用： 癌细胞与实质、间质、内皮及炎症细胞相互作用，促进肿瘤进展（Cancer cells interact with parenchymal, stromal, endothelial, and inflammatory cells, supporting their growth and invasion）。
6
Migration and invasiveness迁移与侵袭性增强： 癌细胞获得迁移能力，突破基底膜，穿透周围组织（Cells gain invasive ability, moving beyond originating tissue boundaries）。
7
Intravasation into bloodstream渗入血管： 癌细胞进入血液或淋巴系统，准备远处扩散（Cells enter blood or lymphatic vessels—intravasation）。
8
Cell survival in blood/lymph vessels循环系统中的生存： 癌细胞在循环中需抵御免疫细胞攻击及机械压力，保障存活（Tumor cells need to overcome immune attack and survive in circulation）。
9
Extravasation外渗至新部位： 癌细胞从血管逸出，到达远处器官（Cells exit vessels—extravasation—to reach distant tissues）。
10
Adaptation to metastatic niche适应转移微环境： 癌细胞需适应新环境才能存活（Cells must adapt to the microenvironment of the new site）。
11
Growth at new site新部位生长： 若适应成功，癌细胞在新部位大量增殖，形成转移灶（If adaptation is successful, metastatic tumors grow at the distant site）。

癌症转移的每一步都需要肿瘤细胞克服新的生理障碍，所以虽然很多肿瘤细胞进入血流，但能在远处定植的比例很低。每个阶段都为未来的治疗提供了潜在干预靶点。

Cancer
a multistep process（肿瘤：一个多步骤过程）
1
癌症发展是一个逐步积累多次基因变异的过程。

Cancer development is a process involving stepwise accumulation of multiple genetic mutations.

2
初始突变通常发生在抑癌基因、DNA修复基因或原癌基因中。

Initial mutations typically affect tumor suppressor genes, DNA repair genes, or proto-oncogenes.

3
首先，正常细胞因突变失去调控能力，开始异常增殖，形成高危细胞或异常细胞群。

First, a normal cell acquires mutations that disrupt regulation, leading to abnormal proliferation and formation of high-risk or abnormal cell populations411.

4
这种细胞群经历克隆扩张，细胞持续分裂，不同细胞进一步获得各自的突变，形成遗传多样性。

This cell population undergoes clonal expansion, and as cells continue dividing, they gain additional mutations, resulting in genetic diversity611.

5
随着变异的积累，细胞逐步出现异常形态和功能，如排列紊乱、分化减少、丧失黏附。

With accumulation of mutations, cells gradually develop abnormal morphology and functions, such as disorganization, reduced differentiation, and loss of adhesion217.

6
这些变化可表现为从正常到轻度增生、再到不典型增生、原位癌，最终发展为侵袭性癌症。

These changes manifest as progression from normal tissue to mild hyperplasia, dysplasia, carcinoma in situ, and finally invasive cancer26.

7
癌症细胞之间存在异质性，同一肿瘤内的细胞也可能拥有不同的突变和表现型。

There is heterogeneity among cancer cells; even within a single tumor, cells may have different mutations and phenotypes626.

8
这种异质性包括患者间、同一肿瘤内部、原发灶与转移灶之间的差异。

This heterogeneity includes differences between patients, within the same tumor, and between primary and secondary tumors26.

9
异质性使癌症的诊断和治疗变得更加复杂，易出现耐药和复发。

Heterogeneity makes cancer diagnosis and treatment more difficult, often leading to drug resistance and relapse36.

课上补充
老师强调，肿瘤异质性解释了为何即使同型癌症也表现不同，且肿瘤随着时间推移内部差异会不断增加。
Lecture addition
The teacher emphasized that tumor heterogeneity explains why cancers of the same type can behave differently and that, over time, the diversity within a tumor increases36.
11
不同肿瘤细胞在基因表达、代谢、迁移能力、增殖速度和转移潜能等方面均有差异。

Tumor cells differ in gene expression, metabolism, motility, proliferation rate, and metastatic potential26.

总结
肿瘤多步骤演变、突变积累与异质性共同决定了其高度复杂性和难以完全治愈的特征。
Summary
The multistep evolution, accumulation of mutations, and heterogeneity of tumors all contribute to their complexity and challenges in achieving complete cure.
Cancer
a multigenic and multifactorial disease癌症：多基因和多因素性疾病
1
癌症的发展是一个历时十到二十年的多步骤过程。

Cancer development is a multistep process that can span up to 10 or 20 years.

步骤一
Initiation（起始阶段）
1
DNA损伤和点突变导致细胞异常启动。

DNA damage and point mutations lead to abnormal cellular initiation.

2
这些损伤可能由遗传、基因毒性因素、放射线和病毒引起。

Such damage may result from heredity, genotoxic agents, radiations, and viruses33.

3
这些因素使细胞易于发生恶性转化。

These factors predispose the cell to malignant transformation.

步骤二
Promotion（促进阶段）
4
在肿瘤促进阶段，细胞经历增生、克隆选择和基因不稳定性变化。

During promotion, cells undergo hyperplasia, clonal selection, and acquire genomic instability18.

5
细胞分裂扩增过程中，一些细胞优势生长，出现更多变异。

As clones expand, some cells grow dominantly and acquire further mutations.

6
促发因素包括细胞因子、生长因子和激素。

Key promoting factors include cytokines, growth factors, and hormones.

步骤三
Progression（进展阶段）
7
肿瘤在进展阶段表现为基因扩增、染色体重排及杂合性缺失。

The progression phase features gene amplification, chromosomal rearrangements, and loss of heterozygosity18.

8
课上补充，老师强调此时DNA层面和表观遗传层面改变不断叠加。
Lecture addition
Teacher emphasized that both genetic and epigenetic changes accumulate at this stage3.
9
表观遗传（Epigenetics）异常可进一步激活原癌基因、抑制抑癌基因。

Epigenetic changes may further activate oncogenes and inhibit tumor suppressor genes186.

步骤四
Metastasis（转移阶段）
10
最终，少数具备侵袭能力的细胞获得入侵和转移特性。

Eventually, a subset of cells acquires the ability to invade and metastasize.

11
这些细胞突破基底膜，侵入血管并在其他部位生根生长。

Such cells breach the basement membrane, enter blood vessels, and colonize distant organs14.

癌症过程的要点补充（结合录音与PPT）

12
癌症不是由单一突变造成，而是持续积累多种遗传和表观遗传改变。

Cancer is not caused by a single mutation, but by a sustained accumulation of both genetic and epigenetic changes118.

13
每个阶段都可由不同外部环境和体内因素驱动，增加肿瘤复杂性。

Each stage may be driven by various internal and external factors, adding to tumor complexity17.

14
课上老师特别提示，肿瘤异质性（内部和患者间）导致同种癌症行为不同，影响诊断和治疗。

The teacher specifically noted that tumor heterogeneity (within and between patients) results in diverse cancer behavior and impacts diagnosis and treatment221.

总结
癌症的多步骤进展和多重影响因素，使其成为复杂且治疗难度高的疾病。
Summary
The multistep progression and multifactorial influences make cancer a complex disease that is challenging to treat.

Transformed cells acquire new properties转化细胞获得新特性

Example:

例子：

Three specific gene modifications are sufficient to convert a normal cell into a transformed one.

仅需三种特定基因改变就可以将正常细胞转化为肿瘤细胞。

Normal cell
正常细胞： Limited number of replications, limited growth factor signal accepted, mortal. 复制次数有限，只对有限的生长因子信号应答，终会衰老死亡。
Step 1 — hTERT
Increased telomerase activity 第一步 — hTERT： 端粒酶活性的增强 After this, unlimited number of replications is possible (escape senescence). 完成这一步后，细胞获得无限复制能力（逃避衰老）。
3
Step 2 — SV40 Large T: Loss of cell cycle suppressors and protection against apoptosis 第二步 — SV40大T抗原： 细胞周期抑制因子的丧失和抗凋亡保护 This leads to immortalization: cells no longer undergo apoptosis and can keep dividing. 这使细胞发生永生化：细胞不再易于凋亡，可以持续分裂。
Step 3 — H-v12Ras
Excessive proliferative signal 第三步 — H-v12Ras： 过度的增殖信号 This step results in enhanced proliferation in addition to immortalization. 这一变化在永生基础上大大增强了细胞增殖能力。
Transformed cell
转化细胞： Unlimited replication, immortalization, enhanced proliferation. 具备无限复制、永生化和增强的增殖能力。
6
Sequential mutations induce new cell properties:

连续的基因突变会赋予细胞新的特性：

Immortality 不死性（永生化）

Decreased sensitivity to growth suppressors 对生长抑制信号不敏感

Increased resistance to cell deaths 抗细胞死亡能力提升

Sustained proliferative signaling 持续的增殖信号

Decreased cell-cell and cell-matrix interactions 细胞间和细胞-基质间结合能力减弱

Avoiding immune destruction 逃避免疫清除

课上补充（结合录音）：

老师讲到，最初正常细胞受限于增殖和信号，逐步积累特定突变后，会获得不死、抗凋亡、持续增殖等新特征，这就是癌细胞的核心本质。

这些性质被称为“癌症标志性特征”，是肿瘤发生学研究和治疗的基础。

三个关键基因（端粒酶、细胞周期抑制、增殖信号）改变后，细胞就能彻底转化为癌变状态。

总结：

癌变过程中，连续获得新突变，使细胞具备肿瘤“六大标志性特征”，推动转化与恶性进展。

During transformation, stepwise mutations endow cells with the “hallmarks of cancer,” which drive malignant progression.

癌症的病因学
一种基因性疾病The etiology of cancer: a genetic disease

All cancers arise from changes in genes (mutations) but NOT all are inherited

所有癌症都源于基因的变化（突变），但不是所有的突变都会遗传

Differentiating hereditary from sporadic cancers

区分遗传性癌症和散发性癌症

Most colorectal cancer cases are sporadic (about 70%). 大多数结直肠癌病例为散发性（约70%）。

These cases have no family history; mutations occur during a person’s lifetime. 这些病例没有家族史；突变是在个人一生中发生的。

30% of cases show familial aggregation, but not all have identifiable hereditary syndromes. 30%的病例表现为家族聚集，但并非所有病例都能确定为遗传性综合征。

Only 5% of all cases have well-identified hereditary syndromes (with known gene mutations passed from parents). 只有5%的所有病例为有明确基因突变（可由父母遗传）的遗传性综合征患者。

In familial cases, people inherit a mutation (“first hit”) from their parents, increasing risk, but cancer still requires accumulation of other mutations (“second hit,” etc). 在家族性病例中，个体从父母遗传一种突变（“第一击”），这增加了风险，但癌症的发生仍需积累其它突变（“第二击”等）。

In sporadic cases, both “hits” (mutations) are acquired during one’s lifetime. 在散发性病例中，所有“击”（突变）都是在个体一生中获得的。

Having an inherited mutation means higher risk and possibly earlier onset, but does not guarantee cancer will develop. 遗传了某个突变意味着风险更高、发病可能更早，但并不保证一定会得癌症。

Key points from the lecture

课上要点补充

Cancer requires accumulation of multiple mutations (not just a single change). 癌症需要多个突变的积累（并非仅依靠一次变化）。

Heredity determines risk level, not certainty. 遗传性决定风险水平，而非必然性。

Most cancers are results of somatic (acquired) mutations, not inherited ones. 大部分癌症源于体细胞（获得性）突变，而非遗传性突变。

Summary

总结

Cancer is a genetic disease caused by gene mutations.

癌症是一种由基因突变引起的遗传性疾病。

But most cancers are sporadic, not inherited,

但大多数癌症为散发性，而非遗传性，

and even in familial cancers, only a small proportion have clearly identifiable hereditary syndromes.

即使在家族性癌症中，也只有很小一部分可以明确鉴定为遗传性综合征。

Hereditary versus Sporadic cancers

遗传性癌症与散发性癌症

Hereditary cancer

遗传性癌症

Inherited cancer syndromes are caused by mutations (changes) in certain genes passed from parents to children

遗传性癌症综合征是由从父母遗传给子女的某些基因中的突变（改变）引起的

With hereditary cancers, the first mutation is inherited and already present at birth. Additional mutations build up over time, leading to cancer. 对于遗传性癌症，首个突变是遗传的，出生时就已存在，随后随着时间积累其他突变，最终导致癌症。

Diagram summary
示意图总结：

First mutation → Second mutation (Cell damage) → Third mutation (Cell damage) → Fourth + mutation → Cancer cell 第一突变 → 第二突变（细胞损伤）→ 第三突变（细胞损伤）→ 第四/多重突变 → 癌细胞

Higher risk 风险更高

Sporadic cancer

散发性癌症

Cancer that occurs in people who do not have a family history of that cancer or an inherited change in their DNA that would increase their risk for that cancer

散发性癌症是指发生在无该癌症家族史或无已知致病遗传突变的人群中的癌症

With sporadic cancers, many mutations build up in cells over time, eventually leading to cancer. 散发性癌症中，细胞在一生中逐步积累多种突变，最终引发癌症。

Causes may include
可能的原因包括：

Virus 病毒

Pollutant exposure 污染物暴露

Tobacco, Alcohol 烟草、酒精

Sunlight 日晒

Radiations 辐射

Aging 老化

Diagram summary
示意图总结：

Normal cell → First mutation (Cell damage) → Second mutation (Cell damage) → Third mutation (Cell damage) → Fourth + mutation → Cancer cell 正常细胞→第一突变（细胞损伤）→第二突变（细胞损伤）→第三突变（细胞损伤）→第四/多重突变→癌细胞

Generally has a later onset 通常发病年龄较晚

Hereditary cancers

遗传性癌症

Mutations that you have inherited from your parents

你从父母那里遗传的突变

Examples include (see diagram):

例子包括（见图示）：

Colorectal cancer (结直肠癌)：MLH1, MSH2, MSH6, PMS2, APC, DPC4, Bmpr1, PTEN, MYH（等基因变异）

Breast & ovarian cancer (乳腺和卵巢癌)：BRCA1 and BRCA2

Prostate cancer (前列腺癌)：HPC1, TLR1, TLR4, TLR6, TLR9

Li-Fraumeni syndrome (Li-Fraumeni综合征)：p53, CHEK2

Others
Neurofibromatosis (NF2); Hemangioblastoma (VHL); Retinoblastoma (RB1); Pancreatic cancer (DPC4); Gastric cancer (TLR4); Multiple endocrine neoplasia (MEN1, RET); Lung cancer (SCLC1); Nasopharyngeal cancer (TLR9); Malignant melanoma (CDKN2); Chronic myeloid leukemia (ABL, BCR); Burkitt lymphoma (MYC)

总结 Summary

Hereditary cancers
Caused by single major inherited mutation plus subsequent mutations; risk is present from birth and cancer often appears at a younger age. 遗传性癌症由主要的遗传突变和后续突变引起，出生即有风险，往往更早发病。
Sporadic cancers
Caused by multiple acquired mutations accumulating over time; generally develops later in life and is associated with environmental or lifestyle factors. 散发性癌症需要多个获得性突变随时间积累，通常发病更晚，与环境或生活方式有关。
遗传性癌症
以BRCA基因为例
Hereditary cancers
the case of BRCA genes
BRCA1 / BRCA2
乳腺癌风险的著名基因
BRCA1 / BRCA2
The most well-known genes linked to breast cancer risk

帮助修复受损的DNA Help repair damaged DNA

作为肿瘤抑制基因发挥作用 Act as tumor suppressor genes

还参与多种细胞过程（如重组、细胞周期进程、基因转录和染色质重塑） Are also involved in a range of cellular processes: recombination, cell cycle progression, gene transcription and chromatin remodeling

常见合作蛋白
BACH1、RAD51、PALB2、ATM、CHK2 Partners: BACH1, RAD51, PALB2, ATM, CHK233
2
BRCA异常的影响
2
Consequences of BRCA dysfunction

基因因启动子甲基化、突变或失去合作因子可被沉默 Gene silencing due to promoter methylation, mutations or loss of partners

会影响细胞周期检查点控制 Will affect checkpoint controls in the cell cycle

影响同源重组（同源重组修复DNA双链断裂） Will affect homologous recombination (homology-directed repair of dsDNA breaks)

BRCA1/2等蛋白可作为支架，组装并稳定一系列修复蛋白大复合体 Act as scaffolds to assemble a cohort of other proteins into large complexes22

3
临床与分子医学发现
3
Clinical & Molecular Medical Findings

约一半的散发性乳腺癌，BRCA1基因拷贝因启动子甲基化被沉默 Half of sporadic breast carcinomas carry inactive BRCA1 gene copies, silenced through promoter methylation

约40%的散发性上皮性卵巢癌也出现BRCA1沉默 This gene suffers the same fate in ~40% of sporadic epithelial ovarian carcinomas22

4
机制小结
4
Mechanistic summary

BRCA基因突变或沉默，细胞DNA修复能力下降，易积累更多突变，提升癌变风险 When BRCA genes are mutated or silenced, the cell's ability to repair DNA decreases, leading to increased risk of accumulating further mutations and developing cancer

不仅是遗传性癌症，许多散发性癌症也有类似的BRCA失活机制（如表观遗传沉默） Not only hereditary cancers, but also many sporadic cancers show BRCA inactivation due to epigenetic silencing2233

5
案例与检测意义
5
Significance for risk and screening

有家族史的女性可进行BRCA1/2基因检测，携带突变时建议早期筛查和医学干预 Women with a family history of cancer can undergo BRCA1/2 genetic testing; mutation carriers are advised to receive early screening and medical interventions15

“BRCA1/2不仅守卫DNA稳定，也是癌症个体化医学的重要靶点。”

“BRCA1/2 not only defend DNA stability, but are also key targets for personalized cancer medicine.”

癌症标志性特征（The Hallmarks of Cancer）

1
概念与发展
1
Concept and progress

这一页介绍了癌症细胞的经典与新兴标志性特征。 This slide introduces the classic and emerging hallmarks of cancer cells.

癌症具有一系列关键的生物学能力，使其能持续发展和扩散。 Cancer possesses a series of key biological abilities that allow its progression and spread.

这些特征来自 Hanahan 和 Weinberg 两篇代表性综述（2000年、2011年）。 These features were summarized by Hanahan and Weinberg in two landmark reviews (2000 and 2011).

2
六大经典特征（2000 年）
2
The original six hallmarks (2000)

维持增殖信号（Sustaining proliferative signaling） Sustaining proliferative signaling

逃避免疫抑制（Evading growth suppressors） Evading growth suppressors

抵抗细胞死亡（Resisting cell death） Resisting cell death

激活侵袭与转移（Activating invasion and metastasis） Activating invasion and metastasis

诱导血管生成（Inducing angiogenesis） Inducing angiogenesis

获得复制永生性（Enabling replicative immortality） Enabling replicative immortality

3
研究进展带来的新特征（2011 年）
3
New hallmarks due to research progress (2011)
新增特征包括
The new features added include:

规避免疫破坏（Avoiding immune destruction） Avoiding immune destruction

肿瘤促发炎症（Tumor-promoting inflammation） Tumor-promoting inflammation

能量代谢调控异常（Deregulating cellular energetics） Deregulating cellular energetics

基因组不稳定与突变（Genome instability and mutation） Genome instability and mutation

4
总结及实际意义
4
Summary and practical significance

癌症标志性特征由最初六项扩展为十项。 The hallmarks expanded from six features to ten.

针对这些通路的治疗极大改善了绝大多数癌症的生存期。 Targeting these pathways has dramatically improved survival in most cancers.

这些特征的出现是癌细胞演化、适应和逃避人体防御的结果。 The emergence of these traits is the result of cancer cells' evolution, adaptation, and escape from human defense systems.

5
课堂讲解补充要点
5
Supplementary points from lecture

教师强调，许多抗癌药物或疗法正是针对这些“标志”开发，如抗血管生成药、免疫检查点抑制剂等。 The teacher emphasized that many current anticancer drugs or therapies directly target these hallmarks, such as anti-angiogenic drugs and immune checkpoint inhibitors.

同时，癌症是多因素、多阶段、多变异积累的复杂过程。 At the same time, cancer is a complex process involving multiple factors, stages, and accumulation of mutations.

了解和识别这些特征有助于早筛查、分型诊断和个体化治疗。 Understanding and identifying these traits aids early screening, classification, and personalized therapy.

图表说明

Diagram description

左图为2000年提出的六大特征，右图为2011年更新的十项特征，包括“新兴特征”和“使癌症有能力获得这些特征的辅助机制”。 The left diagram shows the six original hallmarks (2000), while the right one (2011) presents ten, including emerging features and enabling characteristics.

The Hallmarks of Cancer

癌症的标志性特征

主要蛋白或通路在致癌过程中失调

Main proteins or pathways are dysregulated in the carcinogenesis process

1
持续增殖信号（Sustaining proliferative signaling）
1
持续增殖信号的获得依赖于多种信号通路，特别是MAPK/ERK通路。 Acquisition of sustaining proliferative signaling depends on multiple pathways, especially the MAPK/ERK pathway. 该通路也称为RAS-RAF-MEK-ERK通路，促进癌细胞持续生长。 This pathway, also called the RAS-RAF-MEK-ERK pathway, promotes continuous cancer cell growth. 【EGFR抑制剂可用于阻断该信号。】 EGFR inhibitors can block this signaling.
2
逃避免疫抑制（Evading growth suppressors）
2
抗生长信号多由Rb和P53蛋白控制，这些蛋白常在癌细胞中失活或者突变。 Antigrowth signals are mainly controlled by Rb and P53 proteins, which are often inactivated or mutated in cancer cells. 【细胞周期依赖性激酶抑制剂可干扰该通路。】 Cyclin-dependent kinase inhibitors can interfere with this pathway.
3
抵抗细胞死亡（Resisting cell death）
3
癌细胞常通过Bcl-2蛋白家族抑制细胞凋亡，形成抗凋亡优势。 Cancer cells often suppress apoptosis by overexpressing Bcl-2 protein family members, creating an anti-apoptotic advantage. 【促凋亡BH3模拟物可以诱导凋亡。】 Proapoptotic BH3 mimetics can induce apoptosis.
4
激活侵袭与转移（Activating invasion and metastasis）
4
E-钙黏蛋白（E-cadherin）下调或缺失是上皮-间质转化（EMT）的主要特征，使癌细胞具备迁移侵袭能力。 Downregulation or loss of E-cadherin is a key feature of epithelial-mesenchymal transition (EMT), enabling cancer cell migration and invasion. 而HGF/c-Met信号上调进一步促进侵袭和转移。 Upregulation of HGF/c-Met signaling further promotes invasion and metastasis. 【HGF/c-Met信号抑制剂用于该环节。】 Inhibitors of HGF/c-Met target this process.
5
诱导血管生成（Inducing angiogenesis）
5
血管内皮生长因子（VEGF）及其受体是新生血管形成的主要驱动力。 Vascular endothelial growth factor (VEGF) and its receptors are main drivers of neovascularization. 缺氧可上调VEGF表达促进肿瘤血管生成。 Hypoxia can induce VEGF expression, promoting tumor angiogenesis. 【VEGF信号通路抑制剂可用作抗血管生成治疗。】 Inhibitors of VEGF signaling serve as anti-angiogenic therapy.
6
获得复制永生性（Enabling replicative immortality）
6
端粒酶是端粒维持酶，使染色体末端不再缩短，让癌细胞获得永生。 Telomerase is a telomere maintenance enzyme that prevents chromosome end shortening, allowing cancer cells to become immortal. 过度表达端粒酶防止衰老和凋亡。 Overexpression of telomerase prevents senescence and apoptosis. 【端粒酶抑制剂用于抑制这一特征。】 Telomerase inhibitors inhibit this hallmark.
7
规避免疫破坏（Avoiding immune destruction）
7
程序性细胞死亡-1（PD-1）是一种免疫检查点蛋白，肿瘤细胞大量表达其配体PD-L1，帮助逃避免疫系统。 Programmed cell death-1 (PD-1) is an immune checkpoint protein, and tumor cells overexpress its ligand PD-L1 to escape immune surveillance. 【免疫激活剂如抗CTLA4单抗可逆转免疫抑制。】 Immune activating agents such as anti-CTLA4 monoclonal antibodies can reverse immune suppression.
8
能量代谢重编程（Deregulating cellular energetics）
1
癌细胞普遍采用有氧糖酵解（Warburg效应）快速产生ATP，满足快速增殖的能量需求。 Cancer cells generally favor aerobic glycolysis (Warburg effect) for rapid ATP production to meet the energy demands of proliferation. 【有氧糖酵解抑制剂可用作治疗。】 Aerobic glycolysis inhibitors may be used for therapy.
9
基因组不稳定与突变（Genome instability and mutation）
2
基因组稳定性依赖于如PARP等DNA修复相关蛋白，癌细胞常有这些途径缺陷。 Genome stability depends on DNA repair proteins such as PARP; cancer cells often have defects in these pathways. 【PARP抑制剂用于靶向这一特征。】 PARP inhibitors target this hallmark.
10
肿瘤促发炎症 & 微环境调节（Tumor-promoting inflammation & microenvironment）
3
肿瘤微环境中的炎症细胞可分泌细胞因子及蛋白酶，为肿瘤提供生长及转移所需的额外支持。 Inflammatory cells in the tumor microenvironment secrete cytokines and proteases, supporting cancer growth and metastasis. 【抗炎药物可优化微环境，阻断肿瘤促发炎症反应。】 Selective anti-inflammatory drugs can optimize the microenvironment and block tumor-promoting inflammation.
概要
课堂补充
Summary
Lecture highlights

教师强调，图中每个环节均有明确药物靶点，许多新药专门针对上述标志开发。 The teacher emphasized that each hallmark in the diagram corresponds to drug targets, and many new drugs are developed against them.

癌症是由多步骤、多靶点、多样分子变化驱动的疾病。 Cancer is a disease driven by multistep, multitarget, and various molecular alterations.

系统了解这些特征有助于分子分型、个体化治疗及早筛查。 A systematic understanding of these features aids in molecular subtyping, personalized therapies, and early detection.

癌症标志性特征及对应关键蛋白/通路

The Hallmarks of Cancer and Key Proteins/Pathways

1
持续增殖信号 (Sustaining proliferative signaling)

癌细胞常通过过度表达生长因子受体（如EGFR）或激活下游通路（如RAS-RAF-MEK-ERK）实现持续增殖信号。 Cancer cells often maintain proliferative signaling by overexpressing growth factor receptors (e.g., EGFR) or activating downstream pathways (e.g., RAS-RAF-MEK-ERK).

EGFR抑制剂是针对该特征的重要靶向药物。 EGFR inhibitors are important targeted drugs for this hallmark 1937.

2
逃避免疫抑制 (Evading growth suppressors)

抑制细胞生长的主要蛋白为Rb和p53，肿瘤细胞常出现其失活或突变。 Key proteins inhibiting cell growth are Rb and p53, which are frequently inactivated or mutated in tumor cells.

可通过细胞周期蛋白依赖性激酶抑制剂进行干预。 Cyclin-dependent kinase inhibitors can interfere with this process 19.

3
抵抗细胞死亡 (Resisting cell death)

抑制细胞凋亡的典型蛋白为Bcl-2家族，癌细胞内此类蛋白常表达升高。 Bcl-2 family proteins, which are often upregulated in cancer cells, inhibit apoptosis.

促凋亡BH3模拟物可诱导凋亡并抑制肿瘤。 Proapoptotic BH3 mimetics can induce apoptosis and suppress tumors 38.

4
激活侵袭与转移 (Activating invasion and metastasis)

E-cadherin表达降低或丢失促进上皮-间质转化（EMT），提升癌细胞迁移和侵袭能力。 Downregulation or loss of E-cadherin promotes epithelial–mesenchymal transition (EMT), enhancing migration and invasiveness.

HGF/c-Met信号上调进一步推动转移，可被特定抑制剂阻断。 Upregulation of HGF/c-Met signaling further drives metastasis and can be blocked by targeted inhibitors 38,26,10.

5
诱导血管生成 (Inducing angiogenesis)

VEGF及其受体（VEGFR）是肿瘤新生血管生成的主要调控分子。 VEGF and its receptors (VEGFR) are major regulators of tumor angiogenesis.

缺氧环境刺激VEGF表达，抗VEGF药物被广泛用于抗肿瘤治疗。 Hypoxia stimulates VEGF expression; anti-VEGF agents are widely used for cancer therapy 38,25.

6
获得复制永生性 (Enabling replicative immortality)

癌细胞通过高表达端粒酶，持续维护端粒，从而实现不死增殖。 Cancer cells upregulate telomerase to maintain telomere length, enabling replicative immortality.

端粒酶抑制剂可阻断该机制。 Telomerase inhibitors can block this process 38,3,13.

7
规避免疫破坏 (Avoiding immune destruction)

肿瘤细胞通过高表达PD-L1，抑制T细胞功能，逃避免疫系统清除。 Tumor cells suppress T cell function and evade immune surveillance by upregulating PD-L1.

免疫检查点抑制剂（如抗PD-1/PD-L1抗体）是重要抗癌药物。 Immune checkpoint inhibitors (e.g., anti-PD-1/PD-L1 antibodies) are important anti-cancer drugs 38,36,21.

8
能量代谢重编程 (Deregulating cellular energetics)

癌细胞优先采用有氧糖酵解(沃伯格效应)以适应快速生长的能源需求。 Cancer cells preferentially utilize aerobic glycolysis (the Warburg effect) for rapid ATP production.

糖酵解相关关键蛋白包括GLUT1、PKM2等。 Key proteins involved include GLUT1 and PKM2 40,29,36.

9
基因组不稳定与突变 (Genome instability and mutation)

BRCA1/2等基因与DNA修复密切相关，其突变导致基因组不稳定。 Genes like BRCA1/2 are closely related to DNA repair; their mutations cause genome instability.

PARP抑制剂对携带BRCA突变的肿瘤有特殊疗效。 PARP inhibitors are effective against tumors with BRCA mutations 38,33.

10
肿瘤促发炎症及微环境改变 (Tumor-promoting inflammation & microenvironment)

肿瘤微环境中炎症细胞可分泌促生长与促转移因子（如IL-6、TNF-α等），促进肿瘤进展。 Inflammatory cells in the tumor microenvironment secrete growth- and metastasis-promoting factors (such as IL-6 and TNF-α), facilitating tumor progression.

抗炎药可用于优化肿瘤微环境，干预肿瘤发展。 Anti-inflammatory drugs can optimize the tumor microenvironment and intervene in tumor development 38,8.

课堂总结与实际意义 (Summary & practical value)

教师强调
每个通路都是药物研发的前沿靶点，精准治疗需依据分子分型选择。 Summary by the lecturer: Each pathway represents a frontier target for drug development; precision therapy depends on molecular subtyping.

癌症多步骤、多因素累积，多数患者肿瘤内各标志性特征并存。 Cancer arises through multistep, multifactorial accumulation, and most patients’ tumors exhibit multiple hallmarks simultaneously 17,27.

系统了解这些机制有助于早期筛查、诊断及个体化治疗方案的制定。 Systematic understanding of these mechanisms aids early screening, diagnosis, and personalized therapy planning 22.

表观重编程（Epigenetic reprogramming）

核心观点：

启动子高甲基化影响多种抑癌基因，涉及肿瘤抑制、细胞周期调控、凋亡、DNA修复及转移等过程。

Promoter hypermethylation of genes plays an important role in tumor suppression, cell cycle regulation, apoptosis, DNA repair, and metastatic potential.

1
启动子高甲基化的作用机制

The role of promoter hypermethylation

启动子高甲基化导致抑癌基因沉默，是驱动肿瘤形成和发展的重要表观遗传异常。 Promoter hypermethylation causes the silencing of tumor suppressor genes, which is a key epigenetic alteration driving tumorigenesis and progression.

这类甲基化异常可影响细胞周期调控基因（如p16^INK4a、p14^ARF）、凋亡调控基因（如DAPK）、DNA修复基因（如MGMT、MLH1、BRCA1）等。 Such abnormal methylation affects cell cycle regulators (e.g., p16^INK4a, p14^ARF), apoptosis regulators (e.g., DAPK), and DNA repair genes (e.g., MGMT, MLH1, BRCA1).

2
各肿瘤类型的高甲基化“特征谱”

Hypermethylation signature is specific to each type of cancer

不同肿瘤类型在高甲基化发生的频率和靶基因上有各自特异性。 Different types of cancer harbor distinct frequencies and patterns of promoter hypermethylation.

下图展示了多种抑癌基因在多种肿瘤（如结肠癌、乳腺癌、肺癌、白血病、脑瘤等）中的高甲基化频率差异。 The figure shows the varying frequencies of tumor suppressor gene hypermethylation in different cancers (e.g., colon, breast, lung, leukemia, brain, etc.).

这种“高甲基化特征谱”的存在，为肿瘤的分子分型和诊断提供了依据。 These hypermethylation signatures provide a molecular basis for cancer subtyping and diagnosis.

3
表观遗传调控与癌症标志性特征关系

Relationship with the hallmarks of cancer

启动子高甲基化介导的抑癌基因沉默，会促进持续增殖信号、逃避生长抑制、抵抗细胞死亡、基因组不稳定等癌症标志性特征的获得。 Silencing of tumor suppressor genes by promoter hypermethylation facilitates the acquisition of cancer hallmarks—such as sustaining proliferative signaling, evading growth suppressors, resisting cell death, and promoting genome instability.

表观遗传药物（如DNA甲基转移酶抑制剂）可逆转部分高甲基化改变，具有潜在治疗作用。 Epigenetic drugs (such as DNA methyltransferase inhibitors) can reverse some of these methylation changes and are potential cancer therapies.

4
课堂补充与实际意义

Lecture highlights and practical implications

教师强调，甲基化异常不仅限于基因突变，是揭示肿瘤分子机制和精准诊疗的前沿方向。 The lecturer emphasized that methylation abnormalities, aside from gene mutations, are at the frontier of understanding cancer mechanisms and enabling precision medicine.

个体肿瘤的甲基化谱可作为早期检测、预后评估和靶向治疗的生物标志物。 The individual cancer methylation profile serves as a biomarker for early detection, prognosis, and targeted therapies.

Epigenetic Reprogramming 表观重编程

新兴癌症获得能力
非突变性表观遗传重编程

Newly recognized cancer-enabling trait: nonmutational epigenetic reprogramming

Unlocks phenotypic plasticity of cancer cells. 解锁癌细胞的表型可塑性。

促使细胞以无须DNA序列突变的方式获得新表型。 Enables cells to acquire new phenotypes without changes in DNA sequence.

2
主要表观遗传机制

Main epigenetic mechanisms

DNA methylation DNA甲基化

Histone posttranslational modifications 组蛋白翻译后修饰

Noncoding RNAs (e.g., microRNAs) 非编码RNA（如microRNA）

3
表观遗传调控分子组成

Components of the epigenetic machinery

DNA甲基化酶（如DNMT1, DNMT3A）和去甲基化酶（TET1/2, IDH1/2） DNA methyltransferases (e.g., DNMT1, DNMT3A) and demethylases (TET1/2, IDH1/2)

组蛋白修饰酶和去修饰酶（如MLL, SETD2, EZH2, BMI1, LSD1, CBP/P300） Histone modifying and demodifying enzymes (e.g., MLL, SETD2, EZH2, BMI1, LSD1, CBP/P300)

核小体重塑复合体（如SNF5, BRG1/BRM, ARID1A/1B/2, ATRX） Nucleosome remodeling complexes (e.g., SNF5, BRG1/BRM, ARID1A/1B/2, ATRX)

组蛋白变体与其解读蛋白（如BRD4, TRIM33, ING1, BRCA1） Histone variants and readers (e.g., BRD4, TRIM33, ING1, BRCA1)

microRNAs等非编码RNA Noncoding RNAs, such as microRNAs

4
功能与机制

Functions and mechanisms

表观遗传调控复杂交织，影响基因表达潜力，为细胞命运和功能多样性提供基础。 Epigenetic regulation interweaves complexly, shaping gene expression potential and underpinning cell fate and functional diversity.

癌细胞中，调控失调导致基因表达谱异常，为肿瘤演进提供适应性。 In cancer cells, regulatory imbalance causes abnormal gene expression profiles and confers adaptability for tumor evolution.

5
大型基因组数据库与癌症分子图谱

Genomic cancer atlases

The Cancer Genome Atlas (TCGA) database collects key genomic and epigenomic changes in 33 different human cancers. 癌症基因组图谱（TCGA）数据库收集了33种主要人类癌症的基因组和表观基因组变异。

为肿瘤分子分型、预测与精准医疗提供数据基础。 This underpins molecular subtyping, prediction, and precision medicine for cancer.

课堂补充要点

Lecture highlights

新增癌症获得能力，如衰老细胞（senescent cells）、多型微生物组（polymorphic microbiomes）也赋予肿瘤独特的进化与适应特征。 Newly recognized cancer traits such as senescent cells and polymorphic microbiomes further give cancer unique evolutionary and adaptive features.

这些机制不一定改变DNA序列，但会显著影响癌症发生、发展和治疗反应。 These mechanisms may not alter DNA sequence, but have profound effects on tumor initiation, progression, and therapy response.

① Epigenetic Reprogramming

表观遗传重编程

英文原文中文对照
Epigenetic reprogramming表观遗传重编程
Chromatin structural changes and DNA methylation in cancer cells癌细胞中的染色质结构变化和DNA甲基化
Covalent modification of histones is critical in making regions of chromatin more or less hospitable for transcription组蛋白的共价修饰对改变染色质区域对转录的适宜性至关重要
Hypermethylation of CpG islands in the promoter region of genes inhibits transcription基因启动子区CpG岛的高甲基化会抑制转录
A normal epigenome正常的表观基因组
A cancer epigenome癌症的表观基因组
Promoter hypermethylation of genes playing important roles in processes encompassing tumor suppression, cell cycle regulation, apoptosis, DNA repair, and metastatic potential重要基因（涉及肿瘤抑制、细胞周期调控、凋亡、DNA修复及转移能力）启动子的高甲基化
Hypermethylation signature specific to each type of cancer各类癌症特异的高甲基化特征谱

图示描述对照

Figure
Chromatin structural changes in cancer cells. 图示：癌细胞中的染色质结构变化。

These two photomicrographs were taken from a patient with a squamous cell carcinoma of the skin. 这两幅显微照片来源于一位皮肤鳞状细胞癌患者。

The left panel shows normal epidermal cells within one millimeter of the contiguous tumor shown at the same magnification on the right. 左图显示距离肿瘤1毫米的正常表皮细胞，右图为同倍率下的癌细胞。

The chromatin, which stains purple as a result of its affinity to hematoxylin, appears much more coarse and granular in the cancer cells than in normal epidermis. 染色质因亲合苏木精染成紫色，在癌细胞中比正常表皮显得更粗大、颗粒状。

Such changes in the staining characteristics of chromatin are used by pathologists as diagnostic criteria for cancer. 染色质染色特征的这种变化，被病理学家作为癌症诊断标准之一。

补充机制

DNA methylation, histone modifications, and noncoding RNAs together alter gene expression without changing DNA sequence. DNA甲基化、组蛋白修饰及非编码RNA共同调控基因表达，无需改变DNA序列。

Epigenetic changes enable cancer cell plasticity and adaptation. 表观改变赋予癌细胞可塑性与适应性。

② Sustained Proliferative Signaling

持续增殖信号

英文原文中文对照
Sustained proliferative signaling持续增殖信号
Cancer cells produce their own growth signals癌细胞可自分泌生长信号
Overexpression of receptors, especially growth factor receptors like EGFR高表达受体，尤其是生长因子受体如EGFR
Ligand independent signaling via constitutively active EGFREGFR等受体可无配体也持续激活信号通路
Normal cell: only responds when growth factor binds and activates receptor正常细胞：只有生长因子结合并激活受体后才应答
Cancer cell: receptor is overexpressed or mutated, can activate downstream signals independently癌细胞：受体高表达或突变，可独立激活下游信号
Signaling cascades (e.g., PI3K/Akt, MAPK pathways) are thus constitutively active信号级联（如PI3K/Akt、MAPK通路）持续激活
This drives uncontrolled proliferation—a hallmark of cancer这导致失控的增殖——癌症标志性特征

图示描述对照

In normal cells, receptor dimerization and downstream activation require ligand binding. 正常细胞中，受体二聚化及下游激活须依赖配体结合。

In cancer cells, mutant or overexpressed receptors signal without ligand, keeping cells constantly dividing. 癌细胞内，受体突变或高表达，无需配体也能自激活，使细胞持续分裂。

串联总结

1
Epigenetic reprogramming causes visible chromatin changes, gene silencing or activation, and underlies the diagnostic features and versatility of cancer cells. 表观遗传重编程导致细胞核染色质可见性改变、基因沉默或激活，是癌症细胞诊断特征和功能多样性的分子根源。
2
Sustained proliferative signaling is achieved via self-sufficiency in growth signaling and receptor abnormalities, creating uncontrolled proliferation—a core cancer hallmark. 持续增殖信号靠自发产生信号及异常受体维持，推动癌细胞无约束增殖，是癌症最核心的获得性能力之一。

Sustained proliferation 持续增殖能力

RAS (HRAS, KRAS, NRAS) are proto-oncogenes — RAS（HRAS、KRAS、NRAS）是原癌基因

Transform normal cells into cancer cells when mutated — 突变时能将正常细胞转化为癌细胞

图左
正常细胞：
1
In normal cells:

RAS gene product is inactive (bound to GDP) unless a growth factor signal arrives and binds to the receptor — 正常细胞中，RAS蛋白通常与GDP结合处于失活状态，只有当有生长因子信号结合受体时才被激活

No proliferation — 无细胞增殖

2
After growth factor binding:

Receptor activation leads to RAS exchanging GDP for GTP, becoming active and stimulating cell proliferation — 生长因子结合后，受体被激活，RAS蛋白与GTP结合，启动信号转导，促进细胞增殖

图右
癌细胞：
1
In cancer cells:

The RAS gene product is locked into its GTP-binding shape and does not require a signal at the receptor in order to stimulate cell division — 癌细胞中，RAS蛋白由于突变维持在与GTP结合的活性状态，不需生长因子刺激即可持续激活下游信号通路，促使细胞不断分裂

Proliferation even without growth factor — 即使无生长因子存在也能持续增殖

底部说明：

About half of human tumors have mutant Ras oncogene — 大约一半的人类肿瘤存在RAS癌基因突变

机制本质总结（医学思维公式）

1
正常细胞 受体→生长因子结合→RAS（GDP→GTP）→信号级联→增殖 (Growth factor)+Receptor→RAS-GTP (active)→Proliferation(Growth factor)+Receptor→RAS-GTP (active)→Proliferation
2
癌细胞 RAS基因突变→RAS永久结合GTP→无需配体刺激即可自发传递增殖信号 (Mutant RAS always active)→Continuous proliferation(Mutant RAS always active)→Continuous proliferation

结论

RAS基因是细胞内关键的信号传导分子，正常时需要外界生长信号才激活；

突变后，RAS蛋白常年“自激活”，不经受体调控，细胞增殖失控，肿瘤形成；

临床上，RAS基因突变是许多癌症（如胰腺癌、结直肠癌等）的核心致病因素和潜在靶点—— 持续增殖信号是癌症最关键的获得性能力之一。

The tumor microenvironment orchestrates angiogenesis, proliferation, invasion and metastasis through the secretion of growth factors and cytokines.

肿瘤微环境通过分泌生长因子和细胞因子，调控血管生成、细胞增殖、侵袭与转移。

左侧要点对照

英文中文
✓ immune cells✓ 免疫细胞
✓ stromal cells✓ 间质细胞
✓ blood vessels✓ 血管
✓ extracellular matrix✓ 细胞外基质

机制流程（逐条解读）

1
肿瘤微环境由多种成分组成

免疫细胞（如淋巴细胞、巨噬细胞等）

间质细胞（如成纤维细胞、脂肪细胞等）

血管内皮细胞

细胞外基质（ECM）

2
分泌多种因子调控肿瘤进展

生长因子（如VEGF、PDGF）促进血管新生

细胞因子调节免疫抑制、炎症反应

ECM的降解（借助MMPs）促进细胞迁移与组织重构

3
成纤维细胞激活

普通成纤维细胞（Fibroblast）获得收缩能力后，变为癌相关成纤维细胞（CAF, cancer-associated fibroblast）

CAF广泛存在于恶性肿瘤，促进肿瘤细胞生长、基质重塑、转移及耐药

4
代谢重编程与脂肪细胞

脂肪细胞（Adipocyte）释放游离脂肪酸，提供能量

促进肿瘤生长与转移

5
过程图解

随肿瘤进展，细胞外基质由正常逐渐被重建、降解（改色、断裂表现）

血管发生改变，肿瘤内部出现缺氧区

多种信号分子（如TGF-β、PDGF、VEGF、Leptin等）贯穿调控

不同细胞（CAF、内皮细胞、激活/静止星状细胞等）协同作用，形成促进肿瘤发生、发展的复杂微环境

理论总结（结构化）

Tumor microenvironment（TME，肿瘤微环境）不是静态背景，而是动态支持和推动肿瘤演化的重要“土壤”。

TME中的各类细胞及基质互相交流，促进：

1
Angiogenesis (血管新生)
2
Proliferation (增殖)
3
Invasion (侵袭)
4
Metastasis (转移)

成纤维细胞获得收缩功能并变为CAF是肿瘤发展的标志性事件之一。

细胞外基质反复改造（由ECM→Modified ECM→Degraded ECM）为肿瘤细胞创造迁移甚至进入循环系统的“通道”。

TME也为肿瘤治疗带来挑战，因此成为新药开发和治疗干预的重要靶点。

EMT and increased motility and invasivenessEMT及增强的运动性与侵袭性

核心定义与机制：

英文原文

The Epithelial to Mesenchymal Transition (EMT)

A well polarized epithelial cell is converted into a non-polarized cell that gains migratory and invasive properties to become a mesenchymal stem cell

中文对照

上皮-间质转化（EMT）

一个高度极化的上皮细胞转变为非极化细胞，获得迁移和侵袭特性，变成具备间充质干细胞性质的细胞

示意图解读

左半部分
Epithelial State（上皮状态）

上皮细胞有紧密连接（tight junctions）、黏附连接（adherens junctions）和E-cadherin（钙黏蛋白）等结构，确保细胞极性和侧向黏连。

E-cadherin连接着β-catenin和α-catenin，进而连接至细胞骨架。

细胞排列紧密，不具备迁移和侵袭能力。

EMT发生
E-cadherin丧失（Loss of E-cadherin）

E-cadherin的减少或缺失导致黏附连接解体。

β-catenin游离进入胞质，随后被转运进细胞核，影响基因表达（如Wnt信号通路激活）。

右半部分
Mesenchymal State（间充质状态）

细胞失去极性、获得梭形、纤维母细胞样形态。

细胞间黏附明显减弱，具备更高的运动和侵袭能力。

失去上皮性标志
E-cadherin ↓  ⟹  Cell-cell adhesion ↓ , cell polarity ↓E-cadherin ↓⟹Cell-cell adhesion ↓, cell polarity ↓ 表现为细胞间连接丧失、极性消失
获得间充质属性
N-cadherin ↑, Vimentin ↑, Fibroblast-like ↑N-cadherin↑, Vimentin↑, Fibroblast-like ↑ 表现为支持运动和侵袭的蛋白上调
β-catenin信号变化
Loss of E-cadherin→β-catenin free in cytosol→nuclear translocation→EMT gene transcriptionLoss of E-cadherin→β-catenin free in cytosol→nuclear translocation→EMT gene transcription Wnt等途径启动EMT相关基因转录，促进恶性表型

核心要点归纳

EMT是指上皮细胞转变为间质样细胞，失去黏附、极性，获得迁移、侵袭能力；

伴随分子特征
E-cadherin↓, N-cadherin↑, Vimentin↑, MMPs↑；

EMT促进肿瘤细胞转移和恶性进展，是多种实体瘤难治性的重要机制。

右侧表格详细对照解释

上一行/失去的表型 (Loss of):

英文原文中文对照
Cytokeratin (intermediate filament) expression角蛋白（中间丝蛋白）表达
Tight junctions and epithelial adherens junctions involving E-cadherin紧密连接和以E-cadherin为核心的上皮黏附连接
Epithelial cell polarity上皮细胞极性
Epithelial gene expression program上皮基因表达程序

获得/新表型 (Acquisition of):

英文原文中文对照
Fibroblast-like shape成纤维细胞样形状
Motility运动性
Invasiveness侵袭性
Increased resistance to apoptosis对凋亡的抗性增强
Mesenchymal gene expression program including EMT-inducing transcription factors间充质基因表达程序（含调控EMT的转录因子）
Mesenchymal adherens junction protein (N-cadherin)间充质型黏附连接蛋白（N-cadherin）
Protease secretion (MMP-2, MMP-9)蛋白酶分泌（如MMP-2、MMP-9）
Vimentin (intermediate filament) expression波形蛋白（中间丝蛋白）表达
Fibronectin secretion纤维连接蛋白分泌
PDGF receptor expressionPDGF受体表达
αvβ6αv​β6​ integrin expressionαvβ6αv​β6​ 整合素表达
Stem cell-like traits干细胞样特征

How does the expression of the EMT-inducing transcription factor (Twist) lead to the loss of cell-cell junctions and promote increased motility and invasiveness in cells?

问Twist蛋白（EMT转录因子）的表达如何导致细胞-细胞连接丧失，从而增强细胞运动和侵袭能力？
答
2
图表内容拆解

(A) Immunofluorescence (免疫荧光染色):

上排 (+ control vector)：

E-cadherin, β-catenin, γ-catenin: 荧光强，细胞间有紧密连接（上皮标志）

fibronectin, vimentin: 荧光弱（间质标志低表达）

下排 (+ Twist vector)：

E-cadherin, β-catenin, γ-catenin: 荧光减弱，细胞间连接消失（上皮标志丧失）

fibronectin, vimentin: 荧光增强（间质标志高表达）

(B) Western Blot (蛋白免疫印迹):

Twist表达前（+ control vector）：

Epithelial markers (上皮标志):

E-cadherin, β-catenin, γ-catenin, α-catenin 表达强

Mesenchymal markers (间质标志):

fibronectin, vimentin, N-cadherin, α-SMA 表达弱

Twist表达后（+ Twist vector）：

上皮标志蛋白表达降低（条带变淡/消失）

间质标志蛋白表达增强（条带变强）

3
英文旁白逐句中英文讲解

Expression of epithelial markers, specifically E-cadherin, β-catenin, and γ-catenin, is repressed,

上皮标志的表达，尤其是E-cadherin（E钙黏蛋白）、β-catenin（β联蛋白）、γ-catenin（γ联蛋白）被抑制，

while expression of mesenchymal markers, specifically vimentin and fibronectin is induced

而间充质标志，尤其是vimentin（波形蛋白）和fibronectin（纤维连接蛋白）表达被诱导增加。

4
分子机制一图流（总结）

强制表达Twist（或其它EMT转录因子）导致：

1
上皮细胞间黏附分子（E-cadherin等）下调 → 细胞连接消失，极性丧失
2
间质相关分子（vimentin, fibronectin等）上调 → 细胞变得易运动、易侵袭
3
这就是EMT现象的分子核心

公式化表达：

Twist↑⇒E-cadherin, β-catenin↓Twist↑⇒E-cadherin, β-catenin↓

Twist↑⇒Vimentin, Fibronectin↑Twist↑⇒Vimentin, Fibronectin↑

5
最终结论

The ectopic expression of Twist triggers EMT by repressing epithelial markers and inducing mesenchymal ones, resulting in loss of cell-cell adhesion and increased potential for migration and invasion. Twist的表达启动EMT过程——抑制上皮标志蛋白、诱导间质标志蛋白表达，最终导致细胞-细胞连接丧失、细胞迁移和侵袭能力显著增强。

简明口诀：

“Twist上线，上皮丢失——间质属性一键获得，恶性特征急剧飙升”

增强的血管生成与肿瘤血管化的启动

Enhanced Angiogenesis & Initiation of Tumor Vascularization

1
基本概念 / Basic Concept

肿瘤的发展依赖于促血管生成的微环境。癌细胞及其周围的炎症、免疫细胞能够分泌多种促血管生成的生长因子和蛋白酶，帮助肿瘤创造出有利于自身生长和转移的新生血管系统。

Tumor progression depends on a pro-angiogenic microenvironment. Cancer cells, along with infiltrating inflammatory and immune cells, secrete various pro-angiogenic growth factors and proteases, building a new vascular network that supports tumor growth and metastasis .

2
主要分子机制与因子 / Key Molecular Mechanisms and Factors

主要的促血管生成因子包括：

VEGF-A、VEGF-C、VEGF-D

碱性成纤维生长因子（bFGF）

TNF-α、IL-8、TGF-α

血管生成素（Angiopoietins）、趋化因子（Chemokines）等

这些因子激活血管内皮细胞，促进其增殖和迁移，并通过蛋白酶（如MMP-9）降解细胞外基质，从而促进新生血管的生长。

Major pro-angiogenic factors include VEGF-A, VEGF-C, VEGF-D, bFGF, TNF-α, IL-8, TGF-α, angiopoietins and chemokines. These factors activate endothelial cells, promote their proliferation and migration, and, together with proteases like MMP-9, degrade the extracellular matrix to support new vessel formation .

3
“血管生成开关”（Angiogenic Switch） / The “Angiogenic Switch”

在肿瘤进展的关键阶段，抑制血管生成的因子逐渐被促血管生成的因子所取代，使肿瘤从“无血管新生”阶段转向“有血管新生”阶段。

During tumor progression, there is a critical moment called the “angiogenic switch,” when the balance shifts from anti-angiogenic signaling to predominantly pro-angiogenic signaling, allowing the tumor to start new blood vessel formation 40.

这一转变受致癌基因（如RAS, EGFR, HER2）激活和抑癌基因（如p53, PTEN, VHL）失活的调控。

This switch is driven by oncogene activation (e.g., RAS, EGFR, HER2) and tumor suppressor gene inactivation (e.g., p53, PTEN, VHL), leading to increased angiogenesis 40.

4
新生血管的形成过程 / Steps of Tumor Angiogenesis
1
内皮细胞被激活：受VEGF等因子刺激，血管内皮细胞开始活跃，准备出芽。Endothelial cell activation: Vascular endothelial cells are activated by VEGF and related factors and prepare to sprout.
2
形成“尖端细胞”与“茎端细胞”：VEGF促进部分内皮细胞转变为“尖端细胞”（tip cell），牵引新生血管生长，其余则为“茎端细胞”（stalk cell）负责拉长血管。Formation of “tip cells” and “stalk cells”: VEGF induces some endothelial cells to become “tip cells” that lead the new vessel, while “stalk cells” elongate the sprout. NOTCH-DLL4/VEGFR2 signaling refines this proces
3
内皮芽伸展与融合：新的血管芽不断长入肿瘤组织，通过NOTCH等信号迅速分化和融合，最终形成功能性但结构混乱的新生血管网络。Sprout extension and fusion: New vessel sprouts grow into the tumor mass, rapidly connecting and forming a chaotic vessel network
5
肿瘤新生血管的特征 / Features of Tumor Vessels

肿瘤新生血管与正常血管显著不同，表现为：

结构紊乱、排列无序

内皮细胞连接松散，有渗漏现象

周细胞覆盖丢失，基底膜异常

血管易于被肿瘤细胞侵入，为转移提供通道

Tumor neovessels are significantly different from normal vessels. They are disorganized, poorly structured, have leaky endothelial junctions, loss of pericyte coverage, abnormal basement membranes, and increase the risk of cancer cell intravasation and metastasis .

6
总结 / Summary

肿瘤细胞和它们的微环境（尤其是炎症、免疫细胞）通过分泌多种促血管生成因子，驱动“血管生成开关”的打开，激活内皮细胞，形成新生血管。这些新生血管为肿瘤提供营养、氧气，并为转移提供通道，是肿瘤生物学和抗肿瘤治疗的重要靶点。

Tumor cells and their microenvironment—including inflammatory and immune cells—drive the “angiogenic switch” by secreting pro-angiogenic factors, activating endothelial cells, and forming new blood vessels. These abnormal vessels nourish the tumor and provide pathways for metastasis, making angiogenesis a key target for cancer therapy

1
正常血管结构

英文：In normal vessels, endothelial cells are tightly connected by stable cell-cell junctions.

中文：在正常的血管中，内皮细胞通过稳定的细胞间连接紧密联结在一起。

图示a（Normal vessel）：可见内皮细胞整齐排列，有完整的周细胞包裹，基底膜结构良好，血管通透性低，功能正常。

2
肿瘤血管的异常结构与功能

英文：Tumor vessels are characterized by endothelial cell sprouting, disruption of endothelial cell junctions, loss of pericyte coverage and increased vessel leakiness, resulting in intravasation of tumor cells.

中文：肿瘤血管的特点是内皮细胞出现出芽、细胞之间连接受损、周细胞覆盖丧失以及血管渗漏性增加，从而导致肿瘤细胞能够进入血管（血管内转移）。

图示b（Tumor vessel）：可以看到血管结构紊乱，内皮细胞连接松散，大量肿瘤细胞、炎症细胞浸润，血管渗漏增多，周细胞数量减少。

3
血管生成开关（Angiogenic switch）

从正常血管到肿瘤血管的转变（箭头标注Angiogenic switch），对应肿瘤环境中产生大量促血管生成因子，如：

VEGF、MMPs、细胞因子和趋化因子等。

4
微环境的影响

英文：Hostile tumor microenvironment that fuel cancer progression.

中文：恶劣的肿瘤微环境促进肿瘤的进展。

肿瘤组织图像（右侧绿色荧光显微照片）对比突出了肿瘤区（Tumor vessels）内血管形态异常、排列紊乱，远非正常组织的规则结构（Normal vessels）。

5
图例说明

图下标注图例内容（部分中文化）：

Pericytes：周细胞

ECs：血管内皮细胞

Sprouting ECs：出芽的内皮细胞

Tumor cell：肿瘤细胞

Angiogenic growth factors：促血管生成生长因子

MMPs：基质金属蛋白酶

Leakage：渗漏

Intravasation：血管内转移

核心考点归纳（简明中英双语）

肿瘤血管由于内皮细胞连接松散、周细胞减少和渗漏性增强，会为肿瘤细胞进入血流（血管内转移）和转移扩散提供便利。 Tumor vessels, through disrupted cell junctions and pericyte loss, facilitate tumor cell invasion into the bloodstream and metastasis.

促血管生成因子（如VEGF等）主导了从正常血管到肿瘤异常血管的转变。 Pro-angiogenic factors (e.g., VEGF) are key drivers in the transition from normal to abnormal tumor vasculature.

恶劣的肿瘤微环境持续激活血管生成开关，促进肿瘤进展。 The hostile tumor microenvironment continuously activates the angiogenic switch, advancing cancer progression.

1
微管网络与细胞骨架重塑（页29/30）

微管网络在细胞周期中有关键作用。 The microtubule network plays a key role in the cell cycle.

在间期，微管主要负责细胞内的物流运输和信号传递。 During interphase, microtubules support intracellular trafficking and signaling.

在有丝分裂期，微管重组成纺锤体，实现染色体的排列与分离。 During mitosis, microtubules reorganize to form the spindle, ensuring chromosome alignment and segregation.

微管是多种抗肿瘤药物（如紫杉醇、长春新碱）的作用靶点，通过影响微管动态性抑制癌细胞分裂。 Microtubules are the target of several anticancer drugs, which act by affecting microtubule dynamics and preventing cancer cell division.6

在细胞迁移中，微管的极性网络朝向迁移方向排列，与细胞器局部重排协同，维持细胞“前-后”极性。 In cell migration, the polarized microtubule network is oriented in the direction of movement and coordinates with organelle repositioning to maintain front-rear polarity.24

微管聚合和解聚的动态调节决定迁移效率，并通过与中心体、高尔基体的互动实现定向迁移。 Dynamic polymerization and depolymerization of microtubules regulate migration efficiency, with centrosome and Golgi repositioning supporting directional movement.

2
肌动蛋白网络及侵袭性肿瘤细胞突出结构（页31/32）

肌动蛋白网络通过F-Actin的成核与组装，支持细胞运动及膜突出形成。 The actin network supports cell motility and membrane protrusions through nucleation and assembly of F-actin.

侵袭性癌细胞常见三种突出结构：出芽泡（bleb）、侵袭伪足（invadopodia）、波状伪足（lamellipodia/pseudopodia），分别由不同调控因子控制。 There are three main types of protrusive structures in invasive cancer cells: protruding blebs, invadopodia, and lamellipodia/pseudopodia, each regulated by distinct molecular factors.

出芽泡主要由ROCK、髓蛋白、ERM蛋白和formin介导； Protruding blebs involve ROCK, myosin, ERM proteins, and formin.

侵袭伪足由N-WASP、Arp2/3复合体和Cortactin驱动； Invadopodia formation is driven by N-WASP, Arp2/3 complex, and cortactin.

波状伪足依赖WAVE复合体调控的F-Actin分支聚合。 Lamellipodia depend on the WAVE complex-mediated branching of F-actin.30

肌动蛋白与微管协同调节细胞迁移的不同阶段，包括附着、突出、收缩、回缩。 Actin and microtubules work together to coordinate the phases of cell migration: adhesion, protrusion, contraction, and retraction.

强黏附点和微管阵列为迁移提供稳定动力学基础。 Strong focal adhesions and the microtubule array serve as the basis for stable and efficient migration.

3
Septins：第四类细胞骨架元件及其功能（页33/34）

Septins是新近被重视的骨架系统，常定位于肌动蛋白应力纤维上。 Septins are recognized as the fourth cytoskeletal component, often localized on actin stress fibers.

正常成纤维细胞与肿瘤相关成纤维细胞septin/肌动蛋白结构明显不同。 The septin/actin structures in normal fibroblasts are distinctly different from those in cancer-associated fibroblasts.

肿瘤细胞来源的可溶性因子上调成纤维细胞的Cdc42EP3表达，增加actin-septin作用。 Cancer cell–derived soluble factors upregulate Cdc42EP3 expression in fibroblasts, enhancing actin-septin interactions.30

这种协调促进基质重塑，提高细胞机械反应性与张力，有利于CAF迁移。 This coordination promotes matrix remodeling, increases mechanoresponsiveness and tension, and facilitates CAF migration.

septin通过加强肌动蛋白纤维，促进肿瘤微环境的重构和CAF迁移，产生促肿瘤效应。 Septins, by strengthening actin fibers, promote matrix remodeling and CAF migration, contributing to a pro-tumorigenic environment.

课堂重点补充（结合录音老师解读）

老师强调，细胞骨架重塑和极性迁移是肿瘤细胞侵袭和转移的基础。 The instructor highlighted that cytoskeleton remodeling and cell polarity in migration are fundamental for cancer cell invasion and metastasis. 4

各类骨架元件紧密协作，调控细胞形态变化与方向移动。 Different cytoskeleton components cooperate closely to regulate changes in cell shape and directional movement.

Rearrangements of cytoskeleton elements/The microtubule network细胞骨架元件的重排/微管网络

癌细胞的葡萄糖代谢发生了显著改变，即使在有氧、线粒体功能良好的情况下也会如此。 There is a significant alteration in glucose metabolism in cancer cells, even in the presence of oxygen and fully functioning mitochondria.

这种现象被称为Warburg效应，表现为癌细胞活跃摄取葡萄糖，并将其发酵转化为乳酸。 This phenomenon is called the Warburg effect, where cancer cells show increased glucose uptake and ferment glucose to lactate.

在正常细胞中，葡萄糖通过GLUT1转运入细胞，经糖酵解生成丙酮酸，后者经线粒体有氧氧化生成大量ATP。 In normal cells, glucose enters the cell via the GLUT1 transporter, undergoes glycolysis to form pyruvate, which is then oxidized in mitochondria to generate a large amount of ATP.

正常细胞典型代谢途径产生32个ATP分子；只有在低氧时才通过乳酸发酵，每分子葡萄糖只得2个ATP。 The typical metabolic pathway in normal cells produces 32 ATP molecules, while only 2 ATP are generated per glucose via fermentation under hypoxic conditions.

癌细胞即便有足够氧气也主要依赖糖酵解途径，并产生乳酸，ATP产出少但利于快速供能和生长。 Cancer cells mainly rely on glycolysis and produce lactate even with sufficient oxygen, yielding low ATP but supporting rapid energy demand and growth.

癌细胞GLUT1转运蛋白高度表达，导致葡萄糖大量摄取，这也是临床PET-CT成像能发现肿瘤的原理。 Cancer cells highly express the GLUT1 transporter, leading to excessive glucose uptake, which is also the basis for tumor detection by clinical PET-CT imaging.

课上老师强调，这种葡萄糖代谢重编程有助于细胞获得生物合成前体，为快速增殖提供物质基础。 The instructor emphasized in class that this glucose metabolic reprogramming helps cancer cells acquire building blocks for biosynthesis, supporting rapid proliferation.

Warburg效应的两个核心特征是：①葡萄糖摄取量显著升高；②即便有氧也以乳酸形式发酵葡萄糖。 The two core features of the Warburg effect are: (1) significantly increased glucose uptake; (2) glycolytic fermentation of glucose to lactate even under aerobic conditions.

Escape immune damage逃避免疫损伤

1
癌细胞通过以下机制逐步逃避免疫系统监视和清除： Over time, tumor cells evade immunity by:
2
下调抗原递呈（抗原表达下调） Downregulating antigen presentation
3
上调抑制性细胞表面分子的表达（如PD-L1、FasL），直接抑制或杀伤细胞毒性T细胞（CD8+） Upregulating expression of inhibitory cell surface molecules (PD-L1, FasL) that directly kill cytotoxic T cells (CD8+)
注释：PD1与PD-L1结合会抑制T细胞杀伤作用。 Binding of PD1 on CD8+ to PD-L1 at the surface of tumor cell inhibits cytotoxicity of T cells.
4
招募调节性免疫细胞（如Tregs），减少抗肿瘤免疫反应 Recruiting regulatory cells to attenuate antitumor immunity (e.g., Tregs, M2 macrophages)
注释：通过分泌免疫抑制性细胞因子和改变肿瘤微环境的营养成分实现。 Through the liberation of immunosuppressive cytokines and alterations in the nutrient content of the tumor microenvironment.
5
抑制树突状细胞功能，减少促炎细胞因子的产生，导致巨噬细胞/其他免疫细胞募集不足 Inhibiting dendritic cells that can no longer produce proinflammatory cytokines to attract macrophages or other immune cells
6
调节性T细胞（Tregs）进一步抑制抗肿瘤免疫应答、并促进生长因子的分泌 Tregs dampen anti-tumor immune responses and increase secretion of growth factors

配图要点解读：肿瘤免疫抑制微环境示意图：

Immune suppressive microenvironment:

M2巨噬细胞（M2） 分泌VEGF与IL-4，促进血管新生及免疫抑制

嗜中性粒细胞（Neu） 产生MMP-9与VEGF，助力微环境重塑与血管生成

树突状细胞（DC） 功能被抑制

B细胞 分泌IL-10、TGF-β，抑制免疫反应

Tregs细胞 抑制CD8+ T细胞功能，促进生长因子释放

NK细胞 受IL-2影响，但功能常被削弱

总结

通过这些机制，肿瘤逐渐塑造出“免疫抑制性微环境”，逃避免疫监视并促进自身生长转移。 By these mechanisms, tumors create an “immune suppressive microenvironment”, thus escaping immune surveillance and promoting tumorigenesis.

癌症治疗与「癌症标志特征」的靶向/联合治疗Cancer treatment and targeted/combination therapy based on "cancer hallmarks"

主要内容梳理

1
Sustaining Proliferative Signaling（维持增殖信号）

涉及多种生长因子受体通路（如EGFR, FGFR, PDGFR, MET, TNFR等）。

靶点包括：PI3K-Akt-mTOR、Ras-Raf-MEK-Erk等。

药物例子：针对EGFR的抑制剂。

2
Evading Growth Suppression（逃避生长抑制）

扰动细胞周期调控与自噬相关通路，如p53、Rb通路，mTOR、MAPK等。

典型靶点：CDK4/6 抑制剂，mTOR抑制剂。

3
Avoiding Immune Destruction（逃避免疫破坏）

通过PD-1/PD-L1、CTLA-4等免疫检查点抑制T细胞功能。

药物例子：免疫检查点抑制剂（如PD-1/PD-L1抗体）。

4
Enabling Replicative Immortality（实现复制永生性）

依靠端粒酶（TERT）的活性和相关信号（如MYC/STAT等）。

可用策略：端粒酶抑制或调控相关通路。

5
Tumor Promoting Inflammation（炎症促进肿瘤生长）

肿瘤微环境中的促炎因子（TNF-α, IL-6等），激活NF-κB, STAT3, PI3K, MAPK信号。

抗炎靶点，如阻断NF-κB或相关细胞因子的药物。

6
Activating Invasion & Metastasis（促进侵袭和转移）

通过EMT表型改变、基质重塑因子（如MMPs）、细胞黏附分子变化实现。

可能策略：靶向EMT通路或基质降解相关分子。

7
Inducing Angiogenesis（诱导新生血管形成）

VEGF、IGF、EGF等生长因子促进新血管生成。

代表药物：贝伐单抗（抗VEGF抗体）等。

8
Genome Instability & Mutation（基因组不稳定与突变）

典型例子：BRCA突变相关DNA修复缺陷。

药物例子：PARP抑制剂（针对BRCA突变肿瘤）。

9
Resisting Cell Death（抗凋亡能力增强）

激活抗凋亡通路（如BCL-2上调，p53失活等）。

药物例子：BCL-2 抑制剂。

10
Deregulating Cellular Energetics（能量代谢重编程）

依赖葡萄糖代谢、乳酸发酵（Warburg效应），靶向GLUT1、PI3K等。

可使用代谢抑制剂。

总括

现代肿瘤治疗通过靶向不同的“癌症标志特征”相关信号通路，结合免疫治疗、分子疗法、代谢干预等，实现多靶点联合疗法，有效抑制肿瘤生长和转移，延缓耐药的发生。

022025-10-08（第 2 讲）

表观遗传重编程Epigenetic Reprogramming

表观遗传学通过不改变DNA序列来调控基因表达。

Epigenetics regulates gene expression without alteration of the DNA sequence.

在癌症中，基因的异常激活（如癌基因）或失活（如抑癌基因）是肿瘤发生的重要机制之一。

In cancer, inappropriate activation of oncogenes or inactivation of tumor suppressors is an important mechanism of tumorigenesis.

对细胞稳态重要通路的失调也与表观遗传密切相关，如细胞周期调控、DNA修复、凋亡、炎症反应、信号传导、细胞生长和分化等。

Misregulation of important pathways that control normal cellular homeostasis is also closely related to epigenetics, such as cell cycle regulation, DNA repair, apoptosis, inflammatory response, signaling, cell growth and differentiation.

表观遗传转录调控主要通过三种途径实现：

Epigenetic transcriptional control mainly occurs through three pathways:

1
DNA甲基化
1
DNA methylation
2
组蛋白的共价修饰及染色质重塑（主要指甲基化和乙酰化） 2. Covalent histone modifications and chromatin remodeling (mainly methylation and acetylation)
3
非编码RNA（如miRNA、lncRNA等） 3. Noncoding RNAs (such as miRNAs, lncRNAs, etc.)

开发针对肿瘤治疗的表观遗传药物是当前的研究前沿。

The development of epigenetic drugs for cancer therapy is at the forefront of current research.

机制细节解读 Mechanism Details

1
DNA甲基化（DNA methylation）

启动子区DNA甲基化通常导致基因表达抑制，常见于抑癌基因（如p16, p53, BRCA1）沉默。 DNA methylation at gene promoters usually causes gene expression repression, which frequently results in silencing tumor suppressor genes (such as p16, p53, BRCA1).

基因体高甲基化可能导致癌基因激活（如DLX1, POU3F3）。 Hypermethylation at gene bodies may lead to activation of oncogenes (such as DLX1, POU3F3).

DNA甲基化异常还与药物抗性相关。 Aberrant DNA methylation is also associated with drug resistance.

2
组蛋白修饰与染色质重塑（Histone modifications & Chromatin remodeling）

组蛋白去乙酰化酶（HDACs）通常导致多种基因的沉默，尤其是抑癌基因。 Histone deacetylases (HDACs) usually cause silencing of various genes, especially tumor suppressor genes.

组蛋白乙酰化（H3K9ac、H3K18ac等）发生在启动子、增强子及基因体时促进基因转录，尤其可能激活癌基因。 Histone acetylation at gene promoters, enhancers, and gene bodies promotes gene transcription, especially oncogenes.

组蛋白甲基化（如H3K27M、H3K36M突变）与肿瘤类型特异性强相关——不同甲基化位点决定基因激活（H3K4、H3K36、H3K79）或抑制（H3K9、H3K27、H4K20）。 Oncohistone mutations (such as H3K27M, H3K36M) are strongly associated with specific tumor types. Different methylation sites determine gene activation (H3K4, H3K36, H3K79) or repression (H3K9, H3K27, H4K20).

多数组蛋白修饰酶为底物特异性蛋白。 Most histone methylation enzymes are substrate-specific proteins.

3
非编码RNA（Noncoding RNA）

非编码RNA通过RNA-RNA或RNA-蛋白、RNA-DNA等相互作用调控表观遗传。 Noncoding RNAs function through RNA-RNA, RNA-protein, and RNA-DNA interaction to regulate epigenetics.

某些非编码RNA（如miRNA, lncRNA）在不同肿瘤中表现出促进肿瘤或抑制肿瘤的作用。 Some noncoding RNAs (such as miRNA, lncRNA) play tumor-promoting or tumor-suppressive roles in different cancers.

老师补充说明Teacher’s Extra Notes

甲基化异常可通过启动子抑制抑癌基因表达，增加致癌风险。 Aberrant methylation at promoters suppresses tumor suppressor gene expression, increasing cancer risk.

DNA甲基化增加或去甲基化缺失常见于肿瘤。 Increased DNA methylation and loss of demethylation are common in tumors.

表观遗传药物研究主要针对DNA甲基化、组蛋白修饰及非编码RNA通路。 Current drug development focuses on DNA methylation, histone modification, and noncoding RNA pathways.

总结 Summary

表观遗传调控癌症的核心在于基因表达异常，通路失调，最终影响细胞增殖、转移、DNA修复、凋亡与耐药等肿瘤进展关键环节。 The core of epigenetic regulation of cancer lies in abnormal gene expression and pathway misregulation, ultimately affecting cell proliferation, metastasis, DNA repair, apoptosis, drug resistance—all key points in cancer progression. 图例 Legend

Me = DNA甲基化（DNA methylation）；紫色

Me = 组蛋白甲基化（Histone methylation）；蓝色

Ac = 组蛋白乙酰化（Histone acetylation）；绿色

1
表观遗传重编程概览
1
Overview of Epigenetic Reprogramming

表观遗传重编程包括DNA甲基化、组蛋白甲基化和组蛋白乙酰化，是调控基因表达的三大核心机制。

Epigenetic reprogramming consists of DNA methylation, histone methylation, and histone acetylation—the three key mechanisms for gene expression regulation.

每种表观遗传修饰都涉及“作家”（添加修饰）、“读者”（识别修饰）、“擦除者”（移除修饰）三类关键分子。

Each type of epigenetic modification involves three main classes of molecules: “writers” (add modifications), “readers” (recognize modifications), and “erasers” (remove modifications).

1.1 主要分子类别

1.1 Main Molecular Categories

作家（Writers）：

DNA甲基转移酶
如DNMT1 DNA methyltransferases: e.g., DNMT1
组蛋白甲基转移酶
如SETD2、DOT1L Histone methyltransferases: e.g., SETD2, DOT1L
组蛋白乙酰转移酶
如CBP/p300 Histone acetyltransferases: e.g., CBP/p300

读者（Readers）：

甲基化CpG结合蛋白
如UHRF1 Methyl-CpG binding domain proteins: e.g., UHRF1
溴结构域等其他结合蛋白
如BRCA1 Other binding proteins such as bromodomain-containing proteins, BRCA1

擦除者（Erasers）：

DNA去甲基化酶
如TET1 DNA demethylating enzymes: e.g., TET1
组蛋白去甲基酶
如LSDs Histone demethylases: e.g., LSDs
组蛋白去乙酰化酶
如HDAC1, Sirt1 Histone deacetylases: e.g., HDAC1, Sirt1

1.2 三种修饰的相互作用

1.2 Interplay Between the Three Modifications

DNA甲基化、组蛋白甲基化和乙酰化的不同组合，决定了染色质是否开放，基因能否表达。

The combinations of DNA methylation, histone methylation, and acetylation determine chromatin accessibility and gene activity.

三种修饰通过多种酶协同调控肿瘤发生发展过程中基因表达谱的重塑。

These three modifications, coordinated by various enzymes, reshape gene expression profiles during tumorigenesis 1520.

2
DNA甲基化——作家
2
DNA Methylation—The Writers

DNA甲基化于20世纪80年代被发现，是最丰富的表观遗传修饰之一，对发育和细胞生物学至关重要。

DNA methylation, discovered in the early 1980s, is one of the most abundant epigenetic modifications and is crucial for development and cellular biology.

DNA甲基转移酶（DNMTs）家族包括5个成员：DNMT1、DNMT2、DNMT3a、DNMT3b和DNMT3L。

The DNA methyltransferase (DNMT) family includes five members: DNMT1, DNMT2, DNMT3a, DNMT3b, and DNMT3L.

这些酶催化甲基从S-腺苷甲硫氨酸转移到胞嘧啶的第五位碳原子，特别是在CpG位点。

These enzymes catalyze the transfer of a methyl group to the fifth carbon of cytosine, especially at CpG sites 15.

2.1 生物与肿瘤学意义

2.1 Biological and Oncological Relevance

DNA甲基化是胚胎发育和细胞分化必不可少的调控机制。

DNA methylation is essential in embryonic development and cell differentiation.

在多种癌症中，发现DNMT1、DNMT3a、DNMT3b通常高表达。

Overexpression of DNMT1, DNMT3a, and DNMT3b has been observed in multiple cancers.

启动子区的DNA甲基化一般导致基因沉默（尤其是抑癌基因），而某些基因体内甲基化则与癌基因激活相关。

Methylation in promoters generally results in gene silencing (especially tumor suppressor genes), while methylation within gene bodies may be associated with oncogene activation 1415.

2.2 结构与机制补充（含老师讲解内容）

2.2 Structure and Mechanism (Including Teacher’s Commentary)

老师补充
如果基因启动子区高度甲基化，这通常会抑制对应基因的表达，例如抑癌基因p16、p53等，增加肿瘤风险。
Teacher’s comment
If the promoter region has high methylation, this usually silences its gene (such as tumor suppressor genes like p16, p53), increasing cancer risk 14.

相反，部分癌基因在基因体区域甲基化后可能异常被激活。

Conversely, methylation in gene bodies may lead to abnormal activation of some oncogenes.

DNA甲基化的异常还与耐药相关。

Aberrant DNA methylation is also associated with drug resistance 20.

3
分子图示解读
3
Interpreting Key Molecular Diagrams

左侧图示意了核小体核心内的组蛋白——H2A、H2B、H3、H4形成八聚体，DNA缠绕其上。

The left diagram depicts the nucleosome core, with histones H2A, H2B, H3, and H4 forming an octamer, around which DNA is wrapped.

组蛋白N端尾部是各种修饰的主要区域，包括甲基化（Me）、乙酰化（Ac）、磷酸化（P）、泛素化（Ub）等。

The N-terminal tails of histones are major sites for modifications such as methylation (Me), acetylation (Ac), phosphorylation (P), and ubiquitination (Ub) 15.

DNA双链上的特定胞嘧啶被甲基化，酶包括DNMT1（维护甲基化）、DNMT3a/3b（起始甲基化），去甲基化由TET家族完成。

Specific cytosines in the DNA backbone are methylated by DNMT1 (maintenance), DNMT3a/3b (de novo methylation), and demethylation is carried out by the TET family.

组合修饰决定染色质“打开”还是“关闭”，从而调控基因的活性。

The combination of these modifications determines whether chromatin is “open” or “closed,” thus regulating gene activity.

经典结肠癌演变多步骤模型Classic Model for Sequential Genetic Alterations during Colon Cancer Evolution

1
DNA甲基化异常是结肠癌发生发展的关键推动力之一。
1
Aberrant DNA methylation is a key driving force in the development and progression of colon cancer.

在正常上皮细胞向腺瘤、癌变和转移整个演变过程中，DNA甲基化模式逐渐被扰动。

Throughout the transition from normal epithelium to adenoma, carcinoma, and metastasis, DNA methylation patterns become progressively altered.

2
结肠癌的分期、基因突变与表观遗传沉默高度相关。
2
Disease staging, genetic mutations, and epigenetic silencing are highly correlated in colon cancer.

初期发生APC基因突变及5号染色体丢失，导致上皮细胞高增殖。

Early stages involve APC mutation and chromosome 5 loss, resulting in hyperproliferative epithelium.

随后伴随 SFRP/Wnt 信号基因、CHFR、MGMT 的表观遗传沉默，KRAS/BRAF突变，18号染色体丢失。

Subsequently, epigenetic silencing of SFRP/Wnt signaling genes, CHFR, MGMT, as well as KRAS/BRAF mutations, and chromosome 18 loss occur.

病程中后期会有17号染色体丢失（p53等），MSH2、MLH1等基因突变以及其他染色体改变。

Later stages show chromosome 17 loss (including p53), MSH2/MLH1 mutations, and other chromosomal changes.

3
不同阶段DNA甲基化改变与肿瘤进展密切相关。
3
DNA methylation alterations at each stage are closely associated with tumor progression.

腺瘤各分级（class I〜III）及癌变阶梯，DNA甲基化异常不断累积，使癌症表型逐步显现。

During the stepwise progression through adenoma classes I–III and carcinoma, methylation abnormalities accumulate, gradually driving malignant phenotype.

4
DNA甲基化谱具有疾病诊断和预后评估价值。
4
DNA methylation profiling holds value in disease diagnosis and prognostic prediction.

靶向检测DNA甲基化有助于肿瘤预防、风险评估和早期诊断（左侧蓝色箭头）。

Targeting DNA methylation can aid in prevention, risk assessment, and early diagnosis (blue arrow).

在肿瘤进展、治疗及预后评估中同样可作为分子标记（右侧蓝色箭头）。

It also provides useful molecular markers for tumor progression, treatment, and prognosis assessment (right-side blue arrow).

5
结论/总结
5
Conclusion/Summary

结肠癌的发生发展不是单一基因事件，而是包括多基因突变和表观遗传失调的多阶段累积过程，DNA甲基化扰动在每一阶段中均扮演关键角色。

Colon cancer development is a multi-step, cumulative process involving multigene mutations and epigenetic dysregulation, where DNA methylation perturbation plays a critical role at every stage.

DNA甲基化异常与肿瘤发生发展 | Abnormal DNA Methylation and Tumorigenesis

DNA甲基化异常是肿瘤表观遗传改变最常见的特征。

Abnormal DNA methylation is the most common epigenetic alteration in tumors.

肿瘤细胞中通常存在全基因组甲基化降低（低甲基化）和某些基因启动子区甲基化升高（高甲基化）的现象。

In tumor cells, there is generally a global decrease of DNA methylation (hypomethylation) and an increase of methylation (hypermethylation) in certain gene promoter regions4.

全基因组低甲基化可导致基因组不稳定，并容易激活癌基因。

Global hypomethylation may result in genomic instability and tends to activate oncogenes.

启动子高甲基化常见于肿瘤抑制基因，这会抑制这些基因的表达。

Promoter hypermethylation usually targets tumor suppressor genes and represses their expression32.

肿瘤抑制基因的沉默促进了肿瘤的发生和进展。

Silencing of tumor suppressor genes promotes tumor development and progression.

DNA甲基化模式的变化可作为肿瘤早期筛查和预后的分子标志物。

Changes in DNA methylation patterns can serve as molecular markers for early diagnosis and prognosis in cancer4.

与基因突变不同，DNA甲基化修饰具有可逆性和动态调控的特点。

Unlike genetic mutations, DNA methylation modifications are reversible and dynamically regulated.

检测甲基化异常有助于评估治疗效果和判断肿瘤复发风险。

Detection of aberrant methylation can help evaluate therapeutic effect and predict tumor recurrence risk.

DNA甲基化 读取蛋白 | THE READERS of DNA Methylation

甲基化CpG结合结构域蛋白（MeCP）家族能够读取已建立的甲基化DNA序列。

Methyl-CpG binding domain protein (MeCP) families can read the established methylated DNA sequences.

这些蛋白依据其结合甲基化DNA的功能结构域，被分为三大类：

They are classified into three families based on the functional domains used for binding to methylated DNA.

第一类是含有甲基化CpG结合结构域（MBD）的蛋白。

The first category is Methyl-CpG Binding Domain (MBD)-containing proteins.

第二类是甲基化CpG结合锌指蛋白，例如KAISO（ZBTB33）、ZBTB4和ZBTB38，这些蛋白通过锌指结构与甲基化DNA结合。

The second category consists of Methyl-CpG binding Zinc finger proteins, such as KAISO (ZBTB33), ZBTB4, and ZBTB38, which bind to methylated DNA via zinc-finger motifs.

第三类是两种类似泛素的蛋白，具有PHD和RING结构域，即UHRF1和UHRF2。它们通过Set和RING相关（SRA）结构域识别5-甲基胞嘧啶（5-mC）。

The third group consists of two ubiquitin-like proteins with PHD and RING finger domains, namely UHRF1 and UHRF2, which recognize 5-mC via Set and RING-Associated (SRA) domains.

UHRF1在肿瘤发生中的作用 | Role of UHRF1 in Carcinogenesis

UHRF1（一种含有PHD和RING结构域的泛素样蛋白）在维持DNA甲基化的正确性中起关键作用。

UHRF1 (ubiquitin-like containing PHD and RING finger domains 1) plays a key role in the maintenance of correct DNA methylation.

UHRF1通过其SRA结构域能与HDAC1和DNMT1相互作用，促进表观遗传信息的协调调控。

Through the SRA domain, UHRF1 interacts with HDAC1 and DNMT1, coordinating the regulation of the epigenetic code.

UHRF1也能结合到组蛋白H3上特定的修饰位点（H3K9me2/3），实现表观遗传层次的交叉调控。

UHRF1 binds to H3K9me2/3 modifications on histone H3, achieving crosstalk between different epigenetic modifications.

UHRF1介导的表观遗传交叉调控为DNA甲基化和组蛋白修饰之间的协同提供了进一步证据。

UHRF1-mediated crosstalk provides additional evidence for the synergy between DNA methylation and histone modifications.

通过UHRF1的多种结构域协同作用，它能调控包括组蛋白乙酰化、组蛋白甲基化、组蛋白去乙酰化和泛素化等多种表观遗传标记，最终影响基因表达。

By using its multiple domains, UHRF1 can regulate histone acetylation, methylation, deacetylation, and ubiquitination, ultimately affecting gene expression.

DNA甲基化的读取蛋白
UHRF1在肿瘤发生中的作用DNA methylation readers: Role of UHRF1 in carcinogenesis

UHRF1是一种含有PHD和RING结构域的泛素样蛋白，在调控DNA甲基化中起重要作用。

UHRF1 is a ubiquitin-like protein containing plant homeodomain (PHD) and really interesting new gene (RING) finger domains, playing a crucial role in DNA methylation regulation.

UHRF1在多种人类癌细胞中高表达，可作为多种癌症预后和诊断的潜在生物标志物。

UHRF1 is overexpressed in various human cancer cells and is a potential biomarker for the prognosis and diagnosis of several types of cancer.

UHRF1高表达会导致DNA高甲基化，进而抑制多种肿瘤抑制基因（TSGs），促进肿瘤的发生、进展和侵袭。

High expression of UHRF1 leads to DNA hypermethylation, which inhibits a variety of tumor suppressor genes (TSGs), promoting the occurrence, progression, and invasion of cancer.

⚠️ 这种抑制有时可能有益，比如可抑制多药耐药基因（MDR1）的表达。

⚠️ This repression could be beneficial in some cases, such as repressing the expression of the MDR1 gene (multidrug resistance).

结构与功能

Structure and function:

UHRF1蛋白有多个结构域，包括对组蛋白乙酰化、组蛋白甲基化、去乙酰化及泛素化的识别作用。

The UHRF1 protein contains multiple domains, recognizing histone acetylation, methylation, deacetylation, and ubiquitination.

SRA结构域能与HDAC1和DNMT1相互作用，实现DNA甲基化与组蛋白去乙酰化之间的交互调控。

The SRA domain interacts with HDAC1 and DNMT1, allowing crosstalk between DNA methylation and histone deacetylation.

如果UHRF1与DNMT1的相互作用发生缺陷（Defective interaction），会导致DNA低甲基化（hypomethylation），影响肿瘤抑制基因的表达调控。

If the interaction of UHRF1 with DNMT1 is defective, it leads to DNA hypomethylation, impacting the regulation of tumor suppressor genes.

图示补充说明 | Figure explanation:

高表达UHRF1 → DNA高甲基化 → 抑制TSGs。 High UHRF1 expression → DNA hypermethylation → inhibition of TSGs.

UHRF1与DNMT1作用缺陷 → DNA低甲基化。 Defective UHRF1-DNMT1 interaction → DNA hypomethylation.

Perturbation of DNA methylation（DNA甲基化的紊乱）

不同癌症中，UHRF1过表达导致TSGs（肿瘤抑制基因）被抑制

Overexpression of UHRF1 in various cancers leads to the inhibition of tumor suppressor genes (TSGs), thereby promoting tumorigenesis and progression.

多种癌症中UHRF1过表达对肿瘤抑制基因抑制作用的总结

DNA去甲基化酶TET及其在肿瘤中的作用 | TET DNA-demethylating enzymes and their role in cancer

1
TET酶的发现和成员

TET是一类最近被发现的DNA去甲基化酶，属于Ten-Eleven Translocation(en)，有三种成员：TET1、TET2和TET3。

TET enzymes are a class of recently discovered DNA demethylating enzymes, known as Ten-Eleven Translocation enzymes, with three members: TET1, TET2, and TET3.

2
TET蛋白催化的逐步氧化反应

TET酶通过逐步氧化，将5-甲基胞嘧啶(5mC)转变为5-羟甲基胞嘧啶(5hmC)，再转变为5-甲酰基胞嘧啶(5fC)，最终形成5-羧基胞嘧啶(5caC)。

TET proteins catalyze stepwise oxidation reactions, converting 5-methylcytosine (5mC) to 5-hydroxymethylcytosine (5hmC), then to 5-formylcytosine (5fC), and finally to 5-carboxylcytosine (5caC).

3
进一步修饰与DNA去甲基化

随后，5fC和5caC可以通过胸腺嘧啶-DNA糖苷酶(TDG)等DNA修复酶去羧基作用被切除，从而实现DNA去甲基化。

Subsequently, 5fC and 5caC can be removed by thymine-DNA glycosylase (TDG) or other DNA repair enzymes through decarboxylation, leading to DNA demethylation.

4
TET蛋白在DNA去甲基化中的功能总结图

课件图示展示了TET介导的5mC氧化修饰及后续DNA修复，协同导致DNA去甲基化。

The schematic diagram in the slide illustrates TET-mediated stepwise oxidation of 5mC and subsequent DNA repair, together resulting in DNA demethylation.

5
DNA去甲基化功能紊乱与肿瘤发生的关系

正常DNA去甲基化过程的紊乱被认为与肿瘤发生密切相关。

Disruption of normal DNA demethylation is thought to be closely associated with oncogenesis.

6
多种人类肿瘤中5hmC减少

乳腺癌、肺癌和胰腺癌等多种人类肿瘤组织中，5hmC水平明显降低。

5hmC levels are markedly reduced in various human tumors, such as breast, lung, and pancreatic cancers.

7
5hmC作为肿瘤发生的生物标志物

5hmC的减少可以作为肿瘤发展的生物标志物，其降低与肿瘤发生密切相关。

A decrease in 5hmC can serve as a biomarker for tumor development, as its reduction is closely linked to oncogenesis.

8
5hmC降低伴随着TET基因表达的下调

实验结果显示，肿瘤组织中5hmC的减少伴随着TET基因表达量的大幅下降。

Experimental data show that the reduction of 5hmC in cancer tissues is accompanied by substantial downregulation of TET gene expression.

9
机制尚未完全明确

目前5hmC减少导致肿瘤发生的具体机制尚不完全清楚。

The specific mechanism by which decreased 5hmC leads to oncogenesis is not yet fully understood.

Histone writers, readers and erasers in cancer | 癌症中的组蛋白写手、读者和去除酶

1
Histone H3尾部赖氨酸残基是最常见的PTM修饰位点。

Histone H3 tail lysine residues are frequently subject to post-translational modifications (PTMs).

2
组蛋白修饰在肿瘤发生过程中聚集。

Histone modifications accumulate during tumorigenesis.

3
基因组学研究已经明确指出染色质修饰因子的失调是多种癌症类型的驱动因素。

Genomic studies have clearly implicated dysregulation of chromatin modifiers as drivers in many types of cancer.

4
许多染色质修饰子是癌症中最常见的突变靶点之一。

Many chromatin modifiers are among the most frequently mutated targets in cancer.

5
组蛋白写手、去除酶与读者及其代表酶如下表所示：

Histone writers, erasers, and readers, as well as their representative enzymes, are summarized in the table below:

Writer（写手）Eraser（去除酶）Reader（读者）
MLL1-5, SETD1A/DKDM1A/B, KDM5A/B/CMLL, CHD1, BPTF, TAF3, ING, KDM5
