6
组蛋白修饰的类型及其在调控区域的分布：

H3K4甲基化（Me1, Me3）：增强子（Enhancer）、启动子（Promoter）和基因体（Gene body）上常有；

H3K9、H3K27甲基化
与转录抑制相关；

乙酰化（Ac）：与活化基因密切相关；

其他如H3K36、H3K79甲基化均有特定调控区域。

The types of histone modifications and their distribution across regulatory regions:

H3K4 methylation (Me1, Me3): frequently occur at enhancers, promoters, and gene bodies;

H3K9, H3K27 methylation: often associated with transcriptional repression;

Acetylation (Ac): tightly linked to gene activation;

Other modifications such as H3K36, H3K79 methylation, also have specific regulatory locations.

7
组蛋白写手、读者和去除酶的相互作用共同决定了染色质状态和基因表达。

The interplay among histone writers, readers, and erasers together determines chromatin state and gene expression.

8
癌症发生过程中，癌基因与表观遗传学之间存在复杂的相互作用。

There is a real complexity and interplay between cancer genetics and epigenetics during oncogenesis.

组蛋白写手、读者和去除酶在癌症中的作用 Histone writers, readers and erasers in cancer

组蛋白H3尾部赖氨酸残基是最常见的翻译后修饰位点。

Histone H3 tail lysine residues are frequently subject to post-translational modifications (PTMs).

这些修饰包括甲基化和乙酰化，不同修饰在增强子、启动子和基因体的分布不同。

These modifications include methylation and acetylation, and their patterns differ between enhancers, promoters, and gene bodies.

例如，H3K4的单甲基化和三甲基化主要存在于增强子和启动子区域。

For example, H3K4 mono- and tri-methylation are mainly found at enhancers and promoters.

H3K27和H3K9的甲基化则与基因沉默相关。

Methylation at H3K27 and H3K9 is linked to gene silencing.

H3K27和H3K9的乙酰化则与活跃的基因表达相关。

Acetylation at H3K27 and H3K9 is associated with active gene expression.

组蛋白修饰的“写手”、去除酶和“读者”都在上表中列出。

Histone writers, erasers, and readers are listed in the table above.

写手如MLL1-5、SETD1A/D、SUV39H1/2、CBP/P300、EZH2等负责添加这些标记。

Writers like MLL1-5, SETD1A/D, SUV39H1/2, CBP/P300, and EZH2 add these marks.

去除酶如KDM家族和HDAC家族负责去除这些修饰。

Erasers like the KDM family and HDAC family remove these marks.

读者如MLL, CHD1, BRD4, HP1等识别并介导这些修饰的功能。

Readers such as MLL, CHD1, BRD4, and HP1 recognize and mediate the effects of these modifications.

这些染色质修饰因子是癌症中最常见的突变靶点之一。

These chromatin modifiers are among the most frequently mutated targets in cancer.

组蛋白修饰在肿瘤发生过程中不断积累。

Histone modifications accumulate during tumorigenesis.

基因组学研究明确证明，染色质修饰因子的失调是多种癌症类型的驱动因素。

Genomic studies have clearly implicated dysregulation of chromatin modifiers as drivers in many types of cancer.

癌基因与表观遗传学之间存在复杂且动态的相互作用。

There is a complex and dynamic interplay between cancer genetics and epigenetics.

肿瘤发生发展的过程极为复杂，既涉及遗传变异，也涉及染色质调控的变化。

The oncogenic process is highly complex, involving both genetic alterations and changes in chromatin regulation.

癌症中的组蛋白修饰 Histone modifications in cancer

组蛋白修饰的整体水平可预测多种癌症的预后。

Global levels of histone modifications predict prognosis in different cancers.

在肺癌和肾癌患者中，H3K4me2和H3K18ac水平较低，预示着更差的生存概率。

Lower levels of H3K4me2 and H3K18ac predict poorer survival probabilities in both lung and kidney cancer patients.

曲线图显示，组蛋白修饰水平低的患者（红线组）生存概率显著低于修饰水平正常的患者（黑线组）。

The survival curves show that patients with low levels of histone modifications (red) have significantly lower survival probabilities than those with normal levels (black).

这一规律在不同来源的腺癌中表现出共同的表观遗传预后模式。

This reflects common prognostic epigenetic patterns of adenocarcinomas of different tissue origins.

研究表明，组蛋白修饰不仅是癌症进展的生物标志物，还可作为预后评估工具。

Studies indicate that histone modifications are not only biomarkers for cancer progression but also serve as prognostic tools.

组蛋白甲基化——写手、读者和去除酶在癌症中的作用

Histone methylation—The writers, readers, and erasers in cancer

一、组蛋白甲基化及其功能简述

1
组蛋白甲基化作用于赖氨酸和精氨酸，可以促进或者抑制基因表达。 Histone methylation, acting on lysine and arginine, can either promote or inhibit gene expression.
2
组蛋白甲基转移酶（KMTs）对赖氨酸位点和甲基化程度有高度特异性。 Histone methyltransferases (KMTs) are highly specific enzymes for lysine sites and the degree of methylation.

二、组蛋白甲基化的写手及其与肿瘤类型的关系

3
EZH、SET2、WHSC1、DOT1L等写手在多种肿瘤均有涉及，并决定不同肿瘤内表观遗传异常。

Writers such as EZH, SET2, WHSC1, and DOT1L are involved in various cancers and determine epigenetic changes in tumors.

4
EZH（EZH1/2）在多种癌症（如乳腺癌、前列腺癌、膀胱癌等）可通过促进细胞增殖、迁移、肿瘤干性等驱动肿瘤进展，还与对化疗反应相关。 EZH (EZH1/2) can promote cancer progression in various types (e.g., breast, prostate, bladder cancer) by enhancing proliferation, migration, stemness, and is associated with response to chemotherapy.
5
SET2（KMT3A）主要在实体瘤如肾癌、肺癌中作为基因组的守护者，维护基因组稳定，调控顺铂耐药。 SET2 (KMT3A) mainly acts as a genomic guardian in solid tumors like renal and lung cancer, maintaining integrity and regulating cisplatin resistance.
6
WHSC1家族（NSD2/3等）在多种实体瘤和白血病可促进肿瘤细胞侵袭和转移，与癌症干细胞特性有关。 The WHSC1 family (such as NSD2/3) can promote tumor invasion and metastasis and is linked to cancer stemness.
7
DOT1L在重排型白血病、结直肠癌和乳腺癌等中增加肿瘤干性，必需于特定白血病的发生。 DOT1L increases tumor stemness in MLL-rearranged leukemia, colorectal, and breast cancer, and is essential for certain leukemias.

三、组蛋白甲基化的“读者”及其肿瘤中的作用

8
甲基组蛋白识别蛋白主要有HP1和Chd1，分别识别H3K9me和H3K27me。

The main methyl-histone recognition proteins are HP1 and Chd1, recognizing H3K9me and H3K27me respectively.

9
HP1过表达促进乳腺癌进展，增强细胞侵袭性、DNA修复与耐受治疗能力。 HP1 overexpression promotes breast cancer progression, cell invasiveness, and resistance to DNA-damaging therapies.
10
Chd1在前列腺癌中作用复杂，其丢失会导致更加侵袭的表型。 Chd1 functions as a complex regulator in prostate cancer; loss of Chd1 promotes a more aggressive phenotype.

四、组蛋白去甲基化酶（去除酶）及其分组

11
组蛋白去甲基化酶（KDMs/HDMs）分为两类：氧化胺型（LSDs）和JMJC结构域型。

Histone demethylases (KDMs/HDMs) are divided into two groups: amine-oxidase type (LSDs) and JumonjiC (JMJC) domain-containing type.

12
LSD1（KDM1A）在多种癌症中上调，促进细胞增殖、干性、自我更新及耐受；抑制LSD1则会抑制肿瘤侵袭和转移。 LSD1 (KDM1A) is upregulated in many cancers, promoting proliferation, stemness, self-renewal, and resistance; inhibition of LSD1 suppresses invasion and metastasis.
13
KDM2/3等JMJC家族成员通过调控癌症干细胞和侵袭性，参与乳腺癌、胃癌、宫颈癌等的发生发展。 The JMJC family (KDM2/3) regulates cancer stemness and invasiveness, involved in breast, gastric, cervical cancers, etc.

五、老师课堂补充内容要点

14
老师强调，这些“写手”“读者”“去除酶”作用高度特异，且同一因子不同癌症中的作用（促进或抑制）依赖具体细胞类型及微环境。

The teacher emphasized the highly context-specific roles of writers, readers, and erasers; the same factor may play promoting or suppressing roles depending on the cell type and environment.

15
课上指出，表观遗传酶的突变或表达异常往往可作为癌症的新型药物靶点。 Mutations or abnormal expression of epigenetic enzymes are often novel therapeutic targets in cancer.
16
教授特别提到部分因子既能调控基因表达，也直接影响DNA修复、顺铂耐药等多种致癌过程。 Some factors can regulate both gene expression and directly impact DNA repair, cisplatin resistance, and other tumorigenic processes.

组蛋白甲基化——去除酶（以LSD1为例）在前列腺癌中的作用Histone methylation—The ERASERS (example: LSD1) in prostate cancer

1
LSD1通过表观遗传重编程促进CRPC进展，激活细胞周期基因（包括CENPE）

LSD1-mediated epigenetic reprogramming in CRPC promotes cancer progression by activating cell-cycle genes (including CENPE).

2
去抑制性H3K9甲基化导致雄激素受体（AR）靶基因去抑制

Demethylation of the repressive histone mark at H3K9 leads to derepression of androgen receptor (AR) target genes.

术语解释
AR = 雄激素受体，是前列腺癌关键驱动因子，LSD1为AR转录共激活因子

AR = Androgen Receptor, key driver of prostate cancer; LSD1 acts as a coactivator of AR transcriptional activity.

4
激素剥夺疗法常发展为去势抵抗性前列腺癌（CRPC）

Androgen deprivation therapy often leads to the development of castration-resistant prostate cancer (CRPC).

实验及数据解读 Experimental findings

细胞模型
LNCaP为雄激素依赖型前列腺癌细胞系，abl为其去势抗性衍生株
Cell model
LNCaP is an androgen-dependent prostate cancer cell line; abl is its castration-resistant derivative.
6
在LSD1 mRNA水平，原发及转移性CRPC肿瘤间无显著差异

No significant difference in LSD1 mRNA level between primary and metastatic CRPC tumors.

7
CENPE为动粒相关马达蛋白，关键参与有丝分裂染色体排列及检查点

CENPE is a kinetochore-associated motor protein essential for mitotic chromosome alignment and checkpoint satisfaction.

8
CENPE启动子在abl细胞中LSD1结合显著增强，相比LNCaP细胞，诱导CENPE表达上调

CENPE promoter shows much stronger LSD1 binding in abl cells than in LNCaP cells, leading to increased CENPE expression.

9
LSD1在CENPE启动子的增强结合支持AR转录活性，并加速肿瘤进展

Increased LSD1 binding at the CENPE promoter favors AR transcriptional activity and accelerates cancer progression.

老师补充 Teacher’s highlights and comments

老师强调
LSD1在去势抵抗性前列腺癌（CRPC）中的致癌作用，并不仅仅是因为它自身的表达量升高，而在于其在靶基因启动子的结合显著增强，尤其是在CENPE等细胞周期基因的启动子上。 The teacher emphasized: LSD1’s oncogenic role in castration-resistant prostate cancer (CRPC) results not simply from increased expression, but from its significantly enhanced binding to target gene promoters, especially those like CENPE.
2
特别指出，LSD1对AR靶基因的去抑制，与其“结合模式（位置和结合强度）”的动态变化高度相关，而不是由组蛋白甲基化总体水平变化驱动。 Specifically, the derepression of AR target genes by LSD1 is closely linked to its dynamic change in “binding pattern” (location and intensity), not to overall histone methylation status changes.
3
LSD1除去甲基化修饰的作用相对较弱，更多通过“表观遗传重编程”，选择性激活与肿瘤进展和抗药性相关的基因。 LSD1’s demethylation activity is relatively minor; its main function is “epigenetic reprogramming,” selectively activating genes involved in tumor progression and drug resistance.
4
结合案例，老师补充：这一发现解释了CRPC发展中的耐药现象和肿瘤持续进展，同时提示LSD1的定位与功能的改变有望成为新的治疗靶点。 Using the example, the teacher added: This finding explains the resistance and continued progression in CRPC and suggests that altered localization and function of LSD1 could be a novel therapeutic target.
5
最后，老师总结：LSD1等表观遗传酶的研究突显了肿瘤发生发展背后调控网络的复杂性，其作用具有高度细胞类型和微环境依赖性。 Finally, the teacher concluded: Studies on enzymes like LSD1 highlight the complexity of regulatory networks behind tumorigenesis, where their functions are highly dependent on cell type and microenvironment.

简明总结/Takeaway

LSD1去甲基酶通过改变靶位点结合，促进癌基因活性，驱动去势抵抗性前列腺癌进展。 LSD1 promotes AR transcriptional activity and CRPC progression by altering binding on target promoters, rather than by upregulation of its own mRNA.

组蛋白乙酰化的写手Histone acetylation THE WRITERS

1
组蛋白赖氨酸乙酰化转移酶（KATs，通常也称为组蛋白乙酰转移酶HATs）催化乙酰基加到组蛋白赖氨酸上。 Histone lysine acetyltransferases (KATs), commonly called histone acetyltransferases (HATs), catalyze the addition of acetyl groups to lysine residues on histones.
2
乙酰基可以加在组蛋白尾部的多个赖氨酸残基上。 The addition of an acetyl group can occur at multiple lysine residues on histone tails.
KATs分三大类
There are three major families of KATs:

a) Gcn5相关N-乙酰转移酶家族（GNAT）

a) The Gcn5-related N-acetyltransferase family (GNAT)

b) MYST家族（包括MOZ、Ybf2、Sas2、TIP60）

b) The MYST family (including MOZ, Ybf2, Sas2, TIP60)

c) 孤儿家族（CBP/EP300和某些核受体）

c) The orphan family (CBP/EP300 and some nuclear receptors)

4
组蛋白乙酰化通过中和未修饰赖氨酸的碱性电荷，降低染色质的致密度，削弱DNA与组蛋白之间的静电相互作用。 Histone acetylation influences the compaction state of chromatin by neutralizing the basic charge at unmodified lysine residues, thereby weakening the electrostatic interaction between negatively charged DNA and histones.
5
HAT家族的基因和表观遗传改变，如表达量、稳定性、功能结构域的异常，会影响染色质结构，产生调控后果。 Genetic or epigenetic aberrations affecting HAT expression, stability, or domain function can have chromatin regulatory consequences.
6
（配图）CBP和p300的结构示意，突出了催化区（KAT）、与p53等因子的相互作用位点，说明这些写手蛋白本身结构复杂、与肿瘤发生密切相关。 (The figure) The schematic structure of CBP and p300 highlights the catalytic domains (KAT) and interaction sites with factors such as p53, illustrating the complex structure of these “writer” proteins and their relevance to tumorigenesis.

老师课堂补充 | Teacher’s remarks

1
老师强调不同KAT家族酶的特异性和各肿瘤内作用复杂，因为不同CNAT/MYST/CBP家族成员可能在不同细胞类型内作用差异显著。 The lecturer emphasized the substrate specificity of different KAT enzyme families and the complexity of their roles in cancer, as distinct members from GNAT, MYST, and CBP families may exert different effects in various cell types.
2
老师补充说明，HATs突变或功能障碍常可导致相关基因/抑癌基因的失活和染色质异常开放，是癌症发生的重要机制之一。 The lecturer added that mutations or dysfunction of HATs frequently lead to inactivation of relevant genes/tumor suppressor genes and abnormal chromatin relaxation, constituting a key mechanism in cancer pathogenesis.

Histone acetylation 组蛋白乙酰化

THE WRITERS 写手酶类（乙酰转移酶在肿瘤中的作用）

Appropriate acetylation within cells is important since upregulation or downregulation of HATs is associated with tumorigenesis or poor prognosis. 细胞内适当的乙酰化很重要，因为HATs（组蛋白乙酰转移酶）的上调或下调与肿瘤发生或不良预后相关。

Altered acetylation of H4K16 has been linked to various cancers. H4K16位点的乙酰化改变与多种癌症相关。

Loss of p300 has also been associated with hematological malignancies. Therefore, both CBP and p300 seem to function as tumor suppressors. p300的缺失也与血液系统恶性肿瘤有关，因此CBP与p300似乎都起到抑癌基因的作用。

During cancer development, the expression of HAT genes can be disrupted by chromosomal translocations. 肿瘤发生发展过程中，HAT基因的表达可因染色体易位而受到干扰。   MLL-CBP, and MLL-p300 (MLL, mixed lineage leukemia) have been identified in hematological malignancies. 在血液恶性肿瘤中，已发现有MLL-CBP 和 MLL-p300（MLL，混合谱系白血病）的易位。

Missense point mutations in p300 are found in colorectal adenocarcinoma, gastric adenocarcinoma and breast cancer. p300基因的错义突变见于结直肠腺癌、胃腺癌和乳腺癌。 （出处：Cheng et al., Signal Transduction and Targeted Therapy 2019）

Histone acetylation 组蛋白乙酰化

THE READERS “阅读器”——乙酰化赖氨酸识别蛋白

The BET (bromodomain and extraterminal domain) protein family. BET（溴结构域和外末端结构域）蛋白家族。

Four members
BRD2, BRD3, BRD4, and BRDt. 共有4个成员：BRD2、BRD3、BRD4和BRDt。

The bromodomain (BRD) motif contains a hydrophobic pocket to identify acetyl-lysine of histones. 溴结构域（BRD）具有一个疏水口袋，可以识别组蛋白上的乙酰化赖氨酸。

The specificity of different BRDs depends on the sequences within the loops that form the hydrophobic pocket. Therefore, each BRD has a preference for different histones. 不同BRD的特异性取决于形成疏水口袋的环结构序列，因此每个BRD对不同组蛋白都有选择性。

BET proteins are potential therapeutic targets in various cancers, including nuclear protein in testis (NUT) midline carcinoma, multiple myeloma, lymphoma, lung cancer, and neuroblastoma. BET蛋白是多种癌症潜在的治疗靶点，包括睾丸核蛋白（NUT）中线癌、多发性骨髓瘤、淋巴瘤、肺癌和神经母细胞瘤等。

The BET proteins in cancerBET蛋白在癌症中的作用

1
Overexpression of BRD2 in Breast Cancer
1
BRD2在乳腺癌中的高表达

BRD2基因在乳腺癌组织中的表达量高于正常乳腺组织，在Basal-like、Luminal A、Luminal B及HER2+等多个亚型中均有明显上调。 The expression of the BRD2 gene is significantly higher in breast cancer tissues than in normal breast tissues, and this upregulation appears in multiple subtypes (Basal-like, Luminal A, Luminal B, HER2+).

这种高表达与其它BET家族成员（如BRD3，BRD4）的表达趋势类似。 Similar upregulation trends are observed in other BET family proteins such as BRD3 and BRD4.

BRD2等BET蛋白的高表达提示其表观遗传调控作用在乳腺癌的发生发展中非常重要，是潜在的治疗靶点。 The overexpression of BRD2 and other BET proteins suggests their epigenetic regulatory roles are critical in breast cancer development, making them potential therapeutic targets.

2
The role of BRD-NUT fusion proteins in cancer
2
BRD-NUT融合蛋白在癌症中的作用

NUT中线癌（NMC, NUT midline carcinoma）是一种高度侵袭性的鳞状细胞癌，其特征基因是BRD-NUT融合致癌基因。 NUT midline carcinoma (NMC) is a highly aggressive squamous cell cancer, characterized by the BRD-NUT fusion oncogene.

BRD4-NUT融合蛋白能够结合MYC基因的启动子区域，MYC本身是多种高恶性肿瘤的致癌蛋白。BRD4-NUT的表达促进肿瘤细胞生长并抑制正常分化。 The BRD4-NUT fusion protein binds to the promoter region of the MYC gene, itself a potent driver in multiple aggressive cancers. Expression of BRD4-NUT promotes tumor cell growth and blocks normal differentiation.

BRD4-NUT同时通过双溴结构域结合染色质，NUT部分招募p300组蛋白乙酰转移酶，二者共同在特定基因位点形成大量活性转录修饰，促进如MYC等靶基因表达。 BRD4-NUT anchors to chromatin via its dual bromodomains; its NUT portion recruits the p300 histone acetyltransferase, together creating super-enhancer-like domains at target genes such as MYC, boosting their expression.

BRD-NUT融合导致的MYC异常激活是NMC关键发病机制，提示NMC属于表观遗传驱动的肿瘤类型。 BRD-NUT fusion-mediated MYC activation is central to NMC oncogenesis, indicating NMC is an epigenetically driven tumor.

总结：

BRD2/BET家族蛋白高表达与乳腺癌密切相关，提示其表观遗传调控功能具有重要意义。

特定癌症（如NMC）因BRD-NUT融合蛋白异常调控MYC等基因，强调了表观遗传机制在肿瘤发生发展中的核心作用。

Histone acetylation 组蛋白乙酰化

THE ERASERS “橡皮” —— 组蛋白去乙酰化酶（HDACs）

1
Histone deacetylases (HDACs)
1
组蛋白去乙酰化酶（HDACs）

人类基因组中有18种HDACs。 There are 18 HDACs in the human genome.

2
HDACs的四个类别 Four classes of HDACs
Class I
去乙酰化结构域位于N端，常为转录共抑制因子，如HDAC1、HDAC2、HDAC3和HDAC8。 Class I: Transcriptional corepressors with the deacetylase domain at the N-terminus and diversified C-terminal regions (HDAC1, HDAC2, HDAC3, and HDAC8).
Class II
去乙酰化结构域位于C端，如HDAC4、HDAC5、HDAC6、HDAC7、HDAC9和HDAC10。 Class II: With the deacetylase domain at the C-terminal position (HDAC4, HDAC5, HDAC6, HDAC7, HDAC9, and HDAC10).
Class III
酵母Sir2（沉默信息调节因子2）同源蛋白，包括SIRT1、SIRT2、SIRT3、SIRT4、SIRT5、SIRT6和SIRT7。 Class III: Yeast silent information regulator 2 (Sir2)-like proteins, including SIRT1, SIRT2, SIRT3, SIRT4, SIRT5, SIRT6, and SIRT7.
Class IV
仅包括HDAC11一种蛋白。 Class IV: Involves only one protein (HDAC11).
3
HDACs的底物不限于组蛋白
3
HDACs的底物也不限于组蛋白

和HATs一样，HDACs作用底物并不限于组蛋白。 Like HATs, the substrates of HDACs are not limited to histones.

超过3600个位点的乙酰化修饰已在1750种蛋白上鉴定出来，包括肿瘤抑制蛋白p53和细胞骨架蛋白α-微管蛋白。 More than 3600 acetylation sites on 1750 proteins have been identified, including the tumor suppressor p53 and the cytoskeletal protein α-tubulin.

4
HDACs调控基因转录
4
HDACs调控基因转录

HDACs还能通过去乙酰化作用于其他与表观遗传事件有关的蛋白（如DNMTs、HATs和HDACs本身），实现在基因表达层面的广泛调控。 HDACs can regulate gene transcription by deacetylating other proteins responsible for epigenetic events, such as DNMTs, HATs, and other HDACs.

5
癌症中HDACs的变化 Alterations in HDACs in cancer

癌症中HDACs的改变通常导致异常去乙酰化、肿瘤抑制基因失活。 Alterations in HDACs in cancers usually result in aberrant deacetylation and inactivation of tumor suppressor genes.

非编码RNA（ncRNA）Noncoding RNAs (ncRNAs)

1
ncRNA的主要类型
1
Main types of ncRNAs

microRNAs（miRNAs）：小RNA，长度在19-22个核苷酸之间 microRNAs (miRNAs): small RNAs between 19 and 22 nucleotides in length

small interfering RNAs（siRNAs） small interfering RNAs (siRNAs)

piwi-interacting RNAs（piRNAs） piwi-interacting RNAs (piRNAs)

long noncoding RNAs（lncRNAs） long noncoding RNAs (lncRNAs)

2
非编码RNA的分类
2
Classification of noncoding RNAs

非编码RNA（ncRNA）分为：管家型ncRNA 和 调控型ncRNA Noncoding RNAs are divided into: Housekeeping ncRNAs & Regulatory ncRNAs

（1）管家型ncRNA Housekeeping ncRNAs

rRNA（核糖体RNA，ribosomal RNA）

tRNA（转运RNA，transfer RNA）

snRNA（小核RNA，small nuclear RNA）

snoRNA（小核仁RNA，small nucleolar RNA）

（2）调控型ncRNA Regulatory ncRNAs

可进一步分为短ncRNA（short ncRNAs < 200 nt）和长ncRNA（long ncRNAs > 200 nt） Divided into: Short ncRNAs (< 200 nt) and Long ncRNAs (> 200 nt)

短ncRNA包括：

miRNAs（microRNAs，微小RNA）

siRNAs（small interfering RNAs，小干扰RNA）

piRNAs（piwi-interacting RNAs，piwi蛋白相互作用RNA）

长ncRNA包括：

lncRNAs（long noncoding RNAs，长链非编码RNA）

3
补充说明
3
Additional notes

miRNA、siRNA、piRNA主要属于短ncRNA，lncRNA属于长ncRNA。 miRNAs, siRNAs, and piRNAs are mainly short ncRNAs; lncRNAs are long ncRNAs.

miRNAs在肿瘤发生中的作用 miRNAs in tumorigenesis

miRNAs能够靶向调控超过50%的基因组。 miRNAs can target more than 50% of the genome.

它们是关键的表观遗传调控因子。 They are key epigenetic actors.

所有类型人类癌症中都发现了miRNA表达异常。 Altered expression of miRNAs has been identified in all types of human cancers.

miRNAs既包括促肿瘤型，也包括抑癌型。 There are both tumor-promoting and tumor-suppressing miRNAs.

miRNA具有组织特异性表达，形成分子特征。 miRNAs show tissue-specific expression, serving as molecular signatures.

miRNA可作为预测性标志物，辅助个体化治疗。 miRNAs can be predictive markers used in personal therapy.

老师补充:

miRNA异常表达在癌症中非常常见，影响细胞凋亡、增殖相关通路。 Altered miRNA expression is very common in cancer, affecting apoptosis and proliferation pathways.

一些miRNA通过调控经典的肿瘤相关基因参与肿瘤的发生、发展。 Some miRNAs are directly involved in tumorigenesis by regulating classical cancer-related genes.

例子（消化道肿瘤领域）

Examples (in gastrointestinal cancers):

抑癌型miRNA Potential tumor suppressor miRNAs

miR-15b, miR-16：通过靶向BCL2，调控细胞凋亡，参与胃癌多药耐药的发生。 miR-15b and miR-16 play a role in the development of MDR in gastric cancer cells by modulating apoptosis via targeting BCL2.

miR-34a
作为抑癌基因，促进结肠癌细胞的衰老和生长阻滞，调控E2F通路。 miR-34a functions as a tumor suppressor and induces senescence-like growth arrest through modulation of the E2F pathway in human colon cancer cells.

miR-143, miR-145：在结直肠癌组织中表达下调。 miR-143 and miR-145 are downregulated in colorectal cancer.

致癌型miRNA Potential oncogenic miRNAs

miR-17-92簇
在多种人类恶性肿瘤（包括结肠癌）中过表达，靶向E2F1和TGFBR2。 The miR-17-92 cluster is overexpressed in various human malignancies, including colon cancer, targeting E2F1 and TGFBR2.
miR-21
在人类肿瘤（如胆管癌、胃癌）中过表达，靶向PTEN。 miR-21 is upregulated in various human malignancies, including cholangiocarcinoma and gastric/colon cancers, targeting PTEN.
miR-106a
在结肠癌中上调，靶向RB-1。 miR-106a is upregulated in colon cancer, targeting RB-1.
miR-106b-25簇
在人类胃癌等肿瘤中上调，损害TGF-β途径，靶向p21和Bim。 miR-106b-25 cluster is upregulated in human gastric cancers, impairs the TGF-β pathway and targets p21, Bim, and E2F1.
miR-155
在人B细胞淋巴瘤和结肠癌中高表达，靶向TP531NP1。 miR-155 is overexpressed in B-cell lymphoma and colon cancer, targeting TP531NP1.
总结
miRNA在肿瘤中既可能发挥抑癌也可能发挥促癌作用，通过调控多种关键基因参与肿瘤发生发展，同时兼具诊断和治疗的潜力。 Summary: miRNAs can act either as tumor suppressors or oncogenes in cancer by regulating various key genes, playing roles in tumorigenesis and progression, and also holding potential as diagnostic and therapeutic tools.

长链非编码RNA（lncRNAs）在肿瘤发生中的作用 Long noncoding RNAs (lncRNAs) in cancer development

lncRNA在癌症发生发展中的作用才刚刚被认识。 The role of lncRNAs in cancer development is only beginning to be uncovered.

1
lncRNA的功能类型 Functions of lncRNAs
　 诱饵（Decoy）
答案 A
　 Decoy
答案 A

lncRNA可以作为诱饵分子，通过结合转录因子（TF），阻止其与靶基因启动子结合。 lncRNAs can act as decoys by binding to transcription factors (TFs), preventing them from binding to target gene promoters.

诱饵作用还包括“海绵”结合miRNA，从而释放对应的mRNA，使其能够翻译。 The decoy effect also includes "sponging" of miRNAs, releasing their targeted mRNA for translation.

结果是相关基因的表达被削弱。 As a result, the expression of the relevant gene is attenuated.

　 向导（Guide）
答案 B
　 Guide
答案 B

lncRNA可招募转录因子或染色质修饰酶（如组蛋白修饰酶）到基因组的特定位点。 lncRNAs can recruit transcription factors or chromatin-modifying enzymes (such as histone-modifying enzymes) to specific genomic locations.

指导分子定位促进特定基因的转录或沉默，调控基因表达。 Guide molecules localize these proteins to regulate either transcription or silencing of specific genes, leading to changes in gene expression.

　 支架（Scaffold）
答案 C
　 Scaffold
答案 C

lncRNA作为支架分子，促进蛋白质复合物的组装，尤其是组蛋白修饰复合物。 lncRNAs act as scaffolds to facilitate the assembly of protein complexes, especially histone-modifying complexes.

促进相关修饰（如甲基化）在特定位点发生，导致基因沉默。 They promote associated modifications (such as methylation) at specific sites, resulting in gene repression.

总之，lncRNA通过多种机制调节基因表达，参与肿瘤发生发展。 In summary, lncRNAs regulate gene expression through multiple mechanisms and participate in tumorigenesis and progression.

功能类型作用机制典型示例/备注（肿瘤相关）英文对照
诱饵（Decoy）结合并“诱捕”转录因子或miRNA，阻止其与靶基因结合，从而影响基因表达例如，lncRNA作为“miRNA sponge”，解除mRNA的翻译抑制Acts as a decoy by binding to transcription factors or miRNAs, sequesters them (miRNA sponge), thus relieving target mRNA from inhibition 38
向导（Guide）招募转录因子或染色质修饰酶引导到特定位点，实现基因激活或沉默lncRNA指导组蛋白甲基化酶到特定基因启动子，对基因表达进行调控Recruits transcription factors or chromatin-modifying enzymes to specific genomic loci to regulate gene activation or silencing 38
支架（Scaffold）为蛋白复合物组装提供平台，促进染色质修饰反应协助组蛋白修饰复合物的组装，导致基因沉默Scaffold for the assembly of chromatin-modifying complexes, e.g. histone methyltransferase complex, leading to gene repression 38
肿瘤相关实例lncRNA在多种肿瘤中异常表达，既有促进癌症的（如HOTAIR、MALAT1），也有抑制癌症的（如GAS5）可作为生物标志物，预测预后或辅助治疗Dysregulated lncRNAs in cancers (oncogenic e.g. HOTAIR, MALAT1; tumor suppressive e.g. GAS5). Potential as biomarkers and therapeutic targets 253638

补充说明：

lncRNA具多功能性，其在肿瘤发生发展中的作用机制复杂、具有情境依赖性，有的扮演致癌角色，有的为抑癌因子。

lncRNA异常表达已被认为是多个肿瘤发生、侵袭、预后等过程的重要调控因素，可作为新的分子标志物和潜在的治疗靶点。

lncRNAs在肿瘤发生中的作用｜Role of lncRNAs in Tumorigenesis

1
本页介绍了与紫杉烷类耐药相关的lncRNAs在卵巢癌中的表达变化及作用机制。 This page introduces the expression changes and mechanisms of lncRNAs related to taxane resistance in ovarian cancer.
2
lncRNAs通过调控多种信号通路促进或抑制肿瘤细胞耐药。 lncRNAs regulate multiple signaling pathways to promote or inhibit drug resistance in tumor cells.
3
主要涉及的lncRNA及表达变化如下（↑ 上调，↓ 下调）： The main lncRNAs and their expression changes are as follows (↑ upregulation, ↓ downregulation):
UCA1
耐药细胞株↑，下调miR-129，解除MDR1抑制，增强耐药。 UCA1: ↑ in resistant cell lines, downregulates miR-129, relieves MDR1 inhibition, enhances drug resistance.
FER1L4
卵巢癌组织↓，耐药细胞株↓，下调miR-106a-5p，影响MAPK/PTEN信号，调控细胞周期。 FER1L4: ↓ in ovarian cancer tissue and resistant cell lines, downregulates miR-106a-5p, affects MAPK/PTEN signaling, regulates cell cycle.
LINC01118
卵巢癌组织↑，耐药细胞株↑，下调miR-134，增强ABCC1表达，促进药物外排。 LINC01118: ↑ in ovarian cancer tissue and resistant cell lines, downregulates miR-134, increases ABCC1 expression, promotes drug efflux.
NEAT1
耐药细胞株↑，下调miR-194，激活ZEB1，促进EMT，推动肿瘤转移。 NEAT1: ↑ in resistant cell lines, downregulates miR-194, activates ZEB1, promotes EMT, facilitates tumor metastasis.
4
这些lncRNAs通过调控miRNA及下游信号通路，分别影响药物转运、细胞周期及上皮-间质转化（EMT）等过程。 These lncRNAs regulate miRNAs and downstream pathways, affecting drug transport, cell cycle, and epithelial-mesenchymal transition (EMT).
5
老师强调，NEAT1在肿瘤中过表达（↑）可预测肿瘤转移，对临床具有重要意义。 The lecturer emphasized that NEAT1 overexpression (↑) in tumors predicts metastasis and has important clinical value.
6
图中用箭头明确表示各lncRNA在组织或细胞株中的表达方向，以及作用机制的调控关系。 The diagram uses arrows to clearly indicate expression changes in tissues or cell lines and the regulatory relationships of mechanisms.

表观遗传药物的开发｜Development of Epigenetic Drugs

1
本页主要介绍了表观遗传药物的主要靶点及其相关酶类和抑制剂。 This page mainly introduces the major targets of epigenetic drugs, related enzymes, and inhibitors.
2
表观遗传药物可作用于DNA甲基化和组蛋白修饰等过程，从而影响基因表达。 Epigenetic drugs can target processes such as DNA methylation and histone modification to influence gene expression.
3
DNA甲基化由DNMTs（DNMT1，DNMT3a，DNMT3b）介导，上调（↑）可导致肿瘤相关基因沉默。 DNA methylation is mediated by DNMTs (DNMT1, DNMT3a, DNMT3b); upregulation (↑) can silence tumor-related genes.
4
DNMT抑制剂（如Azacitidine、Decitabine）通过抑制DNA甲基转移酶活性，使被抑制的基因得以重新表达。 DNMT inhibitors (e.g., Azacitidine, Decitabine) inhibit DNA methyltransferase activity and reactivate silenced genes.
5
甲基胞嘧啶羟化酶（TET1、TET2、TET3）促使DNA去甲基化过程，上调（↑）有助于基因再激活。 5-methylcytosine hydroxylases (TET1, TET2, TET3) promote DNA demethylation; upregulation (↑) helps gene reactivation.
6
组蛋白乙酰化由HATs（HAT1, GCN5, p300等）催化，上调（↑）可激活基因表达。
7
组蛋白乙酰化由HATs（HAT1, GCN5, p300等）催化，上调（↑）可激活基因表达。 Histone acetylation is catalyzed by HATs (HAT1, GCN5, p300, etc.); upregulation (↑) can activate gene expression.
8
HDACs（HDAC1-11, Sirt1-7）介导组蛋白去乙酰化，上调（↑）通常抑制基因表达。 HDACs (HDAC1-11, Sirt1-7) mediate histone deacetylation; upregulation (↑) usually suppresses gene expression.
9
HDAC抑制剂（如Valproic Acid、Vorinostat等）通过抑制HDAC，促进基因表达激活。 HDAC inhibitors (e.g., Valproic Acid, Vorinostat) inhibit HDACs and promote gene activation.
10
组蛋白甲基化由HMTs（SUV39s, SET1s, EZHs等）催化，上调（↑）可激活或抑制基因表达，取决于修饰位点。 Histone methylation is catalyzed by HMTs (SUV39s, SET1s, EZHs, etc.); upregulation (↑) can activate or repress gene expression depending on the modification site.
11
HMT抑制剂（如Pinometostat, Tazemetostat等）通过抑制组蛋白甲基转移酶阻断异常甲基化信号。 HMT inhibitors (e.g., Pinometostat, Tazemetostat) block abnormal methylation signals by inhibiting histone methyltransferases.
12
KDMs（LSDs, JHDMs等）为去甲基化酶，可去除组蛋白上的甲基标记，调节基因表达。 KDMs (LSDs, JHDMs, etc.) are demethylases that can remove methyl marks from histones and regulate gene expression.
13
课上老师强调，这些表观遗传酶及其抑制剂已成为肿瘤治疗中的重要药物开发方向。 The teacher emphasized that these epigenetic enzymes and their inhibitors have become major drug development targets in cancer therapy.
14
药物可以针对上述各种酶，调控基因表达状态，从而用于肿瘤等疾病的靶向治疗。 Drugs can target these enzymes to modulate gene expression states for targeted therapy in cancer and other diseases.

Development of epigenetic drugs 表观遗传药物的开发

1
这两页主要介绍了染色质相关蛋白的抑制剂类型、作用机制及其在肿瘤药物开发中的进展。

These slides mainly introduce types of inhibitors targeting chromatin-related proteins, their modes of action, and the progress of these agents in cancer drug development.

2
表观遗传药物按作用方式可分为以下几类：

Epigenetic drugs can be categorized based on their modes of action:

酶活性抑制（Enzymatic inhibition）：抑制关键表观遗传酶的活性，包括DOT1L、EZH2、p300、HDACs等。 Enzymatic inhibition: Inhibiting key epigenetic enzymes such as DOT1L, EZH2, p300, and HDACs.

蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用破坏（PPI disruption）：主要针对如CARM1及MLL复合物等重要蛋白复合体。 PPI disruption: Mainly targeting important protein complexes like CARM1 and MLL complexes.

竞争性结合（Competitive binding）：如BET家族BRD蛋白的抑制剂。 Competitive binding: For example, inhibitors of the BET family of BRD proteins.

蛋白降解（Protein degradation）：如BRD4的降解剂。 Protein degradation: For example, degraders targeting BRD4 33.

3
具体药物及适应症举例：

Specific drugs and indications include:

DOT1L抑制剂，如EPZ-5676，用于MLL重排型白血病。 DOT1L inhibitor (e.g., EPZ-5676) for MLL-rearranged leukemia.

EZH2抑制剂，如EPZ6438、GSK126、CPI-1205，适用于淋巴瘤和恶性横纹肌瘤。 EZH2 inhibitors (e.g., EPZ6438, GSK126, CPI-1205) for lymphoma and malignant rhabdoid tumor.

p300抑制剂（如C646、A-485）可用于血液肿瘤及雄激素受体阳性前列腺癌。 p300 inhibitors (e.g., C646, A-485) for hematological malignancies and AR+ prostate cancer.

HDAC抑制剂（Vorinostat、Romidepsin）用于皮肤T细胞淋巴瘤（CTCL）。 HDAC inhibitors (Vorinostat, Romidepsin) for CTCL.

BET抑制剂，如JQ1、I-BET、I-BET151，用于NUT中线癌和MLL重排型白血病。 BET inhibitors (e.g., JQ1, I-BET, I-BET151) for NUT midline carcinoma, MLL-rearranged leukemia.

BRD4降解剂（如dBET1、ARV-825等）适用于AML、T-ALL、伯基特淋巴瘤、去势抵抗性前列腺癌等。 BRD4 degraders (e.g., dBET1, ARV-825, etc.) for AML, T-ALL, Burkitt's lymphoma, CRPC, etc. 33.

4
药物研发进展按阶段划分：

The progress of drug development is classified in stages:

基础研究
涵盖KDM、KMT、HAT、HDAC抑制剂及溴结构域抑制剂等多种分子工具。 Research: Includes KDM, KMT, HAT, HDAC inhibitors, and bromodomain inhibitors.
临床前研发
例如BETi、EZH2i（CPI-1205）、KDMi（KDM5i）等新型分子进入临床前阶段。 Preclinical: Novel agents like BETi, EZH2i (CPI-1205), KDMi (KDM5i) enter preclinical development.
临床开发
例如iBET762、EPZ6438、OG98、EPZ5676、HDACi和DNMTi等正在进行临床试验。 Clinical development: Agents like iBET762, EPZ6438, OG98, EPZ5676, HDACi, and DNMTi are under clinical trials.
已获批准
目前已有代表性表观遗传药物如Azacitidine、Decitabine（DNMTi）、Vorinostat、Romidepsin（HDACi）获批用于肿瘤治疗。 Approved: Representative approved drugs include Azacitidine, Decitabine (DNMTi), Vorinostat, Romidepsin (HDACi) 21.
5
课堂强调，表观遗传靶向药物的研发已成为多种肿瘤精准治疗的重要方向，且不同阶段均有新药不断进入临床，显示出广阔的发展前景。

It was emphasized in class that the development of epigenetic-targeted drugs has become a key direction for precision therapy in multiple cancers, and novel drugs are continuously entering clinics at different development stages, showing broad prospects.

032025-10-13（第 1 讲）

肿瘤中的代谢重编程与血管生成Metabolic Reprogramming and Angiogenesis in Cancer

1
肿瘤中的代谢重编程与血管生成（封面）

Metabolic Reprogramming and Angiogenesis in Cancer (Title Slide)

本节课介绍肿瘤的代谢重编程及血管生成的基本概念。 This section introduces the basic concepts of metabolic reprogramming and angiogenesis in cancer.

2
癌症的标志特征

Hallmarks of Cancer

癌症细胞拥有一系列标志性功能，包括： Cancer cells possess a series of hallmark capabilities including:

保持增殖信号传导 Sustaining proliferative signaling

逃避生长抑制因子的调控 Evading growth suppressors

抵抗细胞死亡 Resisting cell death

诱导血管生成 Inducing angiogenesis

激活侵袭和转移 Activating invasion and metastasis

使细胞获得无限复制能力 Enabling replicative immortality

这些特征共同支持肿瘤的发生、发展和恶性进展。 These features together support tumorigenesis, progression, and malignancy.

代谢应激
原因与后果
Metabolic Stress
Causes and Consequences

由于血流减少（氧气和营养供应降低），肿瘤中心区域处于缺氧缺养状态。 Due to decreased blood flow (less oxygen and nutrients), the tumor core is hypoxic and nutrient-deficient.

同时，快速生长的肿瘤对营养与代谢的需求显著增加，为了维持失控的细胞生长。 Meanwhile, rapidly growing tumors have a sharply increased demand for nutrients and metabolism to sustain uncontrolled growth.

这些因素造成代谢应激，促进细胞的适应性反应，最终推动新生血管生成（新血管生成）。 These factors result in metabolic stress, promoting adaptive cellular responses, ultimately driving neo-angiogenesis (new blood vessel formation).

然而，这些新生血管通常功能较差，常因血管异常和坍塌导致供血、供氧不足，形成持续的促血管生成信号。 However, these new vessels are often functionally poor, and frequent collapse due to abnormalities leads to continued pro-angiogenic signaling.

4
血管生成（多种机制）

Angiogenesis (Multiple Mechanisms)

血管新生包括多种机制
Tumor angiogenesis involves several mechanisms:

出芽型血管生成（Sprouting angiogenesis）：新血管从已有血管内皮细胞向外生长。 Sprouting angiogenesis: New vessels grow outwards from existing endothelial cells.

血管生成（Vasculogenesis）：内皮祖细胞分化形成新血管。 Vasculogenesis: Endothelial progenitor cells differentiate to form new vessels.

血管内生长分隔（Intussusception）：已有血管内部出现隔断，发生分割形成新血管。 Intussusception: Pre-existing vessels split internally to create new vascular structures.

血管共选（Vessel co-option）：肿瘤细胞沿用宿主已有血管。 Vessel co-option: Tumor cells make use of pre-existing host vessels.

血管拟态（Vascular mimicry）：肿瘤细胞自身形成管道，模拟血管结构从而获得营养。 Vascular mimicry: Tumor cells themselves form vessel-like structures to gain nutrients.

肿瘤干细胞向内皮细胞转分化（Tumor stem cell to EC differentiation）：肿瘤干细胞向内皮细胞分化直接参与血管形成。 Tumor stem cell to endothelial cell 　 differentiation: Cancer stem cells differentiate into ECs and directly participate in vessel formation.
答案 CE

这些机制综合支持肿瘤获得更多血液供应，从而促进肿瘤持续生长与侵袭。 These mechanisms together enable tumors to obtain more blood and nutrients, supporting sustained growth and invasiveness.

血管生成的驱动因素
促/抑血管生成因子的平衡及VEGF的发现与信号通路

Angiogenesis drivers: Balance of pro- and anti-angiogenic factors & VEGF discovery and signaling

血管生成的驱动因素
促/抑血管生成因子的平衡

Angiogenesis drivers: Balance of pro- and anti-angiogenic factors

血管生成受促血管生成因子与抑血管生成因子的平衡调控。 Angiogenesis is regulated by the balance between pro-angiogenic and anti-angiogenic factors.

常见的促血管生成因子有
Common pro-angiogenic factors include:

血管内皮生长因子（VEGF） Vascular Endothelial Growth Factor (VEGF)

成纤维细胞生长因子（FGF） Fibroblast Growth Factor (FGF)

血小板源生长因子（PDGF）等。 Platelet-Derived Growth Factor (PDGF), etc.

常见的抑血管生成因子有
Common anti-angiogenic factors include:

血管内皮生长因子受体抑制物和部分基质成分（如内皮素、肝素等） VEGF receptor antagonists and certain matrix components (e.g., endostatin, heparin, etc.)

各种趋化因子与细胞因子，如干扰素α、转化生长因子β。 Various chemokines and cytokines, such as interferon-α, TGF-β.

这一平衡决定了新血管形成的程度和动态，有助于肿瘤生长或限制其进展。 This balance determines the extent and dynamics of neovascularization, helping tumor growth or restricting its progression 19。

2
血管内皮生长因子（VEGF）的发现历程

Vascular Endothelial Growth Factor (VEGF) discovery timeline

VEGF是最核心的促血管生成因子之一，其发现和命名历经数十年： VEGF is one of the key pro-angiogenic factors; its discovery and naming spanned several decades:

1971年首次提出肿瘤需要血管生成供养（Folkman假说）。 1971: First hypothesized that tumors require angiogenesis for sustenance (Folkman's hypothesis).

1970-1980年代逐步发现能促进血管通透性和新生的因子。 1970s–1980s: Progressively discovered factors that promote vascular permeability and neovascularization.

1989年VEGF被分离和克隆，正式命名。 1989: VEGF was isolated, cloned, and officially named.

此后，针对VEGF的抗体疗法成为肿瘤治疗的新方向。 Afterwards, antibody-based therapies targeting VEGF became a new direction for cancer treatment 5。

3
VEGF信号网络及其复杂性

VEGF signaling system and its complexity

VEGF家族包括多个配体（如VEGF-A, B, C, D, E及胎盘生长因子PlGF）和3种主要受体（VEGFR-1, VEGFR-2, VEGFR-3）。 The VEGF family includes multiple ligands (VEGF-A, B, C, D, E, PlGF) and three main receptors (VEGFR-1, -2, -3).

不同配体与不同受体的结合具有特异性，并决定细胞不同的生物学效应。 Different ligand-receptor combinations confer specificity and decide distinct cellular biological effects.

肿瘤细胞常常高表达VEGF-A，而血管内皮细胞高表达VEGFR-1和-2。 Cancer cells often overexpress VEGF-A, while endothelial cells overexpress VEGFR-1 and VEGFR-2.

VEGFR-2 是介导血管生成、增殖和通透性等最关键的受体。 VEGFR-2 is the major receptor mediating angiogenesis, proliferation, and permeability 5。

4
VEGF信号转导及其多效性

VEGF signaling transduction and its pleiotropy

VEGF与受体结合后，受体二聚化并激活，发生酪氨酸磷酸化从而引发信号级联。 Upon VEGF ligand binding, receptor dimerization and tyrosine phosphorylation initiate downstream signaling cascades.

这一信号传导激活多种下游效应物（适配器蛋白、第二信使等），导致： This triggers activation of various downstream effectors (adaptor proteins, second messengers, etc.), leading to:

细胞增殖（proliferation） Cell proliferation

血管通透性增强（increased vascular permeability）

细胞迁移与黏附、细胞外基质重塑等作用。 Cell migration, adhesion, extracellular matrix remodeling, etc.

这些过程共同支持肿瘤生长相关的新血管生成。 These processes collectively sustain tumor-associated neovascularization 4。

讲解中老师特别提到VEGF信号转导涉及多种下游信号及效应，是“复杂且多效的信号网络”，不仅影响新血管生成，也调控细胞生存、迁移等肿瘤行为。 In lecture, the professor emphasized VEGF signaling involves a complex pleiotropic network, affecting not only angiogenesis but also tumor cell survival and migration 4。

血管生成与代谢应激下肿瘤微环境的异质性、细胞代谢适应

Angiogenesis and Heterogeneity, Metabolic Adaptation of Tumor Microenvironment under Metabolic Stress

第9页：血管生成机制中的细胞行为

Page 9
Cellular Behaviors in Angiogenesis

血管生成依赖于内皮细胞的协调增殖、迁移和连接形成。 Angiogenesis relies on coordinated proliferation, migration, and junction formation of endothelial cells.

顶端细胞（tip cell）负责引导新生血管的生长，受肿瘤分泌的VEGF等促血管生成因子的吸引。 Tip cells guide the growth of new blood vessels, attracted by pro-angiogenic factors (like VEGF) secreted by tumors.

顶端细胞同后方的茎细胞（stalk cell）有分工，后者负责支持结构和腔道延伸。 Tip cells and stalk cells have distinct roles; stalk cells provide structural support and allow lumen extension.

VEGF不仅吸引顶端细胞，还促进茎细胞增殖、腔形成和整合新血管壁。 VEGF not only attracts tip cells but also promotes stalk cell proliferation, lumen formation, and new vessel wall assembly.

这一系列事件保证新生血管有序生长以满足肿瘤的血液供应需求。 This series of events ensures ordered vessel growth to meet the blood supply needs of tumors.

课堂强调
血管生成的不同步骤需要高度协调，任何环节异常都可能影响新生血管功能和肿瘤营养供应。 Lecture highlight: The multi-step angiogenic process requires close coordination; defects in any step can compromise vessel function and nutrient delivery to tumor.

第10页：肿瘤微环境的复杂互作与异质性

Page 10
Complex Interplay and Heterogeneity in Tumor Microenvironment
肿瘤微环境包含多种细胞类型，如肿瘤相关成纤维细胞（CAF）、血管内皮细胞　、免疫/炎症细胞、骨髓来源细胞、癌症干细胞（CSC）等。 The tumor microenvironment includes diverse cell types: cancer-associated fibroblasts (CAF), endothelial cells (EC), immune/inflammatory cells, bone marrow-derived cells, and cancer stem cells (CSC).
答案 CE

不同亚型肿瘤组织（原发、侵袭和转移性）其微环境组成不同，影响肿瘤生存、侵袭和耐药性。 Different subtypes of tumor tissue (primary, invasive, metastatic) have varied microenvironments, affecting tumor survival, invasion, and drug resistance.

讲课补充
微环境中的细胞间互作极为复杂，不同细胞相互影响，包括信号分泌、代谢物交换等。 As explained in class: Cell-cell interactions in the microenvironment are highly intricate, involving signaling, metabolic exchange, and more.

微环境异质性导致肿瘤细胞对代谢应激、缺氧等条件的应答差异，进一步促进肿瘤进化和异质性。 Microenvironment heterogeneity leads to varied tumor cell responses to metabolic stress and hypoxia, promoting tumor evolution and heterogeneity.

第11页：肿瘤细胞与基质细胞的代谢互作及MCT作用

Page 11
Metabolic Interactions and Role of MCT between Cancer and Stromal Cells

肿瘤及其基质细胞存在“代谢异质性”，分为分解代谢型（如部分基质细胞、肿瘤细胞）和合成代谢型细胞（增殖活跃肿瘤细胞）。 Tumors and stroma display "metabolic heterogeneity": catabolic (e.g., some stroma, some tumor cells) and anabolic cells (highly proliferative tumor cells).

分解代谢型细胞大量糖酵解，产生乳酸并通过单羧酸转运体（MCT）将乳酸输出。 Catabolic cells undergo glycolysis, producing and exporting lactate via monocarboxylate transporter (MCT).

合成代谢型细胞通过摄取乳酸等小分子燃料进入TCA循环，用于生物合成和能量供应。 Anabolic cells import lactate and other metabolites for TCA cycle use, supporting biosynthesis and energy supply.

细胞间通过乳酸等物质形成“代谢互馈”，提升肿瘤适应性和生长。 Cells form "metabolic coupling" via lactate and other exchanges, enhancing tumor adaptability and growth.

课堂强调
MCTs是肿瘤代谢适应的重要分子，可作为潜在治疗靶点。 In class: MCTs are key players in tumor metabolic adaptation and promising therapeutic targets.

第12页：肿瘤细胞代谢重编程与稳态细胞代谢对比

Page 12
Comparison of Metabolic Rewiring in Tumor vs. Quiescent Cells

静止细胞以生存为主，通过有氧代谢燃烧葡萄糖、脂肪酸和谷氨酰胺，进入三羧酸（TCA）循环，生成ATP和CO₂。 Quiescent cells focus on survival, using aerobic metabolism of glucose, fatty acids, and glutamine, routing through TCA cycle to produce ATP and CO₂.

增殖性肿瘤细胞重编程代谢，以合成生物大分子（如蛋白、脂质、核酸）为首要任务。 Proliferating tumor cells rewire metabolism to prioritize synthesis of macromolecules (proteins, lipids, nucleic acids).

大量葡萄糖经过有氧/无氧酵解途径被转化为乳酸，同时利用谷氨酰胺循环和部分TCA代谢生成生物合成前体。 High glucose consumption via aerobic/anaerobic glycolysis produces lactate, while glutamine and partial TCA activity provide biosynthetic precursors.

增殖细胞常保留TCA部分功能，用于NADPH等还原力和重要中间产物合成，不完全氧化为CO₂。 Proliferating cells retain partial TCA activity to generate NADPH and key intermediates, not fully oxidizing substrates to CO₂.

讲课总结
肿瘤细胞的代谢重编程本质是“为生长而服务”，牺牲最大能量产出以满足生物合成需求。 Lecture summary: The core of tumor metabolic rewiring is "growth-first," sacrificing maximal energy yield for biosynthetic needs.

肿瘤细胞代谢重编程与Warburg效应、糖酵解偶联乳酸产生及HIF-1信号

Metabolic Rewiring of Cancer Cells, Warburg Effect, Coupling of Glycolysis with Lactate Production, and HIF-1 Signaling

1
肿瘤细胞代谢重编程的要点（第13页）

Key points of metabolic rewiring in cancer cells (Page 13)

为了生长和分裂，细胞需能合成核苷酸、蛋白质和脂质，依赖外源性葡萄糖、谷氨酰胺及必需氨基酸。 To grow and divide, cells must synthesize nucleotides, proteins, and lipids using exogenous glucose, glutamine, and essential amino acids.

控制营养摄取的信号通路（橙色部分）在癌症中频繁激活，直接驱动合成代谢通路。 The signaling pathways controlling nutrient uptake (in orange) are frequently activated in cancer and directly fuel anabolic pathways.

多数代谢酶（蓝色高亮）在癌症发生中扩增、剪接变异或突变。 Many metabolic enzymes (highlighted in blue) are amplified, alternatively spliced, or mutated in cancer pathogenesis.

2
Warburg效应的基础及意义（第14页）

Basis and significance of the Warburg effect (Page 14)

Otto Warburg (1956)提出：癌细胞即使有氧也异常地产生乳酸（即有氧糖酵解）。 Otto Warburg (1956) discovered: Cancer cells produce lactic acid from glycolysis even in the presence of oxygen ("aerobic glycolysis").

在正常细胞中，糖酵解产物丙酮酸进入线粒体彻底氧化为CO₂，通过氧化磷酸化高效生成ATP。 In normal cells, glycolysis produces pyruvate which enters mitochondria for full oxidation to CO₂ via oxidative phosphorylation (OXPHOS), generating ATP efficiently.

ATP通过抑制磷酸果糖激酶-1（PFK-1）构成糖酵解的负反馈调控，缺氧下才有乳酸生成。 ATP negatively feeds back on glycolysis by inhibiting phosphofructokinase-1 (PFK-1); thus, lactate is produced under hypoxic conditions in normal cells.

Warburg观点原先被视为癌症的“症状”而非主因，但这一观点近20年发生了很大变化。 Warburg’s findings were long considered a symptom, not a cause of cancer, but this idea has changed dramatically in the past two decades.

3
糖酵解与乳酸产生偶联的机制（第15页）

Mechanism of coupling glycolysis and lactate production (Page 15)

癌细胞中乳酸脱氢酶（LDH）活性增强，使NAD⁺再生，从而维持高通量的GAPDH活性，保证糖酵解持续进行。 Enhanced lactate dehydrogenase (LDH) activity in cancer cells ensures NAD⁺ regeneration, maintaining high GAPDH activity and supporting persistent glycolytic flux.

癌细胞中果糖-2,6-二磷酸盐水平升高，使PFK-1在高ATP条件下也能活跃，从而解除能量的负反馈。 Cancer cells have elevated fructose-2,6-bisphosphate, allowing PFK-1 to function even when ATP is abundant, thus bypassing feedback inhibition.

这种机制保证大部分葡萄糖最终转化为乳酸，即使在有氧环境下。 This mechanism channels most glucose to lactate regardless of oxygen supply (Warburg effect).

4
HIF-1 信号与氧依赖性降解（第16页）

HIF-1 signaling and oxygen-dependent degradation (Page 16)

HIF-1为异源二聚体转录因子（HIF-1α与HIF-1β），其中HIF-1α对氧敏感并可诱导表达。 HIF-1 is a heterodimeric transcription factor (HIF-1α and HIF-1β); HIF-1α is oxygen-sensitive and inducible.

在有氧条件下，HIF-1α脯氨酸被羟基化，随后被VHL蛋白（E3泛素连接酶）识别、泛素化并通过蛋白酶体降解。 Under normoxia, HIF-1α undergoes proline hydroxylation, is recognized and polyubiquitinated by von Hippel-Lindau (VHL) protein (an E3 ubiquitin ligase), and degraded by the proteasome.

HIF-1α在常氧下半衰期极短（约5分钟）；缺氧时羟基化/降解阻断，HIF-1α稳定积累，诱导下游基因表达，包括葡萄糖代谢、血管生成等相关基因。 HIF-1α has a very short half-life (~5 min) under normoxia; under hypoxia, degradation is blocked, allowing HIF-1α to accumulate, enter the nucleus, and induce genes for glucose metabolism and angiogenesis.

肿瘤细胞代谢重编程与HIF-1/p53/c-Myc信号联合调控↑，及Warburg效应对凋亡与线粒体稳定性的影响

Metabolic Reprogramming in Cancer Cells: Integrated Regulation by HIF-1/p53/c-Myc↑ and Warburg Effect Consequences for Apoptosis and Mitochondrial Stability

1
HIF-1信号机制及稳定化↑

HIF-1 Signaling Mechanisms and Stabilization↑

缺氧时↑，HIF-1α降解受抑（VHL介导通路受阻），可与CBP/p300等转录共激活子结合，驱动一系列靶基因表达（如葡萄糖转运、糖酵解酶、血管生成等）。 Under hypoxia↑, HIF-1α degradation is inhibited (VHL-mediated pathway blocked), allowing association with co-activators CBP/p300, upregulating target genes (glucose transporters, glycolytic enzymes, angiogenesis factors).

常氧下HIF-1α被快速降解（VHL依赖）；缺氧下则积累，促进葡萄糖摄取、糖酵解和乳酸产生↑。 Under normoxia, HIF-1α is rapidly degraded (VHL-dependent); hypoxia leads to its accumulation↑, enhancing glucose entry, glycolysis, and lactate production.

非缺氧情况下，部分生长因子信号（如PI3K/AKT/mTOR、Ras/ERK）亦可上调HIF-1，促进蛋白合成和代谢重编程。 Growth factor pathways (PI3K/AKT/mTOR, Ras/ERK) can also increase HIF-1 activity even without hypoxia, promoting protein synthesis and metabolic rewiring67.

糖酵解代谢调控的关键信号节点
HIF-1↑、c-Myc↑、p53↓

Key Regulators of Glycolysis: HIF-1↑, c-Myc↑, p53↓

HIF-1、c-Myc均可促进谷氨酰胺摄取、乳酸脱氢酶（LDH）表达、糖酵解酶激活以及抑制丙酮酸脱氢酶（PDH），引导代谢流向乳酸↑； Both HIF-1 and c-Myc enhance glutamine uptake, LDH expression, glycolytic enzyme activation, and inhibit PDH, leading to increased pyruvate-to-lactate conversion↑.

p53（肿瘤抑制基因）常在癌症中失活，其突变影响核苷酸/脂质/核糖体生物合成相关代谢途径及自噬。 Tumor suppressor p53 is often inactivated in cancer, affecting nucleotide/fatty acid/ribosome biosynthesis and autophagy pathways.

p53还通过诱导TIGAR降低果糖-2,6-二磷酸，抑制糖酵解；而HIF-1/c-Myc则上调己糖激酶2型、GLUT1（葡萄糖转运蛋白），加快糖代谢。 p53 induces TIGAR to decrease fructose-2,6-bisphosphate and suppress glycolysis; HIF-1/c-Myc upregulate hexokinase II and GLUT1, accelerating glucose metabolism.

3
Warburg效应对线粒体稳定性与细胞凋亡的影响↑

Consequences of the Warburg Effect: Mitochondrial Stability and Apoptosis Suppression↑

Warburg效应导致癌细胞主要依赖糖酵解并产生乳酸，即使氧气充足（有氧糖酵解）。 The Warburg effect drives cancer cells to rely mainly on glycolysis and produce lactate, even under sufficient oxygen (aerobic glycolysis).

癌细胞通过维持线粒体外膜通道（VDAC）开放，以及调控Bcl-2蛋白，显著抑制细胞凋亡↑。 Cancer cells keep the mitochondrial VDAC channel open and regulate Bcl-2 protein to markedly suppress apoptosis↑.

增强型己糖激酶通过结合线粒体VDAC位点，促进葡萄糖磷酸化并阻止促凋亡分子（如tBid）与VDAC结合，从而保护线粒体稳定和细胞存活↑。 Upregulated hexokinase associates with mitochondrial VDAC, aids glucose phosphorylation using mitochondrial ATP, and blocks pro-apoptotic tBid binding, ensuring mitochondrial stability and cell survival↑。

课堂补充
这些机制共同造成肿瘤细胞对代谢应激的高适应性和对化疗诱导凋亡的耐受。 Lecture addition: These mechanisms collectively render tumor cells highly adaptable to metabolic stress and resistant to apoptosis induced by chemotherapy2627.
4
代谢重编程与合成代谢增强↑

Metabolic Rewiring and Enhanced Anabolism↑

HIF-1、c-Myc等通路共同促进核苷酸、脂肪酸、核糖体的生物合成，支撑肿瘤细胞的持续分裂与生长↑。 HIF-1 and c-Myc pathways jointly promote the biosynthesis of nucleotides, fatty acids, and ribosomes, supporting ongoing division and growth of tumor cells↑.

线粒体功能沉默（mitochondrial silencing）为肿瘤细胞提供了更多中间代谢产物，而非将底物完全氧化为CO₂，为生长提供原料。 Mitochondrial silencing provides cancer cells with more metabolic intermediates rather than completely oxidizing substrates to CO₂, supplying biomass for proliferation.

以上为第17-19、21页PPT内容及课堂讲解的严格中英对照听课笔记，重点涵盖HIF-1/c-Myc/p53信号联合调控↑、Warburg效应对线粒体稳定与凋亡抑制的影响及其在肿瘤代谢重编程中的作用。

勘误
此处应为LDH1~5

Warburg效应的后果——自由基生成、代谢物转运与摄取 Consequences of the Warburg Effect—ROS Generation, Metabolite Transport and Uptake

Warburg效应的后果
活性氧（ROS）释放 Consequences of the Warburg effect: Release of Reactive Oxygen Species (ROS)

约2%的吸入氧气在细胞呼吸过程中生成超氧阴离子，这是线粒体呼吸的副产物。 About 2% of inhaled oxygen is converted to superoxide anions as a by-product of mitochondrial respiration.

ROS的生成在缺氧条件下也会增加。 ROS production is also increased by hypoxia.

正常情况下，ROS由抗氧化剂平衡调控。 Under normal conditions, ROS are balanced by anti-oxidants.

若失衡，则出现氧化应激，损伤大分子如DNA、蛋白质和脂质。 If this balance is disrupted, oxidative stress occurs, damaging macromolecules like DNA, proteins, and lipids.

导致线粒体DNA突变，线粒体功能障碍，并促进OXPHOS沉默。 This leads to mitochondrial DNA mutations, mitochondrial dysfunction, and contributes to OXPHOS silencing 331.

2
乳酸产生与乳酸脱氢酶（LDH） Lactate Production and Lactate Dehydrogenase (LDH)

LDH有两种亚基（A和B），以四聚体形式存在，形成LDH1~LDH5异构酶。 LDH consists of two subunits, A and B, assembled as tetramers to form LDH1 to LDH5 isoenzymes.

正常细胞主要以LDH1（B₄）为主，亲和力高于乳酸。 Normal cells mainly have LDH1 (B₄), with higher affinity for lactate.

肿瘤细胞高表达LDHA（HIF-1和c-Myc上调），以LDH5（A₄）为主，亲和力高于丙酮酸，推动丙酮酸→乳酸。 Cancer cells highly express LDHA (upregulated by HIF-1 and c-Myc), predominantly forming LDH5 (A₄), which has higher affinity for pyruvate, promoting pyruvate → lactate conversion.

注意
癌细胞中LDH异构酶谱变化也影响丙酮酸激酶亚型，导致低亲和力的PKM2堆积PEP，增强糖苷分流至磷酸戊糖途径，支持核苷酸合成。 Note: Change of LDH isoenzymes in cancer also affects pyruvate kinase isoforms (e.g., low-affinity PKM2 causes PEP accumulation), driving glycolytic intermediates into the pentose phosphate pathway and supporting nucleotide biosynthesis323.
3
单羧酸转运蛋白（MCT）与乳酸外排 Monocarboxylate Transporters (MCT) and Lactate Export

MCT1和MCT4在肿瘤中高度上调，以H⁺-驱动协同转运方式工作（symporter）。 MCT1 and MCT4 are highly upregulated in cancer and function as H⁺-driven symporters.

H⁺先结合，使结构改变，允许乳酸结合与释放，然后H⁺释放，实现乳酸外排。 H⁺ binds first, causing a conformational change that allows lactate binding and export, followed by H⁺ release.

正常细胞
MCT1根据乳酸浓度梯度转运乳酸。 In normal cells: MCT1 transports lactate along its transmembrane gradient.

肿瘤细胞因乳酸浓度极高，MCT1易饱和，此时低亲和力的MCT4接替外排乳酸和H⁺。 In cancer cells, high lactate saturates MCT1, so low-affinity MCT4 exports lactate and H⁺.

该转运系统对细胞内外pH调节有重要作用。 This system is essential for the regulation of intra- and extracellular pH348.

Warburg效应的后果
代谢物摄取 Consequences of the Warburg effect: Metabolite Import

肿瘤细胞摄取所需营养物（氨基酸、二肽、三肽、叶酸、铁等）多依赖H⁺共转运体（symporters），这由乳酸外排过程产生的胞外酸性环境促进。 The uptake of nutrients (amino acids, di/tri-peptides, folate, Fe²⁺, etc.) by cancer cells relies on H⁺ symporters, and is promoted by the acidic extracellular environment resulting from lactate export.

促进入核苷酸、蛋白质、线粒体蛋白的合成，以及一碳代谢和表观遗传调控。 This supports nucleic acid, protein, mitochondrial protein synthesis, one-carbon metabolism, and epigenetic regulation.

勘误
此处应为LDH15。
Correction
LDH isoforms here refer to LDH15.

Warburg效应的后果——乳酸穿梭与信号传递 Consequences of the Warburg Effect—Lactate Shuttling and Signaling

1
乳酸穿梭于肿瘤微环境中的机制

Lactate shuttling in the tumor microenvironment

肿瘤细胞与癌相关成纤维细胞（CAF）之间通过乳酸穿梭相互作用。 There is lactate shuttling between tumor cells and cancer-associated fibroblasts (CAF).

肿瘤细胞（代谢活跃）通过LDHA将葡萄糖代谢生成乳酸并外排，依赖MCT4转运蛋白。 Tumor cells (metabolically active) convert glucose to lactate via LDHA and export it using MCT4 transporters.

成纤维细胞摄取乳酸（MCT1），逆向分解为丙酮酸供氧化磷酸化。 Fibroblasts take up lactate via MCT1 and convert it back to pyruvate for oxidative phosphorylation.

这种机制也被称为“逆Warburg效应”，即肿瘤细胞诱导周围基质细胞变为分解代谢型，协助乳酸循环与能量供应。 This is termed the “reverse Warburg effect”, where tumor cells instruct stroma to become catabolic, facilitating lactate cycling and energy supply49.

2
乳酸在肿瘤微环境中的促瘤效应

Lactate shuttling in the tumor microenvironment—tumor-promoting effects

乳酸通过单羧酸转运蛋白进入肿瘤相关巨噬细胞，被用作能量来源。 Lactate enters tumor-associated macrophages via monocarboxylate transporters and serves as an energy source.

乳酸消耗细胞内NAD⁺，抑制NAD⁺依赖性酶（如PARP），降低DNA修复和保护功能。 Lactate depletes intracellular NAD⁺, inhibiting NAD⁺-dependent enzymes (such as PARP), reducing DNA repair and protective capability.

乳酸通过抑制HIF羟化酶，间接稳定HIF-1α，促进血管生成。 Lactate inhibits HIF hydroxylase, stabilizing HIF-1α and promoting angiogenesis.

乳酸在肿瘤微环境中其它促瘤影响包括
抑制免疫反应、激活c-Myc、上调TGF-β2、抑制HDAC、增强干性等。 Other tumor-promoting effects include: immune suppression, activation of c-Myc, TGF-β2 upregulation, HDAC inhibition, and increased stemness56.
3
乳酸作为肿瘤细胞外信号分子

Lactate as an extracellular messenger in cancer cells

乳酸可通过GPCR（如GPR81）受体行使自分泌和旁分泌的信号作用。 Lactate acts through GPCRs (e.g., GPR81) as both autocrine and paracrine signaling molecules.

其影响包括
促进血管生成、增强PD-L1表达、抑制DNA修复与促进耐药等。 Effects include promoting angiogenesis, increasing PD-L1 expression, suppressing DNA repair, and enhancing chemoresistance.

乳酸信号能够调节ABCB1和BRCA1的表达，进而影响化疗敏感性。 Lactate signaling can modulate ABCB1 and BRCA1 expression, impacting chemotherapy sensitivity54.

4
乳酸对肿瘤微环境的旁分泌作用

Paracrine effects of lactate on the tumor microenvironment

乳酸影响巨噬细胞、树突状细胞、内皮细胞及脂肪细胞等多种细胞类型。 Lactate impacts various cell types: macrophages, dendritic cells, endothelial cells, and adipocytes.

通过GPR81，乳酸限制炎症反应，增强肿瘤免疫逃逸。 Through GPR81, lactate limits inflammation and promotes immune evasion in tumors.

在脂肪组织内，GPR81受体表达增强，可抑制cAMP及与脂解相关的激素，促进肿瘤生长和化疗耐受。 In adipose tissue, GPR81 receptor expression increases, repressing cAMP and hormone-sensitive lipolysis, facilitating tumor growth and chemoresistance54.

4
乳酸对肿瘤微环境的旁分泌作用

Paracrine effects of lactate on the tumor microenvironment

乳酸影响巨噬细胞、树突状细胞、内皮细胞及脂肪细胞等多种细胞类型。 Lactate impacts various cell types: macrophages, dendritic cells, endothelial cells, and adipocytes.

通过GPR81，乳酸限制炎症反应，增强肿瘤免疫逃逸。 Through GPR81, lactate limits inflammation and promotes immune evasion in tumors.

在脂肪组织内，GPR81受体表达增强，可抑制cAMP及与脂解相关的激素，促进肿瘤生长和化疗耐受。 In adipose tissue, GPR81 receptor expression increases, repressing cAMP and hormone-sensitive lipolysis, facilitating tumor growth and chemoresistance54.

新生血管生成和代谢适应的治疗性靶向 Therapuetic Targeting of Neoangiogenesis and of Metabolic Adaptations

1
有效的瘤生长控制新范式 —— 抗血管生成治疗

Powerful paradigm for tumor growth control – Anti-angiogenic therapy

抗血管生成已成为肿瘤治疗的重要方向，可以有效抑制肿瘤的生长和扩散。 Anti-angiogenic therapy has become an important strategy in cancer treatment, effectively inhibiting tumor growth and spread.

抗血管生成药物通过阻断新生血管的形成，切断肿瘤对氧和营养的供应。 Anti-angiogenic drugs work by blocking the formation of new blood vessels, depriving tumors of oxygen and nutrients.

这类治疗改变了肿瘤治疗范式，被认为是肿瘤生长控制的强有力模式。 This type of therapy has shifted the paradigm of cancer treatment and is considered a powerful approach to tumor growth control53。

2
抗癌新领域 —— 代谢适应及其后果的调控

New field of anticancer therapy – Control of metabolic adaptations and their consequences

针对肿瘤代谢适应（如Warburg效应）及其衍生的各种生物学后果，已成为新的抗癌治疗前沿。 Targeting metabolic adaptations (such as the Warburg effect) and their biological consequences has become a new frontier in anticancer therapy.

通过干预肿瘤细胞的代谢重编程，可以影响肿瘤细胞的生长、存活和耐药性。 By interfering with tumor cell metabolic reprogramming, it is possible to affect tumor cell growth, survival, and drug resistance.

该方向不仅可以与抗血管生成疗法联合，还为控制肿瘤发展带来多维度的干预手段。 This approach can be combined with anti-angiogenic therapy and offers a multifaceted way to control tumor progression53。

简要总结：

Summary:

PPT强调了新生血管生成和肿瘤代谢适应是肿瘤生长和治疗耐药的重要基础，抗血管生成与代谢靶向治疗共同构成了当前及未来抗癌治疗的重要策略。

The slide emphasizes that neoangiogenesis and tumor metabolic adaptation are key foundations for tumor growth and therapeutic resistance. Anti-angiogenic therapy and metabolic targeting together represent major strategies for current and future anticancer therapy.53

042025-10-13（第 2 讲）

细胞骨架 Cell cytoskeleton

细胞骨架由四种主要纤维结构组成
肌动蛋白丝、微管、中间纤维丝和隔膜丝。 The cell cytoskeleton consists of four main types of filamentous structures: actin filaments, microtubules, intermediate filaments, and septin filaments.

肌动蛋白丝（微丝）Actin Filaments (Microfilaments）

肌动蛋白丝的直径为约8纳米，是细胞内最细的骨架成分。 Actin filaments have a diameter of about 8 nm and are the thinnest cytoskeletal components in the cell.

由肌动蛋白单体聚合而成，1942年被发现。 They are polymers of actin monomers, discovered in 1942.

主要负责细胞形状维持、细胞迁移和胞质分裂等。 They are mainly responsible for maintaining cell shape, cell migration, and cytokinesis.

常见于贴壁细胞的皮层区及细胞伪足等部位，如图右侧插图所示。 Frequently located at the cell cortex and pseudopods in adherent cells, as shown in the side illustration.

微管 Microtubules

微管直径约为25纳米，是最粗的细胞骨架成分。 Microtubules have a diameter of about 25 nm, making them the thickest cytoskeletal component.

由微管蛋白二聚体（α-和β-微管蛋白）组成，1967年被发现。 They are composed of tubulin dimers (α- and β-tubulin) and were discovered in 1967.

参与细胞内物质运输、细胞分裂时的纺锤体形成等。 They are involved in intracellular transport and mitotic spindle formation during cell division.

微管网络广泛分布于细胞质内，常形成放射状结构，如插图所示。 Microtubule networks are distributed throughout the cytoplasm and often form radial structures, as illustrated.

中间丝 Intermediate Filaments

中间纤维丝直径大约为10纳米，介于微丝和微管之间。 Intermediate filaments have a diameter of about 10 nm, intermediate between microfilaments and microtubules.

由各种IF蛋白组成（如角蛋白、波形蛋白等），1970年代中后期被发现。 They are made up of various IF proteins (e.g., keratin, vimentin), discovered in the mid/late 1970s.

提供机械强度，保持细胞和细胞核的完整性。 They provide mechanical strength and maintain the integrity of the cell and nucleus.

如右侧插图，广泛分布于胞质和细胞核周围。 As shown in the side illustration, they are widely distributed around the cytoplasm and nucleus.

隔膜丝 Septin Filaments

隔膜丝由septin蛋白组成，直径约5纳米，常为双链结构。 Septin filaments, composed of septin proteins, have a diameter of about 5 nm and often form double-stranded structures.

Septin蛋白1971年被发现，是较新描述的骨架元件。 Septin proteins were discovered in 1971 and are among the more recently described cytoskeletal elements.

参与细胞分裂（如细胞分裂沟），以及细胞的极性和形状保持。 Involved in cell division (e.g., cytokinetic furrow) and maintenance of cell polarity and shape.

在细胞分裂过程中于特定区域形成带状结构，如插图所示。 They form belt-like structures in specific regions during cell division, as depicted in the illustration.

补充说明 Additional Notes:

每类骨架结构对应的发现年代及主要关键蛋白已以彩色标注（如actin 1942、tubulins 1967等）。 The discovery years and key proteins of each cytoskeletal type are color-coded (e.g., actin 1942, tubulins 1967).

图中比例尺表示横向长度为100纳米，便于不同结构的比较。 Scale bars denote a length of 100 nm for easy comparison.

不同骨架结构具备独特功能，协同完成细胞的力学、结构、运输及分裂各项任务。 Different cytoskeletal structures have unique functions and work together to fulfill cellular mechanical, structural, transport, and division tasks.

肌动蛋白微丝Actin microfilaments

1
肌动蛋白微丝是细胞骨架的重要组成部分，广泛存在于真核细胞。 Actin microfilaments are vital components of the cytoskeleton, widely present in eukaryotic cells.
2
人类有多种肌动蛋白同工型，分为普遍型（如γ-cyto、β-cyto）和肌肉型（如α-skeletal、α-cardiac、α-smooth等）。 There are multiple actin isotypes in humans, divided into ubiquitous types (e.g., γ-cyto, β-cyto) and muscle types (α-skeletal, α-cardiac, α-smooth, etc.).
3
G-肌动蛋白（球状）是单体结构，可以结合ATP，具有方向性（+端为barbed，-端为pointed）。 G-actin (globular) is the monomeric form, can bind ATP, and has polarity (+ end is barbed, - end is pointed).
4
F-肌动蛋白是由G-肌动蛋白聚合而成的极性双螺旋结构，宽约8纳米。 F-actin is a polarized double helix polymerized from G-actin, about 8 nm wide.
5
F-肌动蛋白具有极性，+端通常为聚合/生长端，-端为脱聚/收缩端。 F-actin displays polarity: the + end is usually the site of polymerization/growth, and the - end undergoes depolymerization/shrinkage.
6
肌动蛋白微丝的动态包括成核（nucleation）、延伸（elongation）和稳态（steady state）阶段。 The dynamics of actin microfilaments include nucleation, elongation, and steady state phases.
7
成核期为滞后期，需要少量G-肌动蛋白自发聚集形成“种子”结构，随后才能快速延伸。 The nucleation phase is a lag phase, requiring a small number of G-actin molecules to spontaneously form a "seed" structure before rapid elongation.
8
延伸期是快速增长阶段，更多G-肌动蛋白单体加到+端。 The elongation phase is a rapid growth stage where more G-actin monomers are added to the + end.
9
稳态期下，微丝长度保持平衡，单体不断加入+端并从-端脱落，表现为treadmilling现象。 At steady state, filament length remains stable as monomers are continuously added at the + end and lost at the - end, a phenomenon known as treadmilling.
10
肌动蛋白为ATP酶，聚合后ATP水解为ADP促进微丝不稳定，影响微丝动态。 Actin acts as an ATPase, hydrolyzing ATP to ADP after polymerization, promoting filament instability and affecting its dynamics.
11
不同端的临界浓度（C_C）不同，+端C_C较低，有利于聚合，-端C_C较高，易于解聚。 The critical concentration (C_C) differs at each end: lower at the + end (favoring polymerization), higher at the - end (favoring depolymerization).
12
treadmilling 范围内，G-肌动蛋白浓度介于+端和-端临界浓度之间，表现为一端生长、一端缩短。 Within the treadmilling range, the concentration of G-actin is between the critical concentrations of the + and - end, resulting in growth at one end and shrinkage at the other.
13
肌动蛋白可通过多种翻译后修饰（PTMs）调控功能，包括乙酰化、甲基化、磷酸化等。 Actin can be regulated by various post-translational modifications (PTMs), including acetylation, methylation, phosphorylation, etc.
14
这些修饰影响微丝的聚合/解聚、分支形成及与结合蛋白的相互作用。 These modifications affect filament polymerization/depolymerization, branching, and interactions with binding partners.
15
PTMs的添加与去除调节微丝的结构状态及其细胞功能。 The addition and removal of PTMs modulate the structural state of microfilaments and their cellular functions.
来自 <https
//www.asksia.ai/pro/session?autoChat=true&id=3f364baf-f40a-4313-ae06-024c2d496ab3>

肌动蛋白结合蛋白（ABPs）Actin-binding proteins (ABPs)

1
肌动蛋白结合蛋白（ABPs）广泛调控微丝的组装、稳定、连接和功能。 Actin-binding proteins (ABPs) broadly regulate the assembly, stabilization, crosslinking, and functions of microfilaments.
2
成核与（去）聚合类蛋白如Arp2/3复合体和Formin促进微丝起始与延伸，Cofilin/ADF和profilin调控单体添加及脱聚。 Nucleation/(de)polymerization proteins such as the Arp2/3 complex and Formin initiate and elongate filaments, while Cofilin/ADF and profilin regulate monomer addition and depolymerization.
3
Arp2/3复合体能诱导微丝分支，提高细胞骨架的多样性和功能性。 The Arp2/3 complex promotes filament branching, increasing cytoskeletal diversity and function.
4
Formin蛋白介导直链F-肌动蛋白的生长，有利于长纤维的形成。 Formin proteins mediate linear F-actin growth, facilitating the formation of long fibers.
5
Cofilin/ADF蛋白促使微丝解聚和重构，profilin促进G-肌动蛋白加到+端。 Cofilin/ADF proteins promote filament depolymerization and remodeling, while profilin facilitates G-actin addition at the + end.
6
交联和束集型蛋白如Filamin、Fascin、α-Actinin和Spectrin负责将肌动蛋白交联或成束，构建多样结构。 Crosslinking/bundling proteins such as Filamin, Fascin, α-Actinin, and Spectrin are responsible for crosslinking or bundling actin filaments into diverse structures.
7
Filamin形成交叉网架结构，增强细胞柔韧性。 Filamin forms a cross-linked network, increasing cell flexibility.
8
Fascin、α-Actinin等则构建致密的束状结构，支持微绒毛和伪足等特殊细胞结构。 Fascin, α-Actinin, and others create tight bundles supporting structures like microvilli and pseudopodia.
9
某些蛋白如tropomodulin、CAPZα/β等可盖帽/剪切微丝两端，gelsolin和Cofilin/ADF可断裂微丝调控其长度。 Certain proteins like tropomodulin and CAPZα/β cap or sever filament ends, while gelsolin and Cofilin/ADF can sever filaments to control their length.
10
ABPs也促进肌动蛋白与细胞膜的连接，如紧密连接、黏着斑、锚定连接等。 ABPs also promote linkage of actin filaments to cell membranes, including tight junctions, focal adhesions, and anchoring junctions.
11
紧密连接中，claudin、occludin和ZO1/2/3介导膜连接并连接微丝。 In tight junctions, claudin, occludin, and ZO1/2/3 mediate membrane contact and connect to microfilaments.
12
黏着连接和黏着斑由cadherin、catenin、vinculin、talin和整合素等蛋白参与微丝的锚定。 Adherens junctions and focal adhesions use cadherin, catenin, vinculin, talin, and integrin proteins to anchor actin filaments.
13
ERM家族（ezrin、radixin、moesin）联系肌动蛋白和细胞膜，有助于微绒毛和丝状伪足结构的形成。 The ERM family (ezrin, radixin, moesin) links actin to the membrane, aiding in the formation of microvilli and filopodia.
14
ABPs还能介导细胞运输与收缩功能，肌球蛋白V负责货物运输，肌球蛋白II则参与肌动蛋白的收缩活动。 ABPs also mediate cargo transport and contraction; myosin V is responsible for cargo transport, and myosin II is involved in actin filament contraction.
15
肌球蛋白V沿微丝双极运动，通过ATP水解运送细胞器和囊泡。 Myosin V moves bidirectionally along actin filaments, transporting organelles and vesicles via ATP hydrolysis.
16
肌球蛋白II介导应力纤维、细胞分裂环和肌原纤维的收缩，是细胞和肌肉收缩的关键。 Myosin II mediates contraction of stress fibers, cytokinetic rings, and myofibrils, critical for cellular and muscle contraction.

肌动蛋白微丝网络/功能 Actin microfilament networks/functions

1
肌肉收缩 Muscle contraction

肌动蛋白微丝在骨骼肌、心肌和平滑肌中形成有序排列的肌节，是收缩的基础。 Actin microfilaments form ordered sarcomeres in skeletal, cardiac, and smooth muscles and provide the basis for contraction.

低钙浓度时，原肌球蛋白和肌钙蛋白封闭肌球蛋白结合位点，肌肉处于松弛状态。 At low calcium, tropomyosin and troponin block myosin-binding sites, keeping the muscle relaxed.

高钙浓度时，Ca²⁺结合肌钙蛋白，暴露肌球蛋白结合位点，允许肌球蛋白与肌动蛋白结合产生收缩。 At high calcium, Ca²⁺ binds troponin, exposing myosin-binding sites and allowing myosin to bind actin for contraction.

肌节由Z线、M线、+端（capZ蛋白）和-端（tropomodulin蛋白）界定，肌动蛋白与天蓬蛋白、肌联蛋白、肌营养蛋白等结构蛋白共同维持结构稳定。 The sarcomere is defined by Z-line, M-line, + end (capZ), and - end (tropomodulin); actin, titin, nebulin, and dystrophin stabilize the structure together.

2
间充质细胞迁移 Mesenchymal migration

肌动蛋白微丝动态装配构成压力纤维、伪足、板层足及丝足，驱动细胞在基质上的定向移动。 Dynamic actin assembly forms stress fibers, lamellipodia, and filopodia, driving directed cell migration on substrates.

小GTP酶RhoA、Rac1 和Cdc42调控不同类型微丝结构的形成，分别驱动压力纤维（收缩）、板层足（树枝状网络）、丝足（致密平行束）。 Small GTPases RhoA, Rac1, and Cdc42 regulate formation of different actin structures: stress fibers (contraction), lamellipodia (branched network), and filopodia (tight bundles).

纤维束收缩依赖NMII（非肌球蛋白II），突起和迁移主要依赖F-actin的聚合与结合蛋白（如filamin、fascin、Arp2/3复合体）。 Bundle contraction depends on NMII (non-muscle myosin II); protrusion and migration rely on actin polymerization and binding proteins such as filamin, fascin, and the Arp2/3 complex.

3
上皮细胞和神经元中的作用 Role in epithelial cells and neurons

在上皮细胞顶端，微绒毛内F-肌动蛋白由ERM、villin、fimbrin等交联，并以terminal web（终末网）锚定，参与细胞极性和物质转运。 At the apical side of epithelial cells, F-actin in microvilli is crosslinked by ERM, villin, and fimbrin, anchored by the terminal web, supporting polarity and material transport.

皮质肌动蛋白、紧密连接和黏着连接维持细胞形态及相互连接。 Cortical actin, tight, and adherens junctions maintain cell shape and intercellular contact.

内耳毛细胞和部分脑细胞的静纤毛、神经元轴突内有规则的肌动蛋白环，由加戴素、谱蛋白等组成有助于机械信号感应和稳定性。 Stereocilia in inner ear and some brain cells, and axon rings in neurons feature regular actin arrangements with adducin and spectrin for mechanical sensing and stability.

有丝分裂过程中，肌动蛋白和肌球蛋白构建收缩环，完成细胞分裂末期胞质分裂。 During mitosis, actin and myosin form the contractile ring for cytokinesis at the end of cell division.

4
内吞作用与包裹反应 Endocytosis and engulfment

肌动蛋白微丝驱动胞吞（内吞作用）、胞饮和吞噬等包裹反应，促进细胞摄取大分子或颗粒。 Actin filaments drive endocytosis, macropinocytosis, and phagocytosis to facilitate uptake of macromolecules or particles.

细菌或病毒感染时，可诱导“肌动蛋白彗星尾”现象，帮助病原体在胞质中移动与扩散。 During bacterial or viral infection, pathogens can induce "actin comet tails," aiding their movement and spread within the cytoplasm.

Arp2/3复合体在这些过程的分支核化中发挥关键作用。 The Arp2/3 complex plays a key role in branched nucleation during these processes.

肌动蛋白微丝与细胞核Actin microfilaments and nucleus

1
周核肌动蛋白（Perinuclear actin）

周核区域的肌动蛋白通过与核包膜的相互作用，调控细胞核的结构与功能。 Perinuclear actin interacts with the nuclear envelope to regulate nuclear structure and function.

周核肌动蛋白调控核的形状与刚性，进一步影响基因表达和细胞分裂过程。 Perinuclear actin modulates nuclear shape and stiffness, thereby impacting gene expression and cell division.

通过与核包膜连接，肌动蛋白还参与细胞核的运动和定位。 By linking with the nuclear envelope, actin also participates in nuclear movement and positioning.

2
核内肌动蛋白（Nuclear actin）

核内肌动蛋白在转录、DNA损伤修复、染色质重排和同源重组等过程中发挥关键作用。 Nuclear actin plays key roles in transcription, DNA damage repair, chromatin rearrangement, and homologous recombination.

　转录过程中，肌动蛋白与RNA聚合酶II、修复蛋白复合，调控基因表达。 (A) During transcription, actin combines with RNA polymerase II and repair proteins to regulate gene expression.
答案 A
　DNA损伤后，肌动蛋白协助受损DNA在细胞核中的移动，促进高效修复。 (B) After DNA damage, nuclear actin facilitates the movement of damaged DNA within the nucleus to promote efficient repair.
答案 B
　染色质重排依赖于肌动蛋白的调节，影响核内基因可及性与表达状态。 (C) Chromatin rearrangements rely on actin regulation, affecting gene accessibility and expression states in the nucleus.
答案 C
　肌动蛋白参与同源重组修复，维护基因组稳定性。 (D) Actin is involved in homologous recombination repair, maintaining genome stability.
答案 D

这些功能通过肌动蛋白与修复蛋白、染色质、DNA、RNA聚合酶、肌球蛋白的相互作用实现。 These functions are mediated by the interactions of actin with repair proteins, chromatin, DNA, RNA polymerase II, and myosin.

结论 Conclusion:

肌动蛋白不仅在胞质中维持力学结构和参与运动，在细胞核及其周边同样对遗传物质的调控和核结构的完整性至关重要。 Actin not only maintains mechanical structure and motility in cytoplasm, but is also crucial for the regulation of genetic material and nuclear integrity both inside and around the nucleus.

肌动蛋白微丝与疾病 Actin microfilaments and diseases

1
肌动蛋白基因突变 Actin mutations

线状肌病（呼吸肌无力） Nemaline myopathy (respiratory muscle weakness)

心肌病（心脏肌肉） Cardiomyopathy (heart muscle)

巨膀胱结肠-肠道蠕动减弱综合征（肠道平滑肌） Megacystis microcolon-intestinal hypoperistalsis syndrome (enteric muscle)

Baraitser-Winter综合征（脑、心、肾） Baraitser-Winter syndrome (brain, heart, kidney)

家族性胸主动脉瘤（血管平滑肌细胞） Familial thoracic aortic aneurysms (vascular smooth muscle cells)

出血性疾病 Bleeding disorders

耳聋（内耳，听毛） Deafness (inner ear, stereocilia)

2
异常肌动蛋白结合蛋白 Abnormal ABPs

肌病 Myopathies

神经退行性变 Neurodegeneration

Wiskott-Aldrich综合征（免疫功能紊乱） Wiskott–Aldrich syndrome (immune dysregulation)

肌动蛋白微丝与癌症 | Actin Microfilaments and Cancer

1
促进肿瘤细胞迁移与侵袭 Facilitation of Tumor Cell Migration and Invasion

肌动蛋白微丝支持肿瘤细胞的迁移和侵袭，形成如叶状伪足（lamellipodia）和丝状伪足（filopodia）等结构，这些结构有助于细胞穿透组织屏障。 Actin microfilaments support tumor cell migration and invasion, forming structures such as lamellipodia and filopodia, which enable cells to penetrate tissue barriers.

肌动蛋白主导的应力纤维和收缩环也参与肿瘤细胞的运动和分裂。 Actin-based structures like stress fibers and the contractile ring also participate in tumor cell movement and division.

肿瘤细胞通过肌动蛋白依赖的运动方式，包括伪足驱动的间充质运动和胞质收缩驱动的变形虫样运动。 Tumor cells migrate using actin-dependent mechanisms, including mesenchymal movement driven by protrusions and amoeboid movement driven by cytoplasmic contraction.

2
促进肿瘤相关的血管生成 Promotion of Tumor Angiogenesis

肌动蛋白动态调控内皮细胞迁移，有助于新生血管（肿瘤相关血管）的形成，从而为肿瘤生长供给氧气和营养。 Actin dynamics regulate endothelial cell migration, contributing to the formation of tumor-associated new blood vessels, which supply oxygen and nutrients for tumor growth.

表达和调节VEGF（血管内皮生长因子）及其信号通路促进新血管的生成和肿瘤进展。 Expression and regulation of VEGF (vascular endothelial growth factor) and its pathway promote angiogenesis and tumor progression.

3
与肿瘤多种表型相关 Regulation of Multiple Tumor Phenotypes

肿瘤细胞可塑性高，通过改变肌动蛋白微丝结构，切换不同迁移和侵袭方式（如间充质-变形虫迁移转变）。 Tumor cells exhibit high plasticity, switching between different migration and invasion models by remodeling actin microfilaments (e.g., mesenchymal-amoeboid transition).

肌动蛋白骨架调控肿瘤细胞增殖、凋亡及耐药性，关联肿瘤细胞分裂和动力学特性。 Actin cytoskeleton controls tumor cell proliferation, apoptosis, and drug resistance, influencing tumor cell division and mechanical properties.

4
直接靶向肌动蛋白药物治疗的挑战与新策略 Challenges and New Approaches of Actin-Targeted Therapy

靶向肌动蛋白的药物（如phalloidin、jasplakinolide、latrunculin、cytochalasin等）通过稳定或破坏肌动蛋白微丝抑制肿瘤迁移和分裂，但对正常细胞也有严重毒性，临床应用受限。 Drugs targeting actin (e.g. phalloidin, jasplakinolide, latrunculin, cytochalasin) inhibit tumor migration and division by stabilizing or disrupting filaments, but cause severe toxicity to normal cells, limiting clinical use.

更具特异性的策略是针对某些肌动蛋白结合蛋白（如tropomyosin家族），有望降低副作用。 More specific strategies involve targeting actin-binding proteins such as the tropomyosin family, which may reduce side effects.

5
教师讲解强调 Teacher’s Emphasis

老师特别指出，肌动蛋白相关结构如伪足结构和相关动力蛋白，在肿瘤细胞运动、血管新生和分裂中均有核心作用。 The instructor emphasized that actin-based structures such as protrusions and associated motor proteins play key roles in tumor cell motility, angiogenesis, and division.

靶向肌动蛋白虽具潜力，但因其在所有细胞中的普遍功能，目前尚难成为有效癌症治疗手段。 Although targeting actin holds promise, its ubiquitous functions currently prevent it from being an effective anticancer therapy.

更希望的方向是深入研究肌动蛋白结合蛋白的选择性靶向。 Promising directions involve developing selective therapies targeting actin-binding proteins.

微管结构
微管蛋白

Microtubule structure: tubulin

1
微管由空心极性管构成，直径约25 nm。 Microtubules are hollow, polarized tubes with a diameter of about 25 nm.
2
微管的基本结构单元是α-微管蛋白和β-微管蛋白形成的二聚体。 The basic structural unit of microtubules is a dimer composed of α-tubulin and β-tubulin.

13条原丝以平行方式排列，围成微管的管壁。 Thirteen protofilaments are arranged in parallel to form the wall of the microtubule.

4
微管具有极性，分别有+端和-端，+端生长更快。 Microtubules are polarized with a plus (+) end and a minus (–) end; the plus end grows faster.
5
人类具有多种α-和β-微管蛋白异构型，它们在C端尾部表现出变异性。 Humans have multiple α- and β-tubulin isotypes, which vary mainly at their C-terminal tails.
6
C端尾部的序列决定了微管蛋白码（tubulin code），并可发生多种翻译后修饰（PTMs），影响其功能和与结合蛋白的相互作用。 The C-terminal tail sequence determines the “tubulin code” and can undergo various post-translational modifications (PTMs), affecting microtubule function and interactions with associated proteins.
课上老师强调
The instructor emphasized:

微管通过其动态构建与解构支持细胞分裂、物质运输等关键过程。 Microtubules, through their dynamic assembly and disassembly, support key processes like cell division and intracellular transport.

微管蛋白异构型及其修饰丰富，构成亚细胞调控特异性的重要基础。 The diversity and modification of tubulin isotypes form the molecular basis for specific subcellular regulations.

微管动态性｜Microtubule Dynamics

微管的起始组装依赖于γ-微管蛋白环复合体（γ-TuRC），该复合体为微管提供模板催化核化。 The initiation of microtubule assembly depends on the γ-tubulin ring complex (γ-TuRC), which acts as a template to catalyze microtubule nucleation.

1
γ-TuRC主要位于中心体（MTOC，微管组织中心），这里是新微管生长的起点。 The γ-TuRC is primarily located at the centrosome (MTOC, microtubule organizing center), which serves as the starting point for new microtubule growth.
2
初始的不稳定原丝组装通过γ-TuRC稳定下形成稳定的微管种子。 The initial unstable protofilament assembly is stabilized by γ-TuRC to form a stable microtubule seed.
3
中心体结构由一对中心粒和其周围的基质（pericentriolar material）组成，是微管束的锚定点。 The centrosome consists of a pair of centrioles and surrounding pericentriolar material, serving as the anchoring site for microtubule bundles.
4
微管表现出动态不稳定性，其本质是微管的持续生长和缩短。 Microtubules exhibit dynamic instability, which is the continuous process of growth and shrinkage.
β-微管蛋白具有GTP酶活性
GTP结合的微管端（GTP帽）促进微管聚合。 β-tubulin has GTPase activity: the GTP-bound microtubule end (“GTP cap”) promotes polymerization.
6
当GTP水解为GDP，失去GTP帽后，微管侧易出现“灾变”（快速解聚）；有时可被“救援”恢复聚合。 When GTP is hydrolyzed to GDP and the GTP cap is lost, the microtubule tends to undergo “catastrophe” (rapid depolymerization); sometimes, it can be “rescued” and resume growth.
7
微管的+端通常聚合速度快且动态变化显著，-端多锚定于中心体并较为稳定。 The plus end of the microtubule usually grows rapidly and is highly dynamic, while the minus end is often anchored at the centrosome and more stable.
8
动态不稳定性赋予微管在细胞分裂及细胞内运输中灵活调整其结构的能力。 Dynamic instability gives microtubules the ability to flexibly adjust their structure during cell division and intracellular transport.
老师强调
理解微管的动态性及其调控对于认识细胞骨架功能，特别是细胞分裂机制至关重要。 The instructor emphasized that understanding microtubule dynamics and their regulation is essential for comprehending cytoskeletal function, especially the mechanisms of cell division.

微管网络的多样性与定位 Microtubule Networks: Diversity and Localization

1
微管在不同细胞类型中分布和结构各异，执行着多种重要功能。 Microtubules exhibit different distributions and structures in various cell types, performing diverse vital functions.
2
成纤维细胞内，微管以放射状从中心体（MTOC）出发，铺向细胞周边。 In fibroblasts, microtubules radiate from the centrosome (MTOC) towards the cell periphery.
3
细胞分裂时，微管重组为分裂纺锤体，包括星形微管、极间微管和动粒微管（K-fiber），中心体是其组织核心。 During mitosis, microtubules reorganize into the mitotic spindle, comprising astral microtubules, interpolar microtubules, and kinetochore microtubules (K-fibers), with the centrosome as the organizing center.
4
上皮细胞如角质形成细胞，微管主要分布于细胞皮质下，围绕细胞形成皮质微管网络。 In epithelial cells such as keratinocytes, microtubules mainly localize beneath the cell cortex, forming a cortical microtubule network.
5
肌肉纤维中，微管排列成束，沿纤维轴向延伸，且在各个细胞核周围有定位。 In muscle fibers, microtubules are bundled and extend longitudinally along the fiber, also localized around each nucleus.
6
纤毛和鞭毛的微管为轴突结构，由基体（MTOC）发出，呈9+2或9+0排列，驱动运动。 Microtubules in cilia and flagella form the axoneme, organized from the basal body (MTOC), typically in a 9+2 or 9+0 arrangement to drive motility.
7
肠上皮细胞（吸收细胞）中，微管纵向贯穿细胞体，从顶端（apical）延伸到底端（basal）。 In enterocytes, microtubules run longitudinally through the cell, extending from the apical to the basal side.
8
神经元内，轴突中的微管+端朝向末端，树突中为混合排列，有利于物质定向运输。 In neurons, microtubule plus-ends in axons point toward the axon terminal, while dendrites have mixed orientation, supporting directional transport.
老师强调
这些不同的微管网络赋予细胞独特的形态与功能，如支持细胞极性、内部分隔与运输、细胞分裂及运动等。 The instructor emphasized that these diverse microtubule networks endow cells with specific morphologies and functions, such as maintaining cell polarity, intracellular compartmentalization and transport, cell division, and motility.

微管相关蛋白（MAPs）Microtubule-associated proteins (MAPs)

1
微管相关蛋白（MAPs）是调控微管功能和动态的关键因子，能够影响微管的组装、稳定性和分解。 Microtubule-associated proteins (MAPs) are key regulators of microtubule function and dynamics, affecting microtubule assembly, stability, and disassembly.
2
结构型MAPs（如Tau、MAPs）主要负责微管的束缚、交联和稳定，广泛存在于神经元等细胞中。 Structural MAPs (such as Tau and MAPs) primarily function in bundling, crosslinking, and stabilizing microtubules, and are widely present in cells like neurons.
3
剪切型MAPs（如Katanin）具有切割微管的功能，便于细胞内微管网络的动态重构。 Severing MAPs (e.g., Katanin) can cut microtubules, facilitating dynamic remodeling of the microtubule network within cells.
4
+端追踪蛋白（+TIPs），如EBs和XMAP215，作用于微管+端，调控其聚合作用，而MCAK则促进+端解聚，CLIP-170提供连接作用。 +TIPs (plus-end tracking proteins), such as EBs and XMAP215, regulate microtubule polymerization at the plus end; MCAK promotes plus-end disassembly; CLIP-170 serves as a linker.
5
-端追踪蛋白（-TIPs），如CAMSAPs，结合微管-端，维持末端稳定性与定向。 -TIPs (minus-end tracking proteins), such as CAMSAPs, bind to the minus end of microtubules, maintaining its stability and orientation.
6
分子马达蛋白包括驱动向+端运动的驱动蛋白（kinesin），以及向-端运动的细胞质动力蛋白（dynein），二者介导细胞内物质沿微管的运输。 Molecular motors include kinesin (moving toward the plus end) and cytoplasmic dynein (moving toward the minus end), both mediating intracellular cargo transport along microtubules.
7
Stathmin是一种微管异二聚体捕获蛋白，可以与游离的微管蛋白结合，抑制其聚合，从而调节微管的动态不稳定性。 Stathmin is a tubulin dimer-sequestering protein that binds free tubulin dimers, inhibiting their polymerization and regulating microtubule dynamic instability.
8
老师强调，MAPs的种类和分布因细胞类型和功能需求而异，对有丝分裂、细胞迁移及极性建立等过程都至关重要。 The instructor emphasized that the types and distribution of MAPs vary with cell type and functional demand, and are crucial for processes like mitosis, cell migration, and polarity establishment

微管功能 Microtubule functions

间期 Interphase

细胞器定位、膜运输、分子运输
细胞极性、迁移 Organelle positioning, membrane traffic, molecular transport: cell polarity, migration
纤毛/鞭毛运动
呼吸道/大脑/精子 Ciliary/flagella motility: respiratory tract / brain / sperm

初级纤毛信号传导 Primary cilia signaling

微管上有马达蛋白驱动货物运输，动力蛋白向-端移动，驱动蛋白向+端移动 Motor proteins (dynein moves toward the - end, kinesin moves toward the + end) transport cargo along microtubules

有丝分裂 Mitosis

染色体的排列与分离 Chromosome alignment and separation

形成纺锤体，微管组织中心 (MTOC) 向两极伸展微管 Spindle formation, microtubules extend from the Microtubule Organizing Center (MTOC) to the poles

关键过程 Key Processes

1
Kinesin-5 (Eg5) 通过滑移抗平行微管分离两极 Kinesin-5 (Eg5) separates the two poles by sliding antiparallel microtubules
2
动力蛋白通过向两极运输微管和K纤维集中纺锤体两极 Dynein focuses spindle poles by poleward transport of microtubules and K-fibers
3

4
CENP-E (Kinesin-7) 介导着丝点至纺锤体赤道的运输 CENP-E (Kinesin-7) transports kinetochores to the spindle equator

补充说明（结合老师讲解）：

微管异常会在小鼠模型中重现上述多种人类疾病，如生殖、神经、血液、肌肉及细胞分裂相关疾病。 Microtubule defects lead to similar diseases in mouse models as in humans, including reproductive, neuronal, blood, muscle, and cell division–related disorders.

纤毛的功能异常可导致呼吸、视力和生殖相关多系统障碍。 Cilia dysfunctions affect multiple systems such as respiratory, visual, and reproductive functions.

微管在细胞极性、迁移、分裂等方面作用广泛，相关异常可引起广泛病理变化。 Microtubules are essential for cell polarity, migration, and division, so their defects lead to broad pathological consequences.

总结 Summary:

微管功能异常涉及生殖、呼吸、神经、血液、肌肉和分裂多系统疾病。 Microtubule dysfunctions are involved in diseases affecting the reproductive, respiratory, neuronal, blood, muscle, and cell division systems.

小鼠模型是理解人类微管相关疾病的重要工具。 Mouse models are vital for understanding microtubule-associated human diseases

1
微管靶向药物类型 Types of microtubule-targeting agents

课堂补充讲解重点 Key points from lecture

尽管taxane等药物能有效抑制有丝分裂（纺锤体作用），癌组织仅少量细胞处于分裂期，因此药效解释存在悖论 Although taxanes and similar drugs effectively arrest mitosis (spindle effect), only a small fraction of tumor cells are actually in mitosis, raising a paradox in chemotherapeutic efficacy31。

药物另有抗血管新生和转移、促凋亡等多重机制，部分解释其全身疗效 The drugs also exert anti-angiogenic, anti-metastatic and pro-apoptotic effects, partially explaining their systemic efficacy31。

治疗耐药和副作用突出，致使临床疗效受限 The effectiveness is limited clinically by resistance and significant side effects3119。

第二代针对微管相关蛋白的药物单用疗效有限，未来可能需联合多靶点方案 Second-generation antimitotics targeting mitotic kinesins show limited single-agent efficacy, likely requiring future combination strategies31。

结论 Conclusion

微管及其相关蛋白异常在癌症中常见，是多个经典及新型抗肿瘤药物的靶点 Microtubule and associated protein dysregulation is common in cancer and forms the basis for several classic and novel antitumor agents

药物作用机制多元化，但疗效和安全性仍是突破难点 Drug mechanisms are diverse, but efficacy and safety remain major challenges

中间纤维多样性与细胞特异性Diversity of IFs (70 genes): cell specificity

中间纤维是一类由70多个基因编码的细胞骨架蛋白，在不同细胞类型中特异性表达。 Intermediate filaments (IFs) are a class of cytoskeletal proteins encoded by over 70 genes, showing cell-type specific expression.

晶状体特异的“孤儿VI型”中间纤维包括phakinin和filensin，只在晶状体中表达。 Orphan type VI intermediate filaments such as phakinin and filensin are specifically expressed in the eye lens.

角蛋白I和II型主要分布于皮肤、毛发、指甲等上皮组织。I型为酸性，II型为中性或碱性。 Keratins I & II are mainly found in the skin, hair, nails, and other epithelial tissues; type I is acidic, type II is neutral or basic.

简单上皮角蛋白（如7, 8, 18-20型）主要存在于腺体和消化道等单层上皮。 Simple epithelial keratins (such as types 7, 8, 18-20) are present in glandular and gastrointestinal simple epithelium.
