神经系统中主要表达神经丝蛋白（IV型），如neurofilament L/M/H，α-internexin，nestin，以及GFAP（III型）和peripherin（III型）。 The nervous system mainly expresses neurofilaments (type IV), such as neurofilament L/M/H, α-internexin, nestin, as well as GFAP (type III) and peripherin (type III).

肌肉组织（心肌、骨骼肌、平滑肌）内表达III型的desmin、syncoilin及IV型的synemin。 Muscle tissue (cardiac, skeletal, and smooth muscle) contains type III IFs like desmin, syncoilin, and type IV IFs such as synemin.

血管和间充质组织主要表达vimentin（III型），并在各种结缔组织、血管内皮等广泛分布。 Blood vessels and mesenchymal tissues predominantly express vimentin (type III), found in connective tissues and vascular endothelium.

细胞核中特异性表达V型中间纤维，主要包括lamin A, C和lamin B1, B2，参与核结构与功能调控。 The nucleus specifically expresses type V intermediate filaments (lamin A, C, lamin B1, B2), which are essential for nuclear structure and function.

总结
中间纤维种类丰富且具有高度细胞特异性，不同类型IFs负责维持各自细胞的结构和功能特点。 Summary: Intermediate filaments are diverse and highly cell-type specific, with each kind responsible for maintaining structure and function in their respective cells.

中间纤维网络Intermediate filament networks

1
中间纤维的多样性与细胞特异性

Intermediate filaments (IFs) are widely distributed in different cell types, with distinct isoforms and functions.

各种细胞中的中间纤维网络通过免疫荧光染色显示出不同类型的IF分布。 Intermediate filament networks in various cells are visualized by immunofluorescence, demonstrating cell-type-specific IF distribution.

2
具体细胞类型及对应中间纤维

Specific cell types and corresponding intermediate filaments

肺泡癌细胞（左上图）：角蛋白18（绿色，I型）和波形蛋白（红色，III型）共表达，表现为细胞骨架的网络结构。 Alveolar carcinoma cells (top left): co-express Keratin 18 (green, type I) and Vimentin (red, type III) showing a filamentous cytoskeletal network.

成纤维细胞核（右上图）：Lamin A/C（红色，V型）包裹于细胞核周围，维护核结构。 Fibroblast nucleus (top right): Lamin A/C (red, type V) surrounds the nucleus, maintaining nuclear structural integrity.

心肌细胞（左下图）：Desmin（绿色，III型）定位于细胞间盘（细胞-细胞连接处），对心脏收缩结构非常重要。 Cardiac cells (bottom left): Desmin (green, type III) is localized at intercalated discs (cell-cell junctions), crucial for cardiac contraction structure.

星形胶质细胞（右下图）：GFAP（红色，III型）、Vimentin（绿色，III型）和细胞核DNA（蓝色），共同形成神经胶质支架体系。 Astrocyte (bottom right): GFAP (red, type III), Vimentin (green, type III), and DNA (blue), together constitute the glial structural scaffolding.

3
课堂讲解补充要点

Key lecture additions

图例揭示同一细胞内可共表达不同类型中间纤维（如角蛋白和波形蛋白），不同IF亚型共同参与细胞结构与功能的维持。 Figures show that multiple IF types (such as keratin and vimentin) can be co-expressed within the same cell, with different IF isoforms jointly maintaining cell architecture and functions.

中间纤维不仅维持形态，还参与细胞间连接（如心肌细胞间盘）、细胞核稳定（lamin）、细胞运动和神经支架形成等方面。 Intermediate filaments maintain cell shape, are involved in cell-cell junctions (e.g., cardiac intercalated discs), nuclear stability (lamins), cell motility, and neuroglial scaffolding structures.

通过免疫荧光可同时标记不同中间纤维蛋白，帮助识别其在不同细胞和亚细胞结构中的分布与功能。 Immunofluorescence allows simultaneous labeling of different IF proteins, facilitating identification of their localization and function in various cells and subcellular structures.

4
总结Summary

不同类型的中间纤维蛋白在特定细胞和亚细胞部位功能专一，共同保障细胞及组织的结构完整性和功能。 Different types of intermediate filament proteins are functionally specialized in specific cell types and subcellular locations, together ensuring cellular and tissue integrity and functionality.

中间纤维蛋白亚结构域 Intermediate filament protein subdomains

课件标题与概念（中英对照）

中间纤维蛋白亚结构域 Intermediate filament protein subdomains

听课笔记整理（中英文一一对应）

所有中间纤维蛋白（IFs）均由一条长的α螺旋“杆状结构域”构成，位于分子的中央。 All intermediate filament proteins (IFs) share a central long α-helical "rod domain".

α螺旋杆状结构域的两端分别是氨基末端和羧基末端。 The α-helical rod domain is flanked by an amino terminus and a carboxyl terminus.

在α螺旋杆状结构域中，包含重复的“七肽重复”区域，有利于分子聚集成同源/异源二聚体。 The α-helical rod domain contains "heptad repeats" that help assemble into homo-/heterodimers.

不同类型中间纤维蛋白（如Ⅰ/Ⅱ型角蛋白、Ⅲ型波形蛋白、Ⅳ型神经丝蛋白、Ⅴ型核纤层蛋白）均保持这一结构特征。 Different types of intermediate filament proteins (e.g., type I/II keratin, type III vimentin, type IV neurofilaments, type V lamins) share this structural motif.

角蛋白（I, II型）和神经丝蛋白（IV型）除保留共同核心结构外，N端和C端长度及组成有变异，赋予各类型特异功能。 Keratins (type I/II) and neurofilaments (type IV) retain the core structure but display variations in the length/composition of N- and C-termini, giving functional specificity.

波形蛋白（III型）和核纤层蛋白（V型）同样具有中心α螺旋结构域，但C端区域在V型蛋白（如核纤层蛋白）中特别长且卷曲，与其独特的核定位功能有关。 Vimentin (type III) and nuclear lamins (type V) also have the central α-helical domain, but type V (lamins) have an especially long and convoluted C-terminal domain, related to their nuclear localization/functions.

结构和进化分析显示，这些蛋白可能来源于共同祖先，并根据细胞/组织类型继发性地分化形成上述不同亚型。 Structural and evolutionary analysis indicates a common ancestor for these proteins, with specialization into subtypes according to cell/tissue requirements.

总结 Summary

所有中间纤维蛋白共享中心α螺旋结构，N/C端多样化赋予功能特异性，是细胞结构和功能多样性的分子基础。 All intermediate filament proteins share a central α-helical structure, while N- and C- terminal diversity provides functional specificity, forming the molecular basis for structural and functional diversity in cells.

中间纤维结构Intermediate filament structure

1
中间纤维的基本组装单元是缠绕螺旋二聚体。  The basic assembly unit of intermediate filaments (IFs) is the coiled-coil dimer.
2
两个二聚体首尾错位排列形成四聚体（四聚体是组装中的关键对称结构）。  Two dimers align in a staggered, anti-parallel fashion to form a tetramer, which is a key symmetric assembly intermediate.
3
八个四聚体侧向聚集形成“单位长度片段”（ULF），为IFs的主要聚合单位。  Eight tetramers laterally associate to form a unit length fragment (ULF), which is the main polymerization unit of IFs.
4
多个ULF通过端对端延长形成完整的中间纤维，横截面约包含32条螺旋线圈，整体直径约10纳米。  Multiple ULFs join end-to-end to form complete intermediate filaments, with about 32 coiled-coils in cross-section and an overall diameter of about 10 nm.
5
中间纤维具有无极性，无马达蛋白参与运输活动。  Intermediate filaments have no polarity, and no motor proteins are involved for trafficking.
6
IFs聚合不需要ATP或GTP的能量输入，与微管和微丝不同。  IF assembly does not require ATP or GTP input, unlike microtubules or actin filaments.
7
中间纤维相较其它细胞骨架组分寿命长（通常可达几十小时）。  Compared to other cytoskeletal components, IFs are long-lived (often lasting several tens of hours).
8
IFs在高盐或去污剂环境下表现出极低的溶解性，显示其高稳定性。  IFs display very low solubility in high salt or detergent, indicating their high stability.
9
富有弹性的结构，使中间纤维难以被拉断，赋予细胞极强的机械稳定性。  Their highly elastic structure makes IFs difficult to break, providing cells with strong mechanical stability.
10
目前已知中间纤维的结合伙伴较少，具体结合模式尚不完全清楚。  The binding partners of IFs remain incompletely characterized so far.
11
课件中原子力显微镜照片显示了神经丝蛋白（neurofilament）的真实形态。  Atomic force microscopy images in the slide display the real structure of neurofilaments.

补充（均来自老师对IF特性和结构的解读）

Supplement (key points from lecture):

IFs的组装过程独特，对称性强，保证了其稳定和耐受机械应力的能力。 The assembly process of IFs is unique and highly symmetric, ensuring great stability and resistance to mechanical stress.

IFs在细胞内的主要作用不是动力学（如运输），而是维持结构、弹性和抗张力。 In cells, IFs mainly function not for dynamics (like transport), but to maintain structure, elasticity, and resistance to tensile forces.

由于缺乏极性，IFs无法像微管那样指导细胞器或囊泡的定向运动。 Due to their lack of polarity, IFs cannot direct the movement of organelles or vesicles like microtubules can.

中间纤维动态重组与动力学 Intermediate filament dynamic restructuration & dynamics

1
中间纤维的动态重组 Intermediate filament dynamic restructuration

中间纤维网络在细胞内处于持续重组状态，以适应不同生理需求。 Intermediate filament (IF) networks in cells are constantly reorganized to adapt to various physiological needs.

在细胞分化、迁移、有丝分裂和应激反应等状态下，中间纤维网络经历重组、更新、稳定/去稳定和装配/解聚等过程。 During differentiation, migration, mitosis, and stress response, IF networks undergo processes such as reorganization, turnover, stabilization/destabilization, and assembly/disassembly.

细胞静息（稳态）时，IFs通过持续磨损和补充实现动态平衡，维持结构与功能稳定。 In the steady state, IFs maintain dynamic equilibrium through ongoing wearout and replenishment, ensuring stability of structure and function.

老师补充强调，中间纤维不像肌动蛋白和微管那样快速动态，但同样表现出高度可塑性，可根据细胞需求快速重构网络。 The teacher emphasized that, compared to filaments or microtubules, IFs do not show such rapid dynamics, but they also exhibit high plasticity and can rapidly remodel networks as cells require.

2
中间纤维动力学观测与机制 Intermediate filament dynamics: observation and mechanism

利用荧光漂白恢复（FRAP）实验可直接观察IF的动态变化，漂白区域随着时间推移荧光信号恢复，说明中间纤维成分具有流动性和交换性。 Fluorescence recovery after photobleaching (FRAP) directly reveals IF dynamics; the bleached region recovers fluorescence over time, indicating mobility and exchange of IF components.

激光光转换（green to red）以及追踪分析显示，IFs可通过马达蛋白（如kinesin、dynein）介导沿微管运输，在数分钟尺度上发生位置变化。 Laser photoconversion (switching green to red) and tracking show that IFs can be transported along microtubules by motor proteins (e.g., kinesin, dynein), resulting in positional shifts over minutes.

在更长时间（小时）尺度上，IFs可以通过断裂与重新连接（severing and reannealing）以及四聚体常规自我修复实现动态重塑。 On the hours scale, IFs undergo severing and reannealing, plus constant self-repair via tetramers, achieving dynamic renewal.

老师讲解补充，中间纤维的动力学过程确保其网络既能维持细胞形态和结构稳定，又能通过重塑适应外界或细胞内信号变化。 The teacher added that IF dynamics allow their network to maintain cell integrity and structure while being able to remodel in response to external cues or intracellular signals.

3
总结 Summary

中间纤维虽然被认为是“稳定支架”，实则具备适度动态性，在分化、迁移、分裂、应激等事件下积极参与细胞命运调控。 Although IFs are considered "stable scaffolds", they actually have moderate dynamics and actively participate in controlling cell fate during differentiation, migration, division, and stress.

IF动力学基础在于成分的快速交换和网络的可重构性，为细胞提供结构弹性和适应力。 The basis of IF dynamics lies in the rapid exchange of components and network reconfigurability, providing cells with structural elasticity and adaptive capacity.

中间纤维相关蛋白（IFAPs）及连接蛋白：plectin、SUNs、nesprin

Intermediate filament-associated proteins (IFAPs) linkers: plectin, SUNs, nesprin

1
Plectin是一种大型多功能连接蛋白，能同时与中间纤维、微管和肌动蛋白丝发生连接。 Plectin is a large multifunctional linker protein that can bind simultaneously to intermediate filaments (IFs), microtubules, and actin filaments.
2
Plectin拥有可变的N端结构域，不同亚型介导其在细胞内不同结构的定位，包括细胞核、半桥粒、线粒体、微管和Z盘等部位。 Plectin has variable N-terminal domains, with different isoforms mediating targeting to various structures within the cell, such as the nucleus, hemidesmosomes, mitochondria, microtubules, and Z-disks42。
3.Plectin的结构包含肌动蛋白结合结构域　与中间纤维结合结构域（IFBD），实现不同细胞骨架之间的交联与稳固。 Plectin contains an actin-binding domain (ABD) and an intermediate filament-binding domain (IFBD), enabling cross-linking and stabilization among different cytoskeletal networks42。
答案 ABD
4
老师强调，plectin不仅作为结构支架，还参与细胞粘附、细胞迁移、细胞分裂以及机械信号转导。 The teacher emphasized that plectin acts not only as a structural scaffold but also participates in cell adhesion, migration, division, and mechanotransduction.
5
SUNs和nesprin蛋白共同组成LINC复合体（linker of nucleoskeleton and cytoskeleton），将核骨架的lamins与胞质的IFs、微管和肌动蛋白丝连接起来。 SUN and nesprin proteins form the LINC complex (linker of nucleoskeleton and cytoskeleton), bridging nuclear lamins with cytoplasmic IFs, microtubules, and actin filaments42。
6
Nesprin蛋白存在多个亚型，例如nesprin-3通过plectin与IFs连接，nesprin-4则与微管马达蛋白相连，实现细胞骨架与细胞核之间的桥接。 Nesprin proteins have several isoforms; for instance, nesprin-3 connects IFs through plectin, while nesprin-4 connects to microtubule motor proteins, thus bridging the cytoskeleton and the nucleus42。
7
SUN蛋白为跨膜蛋白，位于核膜的内侧，nesprin定位于核膜外侧，与不同类型的细胞骨架蛋白相结合，将外部机械信号传递到细胞核内部。 SUN proteins are transmembrane proteins located on the inner nuclear envelope, while nesprins are positioned on the outer nuclear membrane and associate with various cytoskeletal proteins, transmitting external mechanical signals to the nucleus.
8
老师补充，IFAPs如plectin及LINC复合体对于细胞应对机械应激、信号传导和空间结构调控极其关键，缺陷可导致疾病或细胞功能障碍。 The teacher added that IFAPs such as plectin and the LINC complex are vital for coping with mechanical stress, signal transmission, and spatial organization; defects may cause disease or cellular dysfunction.
总结
这类连接蛋白使中间纤维与其他细胞骨架和细胞核高效连接，为细胞提供整体稳定性、适应力，并实现机械刺激到细胞核的信号转导。 In summary: these linker proteins efficiently connect IFs with other cytoskeletal elements and the nucleus, providing cellular structural stability, adaptability, and transducing mechanical stimuli into nuclear signaling42。
1
中间纤维的主要细胞功能

Functions of intermediate filaments (IFs) in cells

IFs锚定细胞器（如线粒体），维持其正确定位。 IFs anchor organelles (e.g., mitochondria) to maintain their correct cellular positioning.

IFs参与信号通路调控。 IFs participate in the regulation of signaling pathways.

IFs调节细胞力学性质，提高细胞刚性和收缩性。 IFs control cellular mechanics, providing cell stiffness and contractile force.

IFs在细胞粘附、CD44受体等黏附结构的维持和调节中起作用。 IFs contribute to cell adhesion, maintaining/adapting structures such as CD44 receptor interactions.

IFs促进细胞迁移（伪足、片足等结构），调节形态变化。 IFs promote cell migration (structures like filopodia, lamellipodia), regulating cell shape changes.

IFs参与上皮-间质转化、细胞侵袭和细胞增殖过程。 IFs are involved in epithelial-mesenchymal transition, cell invasion, and cell proliferation.

2
IFs在细胞迁移中的作用

Role of IFs in cell migration through confined spaces

核中IFs（lamin类）和胞质IFs（vimentin类）共同协作，保护细胞在狭窄空间迁移时的形态与功能。 Nuclear IFs (lamins) and cytoplasmic IFs (e.g., vimentin) cooperate to protect cell shape and function when migrating through confined spaces.

3
IFs缺失对细胞的影响

Consequences of IF depletion

缺失胞质IFs时，细胞去可变形性增强，细胞和核易破裂，核膜（NE）易破裂，DNA容易受损，细胞死亡率升高。 Upon depletion of cytoplasmic IFs, cells become more deformable, prone to cell and nuclear rupture, nuclear envelope (NE) rupture, increased DNA damage, and higher cell death rates.

缺失核IFs时，细胞核的可变形性增加，核膜损伤更加严重，DNA损伤和细胞死亡风险进一步提升。 With nuclear IF depletion, nuclear deformability increases, NE rupture becomes more severe, DNA damage worsens, and cell death risk further rises.

4
结论

Summary

IFs对于维持细胞形态、DNA完整性及细胞存活至关重要。 IFs are critical for maintaining cell shape, DNA integrity, and cell survival.

老师补充强调，没有IFs时，细胞结构极易受损，DNA损伤和细胞死亡显著增加，这可解释IFs在抵抗机械应力和保护细胞中的重要性。 The teacher emphasized that in the absence of IFs, cells are structurally vulnerable, with significantly increased DNA damage and cell death, highlighting IFs' key role in mechanical stress resistance and cellular protection.

关键词总结
细胞力学、粘附、迁移、增殖、DNA完整性、机械保护
Keywords summary
cell mechanics, adhesion, migration, proliferation, DNA integrity, mechanical protection

中间纤维在细胞应激反应中的作用 Intermediate filaments in cell-stress response

1
细胞在应对不同类型应激时，中间纤维在质膜和核膜处起到力的缓冲和结构保护作用。 When cells face various stresses, intermediate filaments at the plasma membrane and nuclear membrane serve as buffers for force and protect structural integrity.
2
质膜处的中间纤维可以缓解由推拉力和牵张产生的力，并帮助细胞分散外来的机械应力。 At the plasma membrane, intermediate filaments dissipate forces generated by pushing, pulling, and tension, helping the cell to disperse external mechanical stress.
3
在核膜处，中间纤维与微管等结构联合作用，抵抗微管推动引起的核变形，保护细胞核。 At the nuclear membrane, intermediate filaments cooperate with microtubules to resist microtubule-induced nuclear deformation and safeguard the nucleus.
4
中间纤维在细胞内对“应力”的响应面向多种刺激，包括机械（张力、剪切力、压缩）、创伤愈合、缺氧、渗透压、氧化应激、蛋白折叠错误、毒素、辐射、DNA损伤、病原体（病毒或细菌）及热刺激等。 Intermediate filaments respond to a variety of stresses, including mechanical (tension, shear, compression), wound closure, hypoxia, osmotic and oxidative stresses, protein misfolding, toxins, radiation, DNA damage, pathogens (virus, bacteria), and heat.
5
老师特别强调，中间纤维可为细胞核等器官提供稳定性并维持形状，是抵御外部应激和维持细胞功能的关键。 The teacher stressed that intermediate filaments provide stability and preserve the shape of the nucleus and organelles, playing a key role in resisting external stress and maintaining cellular functions.
6
应激后，中间纤维网络会根据应激类型和持续时间发生变化，包括包涵体形成、纤维数量增加、以及纤维网络重组。 Following stress, intermediate filament networks undergo changes that depend on stress type and duration, such as inclusion body formation, filament increase, or network reorganization.
不同颜色标识代表各类细胞骨架
蓝色为胞质中间纤维，红色为核中间纤维，橙色为微丝，黄色为微管。 The color codes in the diagram indicate cytoplasmic intermediate filaments (blue), nuclear intermediate filaments (red), actin filaments (orange), and microtubules (yellow).
课堂补充
在面对持续或强烈的应激时，中间纤维不仅数量增多，也可能重新分布和形成特殊结构，从而实现细胞的适应和保护。 In class, the teacher added that under persistent or intense stress, the number of intermediate filaments increases and they may redistribute or form specialized structures to ensure cell adaptation and protection.
总结
中间纤维是细胞应对多种应激（特别是机械应激和结构损伤）的重要骨架网络，动态调节以保护细胞结构和功能。
Summary
Intermediate filaments are crucial cytoskeletal networks enabling cells to withstand diverse stresses, especially mechanical or structural insults, through dynamic adjustment to protect structure and function.

中间纤维与疾病 Intermediate filaments and diseases

角蛋白I、II型
与皮肤病（如单纯性表皮松解性水疱症）相关，也会增加患肝脏疾病（如脂肪变性）的风险，是癌症标志物。 Keratin type I and II: Related to skin diseases (such as epidermolysis bullosa simplex), predisposition for liver diseases (such as steatosis), and serve as cancer markers.
2
Ⅲ型（如波形蛋白vimentin）：与肌病、亚历山大病、白内障及肿瘤转移有关。 Type III (e.g., vimentin-like): Associated with myopathies, Alexander disease, cataract, and metastasis.
Ⅳ型神经丝蛋白
与神经精神类疾病有关（包括夏科-马里-图斯病、阿尔茨海默病、肌萎缩侧索硬化、帕金森病）。 Type IV neurofilaments: Related to neuropsychiatric diseases (Charcot-Marie-Tooth disease, Alzheimer’s disease, amyotrophic lateral sclerosis, Parkinson’s disease).
Ⅴ型lamins
发生突变会导致laminopathies（如早老症/早发性衰老）。 Type V lamins: Mutation can cause laminopathies (such as progeria / precocious aging).
5
VI型（孤儿型）中间纤维：与白内障相关。 Orphan (Type VI) filaments: Associated with cataract.
6
老师强调，不同类型的中间纤维基因突变往往导致相应器官的疾病，其中部分蛋白还可作为癌症诊断或进展标志物。 The teacher emphasized that mutations in different types of intermediate filament genes often lead to organ-specific diseases, and some of these proteins can serve as markers for cancer diagnosis or progression.
7
印度冬樱（Ashwagandha, Withania somnifera）中的活性成分withaferin A可以破坏vimentin、角蛋白和神经丝网络，目前正在骨肉瘤进行I期临床试验。 Withaferin A, an active compound from Ashwagandha (Withania somnifera), can disrupt vimentin, keratin, and neurofilament networks, and is currently in phase I clinical trial for osteosarcoma.
8
他汀类药物（如辛伐他汀）：常用于降胆固醇，也能破坏vimentin骨架，目前有对癌症患者进行联合用药的II期临床试验。 Statins (e.g., simvastatin): Used as anti-cholesterol drugs, they can also disrupt the vimentin network, and phase II trials (combination therapy in cancer) are ongoing.
总结
中间纤维家族蛋白的异常与多种遗传病、变性病及肿瘤密切相关，部分药物通过作用于中间纤维网络用于疾病治疗或临床试验。 Summary: Aberrations of intermediate filament family proteins are closely related to various hereditary diseases, degenerative conditions, and cancers; some drugs targeting intermediate filament networks are being tested or used in disease therapy and clinical trials.

Septin丝状结构Septin filament structure

1
Septin是一类GTP酶蛋白，能够结合和水解GTP。 Septin belongs to the GTPase protein family and is capable of binding and hydrolyzing GTP.
2
Septin蛋白根据结构和序列分为四个主要家族：SEPT7组、SEPT6组、SEPT2组、SEPT3组。 Septin proteins are classified into four main groups based on their structure and sequence: SEPT7 group, SEPT6 group, SEPT2 group, and SEPT3 group.
3
不同家族的Septin具有各自特有的结构域，包括N端富脯氨酸区、GTP结合区、多碱性区、SUE区和C端螺旋-螺旋区。 Septins from different groups have unique domains, including an N-terminal proline-rich region, GTP-binding domain, polybasic region, SUE region, and a C-terminal coiled-coil domain.
4
Septin蛋白能够组装形成异源寡聚体，典型的有六聚体和八聚体，直径约为5纳米。 Septin proteins can assemble into hetero-oligomers, typically hexamers and octamers, with a diameter of about 5 nm.
六聚体的组成为
sept2-sept6-sept7-sept7-sept6-sept2，八聚体中增加了sept3，排列形式为sept2-sept6-sept7-sept3-sept3-sept7-sept6-sept2。 Hexamers are composed as: sept2-sept6-sept7-sept7-sept6-sept2, while octamers include sept3 and are arranged as sept2-sept6-sept7-sept3-sept3-sept7-sept6-sept2.
6
这些寡聚体排列呈对称“回文”结构，为后续丝、束和环结构的组装提供基础。 These oligomers are arranged in a palindromic manner, providing the foundation for the assembly of filaments, bundles, and rings.
7
Septin寡聚体可进一步多聚形成丝状结构，丝可相互联结形成束，最终还可形成环状高级结构。 Septin oligomers can further polymerize into filaments, which can laterally associate to form bundles, and finally assemble into higher-order ring structures.
课堂补充
老师强调septin环结构在细胞分裂（如胞质分裂）、细胞极性形成及路径细胞中具有重要功能，是一种高度可塑的细胞骨架。 Class note: The teacher emphasized that septin ring structures play crucial roles in cytokinesis, cell polarity formation, and in barrier cells, making septins a highly versatile cytoskeletal system.
总结
Septin蛋白家族通过有序组装形成多种复杂结构，参与多种细胞基本过程，是细胞骨架体系中的重要组成部分，特别是在细胞分裂中的环结构至关重要。
Summary
The septin family forms various complex structures through ordered assembly and participates in fundamental cellular processes. Septins are an essential part of the cytoskeleton system, with ring structures being particularly critical for cell division.

Septin丝状结构的关联Septin filament association

1
Septin丝状结构能够通过连接蛋白（如anillin和BORG）将自身与其他细胞骨架组分（比如肌动蛋白、微管）和膜结构结合。 Septin filaments can associate with other cytoskeletal components (such as actin and microtubules) and membrane structures via linker proteins like anillin and BORG.
2
Septin可结合于细胞膜，其结合与膜的曲率有关（最适曲率半径为0.5-1.5 μm的正曲率），也受特异性脂质如磷脂酰肌醇磷酸（PIPs）和心磷脂的调控。 Septins can bind to cell membranes, and this binding depends on membrane curvature (optimal positive curvature radius 0.5–1.5 μm) and is regulated by specific lipids such as phosphatidylinositol phosphates (PIPs) and cardiolipin.
3
Septin也与微管相互作用，其中Sept9的N端、不同类型的微管蛋白亚型（如TUBB2），以及微管的翻译后修饰（如聚谷氨酸化、多酪氨酸化）和GTP的结合状态，均影响其结合效率。 Septins also interact with microtubules, with factors such as the N-terminus of Sept9, specific tubulin isotypes (e.g., TUBB2), post-translational modifications of tubulin (such as polyglutamylation and tyrosination), and the GTP-binding state modulating their association.
4
Septin不仅能为膜结构和细胞骨架提供稳定，还能作为支架调控膜曲率、信号转导及细胞形态等。 Septins not only stabilize membrane structures and the cytoskeleton but also serve as scaffolds to regulate membrane curvature, signal transduction, and cell morphology.
课堂补充
老师强调septin丝通过与肌动蛋白、微管等多种细胞骨架成分的相互作用，在细胞分裂、极性建立和迁移等过程中具有多重调控作用。 Class note: The teacher emphasized that septin filaments, through interactions with actin, microtubules, and other cytoskeletal elements, play versatile regulatory roles in processes such as cell division, polarity establishment, and migration.
总结
Septin丝状结构通过连接蛋白及膜曲率、脂质和微管修饰等机制，与多种细胞骨架/膜成分形成复合结构，参与细胞形态和动态的调控，是细胞结构和功能调节的重要支架。
Summary
Septin filaments, via linker proteins, membrane curvature, lipids, and microtubule modifications, form complexes with various cytoskeletal and membrane components, participating in cell shape and dynamics regulation and constituting a critical scaffold in cellular structural and functional modulation.

Septin丝状结构的功能Septin filament functions

1
扩散屏障Diffusion Barriers

Septin在原纤毛、树突棘和精子环结构（annulus）处形成屏障，限制膜蛋白或信号分子的横向扩散，保护特定细胞区室的功能。 Septins form barriers at structures like primary cilium, dendritic spine, and sperm annulus to restrict lateral diffusion of membrane proteins or signaling molecules, thereby preserving the specific functions of distinct cellular compartments.

2
支架平台Scaffolding platforms

Septin为膜蛋白提供锚定位点，促进受体、转运蛋白等在膜域的空间分布，同时可连接肌动蛋白和微管等细胞骨架成分，调控信号传导和细胞结构。 Septins provide anchoring sites for membrane proteins, organizing the spatial distribution of receptors and transporters. They also link cytoskeletal elements such as actin and microtubules, thus regulating signaling and cellular architecture.

3
膜刚性调节者Membrane Rigidifiers

Septin通过排列或结合于细胞膜特定区域（如uropod/后伪足、叶状伪足、丝状伪足），增强局部膜的机械强度，维持细胞形态并协助运动。 Septins localize to specific membrane regions (like the uropod, lamellipodia, and filopodia), where they stiffen the membrane, maintain cell shape, and assist in cell motility.

课堂补充
老师强调，septin丝状结构的三大功能——作为扩散屏障、支架平台和膜刚性调节者——共同参与细胞空间区隔、信号精确传递与细胞极性、运动等生命过程的精细调控；此外，septin与肌动蛋白、微管的互作增强了这些功能的灵活性和多样性。 Class note: The teacher emphasized that the three major functions of septin filaments—as diffusion barriers, scaffolding platforms, and membrane rigidifiers—jointly contribute to spatial segregation, accurate signal transduction, cell polarity, and migration. Interactions with actin and microtubules further enhance the versatility and diversity of these functions.

总结Summary：

Septin丝状结构以扩散屏障、支架平台和膜刚性调节者三种方式，广泛参与细胞内部空间组织、信号传导与响应及细胞运动等关键生命功能。

Septin filaments, acting as diffusion barriers, scaffolding platforms, and membrane rigidifiers, play key roles in organizing cellular compartments, facilitating signal transduction and response, and enabling cell motility.

1
细胞分裂（胞质分裂） Cytokinesis

Septin参与收缩环的形成，与anillin和actomyosin共同作用，定位于细胞分裂沟部，稳定结构并促进细胞分裂正确进行。 Septins participate in the formation of the contractile ring, acting together with anillin and actomyosin, and localize at the cleavage furrow during cell division to stabilize the structure and ensure proper cytokinesis.

在胞质分裂末期，Septin与ESCRT复合体（I-II和III）协同，调控包膜断裂（ESCRT-mediated abscission），并与PIP₂/PI3P等脂质相互作用，实现细胞完全分裂。 At the late stage of cytokinesis, septins cooperate with the ESCRT complex (I-II and III) to regulate abscission, and interact with lipids such as PIP₂/PI3P to achieve complete cell division.

2
线粒体分裂 Mitochondrial Fission

Septin与线粒体分裂蛋白Drp1共同富集于线粒体分裂部位，并与actomyosin联动，参与线粒体收缩、分裂和遗传物质的均等分配。 Septins and mitochondrial fission protein Drp1 colocalize at the site of mitochondrial constriction and work with actomyosin to facilitate mitochondrial contraction, fission, and even distribution of mitochondrial DNA.

3
细胞迁移（极性和骨架重塑） Migration (Polarity and Cytoskeletal Remodeling)

Septin分布于细胞皮层、迁移前端和应力纤维（transverse arc & radial stress fibers、ventral stress fibers、branched actin）等结构，协同肌动蛋白网络调控细胞极性、前缘推进及迁移力的产生。 Septins distribute in the cortical region, at the leading edge, and at sites of transverse arc and radial stress fibers, ventral stress fibers, and branched actin, to coordinate with the actin network, regulating cell polarity, leading edge advancement, and generation of migratory forces.

课堂补充 Class Note

老师强调Septin作为细胞骨架成员，不仅本身组装成丝，而且通过与actin、microtubule及相关调控蛋白（如anillin、ESCRT复合体）的相互作用，广泛参与细胞分裂、器官elle分裂和细胞迁移等生命关键过程。 The teacher emphasized that septins, as cytoskeletal members, not only polymerize into filaments themselves but also interact with actin, microtubules, and regulatory proteins (such as anillin and the ESCRT complex) to participate extensively in key cellular processes like cytokinesis, organelle fission, and cell migration.

简要总结 Summary

Septin丝状结构通过组装及与蛋白、脂质的互作，实现细胞分裂、线粒体分裂和细胞迁移等多种生命活动的精准调控，是连接信号、细胞骨架和细胞膜功能的桥梁。

Septin filaments, by assembling and interacting with proteins and lipids, precisely regulate essential processes such as cytokinesis, mitochondrial fission, and cell migration, acting as a bridge integrating signaling, cytoskeletal functions, and membrane activities.

Septin丝状体的功能 Septin filament functions

感染 infection

Septin丝可围绕侵入细菌形成septin环，并与actin协作。 Septin filaments can form septin rings around invading bacteria, working together with actin.

这种结构把细菌包围，可能形成septin笼，限制细菌活动并促进自噬清除过程。 This structure encloses the bacteria, potentially forming a septin cage, restricting bacterial movement and promoting autophagic clearance.

图片中显示actin尾促进细菌移动，而septin环和笼提供物理阻隔，也作为信号参与自噬。 The image shows actin tails driving bacterial movement, while septin rings and cages provide physical barriers and also signal for autophagy.

神经元 neuron

Septin在线粒体微管（MTs）等处形成扩散屏障，尤其是在树突和树突棘部位。 Septins form diffusion barriers on microtubules (MTs), especially at dendrites and dendritic spines.

在树突棘，Septin作为结构分隔区，限制膜蛋白或信号分子的流动，维持神经元分区和功能特异性。 At dendritic spines, septins act as compartmental boundaries, restricting movement of membrane proteins or signaling molecules, maintaining neuron compartmentalization and functional specificity.

在MTs上，Septin 9能阻断kinesin-1（KIF5，携带轴突货物），而允许kinesin-3（KIF1A，携带树突货物）通过，实现货物运输的方向选择性。 On MTs, Septin 9 can block kinesin-1 (KIF5, which carries axon-destined cargo), but allows kinesin-3 (KIF1A, which carries dendrite-bound cargo) to pass, enabling directional cargo transport selectivity.

听课笔记摘要 Lecture Note Summary（中英对照）

1
Septin功能多样，是细胞骨架的关键组成部分，在感染防御和神经元内部分区中都发挥屏障和 scaffold 作用。 Septins have diverse functions, are key components of the cytoskeleton, and play barrier and scaffolding roles in both infection defense and neuronal compartmentalization.
2
在感染中，Septin能围捕病原体，有助于免疫清除（自噬）。 During infection, septins can entrap pathogens and facilitate immune clearance (autophagy).
3
在神经元中，Septin形成的扩散屏障维持信息的定向传递和神经元极性。 In neurons, diffusion barriers formed by septins maintain directional information transfer and neuronal polarity.
4
老师强调，Septin不仅能够与actin协作，还影响微管上的分子马达，实现特定方向的细胞内运输。 The teacher emphasized that septins can cooperate with actin and also influence molecular motors on microtubules, enabling specific directional intracellular transport.
Septin丝状体
疾病/癌症 Septin filaments: diseases/cancer
1
神经系统 Nervous System

Septin异常与多种神经系统疾病相关，如阿尔茨海默病、帕金森病、唐氏综合征、自闭症、双相障碍/精神分裂症、脑癌、癫痫等。Septin在树突棘形成、轴突分支和突触活动等中有关键作用。

Septin dysfunction is linked to several neurological diseases (Alzheimer’s, Parkinson’s, Down syndrome, Autism, bipolar disorder/schizophrenia, brain cancer, epilepsy, hereditary neuropathy). Septins are vital for dendritic spine formation, axon branching, and synaptic activity.

2
心血管系统 Cardiovascular System

Septin参与心肌细胞分化、血小板分泌以及造血干细胞生长。与白血病、Bernard-Soulier综合征和DiGeorge综合征等疾病有关。

Septins are involved in cardiomyocyte differentiation, platelet secretion, and hematopoietic stem cell growth. Diseases include leukemia, Bernard-Soulier syndrome, and DiGeorge syndrome.

3
免疫系统 Immune System

Septin影响T细胞成熟和迁移、巨噬细胞吞噬作用以及宿主对细菌的防御。与霍奇金淋巴瘤相关。

Septins affect T-cell maturation and migration, macrophage phagocytosis, and host defense against bacteria. Associated disease: Hodgkin’s lymphoma.

4
生殖系统 Reproductive System

Septin调控精子发生，异常可导致男性不育症、睾丸癌和卵巢癌。

Septins regulate sperm morphogenesis. Dysregulation can result in male infertility, testicular cancer, and ovarian cancer.

5
皮肤系统 Integumentary System

Septin相关于皮肤鳞状上皮的迁移和毛囊干细胞生长，异常可导致皮肤癌。

Septins play a role in squamous epithelial migration and hair follicle stem cell growth. Abnormalities may lead to skin cancer.

6
呼吸系统 Respiratory System

Septin调控上皮的通透性，异常与肺癌相关。

Septins regulate epithelial permeability; dysfunction is linked to lung cancer.

7
内分泌系统 Endocrine System

Septin影响胰岛素分泌，异常可引发胰腺癌。

Septins are involved in insulin secretion. Dysfunction is related to pancreatic cancer.

8
泌尿系统 Urinary System

Septin调控肾生成、肾上皮形态发生以及足细胞的葡萄糖摄取。异常可能导致肾癌和尿路上皮癌。

Septins regulate kidney ciliogenesis, renal epithelial morphogenesis, and glucose uptake by podocytes. Dysfunction can cause renal cell carcinoma and urothelial carcinomas.

9
消化系统 Digestive System

具体机制未知，但septin相关疾病包括结直肠癌、口腔癌、肝癌等。

Mechanisms are unclear, but septin-related diseases include colorectal, oral, and liver cancers.

10
疾病诊断标志物 Disease Biomarkers

Septin 9甲基化DNA是结直肠癌血液检测的生物标志物，Septin 4同工型是尿路上皮癌尿检的标志物。

Methylated Septin 9 DNA is used as a blood biomarker for colorectal cancer; Septin 4 isoform is a urinary marker for urothelial cancer.

注释 Notes:
1
PPT中不同疾病颜色标注代表septin影响的不同细胞生物过程（如：绿色-微管，蓝色-细胞膜，黄色-膜融合，橙色-凋亡等）。
2
老师强调septin串联了疾病与多系统癌变，是重要的细胞骨架与信号整合枢纽。

The PPT color codes represent the cell biological process influenced by septin. The instructor emphasized septins' central role in disease pathogenesis and cancer across multiple systems.

Septin丝状体/BORG与癌症 Septin filaments / BORG and cancer

细胞骨架动态互作 Cytoskeleton dynamic crosstalks ...

第一页
Septin丝状体/BORG与癌症 Septin filaments / BORG and cancer
1
癌相关成纤维细胞（CAF）通过分泌癌细胞可溶性因子促使普通成纤维细胞转化为CAF，从而促进癌细胞迁移（肿瘤微环境，TME）。 Cancer-associated fibroblasts (CAFs) are induced by cancer cell–derived soluble factors, promoting migration of cancer cells (tumor microenvironment, TME).
2
CAF中Borg2↑，导致Septin与肌动蛋白(Actin)重组，形成促进转移的肌动-肌球蛋白收缩。 Borg2 increases in CAFs, leading to reorganization of septins and actin, forming actomyosin contraction that enhances metastasis.
3
Borg过表达促进转移，Borg活化是Taxol类化疗药物（紫杉醇）敏感性的关键。 BORG overexpression promotes metastasis, and active BORG on actin is essential for the effectiveness of taxanes (e.g., paclitaxel).
4
Septin能结合在肌动蛋白丝（actin）和微管（MTs）上，与Cdc42等蛋白协作；以CAF为例，CAF中的Borg能促使Septins转移到微管上。 Septins bind both actin filaments and microtubules (MTs), cooperating with proteins like Cdc42; in CAFs, Borg promotes the recruitment of septins to MTs.
5
紫杉醇(taxanes)用于肿瘤化疗时，若CAF过多Borg表达，Septin会稳定微管动态，并导致药物耐受（保留微管动力与抗药性）。 When taxanes (like paclitaxel) are used for chemotherapy, excessive Borg in CAFs helps septins stabilize MTs dynamics and leads to drug resistance (preserved MT dynamics and taxol resistance)35.
总结
BORG过表达促进转移，CAF中活化的BORG作用于actin对紫杉醇疗效至关重要。 Summary: BORG overexpression is pro-metastatic, and activated BORG in CAFs is required for taxane treatment efficacy.
第二页
细胞骨架动态互作 Cytoskeleton dynamic crosstalks ...
1
上皮细胞中，细胞骨架（红：肌动蛋白/Septin，绿：微管，蓝：中间丝）高度有序，参与形成微绒毛、黏附连接和半桥粒等结构，支持上皮细胞的极性和稳态。 In epithelial cells, the cytoskeleton (red: actin/septins, green: microtubules, blue: intermediate filaments) is highly organized, forming microvilli, adherens junctions, and hemidesmosomes, ensuring cell polarity and homeostasis.
2
在间充质迁移细胞中，三类骨架丝缠绕分布，肌动蛋白主导细胞前缘（伪足、层伪足）并参与迁移；微管和中间丝在细胞内部形成支持网络。 In mesenchymal migrating cells, all three types of cytoskeletal filaments interweave; actin dominates the leading edge (filopodia, lamellipodium) for migration, while microtubules and intermediate filaments form supportive internal networks.
3
细胞骨架三者相互作用，调节细胞形态、迁移、细胞极性及细胞外基质连接，决定细胞在肿瘤微环境中的动态行为。 Crosstalk among the three cytoskeletal components regulates cell shape, migration, polarity, and extracellular matrix interactions, determining dynamic behaviors in the tumor microenvironment.
4
老师强调，septin不仅参与结构，也参与信号调控，并和actin/microtubule联合参与迁移和肿瘤进展。 The lecturer emphasized that septins are involved not only in structure but also in signaling, working with actin/microtubules in migration and cancer progression79.
重点提炼 Key Takeaways

癌相关成纤维细胞中的BORG与septin共同促进癌细胞迁移和化疗耐药。 BORG and septins in CAFs jointly promote cancer cell migration and chemoresistance.

细胞骨架三组分的高度动态协作构成了肿瘤细胞迁移与侵袭基础。 The dynamic interplay of the three cytoskeletal systems underlies tumor cell migration and invasion.

052025-10-16

肿瘤/新生物的定义 Definitions of Tumor/Neoplasm

1
肿瘤/新生物是一种组织的异常和过度生长。 A tumor/neoplasm is a type of abnormal and excessive growth of tissue.
2
“肿瘤”一词来自拉丁语，意为肿胀。 The word "tumor" comes from the Latin word for swelling.
肿瘤/新生物可以分为
Tumor/neoplasm can be:

良性（例如皮肤痣） Benign (ex: skin mole)

原位（潜在恶性，仍位于起始部位） In situ (potentially malignant, still in the place where it started)

恶性 = 癌症（肿瘤学关注重点），具有侵袭性 Malignant = cancer (focus of oncology), invasive

肿瘤进展阶段可分为
The progression of a tumor can be divided into:

原位（in situ）：肿瘤细胞未突破基底膜。 In situ: Tumor cells do not breach the basement membrane.

侵袭（invasive）：肿瘤细胞突破基底膜，向周围扩散。 Invasive: Tumor cells breach the basement membrane and spread locally.

循环（circulating）：肿瘤细胞（CTCs）进入血液循环，可为单个或簇状。 Circulating: Tumor cells (CTCs) enter the bloodstream, as singles or clusters.

微转移（micrometastasis）：肿瘤细胞通过血管到达其他组织的小聚集。 Micrometastasis: Small clusters of tumor cells arrive at distant tissues.

转移（metastasis）：形成继发性肿瘤，癌症完全侵入新的器官。 Metastasis: Full secondary tumor forms in a distant organ.

5
动图中展示了从原发肿瘤到转移癌的全过程，包括单个或簇状肿瘤细胞的血管内移行（Intravasation）、血流循环、血管外移（Extravasation）、微转移灶及最终继发肿瘤的形成。 The diagram shows the whole process from primary tumor to metastatic cancer, including intravasation as single cells or clusters, circulation, extravasation, micrometastatic foci, and finally establishment of secondary tumors.

肿瘤内异质性 Intratumoral heterogeneity

1
肿瘤内异质性指同一肿瘤内部细胞成分的多样性。 Intratumoral heterogeneity refers to the diversity of cellular components within a tumor.
2
原发位可包含不同类型的肿瘤细胞，如癌症干细胞（CSCs）和不同功能的癌细胞克隆。 At the primary site, there may be several cell types: cancer stem cells (CSCs) and functionally diverse cancer cell clones.
3
与肿瘤血管相关的环境复杂，提供营养并影响细胞表现。 The associated tumor vasculature is complex, supplying nutrients and impacting cellular behavior.
4
循环肿瘤细胞CTCs可通过血流到达二级部位，表现为单个或簇状。 Circulating tumor cells (CTCs) can migrate via the bloodstream as singles or clusters to secondary sites.
5
次级部位可出现休眠状态的播散肿瘤细胞DTCs，这些细胞可长期存在于如血管周围的微环境中。 Dormant disseminated tumor cells (DTCs) can persist at secondary sites, e.g., perivascular niches, for long periods.
6
转移（metastasis）指继发性肿瘤的建立，常见于脑、骨、肺和肝等器官。 Metastasis refers to the formation of secondary tumors, commonly in the brain, bone, lung, and liver.

肿瘤内异质性（复发和死亡原因） Intratumoral heterogeneity (Cause of recurrence and death)

1
癌症干细胞（CSCs）可能是肿瘤复发的原因之一。 Cancer stem cells (CSCs) are potential causes of tumor recurrence.
2
功能异质的癌细胞克隆也参与肿瘤复发或耐药性。 Functionally diverse cancer cell clones also contribute to recurrence or therapy resistance.
3
处于休眠状态的播散肿瘤细胞（DTCs）可能随时被激活，导致癌症复发。 Dormant DTCs at secondary sites may be reactivated, leading to recurrence.
4
肿瘤最致命的阶段是转移形成的继发性肿瘤，是癌症致死的主要原因。 The most lethal stage of cancer is the formation of metastatic secondary tumors, which are the main cause of cancer mortality.

肿瘤内异质性/肿瘤微环境（TME） Intratumoral heterogeneity / Tumor microenvironment (TME)

1
肿瘤微环境包括原发部位存在的不同细胞类型，如肿瘤相关成纤维细胞（CAFs）、肿瘤浸润免疫细胞等。 The tumor microenvironment includes various cell types at the primary site, such as cancer-associated fibroblasts (CAFs) and tumor-infiltrating immune cells.
2
不同功能的癌细胞克隆中，一些具有侵袭性，能促进进一步转移。 Within the functionally diverse cancer cell clones, some exhibit invasiveness, promoting further spread.
3
肿瘤微环境内的免疫细胞既可促瘤（免疫抑制），也可抗瘤（抗癌免疫） Immune cells in the tumor microenvironment may be protumoral (immunosuppressive) or antitumoral (anticancer immunity).
4
血流中存在循环肿瘤细胞（CTCs）和肿瘤教育血小板（TEPs），后者可支持肿瘤生存和转移。 The bloodstream contains circulating tumor cells (CTCs) and tumor-educated platelets (TEPs), the latter supporting tumor survival and metastasis.
5
休眠播散肿瘤细胞（DTCs）能在血管周围微环境（如骨髓等）长期存在。 Dormant DTCs can persist for long periods in perivascular niches (such as bone marrow and others).
6
二级转移灶的形成最终导致癌症的致死性进展。 Formation of secondary metastatic sites ultimately leads to a lethal progression of cancer.

与细胞周期相关的癌症标志 Hallmarks of cancer: related to cell cycle

1
癌症的多种标志特性直接和细胞周期紊乱相关，如维持增殖信号、逃避生长抑制、基因组不稳定和突变等。 Multiple cancer hallmarks are directly related to cell cycle dysregulation, such as sustaining proliferative signaling, evading growth suppressors, genome instability, and mutation.
其它与细胞周期相关的标志还包括
解锁表型可塑性、调节细胞代谢、抵抗细胞死亡、实现无限增殖潜能、诱导血管生成和激活侵袭与转移等。 Other cell cycle-related hallmarks include unlocking phenotypic plasticity, deregulating cellular metabolism, resisting cell death, achieving unlimited proliferative potential, inducing vasculature, and activating invasion and metastasis.

真核细胞周期的四个阶段 Usually 4 phases in eukaryotic cell cycle

真核细胞周期通常分为四个阶段
G1、S、G2 和 M。 The eukaryotic cell cycle is usually divided into four phases: G1, S, G2, and M.
2
其中，S 期是DNA复制，G2期为有丝分裂准备期，M期为有丝分裂，G1期为生长和准备。 S phase is for DNA replication, G2 phase is preparation for mitosis, M phase is mitosis, and G1 phase is for growth and preparation.
3
有丝分裂（M期）可进一步细分为前期（prophase）、前中期（prometaphase）、中期（metaphase）、后期（anaphase）和末期（telophase）。 Mitosis (M phase) can be further subdivided into prophase, prometaphase, metaphase, anaphase, and telophase.
4
有丝分裂前核膜破裂（NEBD），结束后细胞质分裂（cytokinesis）形成两个子细胞。 Nuclear envelope breakdown (NEBD) occurs before mitosis, and cytokinesis at the end produces two daughter cells.
5
G1期有“限制点/START”，细胞须通过以响应刺激进入复制程序。 The G1 phase has a "restriction point/START," through which the cell must pass in response to stimulation to initiate replication.
6
课上提到，在发育早期（如桑葚期，32细胞期）细胞快速分裂，DNA 复制和细胞分裂以产生身体各类细胞。 In class, it was mentioned that during early embryonic development (morula, 32 cell stage), cells divide rapidly, and DNA replication together with cell division gives rise to various body cells.

细胞周期在不同细胞类型中的变化

Cell cycle variation in different cell types

1
不同类型细胞的细胞周期长度和各个阶段比例不同。 The cell cycle length and phase proportion differ among different types of cells.
2
早期胚胎细胞（如桑葚胚）周期很短（大约1-11小时），无转录活动，仅包括S期和M期。 Early embryonic cells (such as morula) have very short cycles (about 1–11 hours), with no transcription and only S and M phases.
3
这些早期胚胎细胞是全能的，能够发育为身体的所有细胞。 These early embryonic cells are totipotent and can develop into all body cell types.
4
胚胎干细胞（如囊胚内细胞团）细胞周期为10-16小时，包括G1、S、G2和M期。 Embryonic stem cells (like inner cell mass of blastula) have cell cycles of 10–16 hours, with G1, S, G2, and M phases.
5
胚胎干细胞为多能性，可分化为多种体细胞类型。 Embryonic stem cells are pluripotent and can differentiate into many somatic cell types.
6
体细胞周期约为24小时，各阶段比例较为均衡。 Somatic cells have about a 24-hour cycle, with balanced phase distribution.
7
M期（有丝分裂期）约为1小时，G1期最长，体细胞可进入G0期（静息期）。 The M phase (mitosis) lasts about 1 hour; G1 is the longest. Somatic cells can enter G0 (quiescent) phase.
8
体细胞在特定刺激下可由G0重返细胞周期。 Somatic cells can re-enter the cycle from G0 upon specific stimulation.
9
后有丝分裂细胞（如神经元）永久退出细胞周期，G1后进入“∞”静止，无法再分裂。 Post-mitotic cells (like neurons) permanently exit the cycle after G1, staying in a non-dividing state ("infinity").

正常细胞的细胞周期控制系统

Cell cycle control system in normal cells

1
体细胞周期控制系统具有四个有序阶段（G1、S、G2、M）。 The somatic cell cycle control system has four orderly stages (G1, S, G2, M).
2
每个步骤都通过开/关二进制开关式严格控制，要么完全进行要么完全停止。 Each step is tightly governed by binary switches (on/off); it's either fully carried out or completely halted.
3
该系统非常稳定和可靠，保证细胞周期过程的完整性。 The system is remarkably robust and reliable, ensuring the integrity of the cell cycle.
4
同时也具备适应性，应对不利环境能做出调整。 It is also adaptable and can adjust in response to unfavorable conditions.
5
细胞可通过进入G0-静息期实现可逆性退出，以备将来重新进周期。 Cells are capable of reversible exit by entering G0-quiescence, allowing the option to re-enter the cycle.
6
另外，还有不可逆的永久退出，包括终末分化、衰老和细胞死亡。 There is also irreversible permanent exit, including terminal differentiation, senescence, and cell death.
7
老师补充指出桑葚期（约32细胞阶段）细胞非常快速分裂，旨在在短时间内大量产生胚胎各类细胞。 The teacher further explained that at the morula stage (around 32 cells), cells divide very rapidly to quickly produce all types of embryonic cells.
8
老师强调细胞周期在不同发育阶段和不同类型细胞间是不同的，并以神经元为例说明分化后通常不再分裂。 The teacher emphasized that the cell cycle varies across developmental stages and cell types, using neurons as an example of cells that typically stop dividing after differentiation.
细胞周期调控因子
CDK周期蛋白依赖性激酶

Cell cycle regulators: CDK cyclin-dependent kinases

1
细胞周期的进程由不同的CDK-周期蛋白复合物驱动，不同阶段对应不同组合。 The progression of the cell cycle is driven by distinct CDK-cyclin complexes, and different stages correspond to different combinations.
2
G1期由CDK4/6-周期蛋白D（Cyclin D）驱动。 G1 phase is driven by CDK4/6-Cyclin D.
3
G1/S期转换时，CDK2-周期蛋白E（Cyclin E）起主要作用。 The G1/S transition is mainly controlled by CDK2-Cyclin E.
4
S期（DNA复制）主要靠CDK2-周期蛋白A（Cyclin A）。 The S phase (DNA replication) mainly relies on CDK2-Cyclin A.
5
G2及有丝分裂期（M期）依赖CDK1-周期蛋白A/B（Cyclin A/B），尤其M期依赖CDK1-周期蛋白B。 The G2 and M phase depend on CDK1-Cyclin A/B, especially CDK1-Cyclin B for the M phase.
6
右图总结了四类主要周期蛋白在细胞周期各阶段的表达峰值和参与复合物。 The right diagram summarizes four major classes of cyclins, showing their expression peaks and corresponding complexes in each cell cycle phase.
7
细胞周期调控研究获2001年诺贝尔生理学或医学奖（Hartwell, Nurse, Hunt）。 Research on cell cycle regulation was awarded the 2001 Nobel Prize in Physiology or Medicine (Hartwell, Nurse, Hunt).
CDK的激活和抑制机制
激酶与磷酸酶调控
Regulation of CDKs
by kinases and phosphatases
1
CDKs的活性受激酶（Kinases）和磷酸酶（Phosphatases）的调控。 The activity of CDKs is regulated by kinases and phosphatases.
2
CDK7/MAT1/周期蛋白H（Cyclin H）（组成CAK复合物）可激活其他CDKs。 CDK7/MAT1/Cyclin H (forming the CAK complex) can activate other CDKs.
3
CDC25家族（A/B/C）为磷酸酶，去除抑制性的磷酸基以激活CDKs。 The CDC25 family (A/B/C) are phosphatases that remove inhibitory phosphates to activate CDKs.
4
Wee1为激酶，可抑制性地磷酸化CDK1，阻止其早期激活。 Wee1 is a kinase that adds inhibitory phosphates to CDK1, preventing premature activation.
5
不同CDK-周期蛋白复合物及调控蛋白协同保证细胞周期各阶段顺利衔接。 The coordination of various CDK-cyclin complexes and regulatory proteins ensures the smooth transition through cell cycle phases.
CDK的抑制机制
CDKI/CKI/CDI的作用

Inhibition of CDKs by CDKI/CKI/CDIs (cyclin-dependent kinase inhibitors)

1
CDK活性受一系列CKI（CDK抑制蛋白）负向调控。 CDK activity is negatively regulated by a range of CKIs (cyclin-dependent kinase inhibitors).
2
CIP/KIP家族成员（如p21^CIP1, p27^KIP1, p57^KIP2）主要抑制CDK2（与Cyclin A/E结合），也可作用于CDK1。 Members of the CIP/KIP family (such as p21^CIP1, p27^KIP1, p57^KIP2) mainly inhibit CDK2 (complexed with Cyclin A/E) and can also act on CDK1.
3
INK4家族成员（p16^INK4A，p15^INK4B，p18^INK4C，p19^INK4D）特异性抑制CDK4/6-周期蛋白D。 The INK4 family (p16^INK4A, p15^INK4B, p18^INK4C, p19^INK4D) specifically inhibit CDK4/6-Cyclin D complexes.
4
不同CKI的表达和活性在细胞周期不同时期受到精细调控，阻止异常增殖。 The expression and activity of various CKIs are tightly controlled at different cell cycle stages to prevent abnormal proliferation.
5
INK为CDK抑制因子（Inhibitor），CIP为CDK结合蛋白（CDK Interacting Protein），KIP为激酶抑制蛋白（Kinase Inhibitory Protein）。 INK stands for Inhibitor of CDK, CIP stands for CDK Interacting Protein, and KIP stands for Kinase Inhibitory Protein.

蛋白酶体调控细胞周期 The cell cycle is regulated by the proteasome

1
细胞周期的精确调控依赖于蛋白酶体介导的蛋白降解。 Precise regulation of the cell cycle depends on proteasome-mediated protein degradation.
2
靶蛋白首先被多聚泛素链标记，再被蛋白酶体降解为氨基酸。 Target proteins are first tagged with polyubiquitin chains and then degraded into amino acids by the proteasome.
3
泛素连接酶（E3）家族，包括APC/C（Cdc20及Cdh1亚型）和SCF（Skp2亚型），精确地识别并促进特定细胞周期蛋白的降解。 Ubiquitin ligase (E3) families, including APC/C (Cdc20 and Cdh1 subtypes) and SCF (Skp2 subtype), specifically recognize and promote degradation of key cell cycle proteins.
4
在G1期，APC/C-Cdh1活性高，介导G1期相关蛋白降解。 In G1 phase, APC/C-Cdh1 is highly active, mediating degradation of G1-phase related proteins.
5
SCF-Skp2在S期及G2早期起主导作用，促使进入S期的蛋白（如p27 KIP1）的降解。 SCF-Skp2 dominates in S phase and early G2, promoting degradation of proteins like p27 KIP1 to allow S phase entry.
6
在有丝分裂（M）期，由APC/C-Cdc20发挥作用，降解抑制分离的蛋白如securin，驱动姐妹染色单体分离。 During mitosis (M phase), APC/C-Cdc20 degrades inhibitors like securin, driving sister chromatid separation.
下方图示每个E3连接酶的典型活性时序
APC/C-Cdh1在G1活跃，SCF-Skp2于S-G2活跃，APC/C-Cdc20在M期活跃。 The activity timeline below shows: APC/C-Cdh1 is active in G1, SCF-Skp2 in S-G2, and APC/C-Cdc20 in M phase.
8
这种蛋白降解调控机制的研究获得了2004年诺贝尔化学奖（Ciechanover、Hershko和Rose）。 The study of ubiquitin-mediated degradation was awarded the 2004 Nobel Prize in Chemistry (Ciechanover, Hershko, and Rose).
9
老师强调，蛋白酶体的时空特异性降解对于确保周期关键点的单向推进和阶段衔接至关重要。 The instructor emphasized that spatial and temporal specificity of proteasome-mediated degradation is essential for unidirectional progression through cell cycle checkpoints and orderly phase transitions.
10
教师补充，蛋白酶体通过选择性降解调节不同类型细胞周期蛋白，如Cyclin D、Cyclin E、Cyclin A和Cyclin B，保证周期各阶段蛋白及时上升和清除。 The instructor added that the proteasome ensures timely rise and removal of different cyclins (such as Cyclin D, E, A, and B) by selective degradation, securing progression through each phase.
G1期限制点
对分裂的承诺 Restriction point in G1: commitment to division
1
G1期限制点是细胞决定是否进入细胞周期并进行分裂的关键检查点。 The restriction point in G1 is a critical checkpoint at which a cell commits to entering the cell cycle and division.
2
细胞需要外源性有丝分裂刺激因子（mitogen）及生长因子（growth factor）激活，才能驱动细胞周期继续进行。 Cells need activation by external mitogens and growth factors to drive further cell cycle progression.
3
有丝分裂刺激因子的信号确保细胞能够开始细胞周期，而生长因子则保障细胞有充足的营养摄取和利用能力。 Mitogen signaling ensures the cell can initiate the cycle, while growth factors ensure sufficient nutrient uptake and utilization.
4
细胞生长和增殖过程依赖于这两个信号的整合调控。 The process of cell growth and proliferation relies on the integrated regulation by these two signals.
5
G1限制点对应于细胞周期中对有丝分裂生长因子的敏感时期，决定是否进入S期（DNA复制期）。 The G1 restriction point corresponds to the period in the cell cycle when cells are responsive to mitogenic growth factors, deciding whether to enter S phase (the DNA replication phase).
6
细胞只有在通过G1期限制点后，才自主不受外部刺激地完成后续周期。 Only after passing the G1 restriction point can cells autonomously complete the rest of the cycle without further external stimuli.
7
细胞大小是进入S期的又一必要条件，过小的细胞会被阻止进入复制阶段。 Cell size is another requirement for S phase entry; cells that are too small are blocked from entering the replication stage.
8
生长缓慢的细胞在G1期停留较长时间，直到体积达标后才能“G1退出”进入S期；生长快的细胞更早通过G1限制点。 Slowly growing cells remain in G1 until they reach the adequate size (“G1 exit”); fast-growing cells reach the restriction point and exit G1 earlier.
9
细胞还需要通过DNA完整性检查，仅在DNA未受损害时才能继续分裂。 Cells must also pass a DNA integrity checkpoint and may only continue division if their DNA is undamaged.
10
如果DNA受损或不满足其他条件，细胞会在此“安检”被阻断，不能进S期。 If DNA is damaged or other requirements are not met, the cell is blocked at this “security check” and cannot proceed to S phase.
11
老师强调，G1期限制点决定了细胞分裂的“不可逆选择”，一旦通过就必须完成整个分裂周期。 The instructor emphasized that the G1 restriction point determines the “irreversible choice” for division: once passed, the cell is committed to completing the entire cycle.
12
教师补充，外界刺激（如生长因子）主要在G1早中期发挥作用，G1末期细胞已不再依赖这些信号。 The instructor added that external cues (like growth factors) primarily act in early/mid G1, while late G1 cells are no longer dependent on these signals.
13
此机制确保细胞具备合适的生长条件和基因组安全，防止异常或有损伤的细胞继续扩增。 This mechanism ensures cells have proper growth conditions and genomic integrity, preventing proliferation of abnormal or damaged cells.
突破限制点
细胞信号传导与转录调控

Progression through the restriction point – Cell signaling and transcriptional regulation

1
有丝分裂刺激因子和生长因子通过结合受体酪氨酸激酶（如EGFR、HER2）启动信号通路。 Mitogens and growth factors bind to receptor tyrosine kinases (such as EGFR and HER2) to activate signaling pathways.
2
这些受体激活后，一部分激活PI3K-AKT-mTORC1通路，促进细胞生长、蛋白合成和血管新生。 After receptor activation, the PI3K-AKT-mTORC1 pathway is stimulated to promote cell growth, protein synthesis, and angiogenesis.
3
另一方面，受体也激活Ras-Raf-MEK1/2-Erk1/2（MAPK）信号级联，诱导转录因子MYC的表达。 Meanwhile, these receptors also activate the Ras-Raf-MEK1/2-Erk1/2 (MAPK) cascade, leading to the expression of the transcription factor MYC.
4
MYC促进细胞周期蛋白D（Cyclin D）和CDK4/6激酶的表达，二者结合驱动G1期进展。 MYC stimulates the expression of Cyclin D and its kinase partner CDK4/6, whose complex drives G1 phase progression.
5
Cyclin D-CDK4/6复合物通过磷酸化Rb蛋白，使其失活，释放转录因子E2F。 The Cyclin D-CDK4/6 complex phosphorylates and inactivates Rb protein, releasing E2F transcription factor.
6
E2F释放后，启动与细胞周期相关蛋白（如Cyclin E、Orc1和Cyclin A）的转录，推动G1/S和S期进展。 Once E2F is released, it promotes transcription of cell cycle proteins (such as Cyclin E, Orc1, and Cyclin A) to drive G1/S and S phase progression.
7
CDK抑制蛋白（如p16、p21、p27）可阻断上述激酶活性，限制Rb的磷酸化，抑制E2F，从而阻止细胞通过限制点。 CDK inhibitors (such as p16, p21, p27) can block kinase activities above, prevent Rb phosphorylation, restrict E2F activity, and inhibit cell cycle progression through the restriction point.
8
没有外源刺激信号时，E2F处于抑制状态，细胞停留在静止期（G0或Qui）。 Without external stimulation, E2F remains repressed and the cell remains in a quiescent state (G0 or Qui).
DNA损伤阻止细胞周期
ATM/ATR和p53的作用

DNA damage prevents cell cycle: role of ATM/ATR, p53

1
DNA损伤（包括双链断裂、单链断裂、交联、复制错误等）通过细胞内多种修复机制处理（如HR、NHEJ、NER、MMR、BER）。 DNA damage (e.g., double-strand breaks, single-strand breaks, crosslinking, replication errors) is handled by multiple cellular repair mechanisms (such as HR, NHEJ, NER, MMR, BER).
2
DNA损伤会激活ATR（主要感应UV、复制错误）和ATM（主要感应电离辐射和双链断裂）激酶。 DNA damage activates ATR kinases (primarily sensing UV and replication errors) and ATM kinases (primarily sensing ionizing radiation or double-strand breaks).
3
ATR激活Chk1，ATM激活Chk2，二者均可抑制CDC25磷酸酶，防止Cyclin-CDK复合物的激活，停滞细胞周期。 ATR activates Chk1 and ATM activates Chk2, which both inhibit CDC25 phosphatase, blocking Cyclin-CDK complex activation and halting cell cycle progression.
4
在G1/S或G2检查点，细胞因CDK2-Cyclin E或CDK1-Cyclin B抑制而周期被阻断。 At the G1/S or G2 checkpoints, cells are blocked due to inhibition of CDK2-Cyclin E or CDK1-Cyclin B complexes.
5
损伤信号可激活p53，p53促使p21表达升高，从而抑制CDK活性并引发细胞周期停滞。 Damage signals can activate p53, which induces p21 expression, suppresses CDK activity, and triggers cell cycle arrest.
6
p53还可诱导细胞修复、衰老或凋亡，保证有损伤细胞不进入新一轮分裂。 p53 can also induce DNA repair, senescence, or apoptosis, ensuring damaged cells do not proceed to division.
7
细胞必须通过DNA损伤检查，只有当DNA完整时才能继续分裂，否则在“安检点”被安全阻断。 Cells must pass DNA damage checkpoints; only when DNA is intact can they proceed with division, otherwise, they are securely blocked at the "security check".

原癌基因/肿瘤抑制基因 Proto-oncogenes / tumor suppressor genes

1
原癌基因如EGFR、HER2、Ras、Myc、Akt、BRAF、c-Src等在癌症中异常激活，也可受病毒蛋白（如v-Src、E6、E7）影响。 Proto-oncogenes like EGFR, HER2, Ras, Myc, Akt, BRAF, c-Src, etc., are aberrantly activated in cancer, and may also be affected by viral proteins (such as v-Src, E6, E7).
2
肿瘤抑制基因（如Rb、p53、p16/INK4、PTEN、BRCA1等）在癌症中常常丢失功能。 Tumor suppressor genes (such as Rb, p53, p16/INK4, PTEN, BRCA1, etc.) are often lost or dysfunctional in cancer.
3
若原癌基因持续激活或抑癌基因功能丧失，细胞周期调控失控，细胞异常增殖导致肿瘤发生。 If proto-oncogenes are constantly activated or tumor suppressor genes are inactivated, cell cycle control fails, leading to uncontrolled proliferation and tumorigenesis.
4
癌症发生通常需要多步突变积累，表现为基因组不稳定和突变负荷（TMB）升高。 Carcinogenesis usually requires a multi-step accumulation of mutations, resulting in genomic instability and increased tumor mutational burden (TMB).
5
遗传性肿瘤患者天生携带一项突变，更容易通过积累新突变导致癌症发生。 Individuals with hereditary cancer carry a congenital mutation and are more susceptible to developing cancer via further mutation accumulation.

致瘤病毒Oncoviruses

1
多种病毒可导致肿瘤，包括：乳头瘤病毒（HPV）、乙型肝炎病毒（HBV）、丙型肝炎病毒（HCV）、EB病毒（Epstein-Barr, EBV）、人类免疫缺陷病毒（HIV）、人类T细胞白血病病毒（HTLV）。 Several viruses can contribute to tumor formation, including: Papillomavirus (HPV), Hepatitis B virus (HBV), Hepatitis C virus (HCV), Epstein-Barr virus (EBV), Human Immunodeficiency Virus (HIV), and Human T-lymphotropic virus (HTLV).
2
这些病毒可引发不同类型癌症，如宫颈癌（HPV）、肝癌（HBV/HCV）、淋巴瘤（EBV）、卡波西肉瘤（HIV）、白血病/淋巴瘤（HTLV）。 These viruses can cause different types of cancer, such as cervical cancer (HPV), liver cancer (HBV/HCV), lymphoma (EBV), Kaposi sarcoma (HIV), and leukemia/lymphoma (HTLV).
3
HPV是一种DNA病毒，和宫颈癌的发生密切相关。 HPV is a DNA virus closely associated with the development of cervical cancer.
4
在宿主细胞基因组中整合后的HPV，会表达两种重要致癌蛋白E6和E7。 After integrating into the host genome, HPV expresses two key oncogenic proteins: E6 and E7.
5
E6蛋白可与p53结合并促进其降解，阻断了p53介导的肿瘤抑制功能。 The E6 protein binds to p53 and promotes its degradation, thereby blocking p53-mediated tumor suppression.
老师补充强调
一旦p53功能被抑制，细胞就失去了通过p53引发细胞周期阻滞和凋亡的能力，即使DNA受损也能不受控增殖。 The instructor further emphasized: Once p53 is suppressed, the cell loses the ability to trigger cell cycle arrest and apoptosis via p53. This allows uncontrolled proliferation even when DNA is damaged.
7
E7蛋白则可与Rb蛋白结合，使Rb功能失活，导致Rb对E2F转录因子的抑制解除。 The E7 protein binds to Rb, leading to Rb inactivation and release of its inhibition over E2F transcription factors.
8
E2F释放后可以激活细胞周期相关基因，驱动细胞持续分裂和恶性转化。 When E2F is released, it activates cell cycle-related genes, driving continuous cell division and malignant transformation.
9
老师举例说明，HPV这两种蛋白的作用等同于“踩油门”和“失去刹车”，细胞既获得增殖信号又失去增殖控制，极易发生癌变。 The instructor used an analogy: the actions of these two HPV proteins are like "stepping on the accelerator" and "losing the brakes"—the cell receives proliferation signals and loses proliferation control, thus cancer can easily occur.
10
这一机制获得了2008年诺贝尔生理或医学奖的表彰（Harald zur Hausen）。 This mechanism was recognized with the Nobel Prize in Physiology or Medicine in 2008 (Harald zur Hausen).
录音补充
病毒还可影响其它关键抑癌蛋白，或通过持续激活原癌基因，协同促进细胞转化为肿瘤细胞。 Additional explanation from class recording: Viruses can also affect other key tumor suppressor proteins or constitutively activate proto-oncogenes, thereby synergistically promoting tumor cell transformation.

每个细胞周期仅有一次基因组复制 One genome replication per cell cycle

1
在G1期，授权系统（licensing system）处于激活状态，主要由cdt1介导，允许组装DNA复制起始复合物。 In G1 phase, the licensing system is active, mainly mediated by cdt1, allowing assembly of the DNA pre-replication complex.
2
授权系统的核心组分包括ORC（origin recognition complex, 起始识别复合物）、MCM（mini-chromosome maintenance, DNA解旋酶）以及相关装载酶。 Key components of the licensing system include ORC (origin recognition complex), MCM (mini-chromosome maintenance helicase), and associated helicase loaders.
3
在G1末，复制起始点获得“许可”，预复制复合物完成组装。 At the end of G1, replication origins are "licensed" and the pre-replication complex is fully assembled.
4
进入S期后，CDK（周期蛋白依赖性激酶）和geminin上调，抑制授权系统，防止起始点再次被授权，确保每条DNA仅复制一次。 Upon entry into S phase, CDKs and geminin are upregulated and inhibit the licensing system, preventing re-licensing and ensuring each DNA is replicated only once.
5
S期启动DNA复制，组装好的预复制复合物启动双链解旋，由MCM复合体进行。 During S phase, DNA replication initiates as the assembled pre-replication complex is activated, DNA unwinding is performed by the MCM helicase complex.
老师讲到
这种监管机制防止DNA重复复制，避免基因组不稳定，对细胞遗传物质完整性至关重要。 The instructor emphasized: This regulatory mechanism prevents re-replication of DNA, avoids genomic instability, and is critical for maintaining the integrity of genetic material in cells.
7
在细胞周期图中可见，G1期DNA含量为2N（每对染色体一条DNA链），S期DNA量逐步增加，G2期为2N、2倍DNA（每对染色体两条姊妹染色单体）。 On the cell cycle chart, DNA content is 2N during G1 phase (one DNA molecule per chromosome), increases during S phase, and reaches 2N with doubled DNA (two sister chromatids per chromosome) in G2 phase.
8
S期后的姊妹染色单体通过cohesin蛋白“粘连”在一起，直至有丝分裂时分离。 After S phase, sister chromatids are held together by the cohesin protein, remaining connected until separation during mitosis.
9
结论：严格控制的授权系统加上Cdk与geminin等因子的周期性调控，确保每个细胞周期只复制一次全基因组。 In conclusion: The tightly regulated licensing system, together with periodic actions of Cdk and geminin, ensures that the entire genome is replicated only once per cell cycle.

中心体在细胞周期中的一次复制 One centrosome duplication per cell cycle

1
每个细胞周期内，中心体只复制一次，保证细胞分裂时每个子细胞都能获得一个中心体。 Each centrosome duplicates only once per cell cycle, ensuring each daughter cell inherits one centrosome during cell division.
2
G1期时，细胞含有一个中心体，由母中心粒（蓝色）和子中心粒（粉色）组成，外围包裹着中心粒周物质（PCM）。 In G1 phase, the cell has one centrosome composed of one mother centriole (blue) and one daughter centriole (pink), surrounded by pericentriolar material (PCM).
3
S期开始时，母-子中心粒彼此分离，各自作为模板启动新中心粒形成，即“分离”阶段。 At the start of S phase, the mother and daughter centrioles separate, each serving as a template to initiate the formation of new centrioles—this is the "separation" step.
4
接着，新中心粒通过蛋白（如PLK4、STIL、SAS-6）的作用，完成生成与延长，即“起始/延伸”阶段。 New centrioles are then nucleated and elongated through proteins such as PLK4, STIL, and SAS-6 in the "nucleation/elongation" stage.
5
G2至M期，中心体成熟，微管组织能力增强，以便后续有丝分裂纺锤体形成。 From G2 to M phase, the centrosomes mature and gain enhanced microtubule organizing capacity in preparation for mitotic spindle formation.
关键调控
CDK1/CycB复合物在G2/M期抑制新的中心粒起始，防止过度复制。 Critical regulation: The CDK1/CycB complex during G2/M inhibits new centriole nucleation, preventing re-duplication.
老师补充
每轮细胞周期仅允许一次复制，失控会导致有丝分裂异常和基因组不稳定。 Instructor added: Only one round of centrosome duplication is allowed per cell cycle; if out of control, it causes mitosis defects and genomic instability.

每个细胞周期一次有丝分裂 One mitosis per cell cycle

1
有丝分裂（M期）在G2后启动，具体包括前期、前中期、中期、后期、末期，再通过胞质分裂结束。 Mitosis (M phase) begins after G2 and progresses through prophase, prometaphase, metaphase, anaphase, and telophase, followed by cytokinesis.
2
前期，父源和母源染色体各以2N（46条染色体）形式存在，染色体开始凝缩。 In prophase, chromosomes from both parents exist in a diploid 2N state (46 chromosomes), and chromosomes start condensing.
3
中期，所有染色体排列于赤道板上，准备分离。 In metaphase, all chromosomes align at the metaphase plate, ready for separation.
4
后期，姊妹染色单体被分开，各自拉向细胞两极，实现遗传物质均分。 In anaphase, sister chromatids are separated and pulled toward opposite cell poles, ensuring equal DNA partitioning.
5
末期，染色体去凝缩，核膜重建，完成核分裂。 In telophase, chromosomes decondense and the nuclear envelope reforms, completing nuclear division.
6
胞质分裂后，产生两个DNA含量、遗传信息完全一致的子细胞（2N, 1n，46条染色体）。 After cytokinesis, two daughter cells are produced with identical DNA content (diploid 2N, 1n, 46 chromosomes).
老师强调
有丝分裂过程中不进行转录，整个过程高度协调，确保染色体分离无误。 Instructor emphasized: There is no transcription during mitosis; the entire process is highly coordinated to ensure accurate chromosome segregation.
8
若分裂调控异常，易导致染色体丢失或数目异常，进而引起遗传不稳定相关疾病。 If regulation of division is abnormal, it may lead to chromosome loss or number abnormalities, causing genomically unstable diseases.

Cdk1-cyclin B在G2/M的激活：激酶/磷酸酶调控

Cdk1-cyclin B activation at G2/M: kinase/phosphatase regulation

1
Cdk1和Cyclin B在G2/M期形成复合物，是有丝分裂启动的关键调控器。 Cdk1 and Cyclin B form a complex at G2/M phase, which is the key regulator for mitosis initiation.
Cdk1的活性受两种磷酸化修饰调控
Y15位点发生抑制性磷酸化（被Wee1激酶添加），T161位点发生激活性磷酸化（被CAK激酶添加）。 Cdk1 activity is regulated by two phosphorylations: inhibitory phosphorylation at Y15 (added by Wee1 kinase), and activating phosphorylation at T161 (added by CAK kinase).
3
当Cdk1-Cyclin B同时被Y15和T161磷酸化时仍处于非活性状态，只有去除Y15位点的抑制性磷酸后才能激活。 When Cdk1-Cyclin B is phosphorylated at both Y15 and T161, it remains inactive; activation requires removal of the inhibitory phosphate at Y15.
4
Cdc25磷酸酶能去除Cdk1的Y15磷酸，使Cdk1-Cyclin B复合物活化，推动细胞进入有丝分裂。 Cdc25 phosphatase removes the phosphate group from Y15, activating the Cdk1-Cyclin B complex and promoting entry into mitosis.
5
活化的Cdk1-Cyclin B还能通过正反馈激活更多Cdc25，同时抑制Wee1，形成“快速一体化”反馈环，确保G2/M转换的彻底与不可逆。 Activated Cdk1-Cyclin B further stimulates more Cdc25 and inhibits Wee1 via positive feedback, creating a "switch-like" feedback loop that ensures the G2/M transition is complete and irreversible.
老师补充
这一激活过程像“开关”，只有达到全部条件后才能彻底翻转，保证细胞周期按顺序进行，不可逆转倒退。 Instructor’s note: This activation functions like a "binary switch"; only when all conditions are met does the system flip completely, ensuring the cell cycle progresses orderly and irreversibly.
7
结论：Cdk1-cyclin B复合物的激活通过激酶、磷酸酶及正反馈调控，实现细胞在G2/M间可靠、一致地进入有丝分裂。 Conclusion: Activation of the Cdk1-cyclin B complex is precisely controlled by kinases, phosphatases, and positive feedback, enabling a robust and coordinated transition into mitosis at G2/M.
前期
染色体凝缩 Prophase: Chromosomes Condensation

前期开始时，染色体逐渐凝缩，易于在有丝分裂过程中移动和分离。 At the beginning of prophase, chromosomes gradually condense to facilitate their movement and segregation during mitosis.

染色体凝缩主要由凝缩素I和II复合物（condensins I & II）介导，将染色质紧密折叠成紧凑结构。 Chromosome condensation is mainly mediated by condensin I and II complexes, which fold chromatin into a compact structure.

每条染色体由两条姊妹染色单体组成，二者通过着丝粒连接。 Each chromosome consists of two sister chromatids held together by the centromere.

免疫荧光图像中，DNA（红色）和微管（绿色）可以清晰分辨，显示染色体区域被微管网络环绕。 In the immunofluorescence image, DNA (red) and microtubules (green) are distinctly visible, showing the chromosomes surrounded by the microtubule network.

扫描电镜下可见高度凝缩的染色体，中央为着丝粒，两端为端粒。 Scanning electron microscopy reveals highly condensed chromosomes, with the central centromere and telomeres at each end.

老师强调
染色体的高度凝缩对后期精确分离至关重要，否则易导致染色体异常分配和基因组不稳定。 The instructor emphasized: The high level of chromosome condensation is critical for accurate separation in later mitotic stages; insufficient condensation can result in chromosome missegregation and genomic instability.
前中期
核膜破裂（NEBD）Prometaphase: nuclear envelope breakdown (NEBD)
1
在间期，细胞核被完整的核膜及核纤层包裹，染色体呈松散分布，微管和中心体位于细胞质中。 During interphase, the nucleus is tightly enclosed by the nuclear envelope and lamina, chromosomes are dispersed, and microtubules and centrosomes are in the cytoplasm.
2
进入前期后，染色体开始凝缩，但核膜结构仍存在。 As prophase begins, chromosomes start to condense, but the nuclear envelope remains intact.
3
到达前中期，核膜发生破裂（NEBD），为微管与染色体结合创造条件。 By prometaphase, the nuclear envelope breaks down (NEBD), allowing microtubules to interact with chromosomes.
4
CDK1激酶通过磷酸化核孔蛋白NUP98和核纤层蛋白Lamin A，导致核孔复合体与核纤层结构解体，引发核膜破裂。 CDK1 kinase phosphorylates nuclear pore protein NUP98 and nuclear lamina protein Lamin A, triggering the disassembly of the nuclear pore complex and lamina, which leads to nuclear envelope breakdown.
5
免疫荧光图像显示，Cyclin B（绿色）在间期主要分布于细胞质中，进入前中期后进入已破裂的核区域。 Immunofluorescence images show that Cyclin B (green) is cytoplasmic in interphase and moves into the former nuclear area after nuclear envelope breakdown in prometaphase.
老师补充
这一过程对于确保微管能够与染色体上的着丝粒正确结合、形成有丝分裂纺锤体、顺利完成染色体分离至关重要。 Instructor's note: This process is essential for enabling microtubules to properly attach to chromosome kinetochores, assemble the mitotic spindle, and ensure faithful segregation of chromosomes.
7
如果核膜破裂受阻，染色体无法与纺锤体微管连接，会导致有丝分裂障碍和遗传信息分配异常。 If nuclear envelope breakdown is inhibited, chromosomes cannot attach to spindle microtubules, resulting in mitotic defects and abnormal genetic material segregation.
有丝分裂中期
有丝分裂纺锤体及主轴组装检查点Metaphase: Mitotic Spindle & Spindle Assembly Checkpoint
1
在有丝分裂中期，所有复制后的染色体（姊妹染色单体）排列在细胞中央的赤道板上，形成有丝分裂纺锤体结构。 In metaphase, all replicated chromosomes (sister chromatids) align at the cell equator, assembling the mitotic spindle.
有丝分裂纺锤体由三类微管组成
星状微管（绿色，负责定位纺锤体）、动粒微管（蓝色，连接动粒）、插极微管（红色，来自两极并在中心重叠）。 The mitotic spindle consists of three types of microtubules: astral microtubules (green, for spindle positioning), kinetochore microtubules (blue, attach to kinetochores), and interpolar microtubules (red, overlap at the center from both poles).
3
动粒是染色体上的一个特殊蛋白结构，负责与微管末端结合，实现染色体与纺锤体的连接。 The kinetochore is a protein complex on the chromosome that mediates the attachment of chromosomes to the spindle by binding microtubule ends.
4
在每条染色体的动粒处，微管通过端对端的方式结合（end-on attachment），主要依赖Ndc80复合物。 At each chromosome kinetochore, microtubules attach in an end-on manner, mainly via the Ndc80 complex.
5
当姊妹染色单体的动粒分别被来自两个相对中心粒的微管拉引时，张力达到最大，实现稳定结合。 When kinetochores of sister chromatids are pulled by microtubules from opposite centrosomes, maximum tension is generated, indicating stable attachment.
6
老师强调，只有所有动粒都被正确且牢固地连接后，才能保证染色体分离的准确性，否则容易导致染色体错分或丢失。 The instructor emphasized that only when all kinetochores are correctly and stably attached, accurate chromosome segregation can be ensured; otherwise, missegregation or loss may occur.
7
纺锤体组装检查点（SAC）负责监控每个动粒与微管的结合状况。如果有未连接的动粒，MCC（有丝分裂检查点复合物）会抑制Cdc20，阻止APC/C的激活，细胞停留在中期，不能进入后期。 The spindle assembly checkpoint (SAC) monitors the attachment status of each kinetochore. If any kinetochore remains unattached, the MCC (Mitotic Checkpoint Complex) inhibits Cdc20, blocking APC/C activation and arresting the cell at metaphase, preventing transition to anaphase.
8
SAC的作用机制保证了只有在所有染色体都准备就绪时，细胞才会进入后期，进行姊妹染色单体的分离，防止了遗传信息分配错误。 The SAC ensures that only when all chromosomes are ready, the cell can proceed to anaphase, allowing sister chromatid separation and preventing genetic missegregation.
老师补充
纺锤体组装检查点就像安全闸门，是细胞对染色体分离安全的二次“安检”机制，也是抗癌药物开发的重要靶点。 The instructor added: The SAC acts like a safety gate, functioning as a secondary "security check" for chromosome segregation and is a major target for anti-cancer drug development.
有丝分裂后期
将姐妹染色单体拉向两极Anaphase: pull the chromatids to opposite ends
1
有丝分裂后期的核心事件是姐妹染色单体被拉向细胞两极，实现染色体的准确分离。 The key event of anaphase is the pulling of sister chromatids toward opposite cell poles, ensuring accurate chromosome segregation.
2
其分子机制依赖于APC/C-Cdc20的激活，促使Securin蛋白被泛素化并由蛋白酶体降解。 This process depends on the activation of APC/C-Cdc20, which leads to the ubiquitination and proteasomal degradation of securin.
3
Securin的降解释放了分离酶（separase），后者从抑制状态（有Securin结合）变为活性状态。 The degradation of securin releases separase, which shifts from its inhibited state (bound by securin) to an active state.
4
活性分离酶切割连接姐妹染色单体的cohesin复合物，使得姐妹染色单体可以被拉向两极。 Active separase cleaves the cohesin complex that links sister chromatids, allowing the chromatids to be pulled apart toward opposite poles.
动力机制分为两个阶段
The mechanical separation consists of two phases:

后期A（Anaphase A）：姐妹染色单体沿微管向两极移动，主要靠动粒微管缩短。 Anaphase A: Sister chromatids move toward the poles, mainly relying on the shortening of kinetochore microtubules.

后期B（Anaphase B）：两极之间的纺锤体结构本身延长，极体进一步分离。 Anaphase B: The spindle structure between poles elongates, causing the spindle poles to move further apart.

6
老师强调，APC/C的激活不仅促使Securin降解，还介导Cyclin B的降解，关闭CDK1活性，保证细胞顺利从有丝分裂后期进入末期。 The instructor highlighted that APC/C activation induces degradation of both securin and cyclin B, leading to CDK1 inactivation and ensuring proper progression from anaphase to telophase.
7
如果该过程受阻，如分离酶未被激活或cohesin未被切割，将导致染色体分离失败，最终引起染色体非整倍体等遗传异常。 If this process is impaired, such as separase not being activated or cohesin not being cleaved, chromosome separation fails, resulting in aneuploidy and other genetic abnormalities.
有丝分裂末期
恢复到间期状态与细胞质分裂Telophase: returning to an interphase state & Cytokinesis: splitting cytoplasm into 2 cells
1
有丝分裂末期，纺锤体解体，细胞进入恢复阶段。 During telophase, the mitotic spindle disassembles and the cell enters a recovery phase.
2
子染色体组定位于两极，新的核膜在每组染色体周围重新组装。 At each spindle pole, a set of daughter chromosomes is positioned and a new nuclear envelope reassembles around each group.
3
同时，核蛋白被重新导入核内，染色体解凝缩恢复到间期状态。 Meanwhile, nuclear proteins are imported into the nucleus and chromosomes undergo decondensation, returning to the interphase state.
4
随着染色质松散，转录活动也重新启动。 As chromatin relaxes, transcription activity resumes.
5
各种细胞器（如高尔基体、内质网、线粒体、内涵体等）在两个子细胞中对称分配，实现细胞组分的均等继承。 Organelles such as Golgi apparatus, endoplasmic reticulum, mitochondria, and endosomes are symmetrically inherited by the two daughter cells, ensuring equal partition of cellular components.
6
进入细胞质分裂阶段，一个由肌动蛋白和肌球蛋白组成的收缩环（actomyosin ring）在两组染色体之间形成。 During cytokinesis, a contractile ring composed of actin and myosin forms between the two groups of chromosomes.
7
该收缩环联结于细胞膜，由肌动蛋白、肌球蛋白和分隔蛋白（septin）共同组成，并依附于微管结构。 The contractile ring is connected to the plasma membrane and consists of actin, myosin, and septin, anchored to the microtubule structure.
8
收缩环逐渐收缩，将细胞质“掐腰”分隔，最终在中央形成中体（midbody），完成胞质分裂。 The ring contracts progressively, pinching the cytoplasm and forming a midbody at the center, resulting in the completion of cytokinesis.
9
细胞质分裂需要大量能量，主要依赖ATP驱动肌球蛋白运动，因此细胞分裂区富集线粒体以满足能量需求。 Cytokinesis requires substantial energy, mainly depending on ATP-driven myosin movement, with mitochondria accumulating in the division zone to meet this demand.
10
免疫荧光和活细胞成像显示，肌球蛋白/肌动蛋白网状结构在细胞分裂最终阶段定位于细胞中央，驱动最终裂解。 Immunofluorescence and live-cell imaging show that the myosin/actin network is centrally localized in the final stage of cell division, driving the final cleavage.
11
老师强调，细胞器与细胞骨架的正确配合、胞质的对称分裂和染色体的均匀分配，对新生子细胞的正常功能至关重要。 The instructor emphasized that correct coordination of organelles, cytoskeleton, symmetrical cytoplasm division, and equal distribution of chromosomes is essential for the proper function of daughter cells.
细胞周期检查点
防止遗传错误The cell cycle checkpoints: prevent genetic errors
早期G1期
防止过早重新进入有丝分裂Early G1: preventing precocious re-entry into mitosis
1
细胞周期中存在多个检查点，能在关键步骤检测和防止基因组错误。 There are multiple checkpoints in the cell cycle that monitor and prevent genomic errors at critical steps.
2
在DNA损伤时，ATM激酶被激活，依次激活CHK2，进而激活p53，p53促进p21表达，抑制CDK2/Cyclin E/A复合物，阻止细胞周期进入S期。 When DNA damage occurs, ATM kinase is activated, which in turn activates CHK2, then p53. p53 promotes p21 expression, which inhibits the CDK2/Cyclin E/A complex, blocking cell cycle progression into S phase.
3
在DNA复制压力下，ATR被激活，进一步激活CHK1和WEE1，WEE1抑制CDK2，阻断S期进展。 Under replication stress, ATR is activated, subsequently activating CHK1 and WEE1. WEE1 inhibits CDK2, halting S phase progression.
4
G2/M期检查中，DNA损伤同样激活ATM-CHK2通路，激活CDC25被抑制，导致CDK1/Cyclin B不能激活，细胞不能进入有丝分裂。 At the G2/M checkpoint, DNA damage also triggers the ATM-CHK2 pathway, suppressing CDC25. This prevents activation of CDK1/Cyclin B, so the cell cannot enter mitosis.
5
纺锤体组装检查点（SAC）由Aurora B激酶和MCC复合物监测动粒附着情况，未正确结合时抑制APC/C，防止提前进入后期，保证染色体稳定分离。 The spindle assembly checkpoint (SAC) is monitored by Aurora B kinase and the MCC complex, which check kinetochore attachment. If improper, they inhibit APC/C, preventing premature anaphase onset and ensuring faithful chromosome segregation.
6
检查点机制保证在关键分裂阶段，先检测、再决定细胞周期能否继续，防止携带遗传错误的细胞继续分裂。 These checkpoint mechanisms ensure that at each critical cell division phase, cells are evaluated before continuing, preventing propagation of cells with genetic errors.
7
在有丝分裂末期（后期），APC/C与Cdc20结合，泛素化Cyclin B和其它底物，促使其降解，终止有丝分裂。 During late mitosis (anaphase), APC/C associates with Cdc20, ubiquitinating Cyclin B and other substrates, promoting their degradation and mitotic exit.
8
随后APC/C与Cdh1结合，保持持续的Cyclin B降解，防止Cyclin B/CDK1复合物再次激活，防止细胞在早期G1期过早进入下一个有丝分裂周期。 Subsequently, APC/C binds Cdh1, maintaining Cyclin B degradation, preventing reactivation of the Cyclin B/CDK1 complex and avoiding premature entry into a new mitosis during early G1 phase.
9
Cdc20-APC/C与Cdh1-APC/C的切换实现了从有丝分裂向G1期的平稳过渡，确保Cyclin B在细胞周期结束时彻底清除。 The switch from Cdc20-APC/C to Cdh1-APC/C ensures a smooth transition from mitosis to G1 phase, guaranteeing complete Cyclin B clearance at the close of the cell cycle.
10
这一机制保证细胞在DNA复制与有丝分裂的每一步都被严格监控，减少癌变与遗传异常风险。 This mechanism ensures every step of DNA replication and mitosis is rigorously monitored, minimizing the risk of cancer and genetic abnormalities.
11
老师强调，检查点蛋白的缺陷或失活常导致基因组不稳定，是肿瘤发生的重要基础，也为抗癌药物开发提供靶点。 The instructor emphasized that defects or loss of checkpoint proteins often result in genomic instability, forming a basis for tumorigenesis and providing targets for cancer therapy development.

TGF-β2在G1期抑制提前进入新细胞周期TGF-β2 in G1: preventing a new precocious cycle

&

纺锤体定向与组织结构Spindle orientation and tissue organization

1
TGF-β2在G1期抑制细胞周期推进

TGF-β2 inhibits cell cycle progression during G1 phase

TGF-β2通过受体激活SMAD通路，启动下游转录调控。 TGF-β2 activates SMAD pathway via its receptor, initiating downstream transcriptional control.

激活后促进CDK抑制蛋白p15和p21的表达，同时抑制CDC25A的活性。 After activation, expression of CDK inhibitors p15 and p21 increases, while CDC25A activity is inhibited.

p15和p21作为CDK抑制因子（CDKI），分别抑制G1期的CDK4/Cyclin D3复合物以及G1/S期的CDK2/Cyclin E2复合物。 p15 and p21, as CDK inhibitors (CDKI), inhibit the G1 phase CDK4/Cyclin D3 complex and the G1/S phase CDK2/Cyclin E2 complex, respectively.

CDC25A的抑制阻止了CDK活性的去磷酸化，进一步抑制细胞周期关键激酶的激活。 Inhibition of CDC25A blocks dephosphorylation and thus activation of key CDKs.

上述机制使Rb蛋白保持未完全磷酸化状态，E2F转录因子被抑制，无法激活S期相关基因表达。 This keeps Rb protein in a hypophosphorylated state, inhibiting E2F transcription factor and preventing S phase gene activation.

结果：细胞阻滞在G1期，不能进入G1/S转折点，防止过早进入新一轮细胞周期。 As a result, the cell is arrested in G1 and cannot pass the G1/S checkpoint, preventing premature entry into a new cell cycle.

老师强调，TGF-β2对细胞周期的负调控保证了细胞不会在条件不适宜的情况下分裂，是抑癌的重要机制之一。 The lecturer emphasized that negative regulation of the cell cycle by TGF-β2 ensures cells do not divide under inappropriate conditions, serving as a crucial tumor-suppressive mechanism.

2
纺锤体定向与组织结构

Spindle orientation and tissue organization

细胞分裂时纺锤体定向决定了子细胞的空间配置。 During cell division, spindle orientation determines the spatial arrangement of daughter cells.

水平定向分裂有助于维持单层上皮的连续性。 Horizontal orientation of division helps maintain a simple epithelial layer.

垂直定向分裂则促使细胞层次增加，实现组织的层化。 Vertical orientation leads to stratification and the formation of multiple cell layers.

在血管生成或新生血管内皮分化过程中，纺锤体定向影响未来“顶端细胞”和“柄细胞”的分布。 During angiogenesis or endothelial differentiation, spindle orientation affects the allocation of future tip cells and stalk cells.

教师指出，纺锤体定向通过调控细胞归属和空间分布，对于发育、组织稳态和再生过程至关重要。 The instructor noted that spindle orientation, by controlling cell fate and spatial positioning, is vital for development, tissue homeostasis, and regeneration.

不对称分裂
两种不同子细胞的产生（以干细胞为例）Asymmetric division: two different daughter cells (Example of stem cells)
1
不对称分裂指的是一个母细胞分裂后产生了两种不同命运的子细胞，一种保持干性，另一种分化。 Asymmetric division refers to a mother cell dividing into two daughter cells with different fates, one retaining stemness and the other differentiating.
2
这种分裂方式在干细胞中非常重要，因为它保证了一部分子细胞继续作为干细胞存在，而另一部分进行分化，满足组织修复和再生需求。 This division mode is crucial in stem cells, ensuring one daughter cell remains a stem cell while the other differentiates, supporting tissue repair and regeneration.
3
不对称分裂可分为外在不对称和内在不对称两种机制： Asymmetric division can be divided into extrinsic and intrinsic mechanisms:
4
外在不对称依赖于细胞周围环境，如与基质或邻居细胞的接触（ECM contact、细胞间接触）以及化学或机械梯度。 Extrinsic asymmetry depends on the microenvironment, such as extracellular matrix contact, cell-cell junctions, and chemical or mechanical gradients.
5
这些外部信号影响纺锤体的位置和细胞极性的建立，导致两个子细胞接收到不同的分化信号。 These external cues affect spindle positioning and cell polarity, causing the two daughter cells to receive different differentiation signals.
6
内在不对称是指细胞内部分化决定子因子的极化分布。例如，在分裂时，将某些命运决定因子只分给其中一个子细胞。 Intrinsic asymmetry refers to the polarized distribution of cell fate determinants within a cell, so that upon division, specific factors are inherited by only one daughter cell.
7
这样，拥有命运决定因子的子细胞会启动分化程序，而没有的那个保持为干细胞。 In this way, the daughter cell with fate determinants initiates differentiation, while the other retains stemness.
8
下图还显示了干细胞“niche（生态位）”和外部信号（cues）在维持干细胞与分化细胞平衡中的作用。 The diagram also shows the roles of the stem cell niche and external cues in balancing stem cells and differentiated cells.
9
老师强调，这种不对称分裂是组织发育、器官稳态和再生的基础。 The instructor emphasized that asymmetric division is fundamental for tissue development, organ homeostasis, and regeneration.
10
在干细胞分裂时，决定干性或分化的因素（asymmetry factors）会极化分布，确保命运分配的精确性。 During stem cell division, asymmetry factors become polarized to ensure precise fate allocation among the daughter cells.
11
实验和成像结果表明，分裂过程中纺锤体的定向、基底膜接触、化学梯度和极性蛋白的分布共同决定了子细胞的命运。 Experiments and imaging show that spindle orientation, basement membrane contact, chemical gradients, and polarity protein localization together determine the fate of daughter cells during division.
12
总体来说，不对称分裂保证了干细胞数量的维持，并让组织能够根据需要产生新的分化细胞。 In summary, asymmetric division maintains the stem cell pool while allowing the tissue to generate new differentiated cells as needed.
有丝分裂缺陷
非整倍体
Mitotic defects
aneuploidy
1
有丝分裂过程中如发生染色体异常分离，会导致细胞出现非整倍体（aneuploidy，染色体数目异常）或者四倍体（tetraploidy）。 In mitosis, abnormal chromosome segregation can lead to aneuploidy (abnormal chromosome number) or tetraploidy (4N).
2
异常染色体分离（Abnormal chromosome segregation）时，细胞周期检查点会被激活，部分细胞因此停滞并走向凋亡（Mitotic cell death）。 When abnormal chromosome segregation occurs, checkpoint arrest is triggered and some cells undergo mitotic cell death.
3
若细胞逃逸检查点，便可能产生2N+1或2N-1的非整倍体细胞，或发生4N的四倍体细胞。 If cells escape the checkpoint, they may become aneuploid (2N+1 or 2N-1) or tetraploid (4N).
4
具有异常基因组（Abnormal genome）状态的细胞可能发生三种结局： Cells with abnormal genome may experience three outcomes:

**细胞死亡（Cell death）：**遗传物质异常严重的细胞会被清除。 Cell death: Cells with severely abnormal genomes are eliminated.

**细胞衰老（Senescence）：**部分异常细胞停止分裂，进入衰老状态。 Senescence: Some abnormal cells stop dividing and enter a senescent state.

**异常增殖/肿瘤发生（Proliferation/Cancer）：**如果细胞缺失p53通路等抑癌机制，仍有可能异常增殖，最终导致癌症。 Abnormal proliferation/cancer: If tumor suppressor mechanisms like p53 are lost, cells may continue to proliferate abnormally, eventually leading to cancer.

下方展示了人乳腺细胞系的核型对比
The panel below shows the karyotype comparison of human breast cell lines:

**HMEC（正常细胞）：**染色体数目和结构规则。 HMEC (normal): Normal chromosome number and structure.

**MCF-7（癌细胞）：**存在明显的染色体数目和结构异常，体现出非整倍体和基因组不稳定。 MCF-7 (cancer): Noticeable chromosome number and structure abnormalities, characteristic of aneuploidy and genomic instability.

总结
有丝分裂缺陷可导致非整倍体、四倍体等基因组不稳定状态，细胞命运依赖于基因组完整性检查及抑癌机制。 Summary: Mitotic defects can cause aneuploidy and tetraploidy, resulting in genomic instability. The ultimate cell fate depends on genome integrity checkpoints and tumor suppressor mechanisms.
癌症药物与细胞周期
近期临床试验的失败

Cancer drugs and cell cycle: failure in recent clinical trials

1
许多获批的化疗药物靶向细胞周期的不同时期，但最近的临床试验显示疗效有限。 Many approved chemotherapeutic drugs target different phases of the cell cycle, but recent clinical trials show limited efficacy.
2
药物主要类别包括抗代谢物、拓扑异构酶抑制剂、抗肿瘤抗生素、微管抑制剂（抗有丝分裂药物）和非特异性DNA损伤药物。 The main categories include antimetabolites, topoisomerase inhibitors, antitumor antibiotics, microtubule inhibitors (anti-mitotic agents), and nonspecific DNA-damaging agents.
不同药物作用于细胞周期的不同阶段
Different drugs act at different cell cycle phases:
S期
抗代谢物（如5-氟尿嘧啶）抑制DNA和RNA合成。 S phase: Antimetabolites (e.g., 5-Fluorouracil) inhibit DNA and RNA synthesis.
G2期
拓扑异构酶抑制剂（如依托泊苷）在DNA错误检查及分裂前起作用。 G2 phase: Topoisomerase inhibitors (e.g., Etoposide) act during DNA error checkpoint and before mitosis.
M期
微管抑制剂（如紫杉烷、长春碱类）阻断纺锤体组装，抑制有丝分裂。 M phase: Microtubule inhibitors (e.g., Taxanes, Vinca alkaloids) block spindle assembly and inhibit mitosis.
C期
非特异性DNA损伤药物（如顺铂）在整个细胞分裂期间造成DNA损伤。 C phase: Nonspecific DNA-damaging agents (e.g., Cisplatin) damage DNA throughout cell division.
4
