这些药物虽可暂时抑制肿瘤生长，但存在副作用大、肿瘤易复发等问题。 Although these drugs can temporarily suppress tumor growth, they have significant side effects and high recurrence rates.
5
近期尝试开发各类有丝分裂激酶和细胞周期蛋白激酶（如CDK、CHK1、PLKs、Aurora激酶、KIFs等）抑制剂，但在临床试验多被证实无效。 Recent attempts to develop various mitotic kinase and cyclin-dependent kinase (CDK, CHK1, PLKs, Aurora kinases, KIFs, etc.) inhibitors have mostly proven ineffective in clinical trials.
课上强调
抗肿瘤药物阻断细胞周期虽有机制基础，但实际效果往往因副作用、耐药、肿瘤异质性等难题而有限。 In class, it was emphasized that although blocking the cell cycle has a mechanistic rationale, the actual effects are often limited by side effects, resistance, and tumor heterogeneity.
7
有丝分裂激酶抑制剂在多个临床阶段“卡壳”，尚未转化为有效药物。 Mitotic kinase inhibitors have stalled at various clinical trial stages and have not been translated into effective drugs.
8
因此，尽管已获批药物在一定条件下有效，但癌症的复杂性导致疗效受限，需不断寻找新靶点和新思路。 Therefore, although approved drugs are effective under certain conditions, the complexity of cancer limits their efficacy, necessitating continuous search for new targets and strategies.

癌细胞增殖速率可低于正常组织Proliferation of cancer cells can be lower than normal tissues

G0/静止期
细胞周期的可逆性停滞G0 / quiescence: reversible arrest of cell cycle
1
癌细胞的分裂速率实际可能低于某些正常组织。 The proliferation rate of cancer cells can actually be lower than that of some normal tissues.
2
课上老师强调，肿瘤的平均倍增时间约为100天，从一个细胞长成1毫米需要6年，长到1厘米需10年。 The instructor emphasized in class that the average doubling time for a tumor is about 100 days; it takes 6 years to grow from one cell to 1 mm and 10 years to 1 cm.
3
癌细胞缓慢分裂的原因之一是其内部和外部环境的多样性和复杂性。 One reason for slow division in cancer cells is the diversity and complexity of their internal and external microenvironments.
4
对比之下，许多正常组织细胞分裂速度非常快。例如： In contrast, many normal tissue cells divide very rapidly. For example:

小肠上皮细胞每2-3天分裂一次； Small intestine epithelial cells divide every 2-3 days;

肺泡细胞每8天分裂一次； Alveolar (lung) cells divide every 8 days;

血小板寿命约为10天； Platelets last about 10 days;

表皮细胞10-30天分裂； Epidermal cells divide every 10-30 days;

肝细胞大约每半年分裂一次； Hepatocytes divide about every half year;

脂肪细胞8年； Fat cells: 8 years;

神经元通常终身不再分裂。 Neurons seldom (or never) divide during a lifetime.

5
抗癌药物往往主要伤及分裂快的组织而非只针对肿瘤，因此副作用大、治疗效率低。 Anticancer drugs often mainly harm rapidly dividing tissues rather than targeting only tumors, leading to high side effects and low therapeutic efficiency.
6
G0/静止期细胞是指以可逆性方式停滞于细胞周期外的细胞，具有保护细胞的主动机制。 Cells in G0/quiescence state are reversibly arrested outside the cell cycle and have active protective mechanisms.
7
成人干细胞（造血、肌肉、神经、毛囊等）及分化细胞（如成纤维细胞、淋巴细胞、肝细胞）可长期保持静止状态，必要时可重新启动细胞周期参与修复或免疫等过程。 Adult stem cells (hematopoietic, muscle, neural, hair) and differentiated cells (e.g., fibrocytes, lymphocytes, hepatocytes) can remain in quiescence long-term and reenter the cell cycle when needed for repair or immune response.
8
静止状态对于组织长期维护和再生至关重要。 The quiescent state is essential for long-term tissue maintenance and regeneration.
9
实验上可通过去血清、失去基质粘附或高密度接触抑制等方式诱导细胞进入G1期静止。 In vitro, quiescence can be induced from G1 phase by serum starvation, loss of adhesion to extracellular matrix, or confluence (contact inhibition).
10
分子调控方面，p21、p27、p16等CDK抑制因子上调，抑制CDK4/6-细胞周期蛋白D和CDK2-细胞周期蛋白E，保持Rb未磷酸化，E2F被抑制，细胞周期停滞于G0。 At the molecular level, CDK inhibitors such as p21, p27, and p16 are upregulated, which inhibit CDK4/6–Cyclin D and CDK2–Cyclin E complexes, keep Rb unphosphorylated, E2F is inhibited, and the cell cycle is arrested in G0.
11
静止细胞表现为Ki-67阴性，是实验和临床上判断细胞不分裂状态的常用标记。 Quiescent cells are Ki-67 negative, which is a marker commonly used in experiments and clinics to identify non-dividing cells.
总结
抗肿瘤治疗面临的根本难题之一，是由于癌细胞分裂速度不高，药物易伤及正常快速分裂细胞，导致常见的副作用。保持静止期和调控细胞周期是机体长期组织健康的基础。 In summary, a key challenge in cancer therapy is the relatively low proliferation rate of cancer cells, making it easy for drugs to affect rapidly dividing healthy tissues and cause side effects. Maintenance of quiescence and cell cycle control is fundamental for long-term tissue health.

G0静止细胞的特征Properties of G0 quiescent cells

1
静止期细胞的转录、翻译、代谢、能量消耗和活性氧水平都很低。 G0 quiescent cells display low transcription, translation, metabolism, energy consumption, and reactive oxygen species (ROS) levels.
2
这些细胞没有增殖、没有凋亡，也没有进入衰老状态。 These cells do not undergo proliferation, apoptosis, or senescence.
3
静止细胞为Ki-67阴性，临床和实验上可用来标记细胞处于非分裂状态。 Quiescent cells are Ki-67 negative, a marker widely used in clinics and experiments to identify non-dividing cells.
4
静止细胞不进行DNA复制，但保持DNA甲基化状态。 These cells do not perform DNA replication, but maintain DNA methylation.
5
静止细胞具有特殊组蛋白，有助于保持着丝粒的稳定，并限制DNA损伤。 Quiescent cells have special histones that help maintain centromere integrity and limit DNA damage.
6
静止细胞溶酶体活性高，自噬水平高，有助于细胞自我保护和成分更新。 They possess high lysosomal activity and autophagy, supporting self-protection and component renewal.
7
细胞表面存在初级纤毛等感应结构，能够感受机械和化学信号，作为信号中枢。 Sensory structures such as the primary cilium detect mechanical and chemical stimuli, acting as a signaling hub.
8
核孔大小降低，有助于分隔细胞核与胞质的进出物质，保护基因组稳定性。 Nuclear pore size is reduced, which helps control nuclear-cytoplasmic exchange and protects genome stability.
9
静止细胞保持低水平的转录与翻译活动，也不会分化、增殖或走向细胞死亡。 Quiescent cells maintain a low level of transcription and translation, and do not differentiate, proliferate, or die.
10
当环境条件允许或机体需要修复、再生时，静止细胞可随时重新进入细胞周期。 When environmental conditions improve or tissue repair/regeneration is required, quiescent cells are ready to re-enter the cell cycle.
课上补充
老师强调，静止状态是一种细胞主动保护机制，减少DNA损伤和能源消耗，对长期组织健康和稳态至关重要。 Additional from class: The instructor emphasized that quiescence is an active cell protection mechanism reducing DNA damage and energy use, which is essential for long-term tissue health and homeostasis.

肿瘤休眠与复发 Cancer dormancy and recurrence

1
肿瘤休眠是肿瘤细胞增殖与凋亡保持动态平衡的状态，肿瘤体积不变或缓慢增长。 Tumor dormancy is a state with balanced proliferation and apoptosis of tumor cells, where tumor mass remains stable or grows slowly.
2
癌症干细胞（CSC）或休眠转移性肿瘤细胞（DTC）可长期处于G0静止期，表现为缓慢分裂或完全不分裂。 Cancer stem cells (CSC) or dormant disseminated tumor cells (DTC) can remain in G0/quiescence for a long time, characterized by slow or absent cell division.
3
癌细胞进入G0静止期有助于它们抵抗不良环境与治疗压力，并逃避免疫清除。 Entry of cancer cells into G0 promotes survival under harsh environments and therapeutic pressure, and helps evade immune attack.
4
如果这些休眠的癌细胞重新进入细胞周期，就有可能在体内形成新的肿瘤灶，导致疾病复发。 If these dormant cancer cells re-enter the cell cycle, they may seed new tumor formation and cause disease relapse.
5
骨髓、肺、脑、肝、肾等血管周围微环境（Perivascular niche）能够维持干细胞的静止状态，并为休眠DTC提供庇护，是未来继发性肿瘤（微转移灶）的重要根源。 Perivascular niches in bone marrow, lungs, brain, liver, and kidney maintain the quiescence of stem cells and dormancy of DTCs, serving as reservoirs for potential future secondary tumors (micrometastases).
典型进程
肿瘤细胞早期播散到异位器官（早期DTC），在原位及转移部位长期静止，治疗后可能因外部刺激或环境变化失去静止，导致肿瘤复发。 Typical process: tumor cells disseminate early to distant organs (early DTCs), stay dormant for extended periods, and may lose dormancy after treatment, triggering tumor relapse.
7
老师强调，很多患者复发的根本原因是残留的静止DTC重新增殖，而非原发肿瘤未清除。 The instructor stressed that relapse in many patients is mainly due to residual dormant DTCs resuming proliferation, not corresponding to incomplete removal of the primary tumor.

与DTC相关的潜在治疗策略 Possible therapeutic strategies related to DTC

“锁定”策略
保持休眠状态，阻止DTC被激活，如乳腺癌患者终身服用他莫昔芬。 "Lock-in" strategy: Maintain DTCs in quiescence to prevent their activation, e.g., lifetime tamoxifen therapy for breast cancer patients.
“唤醒-杀伤”策略
促使DTC脱离休眠、重新进入细胞周期，再用常规细胞毒药物将其杀死。 "Lock-out/awakening" strategy: Induce DTCs to exit dormancy and reenter the cell cycle, then kill them by conventional cytotoxic therapies.
“消除休眠细胞”策略
直接靶向清除静止DTC，使其无法未来导致肿瘤复发。 "Eliminating dormant DTC" strategy: Directly eradicate dormant DTCs to prevent future tumor relapse.
4
微环境（如血管周围的保护性壁垒）能够维持DTC休眠，解离这一保护可能有助于消除耐药DTC。 The local microenvironment (such as the perivascular protective niche) maintains DTC quiescence, and disrupting this support may help eliminate chemoresistant DTCs.
5
终身“锁定”性维持治疗虽然有效，但存在长期毒性及依赖问题，需要权衡生活质量。 Lifetime "lock-in" maintenance therapy is effective but poses concerns about long-term toxicity and treatment dependence, requiring careful management for quality of life.
老师补充
DTC相关的精准治疗仍属前沿领域。实际中，针对休眠细胞的临床转化和疗效监测非常具有挑战性。 Supplement from class: DTC-targeted therapy is still at the research frontier; clinical translation and efficacy monitoring for dormant cell targeting remain highly challenging.

与细胞周期相关的癌症特征 Hallmarks of cancer: related to cell cycle

1
癌症有多种典型特征，许多都与细胞周期异常密切相关。 Cancer has various hallmarks, many closely linked to cell cycle dysregulation.
主要包括
持续增殖信号（sustaining proliferative signaling）、逃逸生长抑制（evading growth suppressors）、表型可塑性、代谢重编程、抵抗细胞死亡、基因组不稳定与突变、复制不朽性（enabling replicative immortality）、炎症、逃避免疫、促进转移和耐药等。 These include sustaining proliferative signaling, evading growth suppressors, phenotypic plasticity, metabolic reprogramming, resisting cell death, genome instability and mutation, enabling replicative immortality, inflammation, immune evasion, promoting metastasis, and therapy resistance.
3
复制不朽性（replicative immortality）和细胞衰老（senescent cells）与细胞周期调控密不可分，是癌症发生和耐受治疗的关键机制。 Both replicative immortality and cellular senescence are tightly linked to cell cycle regulation and are crucial in cancer development and treatment resistance.
细胞衰老
永久性细胞周期阻滞 Senescence: a permanent cell cycle arrest
1
衰老是细胞发生不可逆性周期阻滞（不可退回到增殖状态），可参与胚胎发育、组织修复、免疫防御和抑癌。 Senescence is an irreversible cell cycle arrest state, playing roles in embryonic development, tissue repair, immune surveillance, and tumor suppression.
2
机体可通过清除衰老细胞维持健康，衰老细胞聚集是衰老及多种相关疾病的标志。 Clearing senescent cells maintains health, while their accumulation marks aging and age-related diseases.
主要诱因
端粒缩短（染色体保护端丧失）、DNA损伤、线粒体功能障碍、炎症、活性氧（ROS）过高、表观遗传改变等。 Major triggers include telomere shortening (loss of chromosome end protection), DNA damage, mitochondrial dysfunction, inflammation, excess ROS, and epigenetic changes.
4
癌症中，细胞可因癌基因激活或抗癌药物诱导进入早发衰老（OIS或TIS）。 In cancer, cells may undergo premature senescence due to activated oncogenes or therapy-induced stress (oncogene-induced senescence, OIS, or therapy-induced senescence, TIS).
分子机制
p53→p21CIP1通路维持稳定上调，p16INK4A高表达，共同抑制CDK2/4/6-细胞周期蛋白E/D，促使细胞退出周期（Ki-67阴性，Rb抑制E2F转录）。 Molecularly: p53→p21CIP1 pathway is stably upregulated, p16INK4A is highly expressed, both inhibit CDK2/4/6–cyclin E/D complexes, leading to cell cycle exit (Ki-67 negative, Rb suppresses E2F).
6
老师强调，衰老细胞阻止癌症发生，但高比例积聚反而促癌或导致组织功能衰退。 The instructor highlighted that senescent cells prevent cancer, but excessive accumulation can promote cancer or impair tissue function.
7
临床上，衰老相关表型SA-β-Gal阳性、核膜变化、细胞变大变平、代谢途径改变、分泌多种炎症因子（SASP）。 Clinically, senescent cells are SA-β-Gal positive, show nuclear membrane changes, become enlarged and flattened, alter metabolic pathways, and secrete inflammatory factors (SASP).

衰老细胞的标志性特征 Hallmarks of senescent cells

1
衰老细胞具有典型的分泌表型（SASP），即释放多种细胞因子、基质金属蛋白酶、趋化因子和胞外囊泡，能通过旁分泌作用影响周围细胞。 Senescent cells display a typical secretory phenotype (SASP), releasing cytokines, MMPs, chemokines, and extracellular vesicles, which can influence neighboring cells via paracrine signaling.
2
衰老细胞溶酶体增多，SA-β-半乳糖苷酶（SA-β-Gal）活性上升，是常用的实验检测标志。 These cells have increased lysosomal mass and activity; SA-β-galactosidase (SA-β-Gal) activity is elevated and used as a common experimental marker.
3
染色质重组明显，出现衰老相关异染色质灶（SAHF），且可见DNA-SCARS（持续的DNA损伤区域和感应器）。 Chromatin reorganization is significant: senescence-associated heterochromatin foci (SAHF) are present, along with DNA-SCARs (sites of persistent DNA damage and sensors).
4
衰老细胞高表达p16且Ki-67阴性，反映其细胞增殖完全终止。 Senescent cells show high expression of p16 and are Ki-67 negative, reflecting a complete block of cell proliferation.
5
线粒体功能障碍，活性氧（ROS）↑，代谢重编程增强生存相关通路。 There is mitochondrial dysfunction, increased ROS, and upregulation of pro-survival metabolic pathways.
6
衰老细胞形态巨大且变扁，核膜结构异常（如Lamin B1减少）。 Morphologically, senescent cells are giant and flattened, with disrupted nuclear lamina (such as decreased Lamin B1).

衰老与癌症的关系 Senescence and cancer

1
衰老可通过抑制细胞分裂来预防肿瘤发生。 Cellular senescence can prevent tumorigenesis by blocking cell division.
2
衰老细胞被免疫细胞及时清除，有助于维持组织健康并减少肿瘤风险。 Efficient clearance of senescent cells by immune cells maintains tissue health and reduces cancer risk.
3
若p16/INK4a和/或p53功能丧失（最常见的肿瘤基因突变事件），细胞无法进入衰老，肿瘤易发生。 If p16/INK4a and/or p53 function is lost (most common genetic event in human cancers), cells fail to enter senescence, facilitating tumor development.
4
如果机体不能清除衰老细胞，反而会促进肿瘤进展并导致相关疾病。 Inadequate clearance of senescent cells promotes cancer progression and related diseases.
5
老师强调，衰老细胞的积累与多种年龄相关疾病（包括癌症）密切相关。 The instructor emphasized that accumulation of senescent cells is closely linked to various age-related diseases, including cancer.

临床与治疗策略 Clinical and therapeutic strategies

1
“Senogenic”指诱导细胞进入衰老状态，有助于抑制肿瘤发生。 "Senogenic" means inducing cell senescence, which can help inhibit tumor initiation.
2
衰老细胞过多则需应用“Senolytic”药物（如强心苷类Na/K-ATP酶抑制剂），选择性清除衰老细胞。 If too many senescent cells accumulate, "Senolytic" drugs (e.g., cardiac glycosides, Na/K-ATPase inhibitors) can selectively clear senescent cells.
3
清除衰老细胞有助于抑制肿瘤发生、改善抗肿瘤治疗效果、减少副作用，并能降低炎症和纤维化水平，提高机体状态。 Eliminating senescent cells helps inhibit tumor initiation, improve cancer therapy, reduce treatment side effects, decrease inflammation and fibrosis, and increase organismal fitness.

P53——基因组守护者 P53 the guardian of the genome

1
P53能响应各种细胞压力信号，如增殖过度信号、DNA损伤、端粒缩短和缺氧等。 P53 responds to various cellular stress signals, such as hyperproliferative signals, DNA damage, telomere shortening, and hypoxia.
2
稳定且活化的p53可诱导细胞周期阻滞、细胞衰老或程序性死亡（凋亡）。 Stable and active p53 induces cell-cycle arrest, senescence, or programmed cell death (apoptosis).
3
P53介导的应激反应是防止突变积累和癌症发生的关键屏障。 p53-mediated stress response is a key barrier preventing mutation accumulation and tumorigenesis.

062025-10-16（第 2 讲）

MOLECULAR TARGETS OF CANCER THERAPY: A BRIEF HISTORY

肿瘤治疗的分子靶点
简要历史回顾
1
激素治疗的引入

1939年，Charles Huggins 基于对雌激素对乳腺癌作用的早期观察，首次将激素用于前列腺癌男性，并通过酸性磷酸酶水平下降观察到疗效。

In 1939, Charles Huggins, based on early observations of the effect of estrogens on breast cancer, first treated men with prostate cancer using hormones, and observed the effect by decreases in acid phosphatase levels.

2
氮芥类药物的发现与应用

Alfred Gilman 和 Louis Goodman 发现氮芥可使移植性淋巴瘤小鼠中肿瘤消退。

Alfred Gilman and Louis Goodman found that nitrogen mustard could cause tumor regression in mice with transplanted lymphoid tumors.

1943年，他们说服Gustaf Lindskog将氮芥用于非霍奇金淋巴瘤伴严重气道阻塞的病人，并取得了显著疗效。

In 1943, they convinced Gustaf Lindskog to administer nitrogen mustard to a patient with non-Hodgkin’s lymphoma and severe airway obstruction, achieving marked regression.

该发现促进了更多相关烷化剂如chlorambucil、cyclophosphamide的合成与测试。

This finding boosted the synthesis and testing of related alkylating agents like chlorambucil and cyclophosphamide.

3
叶酸拮抗剂的开发

二战前后，通过对蔬菜中影响骨髓功能的成分研究，发现了叶酸，并发现叶酸缺乏表现类似氮芥损伤骨髓的情况。

Before and during WWII, research on vegetables found folic acid, important for bone marrow function, and found that folate deficiency produced a picture similar to nitrogen mustard effects.

为此，科学家们测试了一系列叶酸类似物，实际上是叶酸拮抗剂，包括aminopterin和现在广泛使用的methotrexate。

Therefore, a series of folic acid analogues (folate antagonists), including aminopterin and methotrexate, were tested and used.

4
二战相关的抗生素药物筛选

二战期间，药企大规模筛选发酵产物，发现了如actinomycin D等活性抗肿瘤抗生素，这些抗生素至今仍广泛使用。

During WWII, large-scale screening of fermentation products by pharmaceutical companies led to the discovery of active antitumor antibiotics, such as actinomycin D, still in common use today.

5
联合用药与周期性给药方案

给药方案的重要性逐步显现，多药联合优于单药使用。

The importance of drug administration schedules became clear, and drug combinations were superior to single agents.

白血病领域，通过新发现的长春碱类药物，如长春新碱，发展出VAMP方案（长春新碱、氨甲喋呤、6-巯基嘌呤、泼尼松），首次采用周期性联合给药，提高了缓解率和缓解持续时间。

In leukemia, the program VAMP (vincristine, amethopterin (methotrexate), 6-mercaptopurine, prednisone) was the first cyclically administered combination to increase remission rate and duration, taking advantage of newly discovered Vinca alkaloids like vincristine.

肿瘤治疗分子靶点的历史进展 I Historical Progress of Molecular Targets in Cancer Therapy

1
筛选系统和药物发现的革新

自1975年起，Developmental Therapeutics Program (DTP)每年筛选约10,000种药物，发展了新的筛选体系。

Since 1975, the Developmental Therapeutics Program (DTP) screened about 10,000 drugs per year and developed new series of screening systems.

用L1210小鼠白血病模型的主要筛选被淘汰，转而采用肿瘤异种移植模型（免疫缺陷小鼠接种人肿瘤）。

The L1210 mouse leukemia model was abandoned as the primary screening method in favor of human tumor xenografts in nude mice.

在这一筛选体系下，紫杉烷类（taxanes）的抗肿瘤效果被发现。

Under this screening system, the antitumor effects of taxanes were identified.

2
单克隆抗体应用的突破

单克隆抗体的出现增强了化疗效果。

The advent of monoclonal antibodies enhanced the effects of chemotherapy.

1975年杂交瘤技术被提出，1990年代中期单克隆抗体被证实在临床上具有显著疗效。

Hybridoma technology was reported in 1975, and monoclonal antibodies were proven clinically useful in the mid-1990s.

3
靶向治疗的诞生与代表性药物

化疗已逐步进入“靶向治疗”时代。

Chemotherapy has transitioned into the era of "targeted therapy."

第一个、最经典的靶向治疗药物为酪氨酸激酶抑制剂伊马替尼(imatinib)，用于慢性髓性白血病(CML)的治疗。

The first and most classic targeted drug is the tyrosine kinase inhibitor imatinib, used for chronic myeloid leukemia (CML).

该药物设计可结合于由“Philadelphia染色体”易位产生的Bcr-Abl蛋白的ATP结合位点，从而抑制该异常激酶功能。

This drug was designed to fit the ATP-binding site of the Bcr-Abl protein produced by the “Philadelphia chromosome” translocation, inhibiting the function of this abnormal kinase.

CML的特点是由单一分子异常驱动，而大部分肿瘤则涉及多重异常靶点。

A unique aspect of CML is that a single molecular abnormality drives the disease, whereas most cancers involve multiple abnormalities that need to be targeted.

抗肿瘤药物的类型与肿瘤生物标志物（Cancer Drug Types and Cancer Biomarkers）

一、抗肿瘤药物分类 Cancer Drug Classification

1
细胞毒性药物

细胞毒性药物包括烷化剂、抗代谢物、拓扑异构酶抑制剂和微管毒素。

Cytotoxic drugs include alkylators, antimetabolites, topoisomerase inhibitors, and microtubule poisons.

这些药物主要通过干扰细胞分裂，杀伤肿瘤细胞，但对正常细胞也有毒性。

These drugs mainly kill tumor cells by interfering with cell division, but they are also toxic to normal cells.

2
靶向药物

靶向药物包括单克隆抗体、激素（内分泌）药物和分子靶向药（如酪氨酸激酶抑制剂TKI）。

Targeted drugs include monoclonal antibodies, endocrine drugs, and molecularly-targeted drugs such as tyrosine kinase inhibitors (TKI).

这些药物能够特异性识别和作用于肿瘤细胞的关键分子或通路，提高疗效并降低对正常细胞的影响。

These drugs can selectively target key molecules or pathways in tumor cells, increasing efficacy and reducing effects on normal cells.

3
免疫治疗药物

免疫治疗是利用或调节自身免疫系统来识别和消灭肿瘤细胞。

Immunotherapy aims to use or modulate the immune system to recognize and eliminate tumor cells.

其代表药物包括单克隆抗体和其他激活免疫系统的治疗手段。

Representative drugs include monoclonal antibodies and other immune-stimulating therapies.

二、肿瘤生物标志物 Cancer Biomarkers

1
生物标志物的定义

肿瘤生物标志物是指肿瘤细胞特异表达的、在正常细胞中不存在的突变核酸序列、蛋白、糖蛋白或蛋白质群。

Cancer biomarkers are unique mutated nucleic acid sequences, proteins, glycoproteins, or groups of proteins expressed by tumor cells but not normally by healthy cells.

2
生物标志物的四大类型 a) 易感性标志物（Pre-disposition）

用于评估某人患癌的可能性。

Used to evaluate the likelihood of developing a disease. b) 诊断标志物（Diagnostic）

用于确证患者是否患有某种特定癌症。

Used to confirm whether a patient has a particular cancer. c) 预测性标志物（Predictive）

判断患者是否可能从某种特定药物治疗中获益。

Used to determine which cohort of patients may benefit from a particular drug therapy. d) 预后性标志物（Prognostic）

判断肿瘤在个体内的进展趋势。

Used to suggest how the cancer may develop in the individual.

三、课堂补充说明 Lecture Supplement

1
早期的细胞毒性药物缺乏特异性，对所有增殖细胞均有影响；

Early cytotoxic drugs lacked specificity and affected all proliferating cells.

2
靶向药物和单克隆抗体由于其高度靶向性，副作用相对较小；

Targeted drugs and monoclonal antibodies have relatively lower side effects due to high specificity.

3
生物标志物的选择和精准检测，有助于个体化用药和提高治疗效果。

The selection and precise detection of cancer biomarkers help guide personalized medication and improve outcomes.

血液肿瘤标志物Blood Stream Tumor Markers

一、定义和分类 Definition and Classification

肿瘤标志物定义为由肿瘤产生或机体对肿瘤反应而产生、能够帮助肿瘤检测和/或监测的任何物质。

A tumor marker is defined as a substance of any type produced either by a tumor or by the body in response to a tumor and that aids cancer detection and/or monitoring.

常见的血液肿瘤标志物类型包括酶、免疫球蛋白、胎盘相关蛋白、细胞角蛋白、激素和黏液素。

Common blood tumor marker types include enzymes, immunoglobulins, fetal-placental proteins, cytokeratins, hormones, and mucins.

典型标志物及适用的癌种：

Typical markers and their associated cancer types:

酶类
LDH、酸性磷酸酶、碱性磷酸酶、PSA，分别用于睾丸肿瘤、前列腺癌、骨肉瘤和前列腺癌。
Enzymes
LDH, acid phosphatase, alkaline phosphatase, PSA, used for NSGCT, prostate, osteosarcoma, and prostate cancer.
免疫球蛋白
副球蛋白、Bence-Jones蛋白，用于B细胞恶性肿瘤。
Immunoglobulins
Paraproteins, Bence-Jones proteins, for B-cell malignancy.
胎盘相关蛋白
CEA用于结直肠及其他腺癌，AFP和HCG用于肝癌、睾丸肿瘤和绒癌。

Fetal-placental proteins: CEA for colorectal and other adenocarcinomas, AFP and HCG for hepatocellular carcinoma, NSGCT, and choriocarcinoma.

细胞角蛋白
TPA、TPS、CYFRA 21-1用于多种腺癌及非小细胞肺癌。
Cytokeratins
TPA, TPS, CYFRA 21-1 for multiple adenocarcinomas and non-small cell lung cancer.
激素
降钙素用于髓样甲状腺癌，VMA、HVA用于神经母细胞瘤，5-HIAA用于类癌。
Hormones
Calcitonin for medullary thyroid carcinoma, VMA and HVA for neuroblastoma, 5-HIAA for carcinoid.
黏液素
CA 19-9（胰腺），CA 15-3/BR 27.29（乳腺），CA 72-4（胃癌）。
Mucins
CA 19-9 for pancreatic, CA 15-3/BR 27.29 for breast, CA 72-4 for gastric cancer.

检测手段主要为免疫分析法。

Measurement is mainly by immuno-assay.

二、医学用途和局限 Clinical Use and Limitations

血液肿瘤标志物一般不用于诊断，因为健康人与患病者之间的标志物水平受个体差异、年龄（如 PSA 随男性年龄增长升高）、激素状态及非肿瘤疾病（如 CA125 在绝经女性中的增加）影响，难以区分。

Tumor markers in blood are generally not used for diagnosis because levels are influenced by inter-individual variations, age (e.g., PSA increases after 50y in men), hormonal status, or non-cancerous diseases (e.g., CA125 increases in menopause), thus making it hard to discriminate between healthy and diseased subjects.

更适于患者随访和疗效监测，特别是器官特异性标志物，通过合适的药代动力学模型评估。

They are more suitable for patient follow-up and treatment monitoring, especially with organ-specific markers, evaluated according to appropriate pharmacokinetic models.

需要参考患者既往的标志物变化曲线才能解释复发/进展，能估算肿瘤倍增时间。

Previous evolution curves are needed to interpret recurrence/relapse, which allows estimation of tumor doubling time.

三、图示解读 Interpretation of Figures

图一展示前列腺手术后PSA下降，其半衰期可量化疗效，T1/2短提示肿瘤被有效清除。

The first figure shows PSA decrease after prostatectomy; the half-life (T1/2) quantifies the efficacy, and a short T1/2 indicates tumor clearance.

图二显示即使标志物水平仍在正常范围，变化趋势仍可能反映肿瘤复发，黄色区域为“正常值”范围。

The second figure shows that recurrence can be detected by upward trends, even when marker values remain within the "normal" range (yellow area).

四、老师补充说明 Additional Notes from Lecturer

重点强调血液肿瘤标志物更适合患者监测和疾病进展判定，而不是直接诊断癌症。

The lecturer emphasized that blood stream tumor markers are better suited for monitoring and disease progression assessment, not for direct diagnosis of cancer.

解释器官特异性标志物对于精准监测效果最好，并提示临床解读时应结合动态变化曲线和个体差异。

The best monitoring effect comes from organ-specific markers; clinical interpretation should consider dynamic curves and individual variability.

肿瘤细胞转移与液体活检Tumor Cell Metastasis and Liquid Biopsy

一、肿瘤细胞转移机制 Mechanisms of Tumor Cell Metastasis

EMT，即上皮-间质转化，是肿瘤细胞由上皮型向间质型转变赋予其迁移能力。

EMT (Epithelial to Mesenchymal Transition) is when tumor cells convert from an epithelial type to a mesenchymal type, giving them migratory ability.

MAT，即间质-变形虫转化，进一步增强细胞的运动和适应性。

MAT (Mesenchymal to Amoeboid Transition) further enhances cell motility and adaptability.

DTCs指解离的肿瘤细胞，这些细胞脱离原发灶，进入血液循环。

DTCs (Dissociated Tumor Cells) are cells that detach from the primary tumor and enter the bloodstream.

这些细胞通过EMT和MAT过程后，能够穿过基底膜，侵入血管，形成循环肿瘤细胞（CTCs），在体内远处定植，导致微转移灶和转移灶。

After EMT and MAT, these cells can cross the basement membrane, invade blood vessels, become circulating tumor cells (CTCs), and establish micro-metastases and overt metastases at distant sites in the body.

在血循环和淋巴结中，CTCs可以与免疫细胞形成簇，或单独存在，最终导致脑、肝、骨等器官的转移。

In blood circulation and lymph nodes, CTCs can form clusters with immune cells or exist singly, eventually causing metastases in organs like brain, liver, and bone.

二、液体活检类型与意义 Types and Significance of Liquid Biopsy

液体活检可通过血液样本，检测循环肿瘤细胞、游离肿瘤DNA、细胞外miRNA与其他非编码RNA、细胞外囊泡（如外泌体、微囊泡）及肿瘤教育血小板。

Liquid biopsy uses blood samples to detect circulating tumor cells, cell-free tumor DNA, cell-free miRNA and other ncRNA, extracellular vesicles (exosomes, microvesicles), and tumor-educated platelets.

这些标志物为肿瘤早诊、最小残留病变监测、治疗反应评估和复发预测提供依据。

These markers provide evidence for early diagnosis, monitoring of minimal residual disease, assessment of therapeutic response, and prediction of recurrence.

三、CTCs的分离与鉴定 Isolation and Characterization of CTCs

1
分离原理 Separation Principles

CTCs在血液中极为稀少，约百-千万分之一，因此需高效分离。

CTCs are extremely rare in blood, about one per million blood cells, so efficient separation is essential.

2
主要分离技术 Main Isolation Technologies
免疫亲和法
正向选择：用针对肿瘤特异性抗原的抗体或微球直接捕获CTCs。 正向选择代表技术如CellSearch系统、AdnaTest等。 Positive selection: Directly captures CTCs using antibodies or beads targeting tumor-specific antigens (e.g., CellSearch, AdnaTest). 反向选择：去除白细胞等阴性组分（如用CD45抗体），剩余CTCs。 Negative selection: Removes leukocytes etc. using anti-CD45, leaving CTCs (e.g., EasySep, MACS). 功能化微流控：利用标记抗体的芯片微流控技术进行捕获与富集。 Functionalized microfluidics: Uses antibody-labeled microfluidic chips to enrich CTCs (e.g., Isoflux, CTC-Chip).
生理物理法
密度梯度离心（如Ficoll等）、微滤（ScreenCell, ISET）、惯性聚焦（Vortex）、电泳分离（DEPArray）。 Physical methods: Density gradient centrifugation (Ficoll, OncoQuick), microfiltration (ScreenCell, ISET), inertial focusing (Vortex), electrophoresis (DEPArray).
3
CTCs的鉴定 Characterization of CTCs

ICC免疫荧光法用于检测CTCs表面或胞内蛋白。

ICC immunofluorescence detects CTC surface or intracellular proteins.

FISH荧光原位杂交可检测染色体异常。

FISH detects chromosomal abnormalities by fluorescence in situ hybridization.

分子检测如基因组/转录组分析，可进行DNA测序与RNA测序，揭示基因突变及表达特征。

Molecular assays like genomic and transcriptomic analysis enable DNA and RNA sequencing, revealing mutations and expression profiles.

四、课堂补充要点 Additional Comments from Lecture

老师强调，液体活检为肿瘤监控提供新手段，特别适合随访和发现残留及转移。

Lecturer emphasized that liquid biopsy offers new tools for tumor monitoring, especially for follow-up and identifying residual disease or metastasis.

CTC及其相关生物标志物的检测发展迅速，已开始用于部分癌症的个性化治疗决策。

The detection of CTCs and related biomarkers has developed rapidly and is being used for personalized therapy in certain cancers.

肿瘤残留病变与细胞/组织肿瘤标志物Residual Disease and Cellular/Tissular Cancer Markers

一、肿瘤残留病变与CTC动态 Residual Disease and CTC Dynamics

残留病变是指在肿瘤治疗和手术切除后，体内仍然残留少量肿瘤细胞，可能导致复发。

Residual disease refers to the presence of a minimal number of tumor cells remaining in the body after treatment and surgical removal, which may lead to relapse.

剧烈的肿瘤负荷下降主要发生在手术、化疗等积极治疗后，但CTC（循环肿瘤细胞）不会完全为零，存在微量残留。

A sharp decline in tumor burden occurs after surgery or intensive chemotherapy, but circulating tumor cells (CTCs) rarely reach zero, so minimal residual disease persists.

随访通过检测外周血中CTC数量的变化，监控患者复发风险。

Follow-up is performed by monitoring the changes in CTC counts in peripheral blood to assess the patient's risk of relapse.

若检测到CTC数量持续上升，提示肿瘤复发或转移的风险增加。

If CTC numbers steadily increase, it indicates a rising risk of tumor recurrence or metastasis.

只有通过长期动态监测，才能真正反映疾病发展和最小残留病变的变化。

Only through long-term dynamic monitoring can we truly capture disease progression and changes in minimal residual disease.

二、细胞/组织肿瘤标志物的分子水平应用 Molecular Level Application of Cellular/Tissular Markers

细胞或组织水平的肿瘤标志物包含基因型与表型两方面，用于精准诊断、预测和分层。

Cellular and tissue-level tumor markers include both genotypic and phenotypic layers, used for precise diagnosis, prognostication, and stratification.

不同肿瘤类型具有特异的分子标志物，分子检测结果可定义病人适宜的治疗方案。

Different tumor types harbor specific molecular markers, and molecular test results can define the optimal treatment strategy for each patient.

肿瘤标志物可以用于诊断、预测疗效与预后、个体化用药决策，并成为潜在的治疗靶点。

Tumor markers serve as diagnostic, predictive, and/or prognostic factors, guide personalized therapy, and can be potential therapeutic targets.

三、基因水平的主要分子变异 Main Molecular Alterations at the Genetic Level

肿瘤相关遗传改变主要包括突变和拷贝数变异。

Cancer-associated genetic changes mainly include mutations and copy number variations.

例如，BCR-ABL基因融合是白血病中特异变异，Her-2/ERBB2基因扩增见于乳腺癌。

For example, BCR-ABL fusion is specific to leukemia; Her-2/ERBB2 amplification is observed in breast cancer.

这些变异多在体细胞中发生，包括癌基因（如MYC）、抑癌基因（如TP53、PTEN）、染色质修饰因子等。

These changes mostly occur in somatic cells, involving oncogenes (such as MYC), tumor suppressor genes (like TP53, PTEN), and chromatin modifiers.

四、典型基因与作用蛋白的举例 Representative Genes and Protein Products

乳腺癌相关典型基因包括BRCA1/2、TP53、PIK3CA、ERBB2、HER2、FGFR2等，编码蛋白涉及细胞增殖、修复、信号通路调控。

Typical breast cancer-related genes include BRCA1/2, TP53, PIK3CA, ERBB2, HER2, FGFR2, etc.; these encoded proteins are involved in cell proliferation, repair, and signaling pathway regulation.

这些基因突变或表达变化的检测推动了肿瘤个体化精准治疗的发展。

Detection of these gene mutations or expression changes drives the development of personalized precision cancer therapy.

五、老师课堂补充要点 Additional Key Points from the Lecture

老师强调了残留病变（minimal residual disease）的重要性，指出手术和化疗后仍需持续监测，防止微小残留导致复发。

The lecturer emphasized the importance of minimal residual disease, highlighting that surgery and chemotherapy must be followed by continuous monitoring to prevent relapse from small residual populations.

细胞/组织标志物的分型与分子测序有助于根据不同患者的分子特征量身定制治疗。

Cellular/tissular marker subtyping and molecular sequencing help tailor treatment based on the molecular characteristics of individual patients.

一些分子变异不仅是生物标志物，也是治疗的直接靶点（如Her-2、BCR-ABL）。

Certain molecular alterations are not only biomarkers but also direct therapeutic targets (such as Her-2, BCR-ABL).

mRNA/蛋白水平与遗传水平的评估mRNA/Protein Level and Genetic Level Assessment

一、mRNA/蛋白水平表达 mRNA / Protein Level Expression

乳腺癌中常检测雌激素和孕激素受体的表达，以及Her-2的表达。

In breast cancer, the expression of estrogen and progesterone receptors, as well as Her-2, is routinely assessed.

鉴定这些分子标志物可实现特异性治疗靶向，比如针对Her-2阳性的治疗。

Identifying these molecular markers enables specific therapeutic targeting, such as Her-2 positive therapy.

如果肿瘤既无雌激素受体、孕激素受体也无Her-2表达，则为三阴性乳腺癌，治疗选择有限，预后较差。

Tumors lacking estrogen, progesterone, and Her-2 receptors are classified as triple-negative breast cancer, which has limited treatment options and a poorer prognosis.

白血病通常通过免疫分型来分析细胞表面特征，以指导诊断和分类。

Leukemias are typically analyzed by immunophenotyping to characterize surface profiles for diagnosis and classification.

二、遗传水平的定性和定量评估 Qualitative and Quantitative Assessment at Genetic Level

遗传评估包括基因组测序，可检测基因突变和结构变异。

Genetic assessment includes genome sequencing, which detects gene mutations and structural variants.

可通过测量细胞周期各期的DNA含量来反映细胞遗传物质变化（如G1期、S期、G2期和有丝分裂）。

Measuring the DNA content at different cell cycle stages (G1, S, G2, mitosis) reflects variations in cellular genetic material.

S期DNA含量增加，G2期DNA含量为G1期的两倍，有丝分裂后DNA含量回落。

DNA content increases during S phase, reaches double in G2 phase compared to G1, and drops after mitosis.

三、流式细胞术原理及应用 Principle and Application of Flow Cytometry

流式细胞术通过激光扫描测定单细胞的DNA含量和其他荧光标记，常用于血液或分离的组织细胞。

Flow cytometry uses laser scanning to assess DNA content and other fluorescent markers in single cells, commonly applied to blood or dissociated tissue cells.

细胞依次排列流经检测器，记录其荧光强度，结果以直方图呈现，不同峰代表不同DNA含量的细胞群。

Cells are aligned in single file and passed through a detector, which records fluorescence intensity; results appear as histograms, with peaks corresponding to populations with different DNA content.

G0/G1期细胞群表现为主峰，S期分布在中间，G2/M期为较小峰。

G0/G1 phase cells form the main peak, S phase is the intermediate region, and G2/M phase is a smaller peak.

四、利用DNA直方图发现遗传物质变化 Detection of Genetic Material Changes Using DNA Histograms

可通过分析DNA直方图判断细胞群体的倍数体变化：

By analyzing DNA histograms, one can identify ploidy changes in cell populations:

如果出现低于正常倍体的群体，说明有“低倍体”细胞。

A population to the left of the main peak indicates "hypodiploid" cells.

主峰反映“正常倍体”群体（2n）。

The main peak reflects the "normodiploid" population (2n).

额外出现偏右的新峰预示“异倍体”或染色体重复。

A new peak shifted to the right indicates "aneuploid" cells or chromosome duplication.

出现四倍体群体表明有全基因组重复。

A tetraploid population reflects whole genome duplication.

这些特征可用于分析肿瘤细胞的遗传不稳定性、监测治疗和预警耐药。

These features are used to analyze genetic instability in tumor cells, monitor treatment, and detect the emergence of resistance.

化疗疗效与耐药性出现Efficacy of Chemotherapy / Emergence of Resistance

一、流式细胞术分析化疗过程中DNA含量变化 Flow Cytometry Analysis of DNA Content During Chemotherapy

利用流式细胞术，可以定量分析肿瘤细胞在化疗前后不同阶段的DNA含量分布。

Flow cytometry enables quantitative analysis of tumor cell DNA content distributions at different stages before and after chemotherapy.

图中横坐标为DNA含量，纵坐标为细胞数，不同峰值分别对应于G0/G1期（2n DNA）、S期（DNA复制中）、G2/M期（4n DNA）。

The horizontal axis represents DNA content and the vertical axis indicates cell number; different peaks correspond to G0/G1 phase (2n DNA), S phase (DNA replication), and G2/M phase (4n DNA).

治疗前，细胞群体中可见典型的G0/G1、S、G2/M三个峰。

Before treatment, typical peaks of G0/G1, S, and G2/M phases are visible in the cell population.

第一次化疗后，细胞分布发生变化，部分细胞在G2/M期积聚，反映部分细胞对化疗药物阻断有反应。

After the first course of chemotherapy, there is redistribution, with some cells accumulating in G2/M phase, reflecting sensitivity to drug-induced cell cycle arrest.

第二次化疗后，G2/M峰显著降低，G0/G1细胞比例增多，说明化疗进一步杀伤肿瘤细胞。

After the second course, the G2/M peak significantly decreases and the proportion of G0/G1 cells increases, indicating further tumor cell killing by chemotherapy.

第三次化疗后，若再次出现G2/M峰或出现异常分布，表明可能出现残留耐药细胞，提示耐药性逐步显现。

After the third course, if the G2/M peak reappears or abnormal distributions emerge, it suggests the possible presence of residual resistant cells and the emergence of drug resistance.

这种动态追踪有助于评价化疗疗效和耐药风险，指导个体化调整治疗方案。

Such dynamic monitoring helps assess chemotherapy efficacy and resistance risk, guiding individualized treatment adjustments.

二、mRNA/蛋白水平表达检测与肿瘤表型分型 mRNA/Protein Expression Detection and Tumor Phenotyping

通过免疫组化、RT-qPCR、蛋白免疫分析法可以检测肿瘤组织中关键蛋白（如Her-2、ER）的表达水平。

Key protein expression (such as Her-2, ER) in tumor tissue can be detected using immunohistochemistry, RT-qPCR, and protein immunoassay.

如Her-2高度表达，表明属于Her-2阳性型乳腺癌，可考虑采用靶向治疗。

High Her-2 expression indicates Her-2 positive breast cancer, which may benefit from targeted therapy.

ER（雌激素受体）表达阳性说明对激素治疗敏感，而三阴性（Her-2-、ER-、PR-）则提示常规靶向治疗无效、预后较差。

Positive ER (estrogen receptor) expression suggests sensitivity to hormone therapy, while triple-negative (Her-2-, ER-, PR-) indicates insensitivity to conventional targeted therapies and poorer prognosis.

检测mRNA和蛋白的表达水平是肿瘤亚型分型和个体化治疗决策的重要依据。

Assessing mRNA and protein expression provides critical information for tumor subtyping and personalized therapy selection.

三、靶向分型与组织学亚型联合分析 Target-based Typing Combined with Histological Subtypes

乳腺癌可依Her-2和ER/PR分为多种分型，如Her-2+、ER+/Her-2-、三阴性等。

Breast cancer can be subtyped based on Her-2 and ER/PR as Her-2+, ER+/Her-2-, and triple-negative etc.

大部分属于ER+/Her-2-型，另有少部分为Her-2+或三阴性型。

The majority are ER+/Her-2-, while a minority are Her-2+ or triple-negative.

联合组织学亚型后分析生存曲线发现，不同亚型预后明显不同：ER+/Her-2-型预后较好，三阴性型（ER-/Her-2-）复发早、预后差。

Survival analysis combined with histological subtyping shows significant prognostic differences: ER+/Her-2- has the best outlook, while triple-negative (ER-/Her-2-) relapses early with poorer prognosis.

该分型结果可进一步整合分子水平、细胞/免疫特征（如干细胞、免疫细胞浸润）完善个体化诊疗。

Such classification can be further integrated with molecular data and cell/immune features (e.g., stem cells, immune cell infiltration) to optimize personalized management.

四、老师课堂强调 Teacher’s Key Emphases

老师强调动态追踪DNA含量直方图对化疗疗效、耐药性和残留病灶判断非常重要。

The instructor emphasized that dynamic tracing of DNA histograms is critical for evaluating chemotherapy efficacy, resistance, and residual disease.

检测Her-2、ER等分子标志物表达不仅直接决定靶向治疗方案，也是患者分层和预后的基础。

Detection of molecular markers such as Her-2 and ER not only directly determines targeted therapy plans but is also fundamental to patient stratification and prognosis.

多维度联合分型（分子+组织+免疫）有助于指导精准个体化治疗。

Multi-dimensional integrated subtyping (molecular + histological + immune) helps in guiding precise, personalized therapy.

免疫分型与细胞特异性表型Immuno-phenotyping and Cell-specific Phenotypes

一、免疫分型在肿瘤细胞检测中的应用 The Application of Immuno-phenotyping in Tumor Cell Detection

免疫分型是利用流式细胞术，通过标记不同细胞表面分子的荧光抗体，对肿瘤细胞进行分群和表型识别。

Immuno-phenotyping uses flow cytometry with fluorescently labeled antibodies against cell surface markers to cluster and phenotype tumor cells.

正常骨髓细胞可在散点图（细胞直方图）上区分为浆细胞、粒细胞、单核细胞、淋巴细胞等亚群。

Normal bone marrow cells are distinguishable on cytograms into plasma cells, granulocytes, monocytes, lymphocytes, etc.

通过CD45荧光通道，结合侧向散射信号（SSC），可清楚区分幼稚细胞（如原始细胞、早期前体细胞）与成熟细胞。

Using CD45 fluorescence and side scatter (SSC), immature cells (such as blasts and early precursors) can be clearly distinguished from mature cells.

二、急性髓系白血病免疫分型 Immuno-phenotyping in Acute Myeloid Leukemia (AML)

以髓母细胞白血病M1型为例，流式细胞术显示髓母细胞亚群在CD45低表达区，SSC信号适中。

For myeloblastic leukemia M1, flow cytometry shows blasts cluster at low CD45 expression with moderate SSC signal.

2D荧光细胞图（cytogram）联合多种标记物（如CD34, CD13, CD38, HLA-DR等），进一步确认异常细胞表型。

2D fluorescence cytograms combine multiple markers (CD34, CD13, CD38, HLA-DR, etc.) to further validate aberrant cell phenotypes.

不同细胞群（如髓母细胞、淋巴细胞）在不同荧光通道散点图上呈现出独特分布模式。

Different cell types (e.g., myeloblasts, lymphocytes) show distinct distribution patterns on scatter plots of different fluorescence channels.

三、常用髓系白血病标志物及表达谱 Common Myeloid Leukemia Markers and Their Expression Patterns

白血病细胞常表达CD34、CD13、HLA-DR、CD38等标志分子。

Leukemia cells commonly express CD34, CD13, HLA-DR, CD38, etc.

2D图展示
CD34、CD11b、Mo2在髓母细胞上为阴性，CD117阳性；CD13和HLA-DR阳性，CD34、CD15阴性。

2D plots show CD34, CD11b, Mo2 negative, CD117 positive in blasts; CD13 and HLA-DR positive, CD34 and CD15 negative.

CD38为阳性，CD7为部分阳性。

CD38 is positive; CD7 is partially positive.

四、特殊白血病亚型—早幼粒细胞白血病M3 Special Leukemia Subtype – Promyelocyte Leukemia M3

M3型白血病细胞（早幼粒细胞）特征为胞质内可见Auer小体，即紫红色杆状颗粒。

M3 leukemia cells (promyelocytes) are characterized by cytoplasmic Auer bodies, which are rod-shaped purple inclusions.

流式细胞术可发现M3分型的高颗粒性APLB（早幼粒细胞白血病原始细胞）群，SSC信号增强，CD45表达模式独特。

Flow cytometry reveals a hypergranular APLB (acute promyelocytic leukemia blasts) cluster with increased SSC signal and unique CD45 expression.

五、免疫分型有助于肿瘤异质性评估与诊断 Immuno-phenotyping Assists in Assessing Tumor Heterogeneity and Diagnosis

老师讲解中强调，免疫分型能精准定位异常细胞，辅助血液肿瘤的确诊及亚型分类。

The lecturer emphasized that immuno-phenotyping enables precise localization of abnormal cells, assisting in diagnosis and subtyping of hematologic malignancies.

通过2D细胞荧光散点图，可识别多种异常细胞及其表型，为精准治疗提供依据。

2D fluorescence scatter plots identify various abnormal cell populations and their phenotypes, providing a basis for precision therapy.

某些标志物（如CD34、CD117、HLA-DR等）在不同白血病亚型具有特异性表达，有助于鉴别诊断。

Certain markers (e.g., CD34, CD117, HLA-DR) have specific expression patterns in different leukemia subtypes, aiding differential diagnosis.

六、课件及老师额外强调内容 Additional Points from Slides and Lecturer

结合细胞形态（如Auer小体）、免疫标志物及流式细胞术数据进行多维诊断，有助于发现难以仅凭形态学区分的异常群体。

Combining cell morphology (e.g., Auer bodies), immunophenotyping, and flow cytometry data supports multidimensional diagnosis, helping detect abnormal populations not distinguishable by morphology alone.

此方法也可用于治疗后残留病变的定量分析和患者随访。

This approach is also used for quantitative analysis of minimal residual disease and patient follow-up after treatment.

癌症生物标志物与精准肿瘤学Cancer Biomarkers & Precision Oncology

一、癌症生物标志物的分子基础 Molecular Basis of Cancer Biomarkers

癌症生物标志物可从基因组、转录组、蛋白质组、代谢组等多层面进行分析。

Cancer biomarkers can be analyzed at the levels of genomics, transcriptomics, proteomics, and metabolomics.

常见分子变化包括单核苷酸变异、结构变异、拷贝数变异和甲基化模式。

Common molecular alterations include single nucleotide variants, structural variants, copy number variations, and methylation patterns.

这些分子变化通过技术手段（如测序）被检测，用于揭示肿瘤的遗传背景和生物学特征。

These molecular changes are detected using techniques such as sequencing to reveal the genetic background and biological characteristics of tumors.

此外，还评估基因表达、融合基因、细胞类型组成和特定标志物的表达水平。

Furthermore, gene expression, fusion detection, cell type composition, and specific marker expression are also evaluated.

蛋白质组和代谢组分析补充了对蛋白质谱和代谢物的检测。

Proteomic and metabolomic analyses complement the detection of protein profiles and metabolites.

二、精准肿瘤学的实施 Implementation of Precision Oncology

精准肿瘤学是基于患者分子层面特征，为每个患者制定最适个体化治疗的策略。

Precision oncology is an approach that tailors the optimal individual treatment strategy based on each patient’s molecular profile.

不同癌种甚至同种癌症的患者，其驱动基因和分子特征可能完全不同。

Patients with different cancer types, or even the same cancer type, can have completely different driver genes and molecular features.

利用全基因组、转录组、蛋白质组等综合信息，可以更精准地进行肿瘤分型和疗效预测。

Comprehensive data from whole-genome, transcriptome, and proteome analyses enables more precise tumor classification and therapy prediction.

典型检测项目包括
HER2、ER、PR（乳腺癌），BRCA（乳腺、卵巢、胰腺、前列腺），PD-L1（多癌种），BRAF（多癌种）等。

Typical tested markers include: HER2, ER, PR (breast cancer), BRCA (breast, ovary, pancreas, prostate), PD-L1 (various cancers), BRAF (various cancers), etc.

检测技术方式有免疫组化、ISH、qPCR/Array、测序及片段分析等。

Detection methods include immunohistochemistry, ISH, qPCR/Array, sequencing, and fragment analysis.

三、多学科数据整合和人工智能支持 Multidisciplinary Data Integration & AI Support

医学影像、蛋白质组、健康记录、基因组与组织学等多维数据需跨学科团队整合分析。

Multidimensional data such as medical imaging, proteomics, health records, genomics, and histology need to be integrated by multidisciplinary expert teams.

专家团队协作进行患者最佳分层，为制定精准治疗方案提供依据。

Through collaborative work, expert teams perform optimal patient stratification to support precise therapy selection.

未来个体化医疗的发展方向是将大数据与人工智能相结合，实现自动化决策支持系统。

The future of personalized medicine lies in big data integration with artificial intelligence to enable automated decision support systems.

决策系统结合知识数据库、药物基因组学、人口信息、病理及分子信息等，为诊断、预后及个体化治疗提供智能建议。

Such systems integrate knowledge databases, pharmacogenomics, population data, pathology, and molecular profiles to provide intelligent recommendations for diagnosis, prognosis, and individualized treatment.

四、课堂重点总结和老师补充 Key Lecture Points and Instructor Additions

老师特别强调，精准肿瘤学的本质是“为每一位患者量身定制最优治疗”，而不是采用“千人一方”的传统方式。

The instructor stressed that the essence of precision oncology is “tailoring the best treatment for each individual patient,” as opposed to the traditional “one-size-fits-all” approach.

随着分子技术不断迭代升级，新的标志物和分型方法持续被发现与应用，整体趋势是更细致、更动态、更智能化分层和决策。

With the rapid evolution of molecular techniques, new biomarkers and subtyping strategies are continuously discovered and applied, leading to increasingly refined, dynamic, and intelligent patient stratification and clinical decision-making.

在多组学数据和人工智能技术辅助下，未来个体化诊疗将成为癌症治疗的主流。

In the era of multi-omics data and AI, personalized precision medicine will become the main trend in cancer therapy.

耐药机制Drug Resistance Mechanisms

一、耐药性的分类与来源 Categories and Origins of Drug Resistance

耐药性分为两类
内在耐药和获得性耐药。

Drug resistance is categorized into two types: intrinsic resistance and acquired resistance.

获得性耐药可由耐药细胞克隆选择或肿瘤干细胞介导。

Acquired resistance may arise from clonal selection of resistant cells or through cancer stem cells.

二、耐药的细胞机制 Cellular Mechanisms of Drug Resistance

细胞可通过多种方式获得耐药性，包括：

Cells can acquire resistance via several mechanisms, including:

1
进入减少↓（减少药物内流）：细胞降低药物摄取能力。
Decreased influx↓
The cell reduces the uptake of the drug.
隔室化
药物被囊泡等亚细胞结构隔离，无法作用于靶点。 2. Compartmentalization: Drug sequestration in vesicles or compartments prevents interaction with the target.
3
排出增加↑（药物外排增强）：细胞通过主动外排泵将药物排除。 3. Increased efflux↑: The cell actively pumps drugs out via efflux transporters.
靶点改变
靶蛋白结构变异导致药物无法结合。 4. Change in cellular target: Mutations in the target protein hinder drug binding.
药物失活/前体药物激活缺陷
药物被降解或失去活性，或前体药物无法被激活。 5. Drug inactivation / defective prodrug activation: Drugs are deactivated or prodrugs fail to be activated.
代谢适应
细胞代谢改变导致药物效力下降。 6. Metabolic cell adaptation: Changes in cell metabolism reduce drug effectiveness.

三、耐药相关的肿瘤特征 Tumor Features Related to Drug Resistance

肿瘤异质性和微环境（如生长因子、pH降低、缺氧）促进耐药产生。

Tumor heterogeneity and microenvironment (e.g., local growth factors, decreased pH, hypoxia) promote resistance development.

肿瘤细胞干性（如休眠、MDR高表达、寿命长、抗逆性强）加剧耐药。

Cancer cell stemness (e.g., dormancy, high MDR expression, long lifespan, strong stress-resistance) exacerbates resistance.

四、多药耐药（MDR）的主要机制 Major Mechanisms of Multidrug Resistance (MDR)

药物外排机制主要由ABC转运蛋白家族（如ABCB1/P-gp和ABCG2）介导。

The main efflux mechanism is mediated by ATP-Binding Cassette 　 transporters, especially ABCB1 (P-gp) and ABCG2.
答案 ABC

通过ATP驱动，药物被主动泵出细胞，减少细胞内药物浓度。

Powered by ATP, these pumps actively expel drugs to lower intracellular drug concentration.

五、减少药物进入的机制 Mechanisms for Decreased Drug Influx

载体蛋白（如SLC家族）下调或亲和力下降导致药物摄取减少。

Down-regulation or reduced affinity of carrier proteins (such as the SLC family) leads to decreased drug uptake.

六、抗癌药物代谢的失活或激活缺陷 Inactivation or Defective Activation of Anticancer Drugs

例如
阿糖胞苷（AraC）需要激酶磷酸化为活性形态，若激酶下调则产生耐药。
Example
Cytarabine (AraC) requires phosphorylation by a kinase for activity; kinase down-regulation induces resistance.

癌细胞还能上调CYP基因（编码P-450酶）加速药物代谢失活。

Cancer cells can upregulate CYP genes (encoding Cytochrome P-450 enzymes) to increase drug inactivation.

七、药物靶点改变 Modification of Drug Targets

靶点基因突变可改变药物结合位点，如：

Mutations in target genes alter drug binding, for example:

拓扑异构酶II（蒽环类药物靶点）

Topoisomerase II (target for anthracyclines)

β-微管蛋白（紫杉类药物靶点）

β-tubulin (target for taxanes)

Bcr-Abl（伊马替尼靶点）：已发现多达70种突变，部分可通过提高药物剂量克服，部分需换用二代药物（如达沙替尼、尼洛替尼）。

Bcr-Abl (target for imatinib): up to 70 mutations reported; some overcome by higher drug doses, others require alternative drugs (e.g., dasatinib, nilotinib).

DNA修复机制上调对抗烷化剂。

Increased DNA-repair counters alkylating agents.

八、药物靶点的基因扩增 Gene Amplification of Drug Targets

某些靶点基因（如二氢叶酸还原酶）可扩增100倍以上，导致甲氨蝶呤耐药。

Certain target genes (e.g., dihydrofolate reductase) may be amplified over 100 times, conferring methotrexate resistance.

九、表观遗传改变 Epigenetic Alterations

CpG岛DNA甲基化（常见于MDR1）、组蛋白修饰可引发耐药。

DNA methylation of CpG islands (frequent in MDR1) and histone modifications contribute to drug resistance.

十、转录水平的调控 Regulation at Transcription Level

miRNA与lncRNA可调控耐药基因表达，有多种miRNA参与不同肿瘤的耐药形成。

miRNAs and lncRNAs regulate resistance gene expression, with many miRNAs implicated in drug resistance in various cancers.

十一、老师课堂补充 Instructor’s Supplement

老师强调耐药性机制常常同时存在且互相影响，实际临床耐药几乎总是多机制协同。

The instructor emphasized that these resistance mechanisms often coexist and interact, and clinical resistance almost always involves multiple mechanisms working together.

其中MDR外排泵的上调和药物靶点的改变最为常见，也是肿瘤耐药研究和干预的重点。

Among these, upregulation of MDR efflux pumps and target modification are most frequent and are key focuses in research and intervention for cancer drug resistance.

临床新药开发需针对具体耐药机制精准设计。

Clinical new drug development needs to target specific resistance mechanisms for precise intervention.

未来展望Future Prospects

一、治疗策略
联合治疗 Therapeutics: Combination of Treatments

未来治疗趋势是多种治疗方式联合，包括化疗、辅助化疗、新辅助化疗（术前）以及免疫治疗和放疗。

The future trend in therapy is the combination of multiple treatment modalities, including chemotherapy, adjuvant chemotherapy, neoadjuvant chemotherapy (before surgery), immunotherapy, and radiotherapy.

联合用药能提升治疗效果，对不同类型肿瘤或不同分期患者制定最优方案。

Combination regimens can improve therapeutic outcomes and allow the design of optimal protocols for different cancer types or stages.

在临床实践中，化疗与放疗、免疫治疗等联用已成为主流。

In clinical practice, combinations of chemotherapy, radiotherapy, and immunotherapy have become mainstream.

二、更优的患者分层与个体化医疗 Better Stratification of Patients and Personalized Medicine

需整合多种大数据源（如医学影像、基因组、蛋白质组等）进行深入分析。

It is necessary to integrate multiple big data sources (e.g., medical imaging, genomics, proteomics) for deep analysis.

人工智能的发展为患者分层和治疗决策智能化带来新的可能。

The development of artificial intelligence brings new possibilities for intelligent patient stratification and therapeutic decision-making.

老师讲到，精准分层可以帮助选择最适合的治疗方案，提升疗效和生存率。

The instructor mentioned that precise stratification helps choose the best treatment plan, improving efficacy and survival rates.

需权衡数据收集与分析的成本，以及益处/风险的平衡。

Cost of data collection and analysis, and the benefit/risk balance, must be carefully considered.

未来医疗仍应注重经济可行性和实际效益。

Future healthcare should also focus on economic viability and practical benefits.

三、更深入理解耐药机制与细胞适应性，开发新药及新策略 Better Understand Resistances & Cell Adaptation Processes to Develop New Molecules or Therapeutic Strategies

深化对肿瘤固有耐药与获得性耐药的机制研究。

Deeper research into mechanisms of intrinsic and acquired resistance in tumors.

探寻肿瘤细胞适应性变化，为新分子靶点和新辅助策略的研发提供依据。

Exploration of cancer cell adaptation provides a basis for new molecular targets and the development of new adjuvant strategies.

老师补充，未来研发应关注解决耐药与肿瘤适应问题，推动联合和个体化治疗进步。

The instructor added that future development should focus on overcoming resistance and adaptation issues to advance combined and individualized therapies.

制定创新靶点及联合用药策略，将成为提升治疗效果的关键。

Designing new drug targets and combination treatment strategies will be key to improving clinical outcomes.

072025-10-23

肿瘤发生的标志性特征Hallmarks of Cancer

上皮细胞的细胞连接与细胞外基质Epithelial cell adhesion & extracellular matrix

EMT/MET在生理与病理中的作用EMT/MET in physiopathology

肿瘤发生的标志性特征Hallmarks of Cancer

1
肿瘤发生有一系列公认的“标志性特征”，指导肿瘤细胞生物学。 Cancer development is defined by a set of recognized "hallmarks" that guide cancer cell biology.
2
这些特征包括持续增殖信号、逃避生长抑制、表型可塑性上调、非突变性表观遗传重编程、调控细胞代谢、逃避免疫破坏、抵抗细胞死亡、获得无限增殖潜能、基因组不稳定与突变、促肿瘤炎症、衰老细胞、多态微生物群、诱导或获取血管、激活侵袭和转移等。 These features include sustaining proliferative signaling, evading growth suppressors, unlocking phenotypic plasticity, nonmutational epigenetic reprogramming, deregulating cellular metabolism, avoiding immune destruction, resisting cell death, enabling replicative immortality, genome instability & mutation, tumor-promoting inflammation, senescent cells, polymorphic microbiomes, inducing or accessing vasculature, and activating invasion & metastasis.
3
激活侵袭和转移，诱导或获取血管，表型可塑性上调等，都是肿瘤恶性进展和转移的关键过程。 Activating invasion & metastasis, inducing or accessing vasculature, and unlocking phenotypic plasticity are all key processes in malignant progression and metastasis of cancer.

上皮细胞的细胞连接与细胞外基质Epithelial cell adhesion & extracellular matrix

1
上皮细胞之间、以及与基底膜/细胞外基质之间通过多种连接结构紧密结合。 Epithelial cells are tightly connected to each other and to the basement membrane/extracellular matrix through various junctional structures.
2
细胞骨架与细胞连接相连，提供机械强度和信号传递。 The cytoskeleton is linked to cell junctions, providing mechanical strength and signal transduction.
3
上皮组织位于最表面，其下为结缔组织和富含细胞的细胞外基质（ECM），基底板（basal lamina）连接两者。 The epithelial tissue is found on the apical surface, followed by connective tissue and a cell-rich ECM beneath, both linked by the basal lamina.
4
EMT（上皮-间充质转化）：上皮细胞丧失极性和连接，获得迁移和侵袭能力。 EMT (epithelial-mesenchymal transition): Epithelial cells lose polarity and junctions, gaining migratory and invasive properties.
5
MET（间充质-上皮转化）：间充质细胞恢复上皮特性。 MET (mesenchymal-epithelial transition): Mesenchymal cells regain epithelial characteristics.
6
EMT/MET在发育过程中是正常现象，但在肿瘤转移中被异常激活。 EMT/MET are normal during development but are abnormally reactivated during cancer metastasis.
7
EMT表现为上皮命运丧失、连接改变、极性转换、细胞外基质重塑和迁移侵袭能力增强。 EMT is characterized by loss of epithelial fate, altered junctions, polarity switch, ECM remodeling, and enhanced migration/invasion.

EMT/MET在生理与病理中的作用EMT/MET in physiopathology

1
EMT在胚胎发育（如原肠运动）和伤口愈合等过程中起重要作用。 EMT plays an important role in embryonic development (e.g., gastrulation) and wound healing.
2
在肿瘤转移过程中，原位癌细胞通过EMT变为侵袭型，进入血液（内渗），形成循环肿瘤细胞（CTCs）。 During cancer metastasis, in situ tumor cells undergo EMT to become invasive, intravasate into blood (intravasation), and form circulating tumor cells (CTCs).
3
CTCs离开血管系统后，通过MET转换回上皮状态，在新部位形成微转移灶（micrometastasis）并最终成为转移瘤。 Once CTCs exit vasculature, they undergo MET to revert back to epithelial state, forming micrometastasis at new sites and eventually establishing metastatic tumors.
4
EMT和MET循环推动癌细胞在肿瘤转移各阶段的可塑性变化。 Cycles of EMT and MET drive the plasticity of cancer cells through different stages of metastasis.
老师强调
EMT和MET是生物体发育时期的正常现象，但在肿瘤转移中被异常激活。 The instructor emphasized: EMT and MET are normal during development, but are aberrantly reactivated during cancer metastasis.

上皮细胞的六类连接类型及蛋白组成6 Types of Junctions in Epithelial Cells & Their Protein Components

1
上皮细胞有六种主要的连接，其中四种属于细胞-细胞连接，另外两种为细胞-基质连接。 There are six main types of junctions in epithelial cells: four are cell-cell junctions, the other two are cell-matrix junctions.
细胞-细胞连接包括
紧密连接、黏着连接、桥粒和缝隙连接。 Cell-cell junctions include: tight junctions, adherens junctions, desmosomes, and gap junctions.
细胞-基质连接包括
半桥粒和黏着斑。 Cell-matrix junctions include: hemidesmosomes and focal adhesions.
4
这些连接结构由上至下，依次排列于上皮细胞的顶端到基底面。 These junctions are arranged from apical to basal on the epithelial cell.

紧密连接（Tight Junction）

5
紧密连接位于最顶端，将相邻细胞在顶端牢固封闭。 Tight junctions are located at the apical-most region and tightly seal adjacent cells at the top.
6
紧密连接的主要组成蛋白为claudin、occludin和JAMs（连接黏附分子），并有ZO1、ZO2、ZO3等细胞质骨架连接蛋白。 Main components are claudins, occludins, JAMs (Junctional Adhesion Molecules), plus cytoplasmic scaffolds ZO-1, ZO-2, ZO-3.
7
其作用一是作为细胞外基质的屏障，阻止液体和大分子通过，仅允许选择性离子通过。 Their first function is as a barrier against the extracellular matrix, blocking fluid or macromolecule passage, allowing only selective ions.
8
其作用之二是作为细胞膜的“围栏”，防止膜蛋白从顶端向侧面扩散，维持细胞极性。 The second function is as a “fence” in the plasma membrane, preventing diffusion of membrane proteins from apical to lateral areas, thus maintaining cell polarity.
9
紧密连接由跨膜同源性黏附蛋白和细胞质支架蛋白共同组成。 Tight junctions are formed by transmembrane homophilic adhesion proteins alongside cytoplasmic scaffold proteins.
10
免疫荧光下可见claudin-1围绕细胞顶部环状分布。 Ring-shaped distribution of claudin-1 at cell apex is observable by immunofluorescence.
锚定连接
黏着连接（Adherens Junctions）与桥粒（Desmosomes）
11
黏着连接主要由cadherin家族蛋白（如E-cadherin）通过钙离子依赖的同源性相互作用形成，确保同类型细胞间的强连接，并与F-肌动蛋白丝连接。 Adherens junctions are mainly formed by cadherin family proteins (e.g., E-cadherin) via calcium-dependent homophilic interactions, ensuring strong adhesion between like cells and linking to F-actin filaments.
12
桥粒由非经典cadherin（如desmoglein, desmocollin）和中间丝连接，增强组织抗拉强度。 Desmosomes use non-classical cadherins (desmoglein, desmocollin) connected to intermediate filaments, increasing tissue tensile strength.
13
黏着连接和桥粒均为钙依赖，能承受机械拉伸和收缩。 Both adherens junctions and desmosomes are calcium-dependent, allowing cells to withstand mechanical stretch and contraction.
14
同类型细胞更易通过同类型cadherin聚集并形成分区，保证组织整合与分化。 Cells with the same cadherin type preferentially adhere and partition with each other, supporting organized tissue structure and segregation during development.

缝隙连接（Gap Junction）

15
缝隙连接由connexin蛋白组成，允许小分子和离子在细胞间自由通过，有助于信号交流和电连接。 Gap junctions are built from connexin proteins, enabling the passage of small molecules and ions between cells, facilitating intercellular communication and electric coupling.

细胞-基质连接（Cell-Matrix Junctions）：半桥粒与黏着斑

16
半桥粒和黏着斑属于细胞-基质锚定连接，其中整合素（integrin）为主要跨膜受体，连接细胞与细胞外基质组分（如纤维连接蛋白、胶原等）。 Hemidesmosomes and focal adhesions are cell-matrix anchoring junctions, where integrins act as primary transmembrane receptors linking cells to ECM proteins (e.g., fibronectin, collagen).
17
整合素为α/β二聚体，能实现异源性结合，对基质机械信号和信号转换至关重要。 Integrins are α/β heterodimers, mediating heterophilic interactions and playing key roles in transmitting mechanical signals from the ECM.

其它黏附分子以及功能总结

18
选择素（Selectin）介导异源性瞬时细胞-细胞连接，常在炎症、白细胞滚动或肿瘤相关转移中扮演重要角色。 Selectins mediate heterophilic, transient cell-cell adhesion, important for inflammation, leukocyte rolling, and tumor metastasis.
19
IgSF细胞黏附分子（IgCAMs）主要参与可调节的细胞黏附和信号传导，多存在于神经细胞和发育过程。 IgCAMs (Immunoglobulin superfamily CAMs) modulate cell adhesion and signaling, commonly found in neural tissue and during development.
20
所有连接结构都与细胞骨架紧密结合，维持组织结构和信号稳态。 All junctions are tightly coupled to the cytoskeleton, supporting tissue architecture and stable signal maintenance.
锚定连接与桥粒
钙黏蛋白（Adherens junctions and Desmosomes: cadherins）
1
黏着连接（Adherens junctions）属于细胞-细胞锚定结构，主要连接胞质中的肌动蛋白丝。 Adherens junctions are cell–cell anchoring structures that primarily link actin filaments in the cytoplasm.
2
黏着连接由经典钙黏蛋白（如E-cadherin）跨膜蛋白介导，两侧细胞的cadherin蛋白相互同源性结合。 Adherens junctions are mediated by classical cadherins (e.g., E-cadherin), which interact homophilically between cells.
3
钙离子对cadherin结构和功能至关重要，缺钙会导致连接松解。 Calcium ions are essential for cadherin structure and function, and the absence of calcium loosens the junction.
4
cadherin胞内基序与catenin（p120、β-catenin、α-catenin）结合，再与vinculin连接至F-肌动蛋白。 The cytoplasmic domain of cadherins binds catenins (p120, β-catenin, α-catenin), which connect to vinculin and then link to F-actin filaments.
5
搭建“带状”结构，围绕细胞皮质环绕，为细胞群提供机械完整性和形态可塑性。 This builds a belt-like structure encircling the cell cortex, providing mechanical integrity and morphological plasticity.
6
同型细胞优先通过同类cadherin聚集，实现“细胞排序”现象，有助于组织分区和发育过程。 Cells with identical cadherin types preferentially cluster together, achieving “cell sorting” and supporting tissue compartmentalization and development.
7
桥粒（Desmosomes）同属锚定连接，但连接中间丝（如角蛋白或结蛋白），赋予组织更高抗拉强度。 Desmosomes are also anchoring junctions, but connect intermediate filaments (e.g., keratin or desmin), endowing tissues with greater tensile strength.
8
桥粒由非经典钙黏蛋白（desmoglein和desmocollin）组成，通过胞质适配蛋白（plakoglobin、plakophilin、desmoplakin）与中间丝连接。 Desmosomes consist of non-classical cadherins (desmoglein, desmocollin) and connect to intermediate filaments via cytoplasmic adaptor proteins (plakoglobin, plakophilin, desmoplakin).
9
桥粒主要为皮肤、心肌等需应对张力的组织提供牢固的细胞黏附。 Desmosomes mainly provide firm adhesion in tissues like skin and myocardium, which are subject to mechanical stress.
细胞间瞬时黏附
选择素（Transient cell-cell adhesion: selectins）
10
选择素是一类介导细胞间瞬时、弱结合的分子，广泛存在于血液与免疫应答过程中，且不限定于上皮细胞。 Selectins are molecules that mediate transient and weak cell–cell adhesion, widely present in blood and immune responses and not restricted to epithelial cells.
11
选择素通过识别糖蛋白/糖脂表面的寡糖结构发挥作用，属于异源性结合。 Selectins function by recognizing oligosaccharide structures on glycoproteins or glycolipids at the cell surface, thus mediating heterophilic interactions.
12
选择素蛋白包括P-选择素（血小板、内皮细胞）、L-选择素（白细胞）、E-选择素（活化内皮细胞）。 Types include P-selectin (on platelets and endothelial cells), L-selectin (on leukocytes), and E-selectin (on activated endothelial cells).
13
选择素-糖结合相对较弱，因此细胞在血管内可“滚动”，这为白细胞/肿瘤细胞在炎症、转移时进入组织提供通道。 The selectin–sugar interaction is relatively weak, which allows cells to “roll” along the vascular endothelium—a prerequisite for leukocyte or tumor cell extravasation during inflammation or metastasis.
14
在肿瘤细胞/白细胞血管滚动过程中，细胞表面的选择素配体可与内皮细胞选择素结合，附着进一步增强后，细胞通过整合素实现强黏附并穿出内皮。 During tumor cell or leukocyte rolling in blood vessels, cell-surface selectin ligands bind endothelial selectins. When adhesion is strengthened, cells use integrins for firm adhesion and transendothelial migration.
15
血小板包裹可保护肿瘤细胞免受NK细胞攻击，进一步促进其转移。 Platelets can shield tumor cells from NK cell attack in the bloodstream, thus promoting metastasis.
通道/缝隙连接
连环蛋白（Channels/gap junctions: connexins）
16
缝隙连接由连环蛋白（connexins）组成，在相邻细胞间形成水溶性通道，连接两侧细胞质。 Gap junctions are composed of connexins, creating hydrophilic channels between adjacent cells and connecting their cytoplasm.
17
每个“连接体”（connexon）由6个连接蛋白组成，两个细胞的连接体对接形成完整通道。 Each connexon consists of 6 connexins, and two connexons from adjacent cells align to form a complete channel.
18
缝隙连接孔径为1.4纳米，可让无机离子和分子量小于1千道尔顿的小分子自由通过。 The pore size of gap junctions is 1.4 nm, allowing passage of inorganic ions and small molecules less than 1 kDa.
19
通道的开闭受Ca2+浓度、pH和电压调控，实现化学和电信号的耦联。 The opening and closing of channels are regulated by Ca2+, pH, and voltage, enabling both chemical and electrical coupling.
20
缝隙连接的结构可为同源或异源（同/不同亚型连环蛋白构成），不同组合影响其生理性质。 Gap junctions may be homomeric or heteromeric, depending on the combinations of connexin subtypes, thus conferring diverse physiological properties.

补充（结合课程讲解）：

21
老师强调，cadherin的类型差异决定细胞“聚集”和“分区”现象，有助于胚胎发育和组织形态塑造。 The instructor emphasized that differences in cadherin subtypes determine “sorting” and “partitioning” behaviors, essential for embryonic development and tissue architecture.
22
desmosome结构尤其增强了皮肤和心脏等需高机械强度组织的稳定性。 Desmosome structure in particular increases the stability of tissues requiring mechanical strength, such as skin and heart.
23
选择素介导的瞬时结合为白细胞迁移、炎症反应及肿瘤细胞转移提供重要调控节点。 Selectin-mediated transient adhesion is a critical regulatory step for leukocyte migration, inflammatory responses, and tumor metastasis.
24
缝隙连接在心肌细胞中尤为重要，可实现细胞间的电信号同步，保障心脏有序收缩。 Gap junctions are especially important in cardiac muscle, allowing electrical signal synchronization for orderly heart contraction.

细胞-细胞连接与疾病 Cell-cell junctions and diseases

紧密连接
claudin Tight junction: claudin Claudin突变导致屏障功能下降，引起肠道功能障碍、哮喘和神经退行性疾病。 Claudin mutations cause barrier defects, leading to enteric disorders, asthma, and neurodegeneration.
锚定连接
黏着连接（经典钙黏蛋白） Anchoring junction: adherens junction – classical cadherins 经典钙黏蛋白（如E-cadherin）异常可导致黄斑营养不良等眼部疾病。 Defects in classical cadherins (such as E-cadherin) can cause eye diseases like macular dystrophy.
锚定连接
桥粒（非经典钙黏蛋白） Anchoring junction: desmosome – non-classical cadherins 桥粒相关蛋白（plakophilin、desmoplakin、desmoglein 或 desmocollin）突变会导致心律失常型心肌病。 Mutations in desmosomal proteins (plakophilin, desmoplakin, desmoglein or desmocollin) cause arrhythmogenic cardiomyopathy. 若desmoglein被自身抗体攻击，会引起大疱性皮肤病（如天疱疮），表现为水疱和皮肤脆弱。 If desmoglein is attacked by autoantibodies, it leads to blistering skin diseases such as pemphigus, manifesting as blisters and skin fragility.
缝隙连接
连环蛋白（connexin） Gap junction: connexin Connexin突变可能导致心房颤动等心律失常。 Connexin mutations can cause heart arrhythmias like atrial fibrillation. 某些connexin变异还可引发外周神经系统疾病，如Charcot-Marie-Tooth病。 Certain connexin abnormalities can also cause peripheral nervous system diseases, such as Charcot-Marie-Tooth disease.
5
有些病毒和细菌能利用细胞连接进入组织，引起感染。 Certain viruses and bacteria can exploit cell junctions to invade tissues and cause infections.
瞬时细胞-细胞黏附
选择素 Transient cell-cell adhesion: selectin 选择素介导的瞬时连接对免疫炎症反应至关重要，功能障碍会影响白细胞迁移、炎症反应等。 Selectin-mediated transient adhesion is crucial for innate immune responses; dysfunction disrupts leukocyte migration and inflammatory reactions.

EMT = 上皮-间充质转分化 EMT = epithelial–mesenchymal transition

MET = 间充质-上皮转分化 MET = mesenchymal–epithelial transition

1
EMT过程中，上皮细胞失去紧密连接、黏着连接和桥粒，出现极性丧失和细胞迁移能力增强。 During EMT, epithelial cells lose tight junctions, adherens junctions, and desmosomes, resulting in loss of polarity and increased migratory capacity.
EMT相关的细胞连接核心构件包括
Claudin（紧密连接）、E-cadherin（黏着连接）、Desmocollin和plakophilin（桥粒）、Connexin（缝隙连接）。 Core cellular junction components in EMT include Claudins (tight junction), E-cadherin (adherens junction), Desmocollin/plakophilin (desmosome), and Connexin (gap junction).
3
这些蛋白不仅作为肿瘤抑制因子，还可在特定情境下促进集体迁移、肿瘤细胞和内皮间的沟通，促进转移。 These proteins act as tumor suppressors but can also support collective migration and communication between tumor cells and endothelium, thus promoting metastasis in some contexts.
4
MET阶段，间充质细胞重新表达E-cadherin等分子，恢复细胞极性与连接，重新形成上皮结构。 During MET, mesenchymal cells re-express molecules such as E-cadherin, restore polarity and adhesion, and re-establish epithelial structures.
5
EMT和MET由多个转录因子（如ZEB家族、SNAIL、SLUG、TWIST1）调控，上调间充质相关蛋白并抑制上皮蛋白表达。 EMT and MET are regulated by various transcription factors (e.g., ZEB family, SNAIL, SLUG, TWIST1), which upregulate mesenchymal proteins and repress epithelial markers.
6
选择素、VCAM及整合素在肿瘤细胞穿出血管、发生微转移（MET）中也起重要作用。 Selectins, VCAM, and integrins play a key role in tumor cell extravasation and micrometastasis (involving MET).
课堂重点强调
细胞连接重塑是肿瘤转移、迁移及细胞表型变化（EMT/MET）不可或缺的步骤，且调控复杂，高度动态。 The lecture emphasized that junction remodeling is an essential and dynamic process for metastasis, migration, and cell phenotype changes during EMT/MET.
细胞-基质锚定连接
黏着斑和半桥粒中的整合素 Cell-matrix anchoring junctions: integrins in focal adhesions and hemidesmosomes
细胞-基质锚定连接
黏着斑中的整合素 Cell-matrix anchoring junctions: integrins in focal adhesions

整合素是一类跨膜受体，介导细胞与细胞外基质（如纤维连接蛋白fibronectin）的连接。 Integrins are transmembrane receptors mediating the connection between the cell and the extracellular matrix (such as fibronectin).

以α5β1整合素为例，其胞外结构与基质结合，胞内尾部通过talin和kindlin连接。 For example, α5β1 integrin binds matrix externally and connects via talin and kindlin intracellularly.

talin和kindlin协助整合素激活，并招募vinculin，形成链式连接，最终将整合素连接到细胞骨架肌动蛋白（actin）上。 Talin and kindlin facilitate integrin activation and recruit vinculin, creating a chain linkage between integrin and cytoskeletal actin.

黏着斑（focal adhesion）包含多种蛋白（如FAK、paxillin、zyxin、α-actinin、VASP等），并呈多层结构：靠近膜为整合素信号层，其下为力传递层，再往下为肌动蛋白调控层。 Focal adhesions contain proteins like FAK, paxillin, zyxin, α-actinin, and VASP, and show multilayered organization: integrin signaling layer near the membrane, force transduction layer below, and actin regulatory layer deeper.

肌动蛋白应力丝与黏着斑连接，通过收缩产生机械力，促进细胞迁移。 Actin stress fibers attach to focal adhesions, whose contraction generates mechanical forces that drive cell migration.

黏着斑的动态组装与解体（周转）对于细胞迁移至关重要，细胞能据机械刺激调整连接。 Focal adhesion turnover (assembly and disassembly) is crucial for migration; the cell adapts adhesion according to mechanical stimuli.

老师补充
当细胞因外部张力增加时，黏着斑数量和肌动蛋白应力丝会同步增加，以增强对基质的附着和张力传递能力。 Teacher's supplement: When external tension rises, both focal adhesions and actin stress fibers increase to strengthen adhesion and force transmission.
细胞-基质锚定连接
半桥粒中的整合素 Cell-matrix anchoring junctions: integrins in hemidesmosomes

半桥粒是位于上皮细胞基底面的结构，将细胞牢牢锚定在基底膜（基膜/基质）上。 Hemidesmosomes are structures at the basal surface of epithelial cells anchoring them tightly to the basement membrane.

半桥粒以整合素α6β4为核心成分，胞外连接层粘连蛋白（laminin）和ⅩⅦ型胶原，胞内连接plectin及斑块蛋白，最终与中间丝（角蛋白keratin）结合。 Hemidesmosomes feature integrin α6β4 as the core, linking extracellularly to laminin and collagen XVII, and intracellularly binding plectin and plaque proteins to keratin intermediate filaments.

这种连接增强了细胞抵抗外界机械应力的能力，对皮肤等高应力环境尤其重要。 This junction markedly strengthens cellular resistance to mechanical stress, vital for tissues like skin under high stress.

电子显微镜下，半桥粒表现为细胞与基底膜间的致密结构。 Under electron microscopy, hemidesmosomes are dense anchoring complexes between cell and basement membrane.

3
细胞-基质锚定连接相关疾病 Cell-matrix anchoring junctions and diseases

半桥粒相关蛋白，如整合素α6β4、ⅩⅦ型胶原、层粘连蛋白或plectin，若发生基因突变，会导致交界型表皮松解性大疱症（JEB），表现为皮肤极易起水疱和剥离。 Mutations in hemidesmosome proteins (integrin α6β4, collagen XVII, laminin, or plectin) lead to junctional epidermolysis bullosa (JEB), characterized by extremely fragile, blister-prone skin.

该病反映出半桥粒在维持上皮组织完整性和机械强度中的关键作用。 This disease highlights the essential role of hemidesmosomes in tissue cohesion and mechanical stability.

老师强调
半桥粒连接的是中间丝（而黏着斑连接的是肌动蛋白），因此两者分别提供“稳定锚定”和“动态迁移”功能。 Teacher’s emphasis: Hemidesmosomes anchor intermediate filaments for stable attachment, while focal adhesions connect actin for dynamic migration.

EMT与MET过程中细胞-基质连接重塑及其功能 Cell-matrix junction remodeling during EMT and MET

1
EMT和MET的基本定义 EMT（上皮-间充质转分化）是上皮细胞获得间充质表型、增强迁移和侵袭能力的过程。 EMT (epithelial-mesenchymal transition) is the process by which epithelial cells acquire mesenchymal traits, leading to increased migration and invasion.

MET（间充质-上皮转分化）则为上述过程的逆转，细胞重新表达上皮标志恢复极性和连接。

MET (mesenchymal-epithelial transition) is the reverse process, where cells regain epithelial markers and reestablish polarity and junctions.

2
EMT/MET过程中的细胞连接变化 在EMT过程中，紧密连接、黏着连接和桥粒逐渐减少或消失；半桥粒解体，细胞极性丧失。 During EMT, tight junctions, adherens junctions, and desmosomes are progressively lost; hemidesmosomes disassemble, and polarity is lost.

同时，细胞开始形成许多黏着斑与肌动蛋白应力纤维，促进定向迁移。

Meanwhile, cells form numerous focal adhesions and actin stress fibers, supporting directed migration.

在MET阶段，这些变化逆转，细胞重新形成紧密连接、桥粒和半桥粒，极性和稳态恢复。

During MET, these alterations are reversed, with re-formation of tight junctions, desmosomes, and hemidesmosomes, restoring polarity and stability.

3
细胞-基质连接类型及作用 半桥粒（hemidesmosome）位于上皮细胞基底，连接基底膜和中间丝骨架，负责强力锚定并起肿瘤抑制作用。 Hemidesmosomes anchor epithelial cells to the basement membrane via intermediate filaments, conferring strong attachment and acting as tumor suppressors.

黏着斑（focal adhesion）通过整合素与肌动蛋白束连接，增强机械感应与信号传递，促进细胞迁移，参与肿瘤的侵袭与转移。

Focal adhesions link integrins to actin bundles, enhancing mechanosensing and signaling, promoting cell migration and tumor metastasis.

EMT阶段，黏着斑增多，细胞粘附增强，迁移活性提升；而半桥粒数量下降，细胞稳固性降低。

During EMT, focal adhesions increase, adhesion and migration are enhanced; hemidesmosomes decrease, reducing tissue stability.

4
EMT与MET的分子调控 EMT受到转录因子（如ZEB家族、SNAIL/SLUG、TWIST1）调控，上调间充质标志物（N-钙黏蛋白、波形蛋白、纤维连接蛋白、beta1和beta3型整合素、基质金属蛋白酶MMPs），下调上皮标志（E-钙黏蛋白、半桥粒蛋白等）。 EMT is regulated by transcription factors (ZEB family, SNAIL/SLUG, TWIST1), upregulating mesenchymal markers (N-cadherin, vimentin, fibronectin, beta1/beta3 integrins, MMPs) and downregulating epithelial markers (E-cadherin, hemidesmosome proteins).

MET过程中上述调控反向发生，细胞重新表达上皮连接蛋白。

During MET, this regulation is reversed and epithelial junction proteins are re-expressed.

5
黏着斑、半桥粒与机械转导 黏着斑和半桥粒共同参与机械信号的感知与转导。 Focal adhesions and hemidesmosomes both participate in mechanosensing and mechanotransduction.

机械力通过黏着斑（整合素-肌动蛋白）、半桥粒（整合素-中间丝）传递至细胞核，影响YAP等转录因子及染色质，调控基因表达。

Mechanical force is transmitted to the nucleus via focal adhesion (integrin-actin) or hemidesmosome (integrin-intermediate filament), modulating transcription factors like YAP and chromatin to regulate gene expression.

黏着斑信号传导活跃，能激活YAP、FAK、PI3K等蛋白，驱动细胞增殖和侵袭。

Focal adhesion signaling is active and can activate YAP, FAK, PI3K, driving cell proliferation and invasion.

半桥粒主要维持组织强度和上皮稳态；其降低使细胞更易迁移，有利于肿瘤转移。

Hemidesmosomes mainly maintain tissue strength and epithelial homeostasis; their reduction facilitates migration and metastasis.

6
分裂时的连接动态 细胞分裂期邻近细胞的紧密连接和黏着连接减弱，但少量连接保留，便于分裂完成后形态与极性的重建。 During mitosis, neighboring cells’ tight and adherens junctions are weakened, but some junctions remain to help restore cell morphology and polarity post-division.
7
课堂补充要点 老师强调，细胞-基质连接的重塑对于迁移、机械信号感知及肿瘤侵袭至关重要，EMT过程中“失去半桥粒、获得更多黏着斑”是关键特征。 The instructor stressed that remodeling of cell-matrix junctions is critical for migration, mechanotransduction, and tumor invasion. During EMT, "loss of hemidesmosomes and gain of focal adhesions" is a hallmark.

机械信号经由细胞骨架网络快速传导至细胞核，调控增殖、分化与癌变等下游生物学效应。

Mechanical signals are rapidly propagated to the nucleus through the cytoskeletal network, modulating proliferation, differentiation, and tumorigenesis.

细胞外基质（ECM）The extracellular matrix (ECM)

1
细胞外基质（ECM）的基本组成 细胞外基质由近300种蛋白质构成，包括蛋白聚糖、胶原纤维、纤维连接蛋白、整合素、糖蛋白和弹性蛋白。 The extracellular matrix is composed of nearly 300 proteins, including proteoglycans, collagen fibrils, fibronectin, integrins, glycoproteins, and elastin proteins.
2
主要结构及其功能 ECM的主要结构成分有约40种蛋白聚糖（如透明质酸、perlecan、decorin、aggrecan）、约40种胶原蛋白、200多种糖蛋白（如层粘连蛋白、nidogen、纤维连接蛋白）以及弹性蛋白。 The main structural components of ECM include about 40 proteoglycans (such as hyaluronan, perlecan, decorin, aggrecan), around 40 types of collagen, over 200 glycoproteins (such as laminin, nidogen, fibronectin), and elastin proteins.

蛋白质部分为纤维状提供支撑，糖胺聚糖（GAGs，例如透明质酸）为基质提供吸水和缓冲能力。

The protein fraction, especially the fibrous proteins, provide support, while glycosaminoglycans (GAGs, such as hyaluronan) allow for water retention and shock absorption in the matrix.

3
ECM的功能和特殊性 ECM对不同组织有不同物理性质：骨和牙齿中为钙化结构；角膜内为透明结构；肌腱中呈绳索状。 The ECM gives different physical properties to tissues: structures are calcified in bone/teeth; transparent in the cornea; and rope-like in tendons.

这些成分主要由稀疏分布的成纤维细胞或类似细胞（如成骨细胞）分泌。

These components are primarily secreted by sparsely distributed fibroblasts or related cells (such as osteoblasts).

4
ECM与细胞连接 纤维连接蛋白通过整合素与细胞膜结合，将ECM连接到细胞内的细胞骨架（如肌动蛋白）上。 Fibronectin binds to the cell membrane via integrins, linking the ECM to the intracellular cytoskeleton (such as actin).

这种连接支持了细胞的机械支撑、信号转导和细胞运动等多种功能。

This connection supports various functions including mechanical support, signal transduction, and cell motility.

5
ECM在组织中的分布示例 电子显微镜下，角膜中的成纤维细胞被大量胶原纤维护持和包围。 Under electron microscopy, fibroblasts in the cornea are supported and surrounded by abundant collagen fibrils.
补充
课堂要点 老师强调，ECM不仅仅是结构骨架，还在细胞迁移、信号转导、分化及组织稳态中有重要作用。 The instructor emphasized that ECM is not just a structural scaffold; it also plays key roles in cell migration, signaling, differentiation, and tissue homeostasis.

不同组织依据ECM成分的差异呈现出不同的物理和生物特性。

Different tissues display distinct physical and biological properties depending on their ECM composition.

糖胺聚糖（GAGs）、蛋白聚糖Glycosaminoglycans (GAGs), Proteoglycans

1
GAG的结构与特性 GAG是一类二糖重复单位组成的大分子，带有负电荷、刚性和体积庞大。 Glycosaminoglycans (GAGs) are large molecules composed of repeating disaccharide units, negatively charged, stiff, and bulky.

这些负电荷使GAG能吸引Na+和水分，从而保持组织膨胀和抵御外部压缩力。

The negative charge allows GAGs to attract Na+ and water, creating tissue turgor that resists compressive forces.

典型代表为透明质酸（hyaluronan），其结构式为(-4GlcUAβ1-3GlcNAcβ1-)n。

A typical example is hyaluronan, with the structure (-4GlcUAβ1-3GlcNAcβ1-)n.

2
GAG的功能与生理作用 GAG主要功能包括抵抗外界压力，促进细胞迁移和伤口愈合。 GAGs mainly function to resist external pressure and facilitate cell migration and wound healing.

老师补充，GAG通过与水和离子的结合，在细胞外形成一个保护层，抑制红细胞等大分子进入，形成“水化屏障”。

The instructor added that GAGs, by binding water and ions, create a protective layer in the extracellular space that blocks large molecules like red blood cells, forming a "hydration barrier".

此外，透明质酸参与修复、迁移及填充作用，也是组织再生的关键成分。

Additionally, hyaluronan is involved in repair, migration, and filling, and is key for tissue regeneration.

3
蛋白聚糖的结构与类型 蛋白聚糖是由蛋白核心和多个GAG链（如硫酸软骨素、硫酸肝素、硫酸角质素）组成的复合物。 Proteoglycans are complexes consisting of a core protein and multiple GAG chains (such as chondroitin sulfate, heparan sulfate, keratan sulfate).

典型蛋白聚糖包括decorin、syndecan、perlecan和aggrecan等，分别分布于纤维母细胞、上皮细胞、基底膜和软骨中。

Typical proteoglycans include decorin, syndecan, perlecan, and aggrecan, found in fibroblasts, epithelial cells, basal lamina, and cartilage, respectively.

4
生理功能 蛋白聚糖及GAG网络能有效抵抗压缩力，保护组织结构，并允许养分、代谢物和激素在血液与组织间弥散。 The network of proteoglycans and GAGs resists compressive forces, protects tissue structure, and permits diffusion of nutrients, metabolites, and hormones between blood and tissues.
5
小结 GAG和蛋白聚糖是细胞外基质的重要成分，对维持组织稳定、信号传导、细胞迁移和再生等多种过程至关重要。 GAGs and proteoglycans are key components of the extracellular matrix and are essential for tissue stability, signal transduction, cell migration, and regeneration.

胶原蛋白Collagens

1
胶原蛋白的结构 胶原蛋白由前胶原α螺旋在高尔基体加工、酶切后形成成熟三螺旋结构。 Collagen is synthesized as procollagen α-helices, processed in the Golgi, and enzymatically cleaved to form mature triple-helical collagen.
2
胶原蛋白的类型和特性 纤维型胶原（如I、III、V型）为亲水性、无弹性结构，广泛存在于皮肤和骨骼中，主要负责抵抗拉伸应力。 Fibrillar collagens (types I, III, V) are hydrophilic, non-elastic, widely found in skin and bone, and mainly resist tensile forces.

它们的空间排列受细胞张力调节。

Their spatial organization is regulated by cell tension.

3
其他类型胶原 此外，还有串珠型（如VI型）、锚定型（如VII型）、FACIT类（如XII、XIV型）以及网状形成型胶原（如IV型）。 There are also beaded (type VI), anchoring (type VII), FACIT (types XII, XIV), and network-forming collagen (type IV).

IV型胶原主要分布在基底膜（基底板）中，形成支撑网络。

Type IV collagen is a main component of the basal lamina, forming a supporting network.

4
小结 胶原蛋白赋予组织高的机械强度和结构完整性，对皮肤张力、骨骼支持等至关重要。 Collagen provides high mechanical strength and structural integrity to tissues, crucial for tension in skin and support in bone.
糖蛋白
纤维连接蛋白Glycoproteins: Fibronectin
1
纤维连接蛋白的结构 纤维连接蛋白是一种N和O糖基化的二聚体大分子，可通过N-糖基化修饰。 Fibronectin is a large dimeric glycoprotein with N- and O-glycosylation, especially N-glycosylation.
2
分布和功能 血流中为可溶性二聚体，在细胞外基质中为纤维状。 It exists as soluble dimers in the bloodstream, and as fibrous fibrils in the extracellular matrix.

纤维连接蛋白通过整合素与细胞膜结合，并连接细胞内肌动蛋白，形成黏着斑，有助于细胞迁移。

Fibronectin binds to cell membranes via integrins, links to intracellular actin to form focal adhesions, and facilitates cell migration.

老师强调纤维连接蛋白能指引迁移路径，是创伤修复和组织再生的关键。

The instructor highlighted that fibronectin provides migration tracks, essential for wound healing and tissue regeneration.

弹性蛋白Elastin

1
弹性蛋白的分布和结构 弹性蛋白主要存在于皮肤真皮、动脉壁和肺泡等承受周期性拉伸的组织中。 Elastin is mainly found in tissues subjected to cyclic stretch, such as skin dermis, artery walls, and lung alveoli.

弹性蛋白形成疏水、可伸缩的网络，使基质在拉伸后能迅速恢复。

Elastin forms a hydrophobic, extensible network, enabling the matrix to rapidly recover after stretch.

2
生理作用 弹性蛋白赋予皮肤、动脉和肺良好的弹性和韧性，在体积和压力变化时维持器官功能稳定。 Elastin provides skin, arteries, and lungs with elasticity and resilience, maintaining organ function under changes in volume and pressure.
3
小结 弹性蛋白确保结缔组织、血管和呼吸道等器官在伸展和放松中反复循环而不受损伤。 Elastin ensures that connective tissues, vessels, and airways can repeatedly stretch and relax without damage.

ECM中营养物质、生长因子、细胞因子与激素的功能与肿瘤微环境的特点

Role of Nutrients, Growth Factors, Cytokines, and Hormones in ECM and Characteristics of the Tumor Microenvironment

1
ECM为细胞提供多种营养和信号分子 细胞外基质（ECM）不仅为细胞提供机械支撑，还含有各种营养物质、生长因子、细胞因子和激素。 The extracellular matrix (ECM) not only provides mechanical support for cells, but also contains various nutrients, growth factors (GFs), cytokines, and hormones.

这些分子通过调节细胞增殖、生存和迁移等过程，对肿瘤生长和微环境具有重要影响。

These molecules regulate processes such as cell proliferation, survival, and migration, significantly impacting tumor growth and the microenvironment.

2
肿瘤区域的营养物质与代谢产物分布不均 肿瘤组织内部，由于远离血管，营养物质、氧气和药物的含量逐渐降低，而乳酸、活性氧和坏死区则向中心积累。 Within tumor tissues, nutrients, oxygen, and drugs gradually decrease as the distance from blood vessels increases, whereas lactate, reactive oxygen species (ROS), and necrotic areas accumulate toward the center.

这导致肿瘤中心区域缺氧、酸性增高、坏死明显，也是药物难以渗透的原因。

This leads to hypoxia, increased acidity, and significant necrosis in the tumor core, and makes drug penetration more difficult.

老师补充
这种分布差异是肿瘤对治疗产生耐药的关键原因之一。

The instructor added: such gradients are key reasons for tumor resistance to therapies.

3
ECM中的胞外囊泡（EVs）特性及作用 ECM中还含有多种胞外囊泡（extracellular vesicles, EVs），如外泌体（30-100 nm）、微泡（100-500 nm）和凋亡小体（500 nm-5 μm）。 ECM also contains a variety of extracellular vesicles (EVs), including exosomes (30-100 nm), microvesicles (100-500 nm), and apoptotic bodies (500 nm-5 μm).

这些囊泡富含蛋白质、脂质、核酸和糖类等，可在细胞间运输信号并参与调控肿瘤转移、药物耐受等过程。

These vesicles are rich in proteins, lipids, nucleic acids, and carbohydrates, and can transfer signals between cells, modulating tumor metastasis and drug resistance.

4
囊泡可远距离或邻近作用，并辅助诊断 胞外囊泡不仅作用于邻近细胞，也可远距离影响其他组织，且可在血液、尿液和唾液中检测到。 Extracellular vesicles can affect both neighboring and distant cells, and are detectable in blood, urine, and saliva.

囊泡的成分在健康细胞和癌细胞之间存在差异，具有诊断和预后价值。

There are compositional differences between vesicles from healthy and cancer cells, providing value for diagnosis and prognosis.

5
囊泡在肿瘤进展和耐药中的作用 老师强调，胞外囊泡在肿瘤发生、进展、转移及化疗耐药中起重要作用，已成为肿瘤生物学研究的新热点。 The instructor emphasized: extracellular vesicles are crucial in tumor initiation, progression, metastasis, and chemotherapy resistance, making them a new frontier in cancer biology research.

细胞对细胞外基质的重塑Extracellular matrix remodeling by cells

1
细胞通过多步骤调节细胞外基质结构 细胞外基质（ECM）重塑包括黏附、收缩/排列、降解、内吞、沉积和交联等步骤。 Cells remodel the ECM through a multi-step process including adhesion, contraction/alignment, degradation, internalization, deposition, and crosslinking.
2
黏附（Adhesion） 细胞首先通过黏着斑（focal adhesion）结合于ECM，为后续重塑提供锚定点。 Cells initially attach to the ECM via focal adhesions, which serve as anchor points for subsequent remodeling.
3
收缩和排列（Contraction/Alignment） 细胞利用肌动蛋白-肌球蛋白系统（actomyosin）收缩、拉伸或排列ECM纤维，改变其空间分布。 Cells use the actomyosin system to contract, stretch, or align ECM fibers, altering their spatial arrangement.
4
