降解（Degradation） 细胞分泌金属蛋白酶（matrix metalloproteinases, MMPs）降解ECM成分，使微环境松解，为细胞迁移或新基质形成创造条件。 Cells secrete matrix metalloproteinases (MMPs) to degrade ECM components, loosening the microenvironment for cell migration or new matrix formation.
5
内吞（Internalization）与溶酶体降解 降解后的ECM片段可被内吞入细胞，随后在溶酶体中降解再利用。 Degraded ECM fragments can be internalized by cells and subsequently degraded in lysosomes for recycling.
6
沉积（Deposition） 细胞合成并分泌新的ECM分子（如胶原、纤维连接蛋白等），沉积于局部以修复或增强基质。 Cells synthesize and secrete new ECM molecules (e.g., collagen, fibronectin) for local deposition, repairing or reinforcing the matrix.
7
交联与修饰（Crosslinking and modifications） 沉积后的ECM分子可通过酶促交联和翻译后修饰（PTM）提高稳定性和刚性，赋予组织所需的生理特性。 Deposited ECM molecules undergo enzymatic crosslinking and post-translational modifications (PTM), increasing matrix stability and stiffness for tissue-specific functions.
8
ECM重塑的动态性和重要性 老师强调，ECM并非静态结构，而是细胞持续重塑的动态网络，对细胞迁移、发育、疾病及组织修复至关重要。 The instructor emphasized that the ECM is not a static scaffold, but a dynamic network continuously remodeled by cells, which is critical for migration, development, disease, and tissue repair.
薄型特化基质层
基底膜A thin specialized matrix sheet: the basal lamina (or basement membrane)
1
基底膜的定义与组成 基底膜是一种包被于上皮及部分其他细胞外，厚度约40-120 nm的致密基质层，包含多种特化分子。 The basal lamina is a dense specialized ECM layer (40-120 nm thick) underlying epithelial and some other cells, comprising multiple specialized molecules.

主要成分有整合素（integrin, 膜受体）、层粘连蛋白（laminin, 糖蛋白）、IV型胶原（collagen IV）、Perrican（蛋白聚糖）、Nidogen（糖蛋白）。

Its main components include integrins (transmembrane receptors), laminin (glycoprotein), collagen IV, perlecan (proteoglycan), and nidogen (glycoprotein).

2
基底膜的生理功能 基底膜连接上皮（或肌肉）与结缔组织，形成机械连接、起细胞屏障和分子过滤作用。 The basal lamina connects epithelia (or muscle) to connective tissue, providing mechanical linkage, a cell barrier, and a molecular filter.

除支架作用外，还调控组织再生、极性建立、细胞分化、增殖、生存和迁移等。

Besides structural support, it regulates tissue regeneration, cell polarity, differentiation, proliferation, survival, and migration.

3
基底膜结构及作用具体例子 在肾小球，基底膜（GBM）作为毛细血管滤过屏障，选择性允许小分子通过，阻止尿液和血液成分异常交换。 In glomeruli, the glomerular basement membrane (GBM) acts as a filtration barrier, selectively permitting small molecules while preventing abnormal exchange between urine and blood.
老师补充
保留完整的基底膜有助于损伤修复；如果受损，组织难以再生。

The instructor added: maintaining an intact basal lamina supports tissue repair; damage impairs regeneration.

4
层粘连蛋白（Laminin）的链结构 层粘连蛋白由α、β、γ三条链自组装成网络，通过结合整合素、ynogdgen、perlecan等稳定在基底膜。 Laminin consists of α, β, γ chains assembling into a network that, via binding integrins, nidogen, and perlecan, stabilizes in the basal lamina.

基底膜相关疾病Basal lamina and diseases

1
分子异常可致基底膜相关疾病 基底膜组分结构改变或缺失会导致胎儿发育异常、皮肤水疱、肾脏疾病等。 Altered or missing basal lamina components cause developmental disorders, skin blistering, and kidney diseases.
2
连接皮肤与基底膜的疾病——交界型表皮松解性水疱症 若层粘连蛋白突变导致上皮与基底膜黏附受损，出现交界型表皮松解性水疱症(JEB)。 In laminin mutations, epithelial-basal lamina adhesion is impaired, resulting in junctional epidermolysis bullosa (JEB) with skin blistering.
3
肾脏疾病举例 IV型胶原或层粘连蛋白异常引起基底膜增厚或断裂，如遗传性肾病等，造成滤过功能丧失，尿液与血液成分发生异常渗透。 Abnormal collagen IV or laminin causes basal lamina thickening or disruption (e.g., hereditary nephropathies), leading to loss of filtration and abnormal exchange between urine and blood.
4
总结 老师强调，基底膜的结构与完整性对器官功能至关重要，是多种疾病的发病基础。 The instructor highlighted that structural integrity of the basal lamina is essential for organ function and is a basis of many diseases.

细胞外基质功能与肿瘤信号Functions of the extracellular matrix (ECM) & ECM: cell signaling in cancer

1
细胞外基质（ECM）的主要功能 ECM为细胞提供黏附底物，使细胞能够附着和迁移。 The ECM provides an adhesive substrate for cells, enabling attachment and migration.

ECM为组织提供三维结构，稳定组织形态。

The ECM provides structural support, maintaining tissue architecture.

ECM能呈递生长因子至细胞表面受体，协助信号传递。

The ECM presents growth factors (GFs) to cell surface receptors, facilitating signaling.

ECM可储存生长因子，调节其释放速率与空间分布，从而调控细胞行为。

The ECM sequesters and stores growth factors, modulating their release and spatial distribution to regulate cell behavior.

ECM向细胞提供机械信号（如硬度），决定细胞分化、增殖和迁移。

The ECM provides mechanical signals (such as stiffness), influencing cell differentiation, proliferation, and migration.

2
癌症中的ECM失调 肿瘤微环境中ECM功能异常，结构和成分改变。 In cancer, the ECM is dysregulated, leading to alterations in structure and composition.

ECM失调可导致肿瘤细胞异常增殖、浸润和形成转移灶。

Such dysregulation promotes abnormal cancer cell proliferation, invasion, and metastasis.

3
侵袭性肿瘤中的ECM重塑和迁移 健康组织中，纤维母细胞维持基底膜和ECM稳态。 In healthy tissue, fibroblasts maintain basement membrane and ECM homeostasis.

肿瘤进展时，癌相关成纤维细胞（CAFs）大量活跃，分泌交联酶（如LOX）与基质金属蛋白酶（MMPs），破坏基底膜，重排胶原纤维。

During tumor progression, cancer-associated fibroblasts (CAFs) secrete crosslinking enzymes (e.g., LOX) and matrix metalloproteinases (MMPs), disrupting the basement membrane and reorganizing collagen fibers.

基底膜张力和断裂导致肿瘤细胞易于穿透和转移。

Tension and rupture of the basement membrane facilitate tumor cell invasion and metastasis.

对齐的胶原纤维形成迁移通道，引导肿瘤细胞沿纤维运动。

Aligned collagen fibers form migration tracks, guiding tumor cell motility.

肿瘤ECM变化表现为
基底膜破坏、组织刚度增加、胶原蛋白沉积增多、重塑酶上调。

ECM alterations in tumors include basement membrane disruption, increased tissue stiffness, deposition of fibrillar collagens, and upregulation of remodeling enzymes.

4
ECM与信号通路调控 细胞膜上的蛋白多糖（如syndecan）、层粘连蛋白、透明质酸受体（CD44）等参与细胞-基质信号传导。 Cell surface proteoglycans (e.g., syndecans), laminin, hyaluronan receptors (CD44), etc., participate in cell-ECM signaling.

整合素与胶原结合，激活FAK、SRC等信号，影响细胞骨架重组、迁移等。

Integrins bind to collagen, activating signals such as FAK and SRC, thus affecting cytoskeletal reorganization and cell motility.

信号进一步作用如RhoA/ROCK、PI3K/AKT、RAS/RAF/MEK/ERK等通路，调控细胞生长、分化、运动和存活。

Signaling cascades including RhoA/ROCK, PI3K/AKT, and RAS/RAF/MEK/ERK regulate cell growth, differentiation, movement, and survival.

YAP/TAZ等关键分子感知机械信号，调控基因表达，促进细胞增殖和肿瘤进展。

Key effectors like YAP/TAZ sense mechanical cues and regulate gene expression, promoting proliferation and tumor progression.

5
ECM降解片段的作用 ECM蛋白降解产物（matricryptins）可作为细胞因子或趋化因子，参与调节肿瘤微环境。 Degradation fragments of ECM proteins (matricryptins) act like cytokines or chemokines, modulating the tumor microenvironment.

部分matricryptin具有抗肿瘤作用（如collagen XVIII衍生的endostatin），而另一些（如Laminin 111片段）有促肿瘤作用。

Some matricryptins have anti-tumor effects (e.g., endostatin from collagen XVIII), while others (such as Laminin 111 fragments) are pro-tumorigenic.

6
总结 ECM是调控细胞行为与肿瘤进展的重要因素，其结构、分子信号、机械特性和动态重塑共同决定微环境特性与癌症恶化。 The ECM is a central regulator of cell behavior and tumor progression; its structure, molecular signals, mechanical properties, and dynamic remodeling jointly define microenvironment features and cancer aggressiveness.

肿瘤相关成纤维细胞包裹原发肿瘤细胞A capsule of cancer-associated fibroblasts (CAFs) that enwraps primary cancer cells

1
CAFs为肿瘤细胞形成高收缩性保护层 肿瘤相关成纤维细胞（CAFs）在肿瘤周围形成致密包裹，具有很强收缩性和刚性，为癌细胞提供物理保护。 Cancer-associated fibroblasts (CAFs) form a dense, highly contractile capsule around primary tumor cells, providing rigid physical protection.
2
CAFs分泌大量细胞外基质，导致间质纤维化 CAFs通过分泌胶原等基质成分，促进肿瘤局部纤维结缔组织增生，产生去分化、纤维化现象（desmoplasia）。 CAFs secrete large amounts of extracellular matrix, driving local fibrosis (desmoplasia) and connective tissue hyperplasia around tumors.
3
CAF包裹导致肿瘤细胞多种功能变化 被CAFs包裹后，癌细胞内部张力降低，YAP信号转录调控发生变化，并表现出对化疗的耐受。 Encapsulation by CAFs decreases cancer cell internal tension, alters YAP-mediated transcriptional regulation, and enhances chemotherapy resistance.
4
促进恶性进展的多重作用 CAF包裹环境下，肿瘤细胞增殖能力、干性、免疫抑制、血管新生和转移能力均增强。 Under CAF encapsulation, tumor cells exhibit increased proliferation, stemness, immunosuppression, angiogenesis, and metastatic potential.
课堂补充
纤维包裹还会阻挡免疫细胞、药物等进入肿瘤中心，保护肿瘤免受外界攻击 老师补充，CAFs的收缩和胶原沉积加剧基质硬度，阻止免疫细胞和药物渗透到肿瘤深部，使肿瘤逃避免疫监视和治疗。 The instructor added that contraction by CAFs and dense collagen further stiffen the matrix, preventing immune cells and drugs from penetrating the tumor core, thereby helping tumors evade immune surveillance and therapy.

细胞极性Cell polarity

1
细胞极性的定义与实例 许多细胞（如肠上皮细胞、神经元、精子、迁移性胶质细胞和T细胞等）拥有极性结构和功能。 Many cells (intestinal epithelial cells, neurons, sperm, migrating astrocytes, T cells) display structural and functional polarity.
2
极性细胞表现为三大特征 极性细胞具有不对称形态、不对称分布的分子和细胞器，以及不对称功能。 Polarized cells exhibit asymmetric shape, an asymmetric distribution of molecules/organelles, and perform specialized asymmetric functions.
3
极性建立与维护的基本要求 细胞极性建立需首先打破对称性，并长期维持，由此形成不同的亚细胞结构区域。 Establishing polarity requires breaking symmetry and then sustaining it, thus generating distinct subcellular domains.
4
骨架系统的基础支撑作用 细胞骨架（主要是微丝、微管、中间纤维等）在极性建立和维持中起决定性作用。 The cytoskeleton (mainly actin filaments, microtubules, intermediate filaments) is essential for both the establishment and maintenance of cell polarity.
课堂补充
肿瘤细胞丧失极性，获得高度可塑性，利于迁移与浸润 老师强调，极性消失后肿瘤细胞的分子和器官分布紊乱，出现前后极性，并获得更强的运动和转移能力。极性的动态调控决定细胞迁移方向和浸润能力，是肿瘤恶性进展的重要基础。 The instructor emphasized that loss of polarity leads to disorganized molecular and organelle localization in tumor cells, helps establish front-rear polarity, and enhances migratory/invasive abilities. Dynamic regulation of polarity determines migration direction and invasiveness, underlying cancer progression.
三大极性复合物
启动与维持Three polarity complexes: initiation and maintenance
两类磷脂
启动与维持Two phospholipids: initiation and maintenance
1
细胞极性的核心调控复合物 细胞极性的建立和维持依赖于三类保守极性蛋白复合物：aPKC-PAR复合物、Crb-Pals1-Patj复合物、Lgl-Dlg-Scrib复合物。 The establishment and maintenance of cell polarity depend on three conserved polarity protein complexes: aPKC-PAR, Crb-Pals1-Patj, and Lgl-Dlg-Scrib complexes.
2
aPKC-PAR复合物与顶端定位 aPKC-PAR复合物（包括PAR3、PAR6、aPKC）主要定位于上皮细胞的顶端，靠近紧密连接部位。 The aPKC-PAR complex (PAR3, PAR6, aPKC) is apically localized in epithelial cells, near the tight junctions.

该复合物通过相互作用形成正反馈环，稳定顶端区域的极性。

This complex forms positive feedback loops through mutual interactions, stabilizing apical polarity.

3
Crb-Pals1-Patj复合物和Lgl-Dlg-Scrib复合物 Crb-Pals1-Patj复合物限定顶端区，而Lgl-Dlg-Scrib复合物定位于基底外侧区。 The Crb-Pals1-Patj complex restricts the apical domain, while the Lgl-Dlg-Scrib complex is localized basolaterally.

三大极性复合物之间存在相互排斥和负调控，实现极性分区。

There is mutual exclusion and negative regulation between these complexes to define discrete polarity domains.

4
极性决定不同细胞类型的专属功能区域 在哺乳动物上皮细胞中，上下方向的aPKC分布决定apical-basal极性。 In mammalian epithelial cells, apical-basal distribution of aPKC defines the apical-basal polarity.

在星形胶质细胞迁移或神经元轴突确定时，aPKC同样标记前缘。

During astrocyte migration or neuronal axon specification, aPKC marks the leading edge.

5
启动与维持极性的两种关键磷脂 细胞极性还有赖于磷脂PIP2和PIP3的空间分布。 Cell polarity also relies on the spatial distribution of phospholipids PIP2 and PIP3.

PI3K催化PIP2转化为PIP3，PTEN磷酸酶则将PIP3脱磷回PIP2。

PI3K catalyzes the conversion of PIP2 to PIP3, and PTEN phosphatase dephosphorylates PIP3 back to PIP2.

6
不同极性细胞的PIP2/PIP3分布与aPKC分布 在上皮细胞中，PIP2富集于apical，而PIP3位于basal侧；aPKC与PIP2区域协同分布。 In epithelial cells, PIP2 is enriched apically, PIP3 basally; aPKC co-localizes with the PIP2 domain.

在趋化细胞和神经元中，PIP2和PIP3分别聚集于细胞前后，指导迁移或轴突生长，aPKC定位于前缘。

In chemotaxing cells and neurons, PIP2 and PIP3 are concentrated at the front and rear respectively, directing migration or axon outgrowth, with aPKC marking the leading front.

课堂补充
极性复合物与疾病的关联 老师强调，极性的破坏不仅影响细胞结构，还会引发肿瘤、发育障碍等病理情况。 The instructor emphasized that disruption of polarity complexes not only affects cell structure but also leads to cancers and developmental disorders.

细胞骨架与极性Cytoskeleton and polarity

1
细胞极性由三大类骨架系统支撑 细胞极性的建立和维持离不开微丝（红/橙色）、微管（绿色）和中间丝（蓝色）三大骨架系统。 The establishment and maintenance of cell polarity depend on three main cytoskeletal systems: actin microfilaments (red/orange), microtubules (green), and intermediate filaments (blue).
2
上皮细胞的极性结构分层明显 上皮细胞自顶端至基底分区，包括顶端的微绒毛、紧邻表面的微丝终末网、黏附连接、桥粒和基底侧的半桥粒，最底部连接基底膜。 Epithelial cells show clear apical-basal polarity: apical microvilli, an underlying actin terminal web, adherens junction, desmosome, basal side hemidesmosome, all anchored to the basal lamina.
3
细胞骨架系统在各层间协作 微丝主要分布于顶端、侧向和迁移前缘，微管纵跨细胞内部，中间丝沿侧面和基底形成支撑。 Actin filaments are concentrated at the apex, lateral membrane, and leading edge of migrating cells; microtubules span the cytoplasm; intermediate filaments provide side and basal support.
4
迁移性间充质细胞表现前后极性 迁移性间充质细胞具有明显前后极性，前缘伸展伪足、板层伪足，细胞骨架重组决定迁移方向。 Migrating mesenchymal cells display clear front-rear polarity, with protruding filopodia and lamellipodia at the front; cytoskeletal remodeling determines migration direction.
5
不同类型的肌动蛋白纤维决定细胞运动方式 迁移细胞内可见背侧张力纤维、腹侧张力纤维、弧形纤维（集体迁移）、核周肌动蛋白帽及树突状网络。 Distinct actin filament structures exist: dorsal and ventral stress fibers, arcs (for collective migration), perinuclear actin cap, and dendritic networks.
课堂补充
骨架变化如何调控极性与形态 老师强调，肌动蛋白和微管的动态重排决定突起形成与细胞前进方向；中间丝提供整体结构稳定，尤其支持黏附连接和基底锚定。 The instructor emphasized that dynamic actin and microtubule rearrangement drives protrusion and migration direction, while intermediate filaments maintain global cell stability and anchor junctions and the basal side.
课堂补充
极性骨架障碍与相关疾病 如果极性骨架蛋白发生突变，会导致极性丧失、迁移障碍及相关疾病如皮肤水疱、心脏病及侵袭性肿瘤等。 Mutations in polarity-related cytoskeletal proteins can cause loss of polarity, migration defects, and diseases such as skin blistering, cardiomyopathies, and aggressive cancers.

上皮-间质转化与细胞迁移 EMT and Cellular Migration

EMT = 上皮-间质转化 EMT = epithelial–mesenchymal transition

MET = 间质-上皮转化 MET = mesenchymal-epithelial transition

1
EMT和MET定义 EMT指上皮细胞失去细胞极性和细胞间连接，获得迁移和侵袭能力，转变为间质表型。 EMT means epithelial cells lose polarity and cell–cell junctions, gain migratory and invasive properties, and convert to a mesenchymal phenotype. MET则是间质细胞重新获得上皮表型和黏附性质。 MET is the reverse transition, where mesenchymal cells reacquire epithelial properties and adhesion.
2
EMT过程中细胞结构变化 上皮细胞含有紧密连接、黏附连接、桥粒和半桥粒，富含E-cadherin、上皮分子、角蛋白（中间丝）等。 Epithelial cells have tight junctions, adherens junctions, desmosomes, hemidesmosomes, and express E-cadherin, epithelial molecules, cytokeratins (intermediate filaments), etc. 随着EMT发生，细胞逐渐丧失细胞极性和连接，转而表达N-cadherin、波形蛋白和纤维连接蛋白等间质标志。 During EMT, cells progressively lose polarity and junctions, start expressing N-cadherin, vimentin, fibronectin and other mesenchymal markers.
3
EMT的调控与表型标志 TGF-β及ZEB家族、SNAIL、SLUG和TWIST1等转录因子抑制上皮基因表达、促进间质基因上调。 TGF-β, as well as ZEB, SNAIL, SLUG, and TWIST1 transcription factors repress epithelial genes and induce mesenchymal gene expression.
4
EMT与细胞骨架变迁 EMT后，细胞骨架重组，角蛋白为主的中间丝转为以波形蛋白为主，突出出现强烈的应力纤维和迁移前后极性。 Following EMT, cytoskeleton is remodeled; cytokeratin-based intermediate filaments are replaced by vimentin, cells show stress fibers and marked front-rear polarity.
5
EMT和肿瘤转移 通过EMT，上皮肿瘤细胞获得间质细胞样的迁移、浸润能力，是肿瘤转移的重要机制。 EMT endows epithelial tumor cells with mesenchymal-like migration and invasion abilities, which are essential for cancer metastasis。

人体细胞迁移 Cellular migration in human

1
精子游动区别于细胞迁移 精子通过鞭毛驱动的游动不属于细胞迁移的范畴。 Sperm motion by flagellar beating is classified as swimming, not as cell migration.
2
爬行性细胞迁移广泛存在于胚胎发育和成年体内 免疫细胞、胚胎发生过程中神经嵴细胞、原始生殖细胞、伤口愈合及肿瘤转移等均涉及细胞迁移。 Crawling-type cell migration is found in immune cell movement, neural crest migration, primordial germ cell movement during embryogenesis, wound healing, and metastasis.
3
成体肠上皮细胞的迁移 如小肠细胞自隐窝区迁移至绒毛顶端，维持黏膜更新和完整性。 In adults, small intestine epithelial cells migrate from the crypts to the villus tip to maintain mucosal renewal and integrity.

间充质细胞二维迁移的五个步骤 Five steps for mesenchymal cellular migration in 2D

1
极化 细胞先建立前后极性，确定迁移方向。 The cell first becomes polarized, defining the migration direction.
2
层伪足及树突状肌动蛋白网络/膜突形成 前缘分布层伪足，肌动蛋白聚集推动膜突出现。 Lamellipodia and dendritic actin networks/protrusions form at the leading edge with actin polymerization driving the membrane forward.
3
新的黏附形成（附着） 前缘产生新的黏附点锚定细胞至基质。 New adhesions are formed at the front (attachment to substrate).
4
细胞体牵拉/黏附斑收缩 细胞体通过应力纤维和黏附斑向前牵拉。 Traction by cell body involves actin stress fibers and focal adhesions.
5
后缘收缩（前推） 尾部收缩，促进身体整体前移。 Rear retraction pushes the cell body forward.

细胞迁移中，RhoA与收缩、Rac与前缘突起密切相关。

During migration, RhoA coordinates contraction, while Rac promotes front protrusion。

Rho、Rac、Cdc42 GTP酶与肌动蛋白骨架和迁移方向的决定因素

Rho, Rac, Cdc42 GTPases and actin cytoskeleton & Determinants of migration direction

1
Rho、Rac、Cdc42小GTP酶调控间充质细胞迁移 Rho、Rac、Cdc42是小GTP酶，G蛋白，活性状态为结合GTP，失活状态为结合GDP，调控肌动蛋白骨架的动态重排。 Rho, Rac, and Cdc42 are small GTPases, G proteins, active when bound to GTP, inactive with GDP, and they regulate dynamic rearrangement of the actin cytoskeleton.
2
不同GTP酶定义不同纤维结构与细胞前后极性 RhoA-GTP主导应力纤维和黏附斑形成，定向在细胞后部和两侧，促收缩。 RhoA-GTP mainly promotes stress fiber and focal adhesion formation, located at the rear and lateral sides of the cell, supporting contraction. Rac1-GTP诱导层伪足和树突状肌动蛋白网络，定位于细胞前缘，促进前进。 Rac1-GTP induces lamellipodia and dendritic actin networks, located at the leading edge, supporting protrusion. Cdc42-GTP控制丝状伪足的紧密平行纤维，与方向感建立相关。 Cdc42-GTP controls tightly parallel filaments of filopodia, linked to direction sensing.
3
细胞迁移中的肌动蛋白结构 应力纤维由收缩型肌动蛋白束组成，主要分布在细胞尾部和侧边，参与牵拉。 Stress fibers are contractile actin bundles, mainly at the rear and sides, aiding traction. 层伪足内部形成树突状肌动蛋白网络，用于推动细胞膜前进。 Lamellipodia contain dendritic actin networks that drive membrane protrusion at the front. 丝状伪足内为平行排列的肌动蛋白纤维，有助于感知和探索环境。 Filopodia have parallel actin filaments, facilitating probing and environmental sensing. 细胞皮质区域为凝胶样网络，增强细胞整体稳定。 Cell cortex presents a gel-like network to stabilize the overall cell shape.
4
黏附点的形成、成熟和解体 细胞迁移依赖焦点黏附的动态装配，黏附点随着细胞前进不断形成与解体。 Cell migration relies on dynamic assembly of focal adhesions; adhesions are constantly formed and disassembled as the cell moves forward. 高张力促进黏附点组装，张力减小时黏附点解体。 High tension promotes focal adhesion assembly, while decreasing tension leads to disassembly.
5
黏附相关分子 焦点复合物包含Talin、Vinculin、Paxillin、FAK等分子，连接整合素与肌动蛋白。 Focal complexes include Talin, Vinculin, Paxillin, and FAK, linking integrins to actin. 成熟焦点黏附还包括Zyxin、α-肌动蛋白结合蛋白和p130Cas等，增强牵拉与信号。 Mature focal adhesions add proteins like Zyxin, α-actinin, and p130Cas to enhance traction and signaling. 纤维状黏附则以Tensin为主，与拉伸及定向迁移相关。 Fibrillar adhesions mainly involve Tensin, associated with stretched, directionally migrating cells.
6
细胞迁移方向的决定因素 细胞迁移靠感知外部线索，如化学梯度（趋化性）、基质刚度（硬度趋化）、黏附位点分布（附着趋化）和电场（电趋化）。 Migration direction depends on sensing cues: chemoattractant gradient (chemotaxis), matrix stiffness (durotaxis), adhesion site distribution (haptotaxis), and electric field (galvanotaxis). 细胞的迁移还受细胞间接触的双向调节：互相碰撞后可抑制迁移（迁移抑制），而接触后也可再次启动迁移（迁移刺激）。 Cell movement is also regulated by cell-cell contact: collision can inhibit migration (contact inhibition), but contact can also trigger migration (contact stimulation).
课堂补充
老师强调Rho/Rac/Cdc42三个GTP酶空间活性分布决定细胞正后极性的建立，直接决定迁移速度和方向。 The instructor emphasized that the spatial distribution of Rho, Rac, and Cdc42 activities defines front-rear cell polarity and thus sets migration speed and direction. 此外，迁移过程中细胞不断组装新黏附斑、解体旧黏附斑，实现“边前进、边解脱”，这是细胞动态迁移的关键机制。 Moreover, during migration, cells continuously form new adhesions and disassemble old ones, enabling "move and detach," which is key for dynamic cell migration.

细胞在2D/3D中的迁移方式Modes of migration adopted by cells in 2D/3D

两种基本迁移方式
间充质型与变形虫型 There are two basic migration modes: mesenchymal and amoeboid.

间充质型迁移速度较慢，细胞形态呈梭形。 Mesenchymal migration is slow and the cell is spindle-shaped.

变形虫型迁移速度较快，细胞常见爆破伪足或泡。 Amoeboid migration is fast, and the cell often shows blebs or pseudopodia.

2
迁移方式受黏附性与物理约束调控 Migration mode is regulated by adhesion and physical confinement.

2D表面高黏附促进间充质迁移； High adhesion on 2D substrates favors mesenchymal migration.

3D空间高约束、低黏附时更易采用变形虫型迁移。 In highly confined 3D spaces with low adhesion, amoeboid migration dominates.

黏附力、物理约束和细胞收缩力共同决定迁移方式。 Adhesion strength, physical confinement, and contractility together determine the migration mode.

3
变形虫迁移进一步分为三种亚型 Amoeboid migration can be subdivided into three types.

气泡型（Bleb）：细胞表面出现瞬时性膨胀。 Bleb type: transient swelling appears on the cell membrane.

伪足型（Pseudopod）：细胞通过伪足推进。 Pseudopod type: cell moves by extending pseudopodia.

稳定泡型（Stable bleb）：细胞形成相对稳定的突起。 Stable bleb type: cell forms relatively stable protrusions.

细胞在3D中的迁移方式Modes of migration by cells in 3D

1
间充质/叶片伪足迁移特征 Characteristics of mesenchymal/lamellipodial migration

细胞依赖黏附点和应力纤维的牵拉力迁移，沿着基质纤维排列。 Cells move by traction from focal adhesions and actin stress fibers, aligning along ECM fibers.

前端以Rac1调控叶片状或丝状伪足推进。 The leading edge forms lamellipodia or filopodia driven by Rac1-mediated actin protrusion.

伴随显著的胞外基质降解（蛋白酶活性高）。 This is accompanied by significant ECM degradation (high proteolytic activity).

细胞核位于后方，微管组织中心在前方。 The nucleus is positioned at the rear, while the microtubule-organizing center is at the front.

2
变形虫型迁移特征 Characteristics of amoeboid migration

细胞依靠收缩力（RhoA-Myosin），易变形挤压通过空间中“最小阻力”的路径。 Cells rely on actomyosin contraction (RhoA-Myosin) and squeeze through the path of least resistance.

黏附力较低，胞外基质降解作用弱或缺失。 Adhesion to ECM is weak, with minimal ECM degradation.

多发泡突或伪足，细胞核多位于前方。 Multiple blebs or pseudopodia, and the nucleus is often at the front.

微管多排列在后方。 Microtubules are usually at the rear.

课程补充
Lecture highlight:

老师强调，间充质型主要依赖“牵拉-降解”机制，而变形虫型则是“推动-穿越”机制。 The teacher emphasizes that mesenchymal migration is based on "traction and proteolysis," whereas amoeboid migration is "propulsion and passing through spaces".

两种迁移方式可互相切换，因应微环境变化。 Cells can switch between the two migration modes depending on the microenvironment.

细胞核与细胞迁移Nucleus and cell migration

1
细胞核作为“路径标尺”与机械应激感受器 Nucleus as "path ruler" and mechanical stress sensor

细胞核直径决定细胞能否穿越狭小空间，起到“核标尺”作用。 The nuclear diameter determines whether the cell can traverse confined spaces, acting as a "nuclear ruler."

细胞核能感知和响应外部机械压力。 The nucleus senses and responds to external mechanical stresses.

2
中间丝蛋白表达影响细胞机械变形能力 Expression of intermediate filaments affects mechanical deformability.

高波形蛋白表达（high vimentin）使细胞更抗变形，利于持续侵袭。 High vimentin expression confers resistance to deformation and favors persistent invasion.

低波形蛋白表达时细胞更易变形，易发生DNA损伤。 Low vimentin makes cells more deformable but prone to DNA damage.

3
EMT与TEM产生“软细胞”，有助于侵袭 EMT and TEM produce "soft cells," aiding invasion.

EMT、TEM相关肿瘤细胞软化，易于通过狭窄组织空间。 Cells undergoing EMT or TEM are softer and more easily migrate through tight spaces.

4
肿瘤细胞的血流转移特点 Features of tumor cell migration in the blood

在EMT（入侵血管）和MET（离开血管）过程中，循环肿瘤细胞多为圆形、刚性高。 During intravasation (EMT) and extravasation (MET), circulating tumor cells in the blood are typically round and stiff.

课程补充
Lecture highlight:

老师指出细胞核不仅是遗传物质载体，更是细胞迁移与路径寻找中的关键力学限制因素。 The teacher indicated that the nucleus is not only the genetic material container but also a key mechanical barrier during migration and pathfinding.

肿瘤细胞可通过调节核骨架和中间丝蛋白表达适应不同迁移环境，提高转移能力。 Tumor cells adapt to various microenvironments by regulating nuclear scaffolding and intermediate filament expression, enhancing their metastatic potential.

EMT及其在肿瘤中的迁移、可塑性与治疗策略

EMT and Its Role in Cancer Migration, Plasticity, and Therapeutic Strategies

EMT及其分子机制 EMT and Its Molecular Mechanisms

1
EMT定义及反激活 EMT表示上皮-间质转化，是上皮细胞向间质细胞表型转变的过程。 EMT means epithelial–mesenchymal transition, a process where epithelial cells transform into a mesenchymal phenotype. EMT与MET在肿瘤转移过程中异常激活。 EMT and MET are abnormally reactivated during metastasis.
2
分子变化与调控因子 EMT过程中细胞极性丧失，微管解聚，细胞间连接解体，细胞散开形成间质极性。 During EMT, epithelial polarity is lost, microtubules are destabilized, cell junctions are disassembled, and cells scatter forming mesenchymal polarity. TGFβ诱导一系列转录因子如TWIST, ZEB1, SNAIL, SLUG，上调间质基因、下调上皮基因。 TGFβ induces key transcription factors such as TWIST, ZEB1, SNAIL, and SLUG to upregulate mesenchymal genes and downregulate epithelial genes. 中间丝蛋白表达由角蛋白→波形蛋白切换，黏附分子由E-钙黏蛋白→N-钙黏蛋白切换，结构锚定由半桥粒→黏着斑转换。 There is a switch of intermediate filaments from keratin to vimentin, cadherins from E-cadherin to N-cadherin, and cell anchorage from hemidesmosomes to focal adhesions.
3
miRNA及环状RNA调控 miRNA和环状RNA等非编码RNA也在EMT调控中起作用。 miRNA and circRNA participate in the regulation of EMT.

EMT在肿瘤细胞迁移中的作用 EMT in Cancer Cell Migration

1
单细胞和集体迁移 Individual and Collective Migration EMT可导致肿瘤细胞脱离上皮，获得单细胞迁移能力，也可实现“部分EMT”状态下的集体迁移。 EMT allows tumor cells to detach from the epithelium and migrate individually, or to migrate collectively under “partial EMT” states.
2
迁移效率与类型 EMT效应增强，细胞间连接减少，黏附分子如E-钙黏蛋白表达减少，迁移能力增强。 As EMT progresses, cell-cell junctions are reduced, E-cadherin expression decreases, and migratory capacity increases. 完整EMT下，细胞单独迁移，集体连接消失；部分EMT下，细胞有“领头-跟随”模式，保留部分连接，实现群体侵袭。 Complete EMT results in single-cell migration with loss of intercellular connections; partial EMT retains some connections, enabling leader-follower collective invasion and migration.
3
E-钙黏蛋白/N-钙黏蛋白切换 “E-Cad/N-Cad切换”是EMT的分子标志。 The "E-Cad/N-Cad switch" is a characteristic molecular hallmark of EMT.

肿瘤细胞迁移方式的可塑性 Plasticity of Cancer Cell Migration

1
多样的迁移模式 肿瘤细胞可在EMT、间质型迁移、变形虫型迁移、MET、集体迁移等不同方式间切换。 Cancer cells can switch between EMT, mesenchymal migration, amoeboid migration, MET, and collective migration.
2
术语解析

EMT（上皮-间质转化）：上皮→间质。

MET（间质-上皮转化）：间质→上皮。

MAT（间质至变形虫）：Mesenchymal to Amoeboid Transition。

AMT（变形虫至间质）：Amoeboid to Mesenchymal Transition。

CAT（集体至单细胞迁移）：Collective to Amoeboid Transition。

pEMT（部分EMT）：Partial EMT。

3
可逆性 这些迁移方式具高度可逆性，肿瘤细胞可根据微环境动态切换迁移方式。 These migration modes are highly reversible, and tumor cells can dynamically switch modes according to the microenvironment.

肿瘤转移的治疗策略 Therapeutic Strategies Related to Metastasis

1
预防EMT/TEM 防止EMT或TEM有潜力抑制肿瘤转移，但实现方式和干预时机尚无定论。 Preventing EMT or TEM may inhibit metastasis, but how and when to intervene remains uncertain.
2
靶向循环肿瘤细胞 直接清除血液中的循环肿瘤细胞效果有限，因为它们在血中停留时间短。 Targeting circulating tumor cells is challenging because they do not remain long in the bloodstream.
最佳策略
靶向休眠性播散肿瘤细胞 休眠性播散肿瘤细胞（DTCs）及其“微环境生态位”是目前干预和抑制复发和转移的最佳机会。 The best chance is to target dormant disseminated tumor cells (DTCs) and their niches to inhibit recurrence and metastasis.
4
诱导细胞静止/衰老 通过调节肿瘤细胞的静止与衰老状态，有望延缓或阻断肿瘤复发。 Modulating the quiescence and senescence of tumor cells may delay or block tumor recurrence.

防癌健康建议 Ways to Reduce Cancer Risk

1
不吸烟，不使用任何烟草制品。 Do not smoke or use any form of tobacco.
2
保持无烟家庭环境。 Make your home smoke-free.
3
均衡饮食。 Enjoy a healthy diet.
4
避免暴晒、使用防晒措施。 Avoid too much sun, use sun protection.
5
减少空气污染。 Reduce indoor and outdoor air pollution.
6
坚持体育锻炼。 Be physically active.
7
控制饮酒。 Limit alcohol intake.
8
母乳喂养可降低母亲患癌风险。 Breastfeeding reduces the mother's cancer risk.
9
给儿童接种乙肝疫苗和HPV疫苗。 Vaccinate your children against Hepatitis B and HPV.
10
参加有组织的癌症筛查程序。 Take part in organized cancer screening programs.

课堂关键补充 Class Note Highlights

1
课上强调，除了单细胞迁移，部分完成EMT的肿瘤细胞还可集体迁移并形成转移灶，这对临床判断和治疗至关重要。 In class, it was emphasized that, besides single-cell migration, tumor cells with partial EMT can collectively migrate and form metastatic foci. This is critical for clinical decision-making and therapy.
2
老师指出，集体迁移通常比完全EMT导致的单细胞迁移更稳定、转移效率更高。 It was mentioned that collective migration is often more stable and may lead to higher metastatic efficiency compared to complete EMT-driven single-cell migration.
3
关于转移治疗，课上特别讨论了预防EMT和靶向休眠肿瘤细胞与微环境，是未来递延复发和转移的重点方向。 The lecture particularly discussed that preventing EMT and targeting dormant tumor cells and their microenvironment are strategic points for future therapy to delay recurrence and metastasis.

082025-10-23（第 2 讲）

癌症特征
抗细胞死亡 Hallmarks of Cancer: Resistance to Cell Death
1
癌症的主要特征
1
Hallmarks of Cancer

癌细胞具备多种关键特征确保其在体内异常生长和存活，包括抗细胞死亡。 Cancer cells possess several key traits that ensure abnormal growth and survival in the body, including resistance to cell death.

主要特征包括
持续增殖信号、逃避免疫清除、基因组不稳定与突变、诱导血管生成、激活侵袭与转移等。 The main hallmarks include: sustaining proliferative signaling, evading immune destruction, genome instability and mutation, inducing angiogenesis, and activating invasion and metastasis.

其中“抗细胞死亡”是癌细胞能长期存活并对治疗耐受的重要原因。 Among these, “resisting cell death” is a major reason cancer cells can survive long-term and tolerate therapy.

2
细胞死亡的不同类型
2
Different Types of Cell Deaths
意外性细胞死亡：如ACD，发生在极端环境条件（如高压、高温、渗透压失衡、异常pH等）下，细胞膜瞬间解体。 Accidental cell death 　 occurs under extreme environmental conditions (such as high pressure, temperature, osmotic imbalance, abnormal pH), causing instant physical disassembly of the plasma membrane.
答案 ACD
调控性细胞死亡
如RCD，受遗传机制调控，具有特征性形态学变化。 Regulated cell deaths (RCDs) are controlled by genetic mechanisms and have characteristic morphological changes.

调控性死亡可通过释放DAMPs（损伤相关分子模式）或Alarmins等信号，向周围细胞发出损伤信号，并可激发无菌性炎症反应。 RCDs can signal damage to neighboring cells via DAMPs (damage-associated molecular patterns) or alarmins, potentially triggering a sterile inflammatory response.

这类死亡有助于清除体内多余、不可逆受损及有害细胞，是维持组织稳态必需过程。 This type of death helps eliminate superfluous, irreversibly damaged, or potentially harmful cells, being essential for tissue homeostasis.

3
调控性细胞死亡的意义与分类
3
Significance and Classification of Regulated Cell Deaths (RCDs)

不同类型的细胞死亡对于生物有机体的生存至关重要。 Different types of cell death are essential for the survival of organisms.

通过调控细胞死亡与增殖的平衡，维持组织和器官的稳态。 Balance of cell death and proliferation underpins tissue and organ homeostasis.

最早期的分类方法，是根据细胞死亡过程中可见的形态学改变进行。 The earliest classification was based on visible morphological alterations during cell death.

经典分类包括
Classic classifications include:
1
活细胞 Live cell
I型
凋亡 Type I: Apoptosis
II型
自噬 Type II: Autophagy
III型
坏死 Type III: Necrosis
4
课堂补充说明
4
Additional Notes from the Lecture

老师强调，癌细胞之所以难以治疗，是因为它们能够有效抑制本应启动的细胞死亡程序。 The instructor stressed that cancer cells are difficult to treat because they efficiently inhibit cell death programs that should be activated.

调控性细胞死亡的研究极大推动了理解癌症发生、发展及抗药性的机制。 Research into regulated cell deaths has greatly advanced our understanding of carcinogenesis, progression, and drug resistance.

初步分类是工具性划分，实际生活中细胞死亡类型更加多样和复杂，未来会继续细化。 The initial classification is a practical tool, but cell death types are actually more diverse and complex, and will become more finely categorized in future research.

调控性细胞死亡
凋亡 Regulated Cell Deaths: Apoptosis
1
凋亡是调控性细胞死亡的一种
1
Apoptosis is a form of regulated cell death

凋亡属于调控性细胞死亡（RCD）范畴，是细胞主动自杀的一种遗传性程序。 Apoptosis is a type of Regulated Cell Death (RCD) and is a genetically programmed process of cellular suicide.

通过分为外源性凋亡（extrinsic apoptosis）和内源性凋亡（intrinsic apoptosis）两大类进行分类。 It is classified into extrinsic apoptosis and intrinsic apoptosis.

2002年，Brenner、Horvitz和Sulston因发现器官发育和程序性细胞死亡的遗传调控，获得诺贝尔奖。 In 2002, Brenner, Horvitz, and Sulston were awarded the Nobel Prize for the discovery of genetic regulation of organ development and programmed cell death.

2
凋亡的生理功能
2
Physiological Function of Apoptosis

凋亡对生物体、胚胎发育和成体维持极为重要。 Apoptosis is essential for organisms, embryo development, and adult maintenance.

（1）形态发生和结构塑造，如胚胎时期去除手指间膜。 (1) Morphogenesis and shaping of body structures, e.g., interdigital space elimination in embryos.

（2）去除器官、结构的冗余部分（如蝌蚪尾巴脱落）。 (2) Elimination of unnecessary organs or structures (e.g., tail elimination in tadpoles).

（3）免疫系统成熟过程中，清除自身反应性T细胞，预防自身免疫病。 (3) Maturation of the immune system by removing autoreactive T lymphocytes, preventing autoimmunity.

（4）神经系统发育及成熟，去除未能与目标区域建立连接的神经元。 (4) Development and maturation of the nervous system, eliminating neurons failing to reach their targets.

（5）去除受损或感染细胞，维护组织健康。 (5) Removal of damaged or infected cells to maintain tissue health.

（6）维持组织/器官体积及细胞稳态，如皮肤、血液、肠道上皮的更新。 (6) Maintenance of tissue/organ size and cellular homeostasis, such as renewal in skin, blood, and intestine.

3
凋亡的形态学和分子特征
3
Morphological and Molecular Features of Apoptosis

凋亡是“干净/无声”的死亡过程，基本不会引发或只引发极轻微炎症。 Apoptosis is a "clean" or "silent" form of death, typically causing no or only minimal inflammation.

细胞膜完整性维持至死亡晚期。 Plasma membrane (PM) integrity is preserved until late stages.

细胞失去与周围细胞的连接，细胞收缩、微绒毛消失。 Cells lose junctions with neighbors, shrink, and microvilli are lost.

细胞膜极性丧失，磷脂酰丝氨酸（PtdSer）外翻到细胞表面。 Loss of plasma membrane asymmetry, with phosphatidylserine (PtdSer) externalized to the cell surface.

核和DNA片段化，染色质致密化。 Nucleus and DNA fragment, chromatin condensation occurs.

出现膜泡化和凋亡小体的形成。 Membrane blebbing and formation of apoptotic bodies.

最后，巨噬细胞等吞噬细胞识别并清除凋亡小体。 Finally, phagocytes such as macrophages recognize and eliminate apoptotic bodies.

凋亡的分子程序
三大阶段
4
Molecular Program of Apoptosis: Three Phases

启动阶段（Initiation）：包括DNA损伤、生长因子缺失、细胞周期阻滞、抗癌药作用、死亡受体激活等。 Initiation: Includes triggers like DNA damage, growth factor withdrawal, cell cycle arrest, anticancer agents, and death receptor activation.

决策阶段（Decision）：Bcl-2家族蛋白调控细胞命运，决定是否进入不可逆的死亡。 Decision: The Bcl-2 protein family regulates the cell's fate, determining whether to irreversibly enter death.

执行阶段（Execution）：激活caspase级联反应，导致细胞蛋白（如结构、黏附、核膜等）切割，产生一系列凋亡特征。 Execution: Activation of the caspase cascade leads to cleavage of cellular proteins (structural, adhesion, nuclear envelope, etc.), resulting in morphological features of apoptosis.

5
课堂补充说明
5
Additional Notes from the Lecture

老师强调凋亡关键在于其高度受控以及不会引发炎症，与坏死等其他细胞死亡方式有本质不同。 The instructor emphasized that apoptosis is highly regulated and does not trigger inflammation, fundamentally different from necrosis and other forms of cell death.

“吃我”信号由外翻的PtdSer和其他分子发出，巨噬细胞等能够快速高效地识别和清除。 "Eat me" signals are sent by externalized PtdSer and other molecules, allowing macrophages and other phagocytes to rapidly and efficiently clear the dying cell.

这个过程对防止自身免疫和维持体内环境平衡极其重要。 This process is crucial for preventing autoimmunity and maintaining homeostasis in the organism.

教授还补充，许多抗癌药物正是通过激活凋亡程序、诱导癌细胞死亡来发挥作用。 The professor also added that many anticancer drugs exert their effects by activating the apoptotic program and inducing death of cancer cells.

调控性细胞死亡
凋亡 Regulated Cell Deaths: Apoptosis
1
凋亡是调控性细胞死亡的一种
1
Apoptosis is a form of regulated cell death

凋亡属于调控性细胞死亡（RCD）范畴，是细胞主动自杀的一种遗传性程序。 Apoptosis is a type of Regulated Cell Death (RCD) and is a genetically programmed process of cellular suicide.

3

课堂补充说明
5
Additional Notes from the Lecture

两种主要的凋亡途径与免疫T细胞/癌细胞中的Fas/FasL机制

Two Main Apoptotic Pathways and Fas/FasL Mechanism in Immune T Cells and Cancer Cells

1
两种主要的凋亡通路
1
Two Main Apoptotic Pathways
细胞凋亡主要通过两类分子程序实现
外源性/死亡受体途径和内源性/线粒体途径。 Apoptosis occurs mainly through two molecular programs: the extrinsic/death receptor pathway and the intrinsic/mitochondrial pathway.

这两种途径最终都在胞质中激活半胱天冬酶（caspases），引发细胞死亡。 Both pathways ultimately activate caspases in the cytosol, resulting in cell death.

Caspase活化是凋亡的共同终点，是实现所有凋亡表现的核心。 Caspase activation is the common endpoint and core of all apoptotic features.

2
外源性/死亡受体凋亡通路
2
Extrinsic/Death Receptor Apoptotic Pathway

外源性途径由死亡配体（如FasL、TRAIL、TNF）与死亡受体（Fas、TRAILR、TNFR）结合启动，二者通常均为三聚体形式。 The extrinsic pathway is initiated by the binding of death ligands (such as FasL, TRAIL, TNF; usually homotrimers) to death receptors (Fas, TRAILR, TNFR; usually homotrimers).

死亡受体胞内含有死亡结构域　，并招募适配蛋白FADD和TRADD。 The cytosolic side of death receptors contains a Death Domain (DD), recruiting adaptor proteins FADD and TRADD.
答案 D
招募下游的Death Effector Domain 　结构域，通过形成死亡诱导信号复合体（DISC）激活启动子caspase（如caspase-8或10）。 These adaptors recruit Death Effector Domain (DED)-containing proteins, forming a Death-Inducing Signaling Complex (DISC) that activates initiator caspases (caspase-8 or -10).
答案 DE

被激活的启动子caspase进一步激活效应子caspase（如caspase-3），最终诱导细胞凋亡。 Activated initiator caspases activate downstream effector caspases (such as caspase-3), resulting in apoptosis.

3
Fas/FasL在免疫T细胞和癌细胞中的作用
3
Fas/FasL Function in Immune T Cells and Cancer Cells

CD8阳性的细胞毒性T细胞和NK细胞利用FasL与靶细胞表面的Fas受体结合，促发靶细胞凋亡。 Cytotoxic T cells (CD8+) and NK cells use FasL to bind Fas receptor on the target cell surface, promoting target cell apoptosis.

靶细胞（如肿瘤细胞或感染细胞）可以通过不同机制逃避免疫杀伤： Target cells (such as tumor or infected cells) may escape immune killing via various mechanisms:

切割Fas和/或FasL，降低杀伤效率。 Cleaving Fas and/or FasL to reduce killing efficiency.

在肿瘤或靶细胞表面表达FasL，使T细胞或NK细胞自杀。 Expressing FasL on their own surface to induce apoptosis of effector T cells or NK cells.

分泌下游抑制分子，阻断Fas信号通路的执行。 Secreting inhibitory molecules that block downstream Fas signaling.

这些机制帮助肿瘤细胞或感染细胞潜逃免疫监视，促进其存活。 These mechanisms help tumor or infected cells evade immune surveillance, enhancing their survival.

4
课堂补充说明
4
Additional Notes from the Lecture

教师特别强调外源性和内源性途径实际上在细胞内可以有交互：例如外源性途径激活的caspase-8可以切割Bid蛋白，通过tBid启动线粒体通路，形成信号放大回路。 The lecturer emphasized that the extrinsic and intrinsic pathways can interact inside the cell: for example, caspase-8 from the extrinsic pathway can cleave Bid to tBid, triggering the mitochondrial pathway and creating a signal amplification loop.

实际临床中，肿瘤细胞经常利用上述逃逸机制，如下调Fas表达或分泌抑制型配体来逃脱T细胞攻击，这也是肿瘤免疫治疗中的一个核心难题。 In clinical reality, tumor cells often use such evasion strategies, like downregulating Fas or secreting inhibitory ligands to escape T cell attack, representing a key challenge in cancer immunotherapy.

Fas/FasL通路的活性也和部分自身免疫病、免疫缺陷、慢性炎症状态密切相关。 The activity of the Fas/FasL pathway is also closely related to some autoimmune diseases, immune deficiencies, and chronic inflammation conditions.

5
总结
5
Summary

外源性和内源性两条凋亡信号途径协同激活caspase，决定细胞命运。 The extrinsic and intrinsic apoptotic pathways collaborate to activate caspases, determining cell fate.

Fas/FasL系统不仅是免疫清除的重要武器，也被肿瘤等靶细胞反向利用以抵抗清除。 The Fas/FasL system is a major tool for immune clearance and is also hijacked by targets such as tumors for resistance.

对凋亡信号网络的深入理解对开发新型抗肿瘤、抗病毒等免疫调节疗法具有重要意义。 Understanding apoptotic signaling is critical for developing new anti-cancer, anti-viral, and immune-modulatory therapies.

线粒体通路介导的内源性凋亡
Bcl2蛋白家族与凋亡信号整合

Intrinsic / Mitochondrial Apoptotic Pathway: The Bcl2 Family Proteins and Integration of Apoptotic Signals

1
Bcl2蛋白家族的分类与功能
1
Classification and Functions of Bcl2 Family Proteins
Bcl2家族可分为三类
保护者（protectors）、杀手（killers）、调节者（regulators）。 The Bcl2 family is divided into three classes: protectors, killers, and regulators.

保护者（如Bcl2, BclXL, MCL1等）具有抗凋亡功能，通过抑制线粒体外膜通透性保护细胞。 Protectors (such as Bcl2, BclXL, MCL1) have anti-apoptotic functions, protecting cells by inhibiting mitochondrial outer membrane permeabilization.

杀手（如Bax, Bak, Bok）是促凋亡效应蛋白，促进线粒体释放凋亡因子。 Killers (such as Bax, Bak, Bok) are pro-apoptotic effectors that promote the release of apoptogenic factors from mitochondria.

调节者/BH3-only蛋白（Bad, Bim, tBid, Puma, Noxa等）也是促凋亡，但主要通过调节杀手和保护者的活性起作用。 Regulators/BH3-only proteins (Bad, Bim, tBid, Puma, Noxa) are also pro-apoptotic, mainly acting through modulation of killer and protector activities.

三类蛋白均有Bcl2同源域（BH），不同家族成员带有不同数量和类型的BH域。 All three classes contain Bcl2 Homology (BH) domains, with each family member possessing a distinct set of BH domains.

2
Bcl2蛋白调控机制与线粒体通透性转换
2
Regulation Mechanism and Mitochondrial Permeabilization by Bcl2 Proteins

正常情况下，抗凋亡Bcl2蛋白结合并抑制促凋亡Bax/Bak等，使其保持无活性聚集状态。 Under normal conditions, anti-apoptotic Bcl2 proteins bind to and sequester pro-apoptotic Bax/Bak, keeping them inactive.

当细胞收到凋亡信号，例如DNA损伤、UV、药物压力等，BH3-only蛋白被激活，抑制保护者Bcl2，同时激活Bax/Bak。 Upon apoptotic stimuli (e.g., DNA damage, UV, drugs), BH3-only proteins are activated, inhibiting Bcl2 protectors and at the same time activating Bax/Bak.

Bax/Bak在外线粒体膜上聚集形成孔道，导致线粒体外膜通透性增加，释放细胞色素c（cytochrome c）等凋亡因子。 Bax/Bak oligomerize on the outer mitochondrial membrane, forming pores that increase permeability and allow the release of apoptogenic factors such as cytochrome c.

3
内源性/线粒体凋亡信号过程
3
Intrinsic/Mitochondrial Apoptotic Signaling Process

细胞发生应激（如UV、DNA损伤、内质网应激、药物等）时，Bcl2蛋白调控线粒体膜通透性增高（Δψm降低），释放凋亡促进因子。 When the cell undergoes stress (UV, DNA damage, ER stress, drugs), Bcl2 proteins regulate increased mitochondrial membrane permeability (reduced Δψm), leading to release of apoptogenic factors.

主要相关因子包括细胞色素c、AIF和EndoG。 Main factors released include cytochrome c, AIF, and EndoG.

细胞色素c入胞质后与Apaf1结合形成“凋亡体”（apoptosome）。 After release, cytochrome c binds Apaf1 in the cytosol to form the apoptosome.

凋亡体招募和激活前体caspase-9，继而激活下游caspase-3/7，导致细胞凋亡表型发生。 The apoptosome recruits and activates procaspase-9, which then activates effector caspases-3/7, resulting in apoptotic features.

AIF和EndoG则介导caspase非依赖性的DNA浓缩和断裂，也是凋亡的一个组成部分。 AIF and EndoG mediate caspase-independent DNA condensation and fragmentation, contributing to apoptosis.

4
外源性与内源性凋亡信号的交联
4
Crosstalk Between Extrinsic and Intrinsic Apoptotic Pathways

外源性凋亡途径激活的caspase-8可切割Bid为tBid，tBid属于BH3-only家族，它迁移至线粒体膜促进Bax/Bak寡聚，进一步启动线粒体凋亡通路。 Caspase-8 activated by the extrinsic pathway cleaves Bid into tBid, a BH3-only protein that translocates to mitochondria and induces Bax/Bak oligomerization, thus initiating the mitochondrial pathway.

这样，外源性和内源性凋亡信号之间能形成信号放大和协同效应。 This establishes a signal amplification and coordination between extrinsic and intrinsic apoptotic pathways.

5
内源性凋亡通路在免疫细胞对靶细胞杀伤中的作用
5
Role of Intrinsic Apoptotic Pathway in Immune-Mediated Cell Killing

细胞毒性T淋巴细胞（CTL）和自然杀伤细胞（NK）可释放穿孔素（Perforin）和颗粒酶B（Granzyme B）进入靶细胞。 Cytotoxic T lymphocytes (CTLs) and NK cells release perforin and granzyme B into target cells.

Granzyme B可直接裂解Bid，生成tBid，激活内源性凋亡信号，协同Bax/Bak介导线粒体释放细胞色素c，引发靶细胞凋亡。 Granzyme B can directly cleave Bid to tBid, activating the intrinsic apoptosis pathway and facilitating Bax/Bak-mediated cytochrome c release, leading to target cell apoptosis.

这是免疫系统利用线粒体凋亡机制清除感染细胞和肿瘤细胞的重要策略之一。 This is a key mechanism by which the immune system uses mitochondrial apoptosis to clear infected and tumor cells.

6
课堂讲解补充和强调
6
Additional Explanations and Emphases from Lecture

老师特别强调三类Bcl2蛋白的精准调控对于细胞命运的决定性意义，任何一类的过度表达或缺失都可导致疾病（如癌症或相关退行性疾病）。 The instructor emphasized the decisive role of precise regulation among the three classes of Bcl2 proteins in determining cell fate, with overexpression or loss of any class leading to diseases such as cancer or degenerative disorders.

课上还举例说明，临床上开发的多种抗肿瘤药物（如BH3 mimetic类），即以Bcl2家族相互作用为靶点。 The lecture provided examples that many anticancer drugs developed in the clinic target Bcl2 family interactions, such as BH3 mimetics.

此外，强调凋亡通路的激活不仅是细胞自杀的基础，也是机体抵御肿瘤、病毒感染等的核心免疫机制。 It was reiterated that activation of apoptotic pathways underpins cell suicide and is central to immune defense against tumors and viral infections.

细胞凋亡在疾病中的作用 Apoptosis in Pathologies

1
增强型与缺失型凋亡的疾病相关性
1
Disease Associations of Enhanced and Deficient Apoptosis

增强型凋亡出现在AIDS、神经退行性疾病和暴发性肝炎等。 Enhanced apoptosis is found in AIDS, neurodegenerative diseases, and fulminant hepatitis.

缺乏凋亡见于自身免疫病和病毒感染。 Lack of apoptosis occurs in autoimmune diseases and virus infections.

2
缺乏凋亡与肿瘤治疗耐药
2
Lack of Apoptosis Leads to Cancer Therapy Resistance

缺乏凋亡是肿瘤治疗耐药的关键机制。 Lack of apoptosis is a key mechanism of cancer therapy resistance.

肿瘤细胞常通过三种主要方式获得抗凋亡能力： Cancer cells often acquire anti-apoptotic capacity via three major routes:

激活抗凋亡蛋白（如Bcl2、IAP/BIRC、cFLIP）。 Activation of anti-apoptotic proteins (e.g., Bcl2, IAP/BIRC, cFLIP).

抑制促凋亡蛋白（如death receptor和Bax）。 Inhibition of pro-apoptotic proteins (e.g., death receptors and Bax).

抑制关键的凋亡信号（如p53）。 Inhibition of key apoptotic signaling molecules (e.g., p53).

3
肿瘤细胞缺乏凋亡的分子机制
3
Molecular Mechanisms of Apoptosis Deficiency in Cancer Cells

线粒体通路被Bcl2等抗凋亡蛋白阻断，Bax/Bak无法完成外膜溶孔。 In the mitochondrial pathway, anti-apoptotic proteins such as Bcl2 block Bax/Bak-mediated outer membrane permeabilization.

IAP/BIRC蛋白（如NAIP、c-IAP1/2）直接抑制caspase-9和caspase-3/7的活性。 IAP/BIRC proteins (e.g., NAIP, c-IAP1, c-IAP2) directly inhibit the activities of caspase-9 and caspase-3/7.

SMAC/DIABLO本可结合IAP/BIRC解除抑制，但肿瘤细胞中其释放受限。 Normally, SMAC/DIABLO can bind IAP/BIRC and relieve inhibition, but its release is limited in cancer cells.

外源性通路中，cFLIP蛋白（短型和长型）通过竞争性机制抑制caspase-8的激活。 In the extrinsic pathway, cFLIP proteins (both short and long isoforms) inhibit activation of caspase-8 by competition.

p53经常被MDM2（一种E3泛素连接酶）降解，使促凋亡基因（如PUMA、NOXA、BIM）无法转录。 p53 is often degraded by MDM2 (an E3 ubiquitin ligase), thus preventing transcription of pro-apoptotic genes (e.g., PUMA, NOXA, BIM).

若p53功能受抑，Bax、Noxa等促凋亡因子无法激活，线粒体释放Cyt c能力降低。 If p53 is suppressed, pro-apoptotic factors like Bax and Noxa are not activated, limiting cytochrome c release from mitochondria.

4
课堂补充与强调
4
Additional Notes and Teacher’s Emphasis

老师举例说明，耐药癌症细胞常同时激活多条抗凋亡机制，例如上调多个IAP家族成员，同时p53失活。 The lecturer provided examples where resistant cancer cells upregulate multiple anti-apoptotic pathways, including various IAP family members and concurrent p53 inactivation.

课堂特别提到，特异性抗凋亡机制的抑制，可显著增强肿瘤对治疗的敏感性。 It was emphasized that targeted inhibition of anti-apoptotic mechanisms can significantly increase tumor sensitivity to therapy.

课堂还指出，理解这些机制也是开发新一代凋亡靶向抗癌药物的理论基础。 Understanding these mechanisms is the theoretical foundation for developing the next generation of apoptosis-targeted anticancer drugs.

5
靶向凋亡信号通路的典型抗癌药物及策略
5
Examples of Cancer Therapies Targeting Apoptosis

SMAC模拟物用于解除IAP/BIRC的抑制，从而恢复caspase活性。 SMAC mimetics are used to relieve IAP/BIRC inhibition, enabling caspase activation.

MDM2抑制剂（如Idasanutlin）可稳定p53，提高促凋亡信号。 MDM2 inhibitors (e.g., Idasanutlin) stabilize p53 and increase pro-apoptotic signaling.

Bcl2（及BclXL）抑制剂（如Venetoclax、Navitoclax）归属于BH3模拟物，可解除对Bax/Bak的抑制。 Bcl2 (and BclXL) inhibitors (e.g., Venetoclax, Navitoclax), known as BH3 mimetics, relieve repression of Bax/Bak.

死亡受体激动剂（如GEN1029）直接激活外源性凋亡通路。 Death receptor agonists (e.g., GEN1029) directly activate the extrinsic apoptosis pathway.

课堂指出，这些药物联合使用往往能协同克服肿瘤细胞多通路耐药。 It was mentioned that combination therapy with these agents can work synergistically to overcome multi-pathway resistance in tumor cells.

6
总结
6
Summary

肿瘤细胞抗凋亡机制的多样性和冗余性是治疗耐药的基础。 The diversity and redundancy of anti-apoptotic mechanisms in tumor cells form the basis for therapy resistance.

未来抗肿瘤策略需针对凋亡关键通路联合精准干预。 Future cancer therapies require combined, precise interventions targeting critical apoptotic pathways.

程序性细胞死亡
坏死性凋亡 Regulated Cell Deaths: Necroptosis
1
程序性细胞死亡的类型
1
Types of Regulated Cell Death (RCD)

程序性细胞死亡可根据形态和分子机制分为多种类型。 Regulated cell deaths are classified into several types based on morphology and molecular mechanisms.

主要包括
内源性凋亡、外源性凋亡、MPT驱动性坏死、坏死性凋亡、铁死亡、焦亡、旁亡等。 They mainly include: intrinsic apoptosis, extrinsic apoptosis, MPT-driven necrosis, necroptosis, ferroptosis, pyroptosis, parthanatos, etc.

细胞死亡整体分为RCD（程序性细胞死亡），并可表现为凋亡性或坏死性形态。 All these fall under RCD (Regulated Cell Deaths) and may show apoptotic or necrotic morphology.

2
坏死性凋亡（Necroptosis）的定义与特点
2
Definition and Features of Necroptosis

坏死性凋亡是受调控的坏死性细胞死亡，兼具程序性和坏死性双重特征。 Necroptosis is a regulated necrosis-like cell death, combining both programmed and necrotic features.

坏死性凋亡属于与经典凋亡（apoptosis）和坏死（necrosis）不同的新型死亡方式。 Necroptosis represents a novel mode of death distinct from classical apoptosis and necrosis.

3
坏死/坏死性凋亡的基础机制与形态学差异
3
Mechanistic and Morphological Features of Necrosis/Necroptosis

坏死性凋亡由特定信号（如死亡受体、细胞应激等）启动，不同于“意外性坏死”那样随机发生。 Necroptosis is triggered by specific signals (such as death receptors, cellular stress), unlike accidental necrosis which is random.

凋亡性死亡典型形态包括细胞皱缩、核浓缩和凋亡小体形成。 Typical apoptosis shows cell shrinkage, nuclear condensation, and apoptotic body formation.

坏死性死亡表现为细胞肿胀、胞膜破裂和细胞内容物释放，常引发炎症反应。 Necrotic death is characterized by cell swelling, plasma membrane rupture, release of cellular contents, often causing inflammation.

坏死性凋亡常作为凋亡缺陷时的替代死亡路径。 Necroptosis often acts as an alternative death route when apoptosis is impaired.

4
坏死/坏死性凋亡相关的疾病及生理功能
4
Disease Associations and Physiological Roles of Necrosis/Necroptosis

坏死和坏死性凋亡参与缺氧、心梗、中风、肿瘤、感染等多种病理过程。 Necrosis and necroptosis are involved in hypoxia, myocardial infarction, stroke, tumors, infections, and other pathologies.

坏死性凋亡同时在发育、免疫应答、清除受损细胞等生理过程中发挥作用。 Necroptosis also functions in development, immune responses, and clearance of damaged cells in normal physiology.

5
课堂讲解及补充说明
5
Teacher's Additional Explanations

老师特别指出，坏死性凋亡与凋亡一样，是“受程控”的，不是“随机的意外坏死”。 The instructor emphasized that, like apoptosis, necroptosis is regulated and not random/accidental.

坏死性凋亡有独特的信号和分子网络控制（后续ppt将进一步介绍分子机制和调控要点）。 Necroptosis is controlled by distinct signaling and molecular networks (detailed molecular mechanisms are shown in subsequent slides).

老师补充，凋亡和坏死（包括坏死性凋亡）在形态、电镜下和免疫标记下可准确区分。 It was noted that apoptosis and necrosis (including necroptosis) can be distinguished by morphology, electron microscopy, and molecular markers.

6
小结
6
Summary

坏死性凋亡是现代细胞死亡分类体系中的重要一环，是细胞在应对某些损伤和凋亡途径失效时的关键“备用死亡路径”。 Necroptosis constitutes an important branch of modern cell death classification, serving as a crucial "backup death pathway" when cells face certain injuries or apoptosis pathway failures.

MPT驱动性坏死
线粒体通透性转换孔（MPTP）MPT-driven necrosis: Mitochondrial Permeability Transition Pore (MPTP)
1
线粒体通透性转换孔驱动的坏死机制
1
Mechanism of MPT-driven Necrosis

MPT驱动性坏死是一种调控性坏死，主要由线粒体通透性转换孔（MPTP）开放引发。 MPT-driven necrosis is a form of regulated necrosis mainly triggered by the opening of the mitochondrial permeability transition pore (MPTP).

在稳态下，MPTP关闭，细胞处于正常状态，线粒体功能正常。 At steady state, the MPTP is closed, and the cell remains normal with properly functioning mitochondria.

受到活性氧（ROS）、钙离子（Ca2+）等刺激时，MPTP开放，导致离子和水的失调流入，线粒体钙超载。 Upon stimuli such as reactive oxygen species (ROS) and calcium ions (Ca2+), the MPTP opens, causing dysregulated influx of ions and water, and mitochondrial Ca2+ overload.

线粒体失去跨膜电位，ATP迅速耗竭，氧化损伤加重，最终导致细胞能量危机和坏死性死亡，表现为细胞肿胀、膜破裂。 Mitochondrial membrane potential is lost, ATP is rapidly depleted, oxidative damage escalates, and the cell undergoes energy crisis and necrotic death, featuring swelling and membrane rupture.

2
关键分子与通路
2
Key Molecules and Pathways

CYPD（Cyclophilin D）是调控MPTP开放的核心调节蛋白，其与线粒体内膜蛋白复合形成通透性转换孔。 CYPD (Cyclophilin D) is a key regulator for MPTP opening, interacting with inner mitochondrial membrane proteins to form the permeability transition pore.

ANT/VDAC孔、ATP合酶及CYPD等参与MPTP的形成和调控。 ANT/VDAC pores, ATP synthase, and CYPD participate in the formation and regulation of the MPTP.

MPTP开放是区别于Bax/Bak1介导的细胞色素c释放（凋亡）的另一种通路，主要导致坏死而非经典凋亡。 MPTP opening represents a pathway distinct from Bax/Bak1-mediated cytochrome c release (apoptosis) and mainly leads to necrosis rather than classical apoptosis.

3
诱发条件和后果
3
Triggers and Consequences

MPTP开放由ROS、钙超载、氧化损伤等多重因素诱导。 MPTP opening is induced by factors such as ROS, calcium overload, and oxidative injury.

这些变化导致线粒体内外物质混乱、氧化磷酸化障碍和能量耗竭。 These changes cause disruption of mitochondrial homeostasis, impaired oxidative phosphorylation, and energy depletion.

结果是细胞高度肿胀与坏死，常见于如心梗、脑卒中等缺血再灌注损伤、某些中毒或严重病理状态。 As a result, cells undergo swelling and necrosis, commonly observed in ischemia-reperfusion injury (e.g., heart attack, stroke), specific toxicities, or severe pathological conditions.

课堂强调，临床上心脑组织再灌注损伤中MPTP异常开放是主要致死事件之一。 The lecture highlighted that MPTP abnormal opening is a major lethal event in clinical ischemia-reperfusion injury in heart and brain tissues.

4
老师课堂补充
4
Additional Teacher’s Notes
老师补充，MPT驱动性坏死是区别于经典意外性坏死　的“程序化坏死”，属于RCD（Regulated Cell Death）体系。 The instructor added that MPT-driven necrosis is a “programmed necrosis”, different from classical accidental necrosis (ACD), and is part of the RCD (Regulated Cell Death) family.
答案 ACD

该通路在肿瘤治疗、器官保护等领域具有重要研究和药物靶向价值。 This pathway holds significant potential as a target for drug development in cancer therapy and organ protection.

调控性细胞死亡：自噬依赖性细胞死亡Regulated cell deaths: Autophagy-dependent cell death 　
答案 ACD
1
ADCD的定义与发展简史
1
Definition and Historical Milestones of ADCD
自噬依赖性细胞死亡　是指细胞自噬过度活跃，导致细胞死亡而非生存的一类调控性死亡。 Autophagy-dependent cell death (ADCD) refers to a type of regulated cell death where excessive autophagy leads to cell death instead of survival.
答案 ACD

最初作为“Ⅱ型细胞死亡”发现，其特征为自噬囊泡大量积累和极端自我消化。 It was initially described as “type II cell death”, characterized by massive accumulation of autophagic vacuoles and extreme self-digestion.

ADCD常见于果蝇（Drosophila melanogaster）、变形虫（Dictyostelium discoideum）等模型生物中。 ADCD is often observed in model organisms such as Drosophila melanogaster and Dictyostelium discoideum.

相关研究获1974年、2016年诺贝尔奖，自噬基因Atg于1993年首次发现。 Research on this topic received Nobel Prizes in 1974 and 2016, with the first autophagy gene Atg discovered in 1993.

2
自噬基本定义和功能
2
Basic Definition and Function of Autophagy

自噬是细胞内降解与循环利用通路，通过溶酶体将大分子和细胞器降解成小分子。 Autophagy is an intracellular degradation and recycling pathway where lysosomes degrade macromolecules and organelles into small molecules.

自噬有助于维持细胞稳态，剔除受损细胞器及蛋白质，应对压力或缺营养状态。 Autophagy helps maintain cellular homeostasis by removing damaged organelles and proteins, and by coping with stress or nutrient deprivation.

3
自噬的形态学与分子特征
3
Morphological and Molecular Features of Autophagy

自噬过程分为起始、核化（泡结构形成）、延长、闭合形成双层膜自噬体、与溶酶体融合、降解并回收小分子。 The autophagy process consists of initiation, nucleation (formation of phagophore), elongation, closure to form a double-membrane autophagosome, fusion with lysosome, and degradation to recycle small molecules.

自噬体与溶酶体融合后称为自噬溶酶体（autophagolysosome），内容物降解为氨基酸、脂肪酸、单糖等基础分子。 Once the autophagosome fuses with a lysosome, it becomes an autophagolysosome, and the contents are degraded into amino acids, fatty acids, monosaccharides, and other building blocks.

形成和成熟涉及ULK1复合物、PI3K、Atg蛋白家族（约40种基因），以及LC3脂化和膜动力学。 Formation and maturation involve the ULK1 complex, PI3K, about 40 Atg gene family proteins, and lipidation of LC3 and membrane dynamics.

自噬的两种形式
整体性与选择性
Forms of Autophagy
Bulk and Selective

整体性自噬随机吞噬细胞成分，是基础和应激状态下无特异性回收过程。 Bulk autophagy randomly sequesters cytosolic components; it is a non-specific recycling process under basal or stress conditions.

选择性自噬由受体介导，专门识别并清除特定货物（如病原体、损伤线粒体、蛋白聚集体）。 Selective autophagy is mediated by receptors that specifically recognize and remove particular cargos, such as pathogens, damaged mitochondria, and protein aggregates.

选择性自噬通过自噬受体—如p62等—连接标记物（如泛素）和自噬体膜的LC3家族蛋白。 Selective autophagy utilizes autophagy receptors, such as p62, to connect tags (e.g., ubiquitin) with LC3 family proteins on the autophagosomal membrane.

5
电子显微镜下ADCD形态学特征
5
Morphological Features of ADCD under Electron Microscopy

ADCD细胞内可见大量自噬泡（autophagic vacuole）、自噬体与自噬溶酶体。 In ADCD, cells display numerous autophagic vacuoles, autophagosomes, and autophagolysosomes under electron microscopy.

这些结构标志着细胞被极度自噬消化，最终导致细胞死亡而不是生存。 These structures indicate that cells are undergoing extreme self-digestion via autophagy, leading to cell death rather than survival.

6
课堂补充说明
6
Teacher’s Additional Explanations

老师强调ADCD不同于保护性自噬，是自噬过盛导致的死亡，常见于发育过程和某些肿瘤治疗情境。 The teacher emphasized that ADCD differs from protective autophagy, as excessive autophagy leads to death, commonly in development and some cancer therapy contexts.

课堂补充最新进展，包括自噬相关基因在肿瘤、神经退行等疾病研究中的重要意义。 Latest research highlights were added, noting the key role of autophagy-related genes in cancer and neurodegenerative disease studies.

选择性自噬及其受体种类多样性Selective autophagy: a diverse set of receptors

1
选择性自噬的主要类型与对应受体
1
Major Types of Selective Autophagy and Corresponding Receptors

选择性自噬是指细胞能够特异性识别并选择性降解特定细胞成分。 Selective autophagy refers to the cell's ability to specifically recognize and selectively degrade certain cellular components.

常见类型包括
溶酶体自噬（lysophagy）、过氧化物酶体自噬（pexophagy）、铁蛋白自噬（ferritinophagy）、核自噬（nucleophagy）、异种吞噬（xenophagy）、线粒体自噬（mitophagy）、脂滴自噬（lipophagy）、内质网自噬（reticulophagy）、聚集体自噬（aggrephagy）等。 The common types include: lysophagy, pexophagy, ferritinophagy, nucleophagy, xenophagy, mitophagy, lipophagy, reticulophagy, aggrephagy, etc.

每种自噬类型对应一组特异性受体，如p62、NBR1、NCOA4、FAM134B等。 Each type is associated with specific receptors, such as p62, NBR1, NCOA4, FAM134B, and others.

例如，p62和NBR1共同介导过氧化物酶体自噬，NCOA4特异调控铁蛋白自噬。 For example, p62 and NBR1 work together in pexophagy, and NCOA4 specifically regulates ferritinophagy.

2
选择性自噬的机制大致分为两类
Mechanisms
Ubiquitin-Dependent vs. Ubiquitin-Independent

有的选择性自噬依赖泛素（ubiquitin）标记，有的则不依赖泛素。 Some types are ubiquitin-dependent, while others are ubiquitin-independent.

受体蛋白（如p62、NDP52、OPTN等）通过结合LC3蛋白招募自噬体并介导底物识别。 Receptor proteins (such as p62, NDP52, OPTN) recruit autophagosomes by binding to LC3 and mediate substrate recognition.

3
线粒体、脂滴、细胞核等多种细胞组分都能被选择性自噬特异识别
3
Various Cellular Structures (e.g. mitochondria, lipid droplets, nucleus) Can Be Selectively Recognized

线粒体自噬（mitophagy）：受体包括p62、HDAC6、NIX、NBR1、OPTN、NDP52等，能清除损伤线粒体，维护线粒体质量。 Mitophagy: Receptors include p62, HDAC6, NIX, NBR1, OPTN, NDP52, etc., to clear damaged mitochondria and maintain mitochondrial quality.

脂滴自噬（lipophagy）：机制和相关受体尚不完全明确，已知涉及多种受体。 Lipophagy: Mechanism and relevant receptors are not fully clarified, but multiple receptors are involved.

聚集体自噬和核自噬等，可通过p62/NBR1等识别异常蛋白聚集体或损伤细胞核。 Aggrephagy and nucleophagy rely on receptors such as p62/NBR1 to recognize abnormal protein aggregates or damaged nuclei.

4
课堂补充及强调
4
Teacher’s Additional Explanations

老师特别强调，选择性自噬对于清除特定损伤物质、病原体和异常结构至关重要；也是细胞应对压力及维持稳态的关键手段。 The instructor emphasized that selective autophagy is critical for removing specific damaged materials, pathogens, and abnormal structures; it is also essential for stress response and cellular homeostasis.

选择性受体的多样性使得自噬调控具高度特异性和适应性。 The diversity of selective receptors provides high specificity and adaptability to autophagy regulation.

自噬的生理和人工调节因子Physiological and artificial regulators of autophagy

1
mTOR信号通路为核心调控靶点
1
mTOR Pathway as Core Regulatory Node

mTOR（哺乳动物雷帕霉素靶蛋白）是自噬启动的抑制因子。能量耗竭、饥饿与缺氧均能抑制mTOR，诱发自噬。 mTOR (mechanistic target of rapamycin) is the key inhibitor of autophagy initiation. Energy depletion, starvation, and hypoxia all inhibit mTOR and induce autophagy.

生长因子促进mTOR活性，从而抑制自噬。 Growth factors promote mTOR activity, suppressing autophagy.

mTOR下游是ULK1复合物和一系列自噬起始分子，包括ATG101、ATG13、FIP200等。 Downstream of mTOR lies the ULK1 complex and initiator proteins, including ATG101, ATG13, and FIP200, among others.

2
自噬的药物干预与常见调节剂
2
Pharmacological Modulation and Common Autophagy Regulators

雷帕霉素（Rapamycin）、Torin1等通过抑制mTOR诱导自噬。 Rapamycin and Torin1 induce autophagy by inhibiting mTOR.

3-MA通过抑制Vps34/PI3K III阻断自噬的核化/起始过程。 3-MA inhibits Vps34/PI3K III and blocks autophagy nucleation/initiation.

Bafilomycin A1和氯喹（chloroquine）等为通量抑制剂，阻断自噬体与溶酶体融合或溶酶体酸化。 Bafilomycin A1 and chloroquine are flux inhibitors, blocking autophagosome-lysosome fusion or lysosome acidification.

Vinblastine、nocodazole等影响微管，阻碍自噬体运输；pepstatin、E64d等为蛋白酶抑制剂。 Vinblastine and nocodazole disrupt microtubules and block autophagosome transport; pepstatin and E64d inhibit proteases.

3
课堂补充说明
3
Additional Teacher’s Notes

老师补充，通过调节mTOR和PI3K等关键信号分子，药物可精确调控自噬过程。 The instructor added that by regulating key signals such as mTOR and PI3K, drugs can finely modulate autophagy.

课堂特别举例，不同节点的药物抑制作用有助于科学研究和疾病治疗。 It was particularly illustrated that drug inhibition at different steps is useful for research and therapy.

基础与压力诱导自噬对于细胞生存至关重要Basal and stress-induced autophagy are key for cell survival

1
基础自噬的生理功能
1
Physiological Roles of Basal Autophagy

基础自噬去除蛋白聚集体与损伤细胞器，对胞质质量控制起核心作用。 Basal autophagy removes protein aggregates and damaged organelles, ensuring cytosol quality control.

通过抑制ROS产生、维持稳态，有助于细胞长期存活。 It limits ROS production and maintains cellular homeostasis, enabling long-term cell survival.

2
压力诱导自噬的适应性功能
2
Adaptive Roles of Stress-induced Autophagy

压力下，自噬分解细胞自身组分，为新合成供能和提供原料，实现能量与营养适应。 Under stress, autophagy degrades cell components for new macromolecule and ATP biosynthesis, adapting to energy and nutrient shortages.

有助于应对代谢、蛋白质聚集、氧化损伤、机械力、治疗、紫外、感染等多种胁迫。 It helps adapt to metabolic changes, protein aggregation, oxidative stress, mechanical stress, therapy, UV, infection (for degradation, immunity), etc.

3
课堂补充说明
3
Teacher’s Comments and Emphasis

老师强调，基础与压力诱导自噬均维持细胞生存，但两者在调控和作用机制上有差别。 The teacher emphasized that both basal and stress-induced autophagy maintain cell survival, but differ in their regulation and mechanisms.

自噬与细胞死亡Autophagy and cell deaths

1
自噬相关细胞死亡的形态学和物质基础
1
Morphology and Material Basis of Autophagy-related Cell Death
自噬相关细胞死亡　特征为自噬泡大量积累和极度自我消化。 ADCD is characterized by massive accumulation of autophagic vacuoles and excessive self-digestion.
答案 ACD

此现象在果蝇和变形虫等模型中观察较多，并非普遍适用于所有生物。 This is mainly observed in Drosophila and Dictyostelium models and is not universal for all organisms.

2
自噬与程序性死亡（坏死、凋亡、铁死亡）间的关系
2
Links Between Autophagy and Programmed Death (Necroptosis, Apoptosis, Ferroptosis)

自噬通过降解关键因子（如catalase、caveolin-1、ferritin）影响细胞是否倾向坏死、凋亡或铁死亡。 Autophagy affects the tendency towards necroptosis, apoptosis, or ferroptosis by degrading key factors (like catalase, caveolin-1, ferritin).

ATG5、7、16不仅仅驱动自噬本身，还作为调节性死亡（凋亡、坏死性凋亡等）的信号平台。 ATG5, 7, and 16 serve as scaffolds for regulated cell death (apoptosis, necroptosis) signaling in addition to mediating autophagy.

3
课堂重点与补充
3
Teacher’s Key Points and Supplement

老师补充，ADCD往往在凋亡或坏死性凋亡不能启动时发生，且在某些发育、肿瘤或神经退行疾病中显著。 The teacher highlighted that ADCD often occurs when apoptosis or necroptosis cannot initiate and is prominent in development, cancer, or neurodegenerative diseases.

图示强调，自噬的失控降解会破坏细胞基本功能，导致死亡。 The diagram underscores that uncontrolled degradation by autophagy impairs vital cell function and triggers death.

自噬与疾病生理病理学Autophagy in physiopathology

1
自噬开启与抑制在疾病中对立作用↑↓
1
Antagonistic Effects of Autophagy Activation and Suppression in Disease ↑↓

自噬开启时，有助于稳态、代谢、分化、发育、延长寿命等。 When autophagy is ON, it supports homeostasis, metabolism, cell differentiation and development, and longevity.

关闭自噬则关联癌症、炎症、自身免疫、神经退行和衰老等。 When autophagy is OFF, it is linked to cancer, inflammation, autoimmune diseases, neurodegeneration, and aging.

2
触发自噬的常见生理及病理因素
2
Common Triggers for Autophagy Activation

细胞压力、能量/营养剥夺、运动等都可激活自噬。 Cellular stress, nutrient and energy deprivation, and exercise can trigger autophagy.

3
课堂补充说明
3
Additional Teacher’s Comments

老师补充，自噬既可维持生理功能，也可决定疾病进展，是治疗新靶点。 The instructor added that autophagy is essential for maintaining physiological functions and determining disease progression, making it a potential therapeutic target.

自噬在肿瘤中的双重作用
时机决定成败

Dual role of autophagy in cancer: a matter of timing

1
自噬与肿瘤发生分期的关系
1
Relationship between autophagy and stages of tumorigenesis

在肿瘤发生的早期阶段（起始、增生、不典型增生、原位癌），自噬的活性通常起抑癌作用。 During early stages of tumorigenesis (initiation, hyperplasia, dysplasia, in situ cancer), autophagy activity typically acts as a tumor suppressor.

自噬有助于维持基因组稳定、能量代谢稳态和胞质质量控制，限制ROS产生、DNA和蛋白损伤，减少坏死和炎症。 Autophagy maintains genome stability, energetic homeostasis, and cytoplasmic quality control, limits ROS production, and reduces DNA/protein damage, necrosis, and inflammation.

此时的自噬还能清除病原体，促进抗肿瘤免疫。 At this stage, autophagy can also clear pathogens and promote anti-tumor immunity.

2
自噬在肿瘤进展和转移中的促癌作用
2
Pro-tumor Role of Autophagy in Tumor Progression and Metastasis

在肿瘤侵袭和转移阶段，自噬反而可能促进癌细胞生存和适应恶劣环境。 During advanced stages, especially invasion and metastasis, autophagy may instead promote cancer cell survival and adaptation to harsh conditions.

自噬在此阶段赋予肿瘤细胞悬浮死亡抗性（Anoikis resistance）、干性、转移能力、能量与营养供应、休眠（dormancy）及耐药性。 At these stages, autophagy confers anoikis resistance, stemness, metastatic potential, sustains energy/nutrient supply, favors dormancy, and drug resistance.

自噬的功能总结
抗癌VS促癌
Functional Summary
Anti-tumor vs. Pro-tumor
早期自噬功能
保护细胞应对压力与损伤，防止基因不稳定和细胞恶变。 Early autophagy function: protects against stress and damage, prevents genome instability and malignant transformation.
晚期自噬功能
助力肿瘤细胞生存、转移和逃避免疫。 Late autophagy function: helps cancer cell survival, metastasis, immune evasion, and drug resistance.

总体而言，自噬在肿瘤不同阶段具有时机依赖性的“双刃剑”作用。 In summary, autophagy is a "double-edged sword" with stage- and timing-dependent roles in cancer.

4
课堂强调与补充说明
4
Teacher’s Emphasis and Further Notes

老师补充，自噬与细胞生存和死亡密切相关，调控失衡可决定肿瘤的演变。 The teacher emphasized that autophagy is tightly linked to cell survival and death; its dysregulation determines tumor evolution.

在实际治疗上，抑制或激活自噬的效果需结合肿瘤阶段综合考虑。 In practical therapy, whether to inhibit or activate autophagy must be considered in the context of tumor stage.

调控性细胞死亡
胞内噬体死亡 EntosisRegulated cell deaths: Entosis
1
调控性细胞死亡类型与胞内噬体死亡定位
1
Types of Regulated Cell Death and Entosis Position

调控性细胞死亡（RCDs）包括多种不同类型，Morphology上分为凋亡型和坏死型细胞死亡。 Regulated cell deaths (RCDs) encompass various types, morphologically classified as apoptotic or necrotic death.

胞内噬体死亡（Entosis）是RCD中的一种，其机制兼具独特性。 Entosis is one specific form of RCD with unique mechanism.

2
胞内噬体死亡的核心定义与机制
2
Definition and Mechanism of Entosis

胞内噬体死亡是“细胞包裹细胞”现象，即一个活细胞进入并被另一个细胞包裹。 Entosis is the "cell-in-cell" phenomenon, where a live cell is internalized by another cell.

这种结构往往发生在失去依附的上皮细胞之间，体现为细胞间竞争。 This structure typically occurs between detached epithelial cells as a competition mechanism.

形成胞内噬体死亡依赖E-钙黏蛋白和黏着连接（adherens junctions）以及细胞骨架动力（如Rho/肌动蛋白）。 Entosis formation requires E-cadherin, adherens junctions, and actomyosin contractility (e.g., Rho pathway).

3
胞内胞结构的可能结局
3
Fates of Cell-in-Cell Structures
被包裹的“内细胞”可以经历三种结局
死亡（胞内噬体死亡）、增殖、逃逸。 The "inner cell" may undergo one of three fates: death by entosis, proliferation, or escape.

真正的胞内噬体死亡依赖自噬相关基因（如VPS34、Atg5、LC3）及溶酶体参与降解。 Authentic entotic cell death depends on autophagy-related genes (VPS34, Atg5, LC3) and lysosomal fusion for degradation.

4
生理及病理学意义
4
Physiological and Pathological Significance

胞内噬体死亡可见于胚胎种植（滋养层消化子宫内膜）、龟类冬眠期精子清除等多种生理场景。 Entosis is observed in various physiological contexts, like embryonic implantation (trophoblast digestion of uterine epithelium), and sperm removal in turtle hibernation.

在癌症中，胞内噬体结构广泛存在超过百年，特征是肿瘤细胞“吞噬”其他细胞。 In cancer, cell-in-cell structures have been observed for over a century, typically where tumor cells engulf others.

5
胞内外两细胞的意义
5
Functional Consequences for Inner and Outer Cells

“内细胞”死亡有助于去除潜在有害细胞，具有抗转移作用；但内细胞也可能通过分裂或逃逸增强肿瘤异质性和侵袭性。 Death of the inner cell can eliminate potentially harmful cells (anti-metastatic); but inner cell division/escape increases tumor heterogeneity and aggressiveness.

“外细胞”通过摄取内细胞的营养物质，导致异倍体生成和促肿瘤效应。 The "outer cell" can recover nutrients from the inner cell, leading to aneuploidy (abnormal chromosome number) and pro-tumor effects.

6
课堂补充强调
6
Teacher's Supplementary Comments

老师强调，胞内噬体死亡的发生通常与细胞应激、非依赖性生长及细胞竞争有关。 The teacher highlighted that entosis usually relates to cellular stress, anchorage-independence, and competition between epithelial cells.

此外，临床和模型生物中都已证实胞内噬体死亡与癌症发生发展息息相关。 Entosis and entotic cell death have been confirmed to closely correlate with cancer initiation and progression in clinical and model systems.

程序性细胞死亡
焦亡与NETosis

Regulated cell deaths: Pyroptosis and NETosis

1
程序性细胞死亡类型概览
1
Overview of Regulated Cell Death Types
程序性细胞死亡包括多种类型，如：焦亡、NETosis、内源性/外源性凋亡、铁死亡、坏死性凋亡、MPT驱动坏死、自噬依赖性死亡、溶酶体依赖性死亡、胞内噬体死亡等。 Regulated cell death includes various forms, such as: pyroptosis, NETosis, intrinsic/extrinsic apoptosis, ferroptosis, necroptosis, MPT-driven necrosis, autophagy-dependent cell death 　, lysosomal-dependent cell death (LDCD), and entotic cell death.
答案 ACD

细胞死亡形态可分为凋亡型和坏死型。 Cell death morphologies can be classified as apoptotic or necrotic.

2
焦亡定义与基本机制
2
Definition and Basic Mechanism of Pyroptosis

焦亡（Pyroptosis）是一种由免疫因子介导的程序性坏死，常见于巨噬细胞等免疫细胞，是抗感染防御的重要机制。 Pyroptosis is an immune factor-mediated, programmed necrosis mainly seen in immune cells like macrophages and plays a key role in host defense against infections.

其机制依赖Caspase-1介导的GSDMD（Gasdermin D）穿孔。 The mechanism depends on Caspase-1-mediated GSDMD (gasdermin D) pore formation in the plasma membrane.

3
焦亡形态学与分子特征
3
Morphological and Molecular Features of Pyroptosis
焦亡特征包括
细胞膜上形成GSDMD孔、细胞肿胀、焦亡小体、渗透性溶解。 Pyroptosis is characterized by GSDMD pores in plasma membrane, cell swelling, pyroptotic bodies, and osmotic lysis.

与凋亡（细胞收缩、凋亡小体、吞噬清除）和坏死性凋亡（细胞膨胀、膜破裂）相比，焦亡具有独特的小泡结构与剧烈炎症反应。 Compared with apoptosis (cell shrinkage, apoptotic bodies, phagocytosis) and necroptosis (cell swelling, membrane rupture), pyroptosis features distinct ballooning and major inflammatory response.

焦亡在清除感染细胞中，通过释放促炎细胞因子（IL-1β、IL-18等），激活炎症反应。 Pyroptosis eliminates infected cells and triggers inflammation by releasing pro-inflammatory cytokines such as IL-1β and IL-18.

炎症小体激活的焦亡
免疫细胞中的分子特征
4
Inflammasome-Activated Pyroptosis: Molecular Features in Immune Cells

模式识别受体（如NLRP3等）识别PAMPs或DAMPs（如细菌、病毒、HSP），启动胞内炎症小体的组装（NLRP/ASC/Caspase-1）。 Pattern recognition receptors (e.g., NLRP3) sense PAMPs/DAMPs (bacteria, viruses, HSPs) and trigger intracellular inflammasome assembly (NLRP, ASC, Caspase-1).

Caspase-1被激活，水解GSDMD形成膜孔，引发离子和水流入，细胞肿胀并最终破裂，促炎因子通过孔释放。 Caspase-1 activation cleaves GSDMD to form membrane pores, leading to ion/water influx, cell swelling and rupture, and pro-inflammatory cytokine release through the pores.

细胞膜破裂时，伴随危险信号（DAMPs，如HMGB1、ATP等）外泄，进一步放大炎症反应。 Plasma membrane rupture is accompanied by the release of DAMPs (e.g., HMGB1, ATP), amplifying the inflammatory response.

5
非经典焦亡通路分子特征
5
Non-Canonical Pyroptosis: Molecular Features

非经典焦亡主要见于感染上皮细胞，不依赖炎症小体。 Non-canonical pyroptosis predominantly occurs in infected epithelial cells and does not require inflammasome assembly.

该通路通过胞质LPS识别，激活Caspase-4/5（人类）或Caspase-11（小鼠）。 This pathway recognizes cytosolic LPS and activates Caspase-4/5 (humans) or Caspase-11 (mice).

Caspase-4/5切割GSDMD并形成膜孔，实现促炎因子释放。 Caspase-4/5 cleaves GSDMD, resulting in pore formation in the plasma membrane and pro-inflammatory cytokine release.

6
NETosis定义与机制简述
NETosis
Definition and Brief Mechanism

NETosis是一种中性粒细胞特有的死亡方式，由免疫因子诱导，表现为NETs（胞外捕获网）形成。 NETosis is a specific form of death in neutrophils, induced by immune factors, resulting in the formation of neutrophil extracellular traps (NETs).

NETs由脱凝聚的染色质和抗菌蛋白组成，可捕获病原体。 NETs are composed of decondensed chromatin and antimicrobial proteins, capturing pathogens.

7
课堂补充和应用价值
7
Additional Teacher’s Points and Applications

老师补充，焦亡和NETosis二者皆联结免疫防御和炎症，也可能被肿瘤细胞利用，促进肿瘤进展或抑制肿瘤。 The teacher emphasized that both pyroptosis and NETosis link immunity and inflammation, and can be co-opted by tumors to facilitate either progression or suppression.

抗肿瘤治疗（如某些化疗药物）能够激活焦亡，有助于激发机体抗癌免疫。 Certain anti-cancer therapies (e.g., some chemotherapeutics) can induce pyroptosis, helping activate anti-tumor immunity.

癌相关焦亡（CAP）的分子特征及NETosis与肿瘤的关系

Molecular features of Cancer-Associated Pyroptosis (CAP) and the relationship between NETosis and cancer

1
癌相关焦亡（CAP）分子机制
1
Molecular mechanisms of cancer-associated pyroptosis (CAP)

化疗可以诱导癌细胞发生焦亡。 Chemotherapy can induce pyroptosis in cancer cells.

癌细胞的GSDM（Gasdermin家族）形成孔道并导致焦亡。 Cancer cell GSDMs (Gasdermin family) form pores, leading to pyroptosis.

除GSDMD外，肿瘤中还可激活其他家族成员（如GSDMB、GSDMC、GSDME），由不同分子通路（如Caspase-3, -8, 酶类蛋白、TNF等）切割激活。 Besides GSDMD, other gasdermins (GSDMB, GSDMC, GSDME) are activated in tumors by various pathways (such as Caspase-3, -8, TNF, granzyme proteases, etc.).

例如，细胞毒性免疫细胞（NK/CD8+ T, CAR-T）释放颗粒酶和TNF，诱导癌细胞焦亡。 For example, cytotoxic immune cells (NK, CD8+ T, CAR-T) release granzymes and TNF, inducing cancer cell pyroptosis.

2
CAP的抗肿瘤免疫作用
2
Anti-tumor immunity role of CAP

焦亡过程中，DAMPs（损伤相关分子模式，如ATP、IL-1β、HMGB1等）释放，促进肿瘤微环境中免疫细胞招募。 During pyroptosis, DAMPs (damage-associated molecular patterns such as ATP, IL-1β, HMGB1) are released, promoting recruitment of immune cells in the tumor microenvironment (TME).

焦亡可增强肿瘤免疫反应，有助于免疫治疗。 Pyroptosis can strengthen anti-tumor immune responses and benefit immunotherapy.

焦亡下调在多种癌症中普遍存在（GSDM表达水平下降），提示其抗肿瘤功能可被逃避。 Downregulation of GSDMs is found in many cancers, suggesting escape from their anti-tumor function.

3
焦亡在某些情形下亦可促肿瘤
3
Pyroptosis can be pro-tumor in some cases

长期或过度的炎性小体激活和持续性焦亡可引发慢性炎症，促进肿瘤进展。 Prolonged or excessive inflammasome activation and persistent pyroptosis can lead to chronic inflammation and promote tumor progression.

GSDMB、GSDMC、GSDMD高表达与高转移率及低生存率相关（例如乳腺癌、黑色素瘤、非小细胞肺癌）。 High levels of GSDM 　 are associated with high metastasis rates and low patient survival (e.g., breast cancer, melanoma, NSCLC).
答案 BCD

焦亡释放的细胞因子可通过重塑肿瘤免疫微环境，对肿瘤具有双向调节作用（既可促肿瘤，也可抗肿瘤）。 Cytokines released during pyroptosis reshape the tumor immune microenvironment, exerting both pro- and anti-tumor effects.

焦亡是免疫系统与肿瘤间的桥梁。 Pyroptosis acts as a bridge between the immune system and tumor.

NETosis
粒细胞NET样死亡机制
NETosis
Mechanism of granulocyte NETotic death

NETosis是中性粒细胞死亡的一种特殊方式，通过释放胞外捕获网（NET）将病原体包裹并清除（属于先天免疫）。 NETosis is a specific form of neutrophil death, releasing neutrophil extracellular traps (NETs) to capture and eliminate pathogens (innate immunity).

NET主要由解凝集的染色质、弹性蛋白酶（elastase）、髓过氧化物酶（MPO）、瓜氨酸化组蛋白等成分组成，具有杀菌毒性。 NETs consist of decondensed chromatin, elastase, myeloperoxidase (MPO), citrullinated histones, and have cytotoxic, pathogen-killing effect.

NETosis分为溶解性NETosis（中性粒细胞死亡释放网）和活性NETosis（活细胞快速释放网）。 NETosis includes lytic NETosis (NET release upon neutrophil death) and vital NETosis (rapid NET release from live cells).

5
NETosis与肿瘤的系统性促进作用
5
Pro-tumoral systemic effects of NETosis

肿瘤来源的因子能诱导NETosis，促进肿瘤细胞扩散和免疫抑制环境的形成。 Tumor-derived factors can induce NETosis, fostering tumor cell dissemination and formation of an immunosuppressive microenvironment.

NETs可促进肿瘤细胞增殖、刺激血管通透性、促进肿瘤细胞外逸（血管穿出）并建立转移灶。 NETs promote tumor cell proliferation, increase vascular permeability, capture and transport circulating tumor cells, and mediate metastasis formation.

NETosis相关的系统效应包括肿瘤相关血栓、血管堵塞、炎症和器官功能衰竭。 Systemic effects of NETosis include tumor-associated thrombosis, blood vessel occlusion, inflammation, and organ failure.

课上补充
抗NET治疗和中性粒细胞募集抑制成为潜在抗癌新策略。 Additional in-class note: Anti-NET therapy and neutrophil recruitment inhibition are emerging as potential anti-cancer strategies.
6
课堂补充与强调要点
6
Teacher’s additional explanations and emphasis

老师说明，焦亡和NETosis虽均起免疫防御作用，但在肿瘤环境中可能被“劫用”，反过来促进癌细胞生存、转移及耐药。 The instructor explained that while pyroptosis and NETosis mediate immune defense, in the tumor setting they may be hijacked to promote survival, metastasis, and drug resistance of cancer cells.

因此，肿瘤治疗时需综合考量焦亡/NETosis的抗肿瘤与促肿瘤效应，努力实现“免疫耐受-抑制-激活”平衡。 Therefore, cancer treatment should consider both anti- and pro-tumor effects of pyroptosis/NETosis, seeking to balance immune tolerance, suppression, and activation.

程序性细胞死亡
铁死亡与帕森顿死亡

Regulated cell deaths: ferroptosis and parthanatos

铁死亡Ferroptosis
铁依赖型细胞死亡
Ferroptosis
iron mediated death

铁死亡是一种依赖铁的程序性细胞死亡形式，由细胞内铁积累和脂质过氧化引发。 Ferroptosis is a form of regulated cell death dependent on iron, triggered by intracellular accumulation of iron and lipid peroxidation.

铁通过促进活性氧生成，使多不饱和脂肪酸发生过氧化，损伤细胞膜，导致死亡。 Iron promotes reactive oxygen species (ROS) production, leading to peroxidation of polyunsaturated fatty acids in membranes and cell death.

铁死亡常导致细胞对化疗出现耐药性。 Ferroptosis is often related to chemotherapy resistance in cancer.

帕森顿死亡Parthanatos
线粒体介导的死亡
Parthanatos
mitochondria mediated death

帕森顿死亡是由DNA修复酶PARP1过度激活诱发的细胞死亡形式。 Parthanatos is a type of cell death caused by hyperactivation of the DNA repair enzyme PARP1.

线粒体因应激（如DNA损伤、氧化）而释放AIF和MIF，促使DNA碎片化，导致细胞死亡。 Mitochondria release AIF (apoptosis-inducing factor) and MIF upon stress (e.g., DNA damage, oxidative stress), resulting in DNA fragmentation and cell demise.

PARP1高表达在多种癌症中普遍存在，且不一定总伴随帕森顿死亡。 PARP1 is frequently overexpressed in cancers, but this does not always indicate occurrence of parthanatos.

程序性细胞死亡
溶酶体依赖性细胞死亡（LDCD）

Regulated cell deaths: lysosomal-dependent cell death (LDCD)

1
LDCD定义与机制
1
Definition and mechanism of LDCD

溶酶体依赖性细胞死亡是指由溶酶体膜通透性升高和酶（如cathepsin）释放引发的细胞死亡。 Lysosomal-dependent cell death (LDCD) refers to cell death triggered by increased lysosomal membrane permeabilization and release of cathepsins.

溶酶体酶释放到细胞质后，消化细胞成分并酸化胞质，导致细胞死亡。 After release into the cytosol, lysosomal enzymes digest cellular components and acidify the cytoplasm, resulting in cell death.

LDCD与坏死、铁死亡、焦亡等类型间有交叉。 LDCD can overlap mechanistically with necrosis, ferroptosis, and pyroptosis.

癌相关溶酶体可能是特异性诱导LDCD的新靶点。 Cancer-associated lysosomes may be suitable for specific induction of LDCD as a therapeutic target.

程序性细胞死亡
免疫原性细胞死亡（ICD）

Regulated cell deaths: immunogenic cell death (ICD)

1
ICD定义及特点
1
Definition and features of ICD

免疫原性细胞死亡是一类能激发适应性免疫应答的死亡方式，常见于治疗或感染后。 Immunogenic cell death (ICD) is a form of cell death that triggers adaptive immune responses, commonly observed following therapy or infection.

ICD可由多种死亡（如坏死性凋亡、焦亡、铁死亡等）诱发。 ICD can be induced by different cell death modalities such as necroptosis, pyroptosis, and ferroptosis.

死亡细胞释放DAMPs（如ATP、核酸、HMGB1等），促进炎症反应、树突状细胞成熟和特异性抗体/T细胞产生。 Dying cells release DAMPs (e.g., ATP, nucleic acids, HMGB1), which promote inflammation, dendritic cell maturation, and specific antibody/T cell responses.

ICD有助于抗肿瘤免疫响应，在抗癌治疗后尤其重要。 ICD contributes to anti-tumor immunity and is especially critical after anti-cancer treatment.

一些恶性肿瘤常对ICD耐受，导致复发。 Some aggressive cancers often resist ICD, leading to relapse.

程序性细胞死亡发现历程

Timeline of the discovery of programmed cell deaths

1
重要类型的发现时间线
1
Timeline of key discoveries

最早的细胞死亡观察记录于1800s，1972年被正式定义为“凋亡”。 Earliest observations of cell death date to 1800s; "apoptosis" was formally defined in 1972.

溶酶体死亡（1983）、自噬性死亡（1999）、焦亡（2001）、坏死性凋亡/免疫原性死亡（2005）、胞内噬体死亡（2007）、NETosis（2004）、帕森顿死亡（2009）、铁死亡（2012）等先后被定义。 Lysosomal cell death (1983), autophagic cell death (1999), pyroptosis (2001), necroptosis/immunogenic cell death (2005), entosis (2007), NETosis (2004), parthanatos (2009), ferroptosis (2012) have been defined over time.

不同类型背后有独特分子机制和生理/病理意义。 Each type has unique molecular mechanisms and physiological/pathological implications.

092025-10-24

肿瘤的异质性与癌细胞异质性及癌症干细胞（CSCs）

Tumor Heterogeneity, Cancer Cell Heterogeneity and Cancer Stem Cells (CSCs)

1
肿瘤是异质性的
1
Tumors are heterogeneous

肿瘤内部细胞种类复杂，不仅包括肿瘤细胞，还有癌相关成纤维细胞、血管周细胞、内皮细胞、淋巴管细胞和不同类型的免疫细胞。

The tumor mass is a complex mixture, including not only tumor cells but also cancer-associated fibroblasts, pericytes, endothelial cells, lymphatic vessel cells, and various immune cells.

肿瘤细胞本身也不是同质化的，可以包含多个亚克隆及癌症干细胞。

Tumor cells themselves are heterogeneous, containing multiple subclones and cancer stem cells.

肿瘤微环境（TME）包括癌相关成纤维细胞（CAFs）、血管和各种浸润性免疫细胞，共同影响肿瘤的发展和进化。

The tumor microenvironment (TME) consists of CAFs, vascular networks, and various infiltrating immune cells, which together influence tumor progression and evolution.

2
癌细胞异质性——克隆选择
2
Cancer cell heterogeneity – clonal selection
癌细胞异质性的本质在于
同一肿瘤内存在多个经过不同突变进化出来的亚克隆。

Cancer cell heterogeneity means that multiple subclones with different mutations exist within a single tumor.

在肿瘤进化过程中，主要有两种模式：线性进化和分支进化。

During tumor evolution, two main patterns are observed: linear evolution and branched evolution.

2.1 线性进化

2.1 Linear evolution

癌细胞通过连续获得优势突变，不断产生“新克隆”，新兴克隆替代前一克隆成为主导。

Through continuous acquisition of advantageous mutations, tumor cells generate new clones, with emerging clones replacing previous ones as dominant.

幸存主导克隆携带所有祖先克隆的突变。

The surviving dominant clone retains all ancestral mutations.

2.2 分支进化

2.2 Branched evolution

一部分祖先癌细胞发生遗传分化，同时出现多个新的亚克隆，各自带有不同突变。

Some ancestral cancer cells diverge genetically, resulting in the emergence of multiple new subclones, each with distinct mutations.

这些亚克隆具有不同的生物学特性，导致更高的肿瘤细胞异质性。

These subclones have different biological characteristics, contributing to greater tumor cell heterogeneity.

3
癌细胞异质性——肿瘤干细胞（CSCs）
3
Cancer cell heterogeneity – Cancer Stem Cells (CSCs)

干细胞具有无限增殖能力，并能通过非对称分裂产生两类子细胞：

Stem cells have unlimited proliferation capacity, and can undergo asymmetric division to produce two types of daughter cells:

一类维持干细胞自我更新；

One type maintains self-renewal of stem cells;

另一类短暂高增殖能力，随后发生分化。

The other type has transient high proliferative capacity and then differentiates.

4
肿瘤干细胞的来源假说
4
Two hypotheses for the origin of cancer stem cells
1 假说一
组织常驻干细胞转化
1 Hypothesis 1
Transformation of tissue-resident stem cells

组织中的正常干细胞通过获得关键突变被转化，出现成熟阻滞或去分化，最终形成肿瘤和癌症干细胞群。

Normal tissue stem cells may acquire oncogenic mutations, leading to maturation arrest or dedifferentiation, and eventually form tumors with cancer stem cell populations.

2 假说二
分化细胞逆转及再获得干细胞特性
2 Hypothesis 2
Differentiated/progenitor cells acquire mutations and undergo reprogramming

分化细胞或前体细胞在获得突变后，发生分化障碍或去分化，重新获得干细胞表型，形成癌症干细胞，推动肿瘤发生。
