After accumulating mutations, differentiated or progenitor cells may experience blocks in maturation or undergo dedifferentiation, reacquiring stem cell properties, thus forming cancer stem cells and driving tumorigenesis.

5
课堂补充/细节
5
Additional remarks from the lecture

老师强调，肿瘤异质性不仅来源于不同类型细胞的共存，更在于肿瘤细胞本身由于进化过程和环境选择导致的基因多样性。

The lecturer emphasized that tumor heterogeneity arises not only from the coexistence of different cell types but also from genetic diversity among tumor cells caused by evolutionary processes and environmental selection.

癌症干细胞在肿瘤治疗过程中具有高度耐药性，可导致肿瘤复发与进展。

Cancer stem cells are highly resistant to therapy and contribute to cancer relapse and progression.

持续的炎症和细胞衰老可促进癌症干细胞的维持与扩增，加剧肿瘤恶性化。

Persistent inflammation and cellular senescence promote the maintenance and expansion of cancer stem cells, aggravating tumor malignancy.

癌细胞异质性—癌症干细胞（CSCs） Cancer cell heterogeneity – Cancer Stem Cells (CSCs)

癌症干细胞、耐药与复发 Cancer stem cells, resistance to treatment and relapse

1
含癌症干细胞的肿瘤表现为侵袭性强、对治疗高度耐药。 Tumors with cancer stem cells are aggressive and highly therapy-resistant.
A
治疗前，肿瘤细胞具有异质性。 A: Before treatment, the tumor is composed of heterogeneous cell populations.
B
化疗或放疗后，大多数增殖的肿瘤细胞和癌相关成纤维细胞死亡或进入衰老，吸引免疫细胞（炎症反应）；但癌症干细胞（CSCs）存活。 B: After chemotherapy or radiotherapy, most proliferating tumor cells and cancer-associated fibroblasts die or become senescent, attracting immune cells (inflammation); however, cancer stem cells (CSCs) survive.
C
未被清除的衰老细胞及持续炎症刺激促进癌症干细胞的维持和增殖，导致耐药或肿瘤复发。 C: Uncleared senescent cells and sustained inflammation stimulate the maintenance and proliferation of CSCs, resulting in resistance or tumor relapse.

癌症干细胞识别与生物标志物 Cancer stem cell identification and biomarkers

5
检测癌症干细胞有助于评估耐药风险和复发预测。 Detection of cancer stem cells can help assess resistance risk and predict relapse.
6
衰老相关生物标志物正在评估中，包括β-半乳糖苷酶活性和p53水平及其在细胞核内的定位。 Senescence biomarkers under evaluation include β-galactosidase activity and p53 level and nuclear localization.
癌症干细胞相关生物标志物也在研究
CSC biomarkers under evaluation:
实体瘤
CD44、CD133；
Solid tumors
CD44, CD133;
血液系统肿瘤
CD44、CD123、CD33。

Hematological cancers: CD44, CD123, CD33.

前景展望
靶向癌症干细胞以改善治疗效果 Prospects: Target cancer stem cells (CSCs) to improve cancer treatment
8
肿瘤活检可以用于多重生物标志物筛查，包括CSCs、衰老与持续炎症的标志。 Tumor biopsies can be used for multiplex biomarker screening, including markers for CSCs, senescence, and sustained inflammation.
新的治疗策略包括
New therapeutic strategies include:
第二线治疗
CAR T细胞疗法，靶向CSCs（如CD123+、CD133+）；

2nd line of treatment: CAR T cell therapies targeting CSCs (e.g., CD123+, CD133+);

清除衰老细胞的药物（如槲皮素、达沙替尼）。

Senolytic drugs (such as quercetin, dasatinib) to deplete senescent cells.

10
老师强调，癌症干细胞参与肿瘤耐药和复发，高度耐受放疗和化疗；肿瘤中CSCs比例低但作用关键，持续炎症和未清除的衰老细胞可促进CSCs的生存与复发风险。 The lecturer emphasized that cancer stem cells contribute to tumor resistance and relapse, are highly resistant to radiotherapy and chemotherapy; although CSCs account for a low percentage of tumor mass, they are crucial. Persistent inflammation and uncleared senescent cells promote CSC survival and relapse risk.

癌细胞异质性——课堂要点总结 Cancer cell heterogeneity – Take home messages

癌细胞通过基因不稳定性和多个克隆的共存导致肿瘤异质性。 Cancer cells contribute to tumor heterogeneity due to genetic instability and co-existence of cancer cell clones.

癌症干细胞（CSC）也是肿瘤异质性的重要来源。 Cancer stem cells (CSC) contribute to tumor heterogeneity.

CSC在肿瘤发生之初即产生。 CSC form upon tumor initiation.

CSC对常规肿瘤治疗具有抗性，促使肿瘤复发。 CSC are resistant to conventional cancer treatments and favor relapse.

其他细胞衰老及炎症可促进CSC的维持和增殖。 CSC maintenance and proliferation is favored by other cell senescence and inflammation.

CSC的生物标志物有望用于预测复发风险。 Biomarkers of CSC could be exploited to predict relapse.

靶向CSC以改善肿瘤治疗效果目前正处于研究阶段。 The targeting of CSCs to improve cancer treatment is currently under investigation.

补充课堂讲解要点 Additional lecture highlights

老师强调，癌症干细胞的高抗药性是导致恶性肿瘤难以根治和反复复发的核心因素。 The lecturer emphasized that the high therapy resistance of CSCs is a key factor making malignant tumors hard to cure and prone to relapse.

CSC往往仅占肿瘤细胞群体少数，但影响巨大；因此治疗创新需着眼于精准检出与靶向清除CSC。 CSCs usually represent only a small percentage of tumor cells, but have profound impact; thus, therapeutic innovations should focus on precise detection and targeting of CSCs.

持续炎症和未能及时清除的衰老细胞为CSC的存活和扩散提供了“温床”，这加剧了治疗困难。 Persistent inflammation and uncleared senescent cells provide a “niche” for CSC survival and expansion, aggravating therapeutic challenges.

肿瘤微环境（TME）Tumor microenvironment (TME)

肿瘤内浸润细胞 Tumor infiltrating cells

肿瘤微环境不仅包含肿瘤细胞，还含有多种类型的浸润细胞。 The tumor microenvironment contains not only tumor cells but also various types of infiltrating cells.

在肿瘤组织中可以见到巨噬细胞、肥大细胞、中性粒细胞、髓系抑制细胞（MDSC）、树突状细胞、间充质干细胞（MSC）、成纤维细胞、血管内皮细胞、外周血细胞、免疫细胞等。 Within tumors, macrophages, mast cells, neutrophils, myeloid-derived suppressor cells (MDSCs), dendritic cells, mesenchymal stem cells (MSCs), fibroblasts, vascular endothelial cells, pericytes, epithelial and immune cells can all be found.

大规模分析显示，在肿瘤内浸润的免疫细胞中，巨噬细胞比例最高，其次是CD4 T细胞和CD8 T细胞，其他如B细胞、NK细胞、中性粒细胞等比例相对较低。 Large cohort analyses reveal macrophages are the most abundant immune cells infiltrating tumors, followed by CD4 T cells and CD8 T cells; B cells, NK cells, neutrophils and others are less frequent.

浸润免疫细胞影响肿瘤进展 Immune cells infiltrating tumors influence cancer progression

浸润肿瘤的免疫细胞根据其功能可划分为抗肿瘤和促肿瘤两类。 Infiltrating immune cells within tumors can be classified as anti-tumor or pro-tumor based on their function.

抗肿瘤的免疫细胞可发挥抑制肿瘤生长作用，主要包括某些T淋巴细胞和炎症型巨噬细胞。 Anti-tumor immune cells suppress tumor growth, mainly including some T lymphocytes and inflammatory-type macrophages.

促肿瘤的免疫细胞则有利于肿瘤发展，如某些免疫抑制细胞或表型发生极化的巨噬细胞。 Pro-tumor immune cells support tumor progression, such as some immunosuppressive cells or alternatively polarized macrophages.

肿瘤相关成纤维细胞等非免疫细胞也参与调控肿瘤免疫微环境。 Non-immune cells like cancer-associated fibroblasts also modulate the tumor immune microenvironment.

外源性因素（如肿瘤微环境）和基因组不稳定性共同影响免疫浸润细胞的功能和分布。 Extrinsic factors like the tumor microenvironment and genomic instability together influence the function and distribution of infiltrating immune cells.

浸润免疫细胞特征与患者预后 Immune infiltrate features and patient prognosis

基于癌症基因组图谱（TCGA）分析，可将肿瘤按免疫浸润特征分为C1到C6六种亚型。 TCGA-based analysis defines six immune subtypes (C1 to C6) for tumors according to immune infiltration characteristics.

其中C3型（丰富的炎症型巨噬细胞、T细胞浸润和肿瘤新抗原多样性）与患者良好预后相关。 Subtype C3 (inflammatory macrophages, abundant infiltrating T cells, high tumor neoantigen diversity) is associated with good prognosis.

相反，C6型则提示较差的生存结局。 In contrast, C6 subtype is linked to poor patient survival.

老师补充，尽管免疫浸润在不同肿瘤间差异较大，但炎症型巨噬细胞和丰富的T细胞常提示免疫活跃、抗肿瘤能力较强，预后更好。 The lecturer added that, despite high inter-tumor variability, inflammatory macrophages and abundant T cells usually indicate immunological activity and better anti-tumor potential, correlating with improved prognosis.

肿瘤免疫编辑Cancer immunoediting

（一）清除阶段 (a) Elimination

免疫细胞检测并清除转化细胞。 Immune cells detect and eliminate transforming cells.

诱因包括致癌物、辐射、病毒感染、慢性炎症和遗传突变。 Triggers include carcinogens, radiation, viral infections, chronic inflammation, and inherited genetic mutations.

主要参与细胞有CD8+ T细胞、CD4+ T细胞、巨噬细胞、γδT细胞、NKT细胞、NK细胞和树突状细胞。 Key involved cells are CD8+ T cells, CD4+ T cells, macrophages, γδ T cells, NKT cells, NK cells, and dendritic cells.

相关分子包括MHC I类分子、细胞压力配体和CD1d。 Related molecules include MHC class I, cell stress ligands, and CD1d.

细胞因子如干扰素、IL-12、TNF和穿孔素参与清除。 Cytokines such as interferons, IL-12, TNF, and perforin are involved in elimination.

免疫识别和效应分子包括NKG2D、CD226和TRAIL。 Immune recognition and effector molecules include NKG2D, CD226, and TRAIL.

（二）平衡阶段 (b) Equilibrium

清除期存活下来的肿瘤细胞与免疫系统之间处于动态平衡。 Tumor cells that survive elimination enter a dynamic equilibrium with the immune system.

此阶段表现为肿瘤休眠，肿瘤细胞被持续抑制，但没有完全被清除。 This stage is marked by tumor dormancy—the cells are suppressed but not eradicated.

主要细胞因子有IL-12和IFN-γ，它们抑制肿瘤细胞生长。 Key cytokines include IL-12 and IFN-γ, which inhibit tumor cell growth.

IL-23等因子也参与调控。 Factors like IL-23 are also involved in regulation.

在平衡阶段，部分肿瘤细胞可能通过基因与表型变化逐渐逃避免疫监控，形成新克隆。 During equilibrium, some tumor cells may gradually escape immune surveillance via genetic and phenotypic changes, leading to clone emergence.

（三）逃逸阶段 (c) Escape

部分免疫原性较低的肿瘤细胞克隆突破免疫系统的压力，导致肿瘤进展和临床可检测性。 Clones of less immunogenic tumor cells escape immune pressure, leading to tumor progression and clinical detectability.

肿瘤微环境中免疫抑制细胞比例升高，如TAM（肿瘤相关巨噬细胞）、MDSC（髓系抑制细胞）和T调节细胞。 The tumor microenvironment harbors increased immunosuppressive cells such as TAMs (tumor-associated macrophages), MDSCs (myeloid-derived suppressor cells), and Treg cells.

肿瘤细胞表面分子上调，包括HLA-G/HLA-E、CD155/CD112、CD47、PD-L1/PD-L2、CD39/CD73。 Upregulation of surface molecules occurs, including HLA-G/HLA-E, CD155/CD112, CD47, PD-L1/PD-L2, and CD39/CD73.

分泌信号分子的紊乱，例如TGFB、CCL4、CCL2、IL-10等增加，IL-2、IL-15、IL-12降低。 Dysregulation of secreted signaling molecules such as increased TGFB, CCL4, CCL2, IL-10 and decreased IL-2, IL-15, IL-12.

肿瘤微环境内代谢紊乱
腺苷上升，色氨酸下降。 Metabolic dysregulation in the microenvironment: increased adenosine, decreased tryptophan.

逃逸阶段肿瘤快速生长，临床上可检测。 In the escape phase, tumor grows rapidly and is clinically detectable.

补充课堂讲解要点 Additional lecture highlights

老师强调，肿瘤免疫编辑过程分为三步：清除、平衡和逃逸，且各阶段的免疫细胞功能与肿瘤演化密切相关。 The lecturer emphasized cancer immunoediting includes three steps: elimination, equilibrium, and escape, with immune function and tumor evolution closely linked at each stage.

免疫逃逸与免疫抑制细胞的增加及关键分子的异常表达有关，是免疫治疗的新靶点。 Immune escape is associated with increased immunosuppressive cells and upregulation of key molecules, becoming new targets for immunotherapy.

在平衡与逃逸交替过程中，肿瘤细胞可能丧失抗原性或调整分子表达以躲避免疫监视，最终促进肿瘤进展。 During equilibrium and escape, tumor cells can lose antigenicity or adjust molecule expression to evade immune surveillance, ultimately promoting tumor progression.

癌症免疫编辑——清除阶段Cancer immunoediting - Elimination

1
细胞毒性CD8+ T细胞介导的肿瘤细胞清除 Elimination of cancer cells by cytotoxic CD8+ T cells

肿瘤细胞通过可变的蛋白质加工产生新生抗原，这些抗原借助MHC-I分子在细胞表面呈递。 Tumor cells generate neoantigens through processing of variable endogenous proteins, presenting them on the cell surface via MHC-I molecules.

CD8+ T细胞通过TCR识别肿瘤细胞表面的新生抗原-MHC-I复合物。 CD8+ T cells recognize neoantigen-MHC-I complexes on tumor cell surfaces via their TCR.

如果肿瘤细胞仅呈递共享抗原，TCR亲和力较低，T细胞无反应或肿瘤浸润T细胞（TILs）数量较少。 When only shared antigens are presented, TCR affinity is low, leading to anergic T cells or low numbers of tumor-infiltrating lymphocytes (TILs).

如果肿瘤细胞呈递新生抗原，TCR亲和力高，可驱动T细胞扩增和大量TILs。 If neoantigens are presented, TCR affinity is high, driving T cell expansion and high numbers of TILs.

CD8+ T细胞激活后释放细胞毒性分子，直接杀伤肿瘤细胞。 Activated CD8+ T cells release cytotoxic molecules to directly kill tumor cells.

2
肿瘤突变负担及新生抗原 Tumor Mutational Burden (TMB) and Neoantigens

肿瘤突变负担（TMB）指的是肿瘤细胞DNA中突变的总数。 Tumor Mutational Burden (TMB) indicates the total number of mutations in tumor cell DNA.

TMB高的肿瘤更有可能表达可被细胞毒性T细胞识别的免疫原性新生抗原。 Tumors with high TMB are more likely to express immunogenic neoantigens that can be recognized by cytotoxic T cells.

不同类型肿瘤的TMB存在明显差异，例如黑色素瘤和肺癌TMB较高，急性髓系白血病（AML）等较低。 TMB varies greatly between tumor types; for example, melanoma and lung cancer have high TMB, while AML is much lower.

3
T细胞介导的抗肿瘤免疫反应过程 T cell-mediated antitumor immune process

肿瘤细胞表面表达新生抗原，为APCs（抗原提呈细胞）摄取。 Tumor cells express neoantigens on their surface, which are ingested by antigen-presenting cells (APCs).

APCs处理肿瘤抗原，并在淋巴器官中呈递给CD4+ T细胞和CD8+ T细胞。 APCs process and present tumor antigens to CD4+ and CD8+ T cells in lymphoid organs.

CD4+ T细胞分泌细胞因子，促进CD8+ T细胞的激活与增殖。 CD4+ T cells secrete cytokines that promote activation and proliferation of CD8+ T cells.

激活的CD8+ T细胞返回肿瘤部位，杀伤肿瘤细胞，实现细胞裂解。 Activated CD8+ T cells migrate to tumor sites, executing tumor cell lysis.

4
T细胞杀伤肿瘤的分子机制 Mechanisms of T cell-mediated tumor killing
一种为分泌介导的细胞毒作用
CD8+ T细胞释放干扰素γ、穿孔素、颗粒酶B，诱导靶细胞凋亡或焦亡（气体神经元D裂解）。 Secretion-mediated cytotoxicity: CD8+ T cells release interferon-γ, perforin and granzyme B to induce target cell apoptosis or pyroptosis (via gasdermin-D cleavage).
另一种为外源性凋亡通路
表达死亡配体（如FasL、TRAIL）与对应受体结合，诱导肿瘤细胞凋亡。 Extrinsic apoptosis induction: CD8+ T cells express death ligands (FasL, TRAIL) engaging their receptors (Fas, TRAILR) on tumor cells, triggering apoptosis.

IFN-γ还可抑制肿瘤细胞增殖和新生血管生成。 IFN-γ also inhibits tumor cell proliferation and angiogenesis.

补充课堂讲解要点 Additional highlights from lecture

老师强调新生抗原数量丰富的肿瘤（高TMB）更易招募并激活强效免疫反应，是免疫检查点治疗的潜在良好受益对象。 The instructor stressed that tumors with many neoantigens (high TMB) more effectively recruit and activate strong immune responses, potentially benefiting from immune checkpoint therapies.

T细胞的杀伤机制多途径协同，包括细胞毒分子的释放和死亡配体诱导的凋亡，是高效清除肿瘤细胞的关键。 Multiple T cell cytotoxic pathways (cytotoxic molecule secretion and death ligand-induced apoptosis) synergize and are vital for effective tumor cell eradication.

癌症免疫编辑-平衡阶段Cancer immunoediting - Equilibrium

1
浸润性免疫细胞 Infiltrating immune cells

肿瘤微环境中免疫调节性细胞（如Tregs、髓系抑制细胞）与具有抗肿瘤活性的免疫细胞（如CD8+ T细胞、树突状细胞、NK细胞）之间形成动态平衡。 A dynamic balance exists in the tumor microenvironment between immunosuppressive cells (such as Tregs and myeloid-derived suppressor cells) and immune cells with antitumor activity (such as CD8+ T cells, dendritic cells, and NK cells).

促进肿瘤的细胞因子（如IL-23和IL-10）与抗肿瘤细胞因子（如IL-12和IFN-γ）之间也存在平衡。 There is also a balance between pro-tumor cytokines (such as IL-23 and IL-10) and antitumor cytokines (such as IL-12 and IFN-γ).

肿瘤细胞
免疫原性逐步丧失 Tumor cells: progressive loss of immunogenicity

肿瘤细胞在基因和表观遗传（如组蛋白修饰、小RNA调控）层面发生改变，导致逃避免疫监视。 Tumor cells undergo genetic and epigenetic changes (such as histone modifications and small RNA regulation), which contribute to immune evasion.

随着肿瘤细胞进化，持续性丢失特异性肿瘤抗原表达。 As tumor cells evolve, there is a progressive loss of tumor antigen expression.

MHC-I分子的表达逐渐下调，进一步削弱T细胞对肿瘤抗原的识别能力。 Progressive downregulation of MHC-I molecules further diminishes T cell recognition of tumor antigens.

肿瘤细胞表面PD-L1表达持续升高，抑制T细胞功能，有助于免疫逃逸。 PD-L1 expression on the surface of tumor cells is progressively increased, suppressing T cell function and facilitating immune escape.

3
选择性生存的低免疫原性肿瘤细胞克隆 Selection for less immunogenic tumor cell clones

在平衡阶段, 免疫系统的压力促使肿瘤中低免疫原性的细胞克隆获得生存优势，最终主导肿瘤。 During the equilibrium phase, immune pressure selects for tumor cell clones with low immunogenicity, which ultimately become dominant in the tumor mass.

肿瘤可进入休眠状态，表现为肿瘤细胞增殖和免疫清除的动态平衡。 The tumor may enter a dormant state, showing a dynamic balance between tumor cell proliferation and immune-mediated elimination.

T细胞抑制
PD-L1/PD-1通路 T cell inhibition: the PD-L1/PD-1 axis

肿瘤细胞表达程序性死亡配体1（PD-L1），T细胞表达程序性死亡受体1（PD-1）。 Tumor cells express programmed death ligand 1 (PD-L1), and T cells express programmed death 1 receptor (PD-1).

PD-L1与PD-1结合后，激活T细胞中的抑制性信号通路，如SHP-1和SHP-2，导致T细胞活化受阻。 The binding of PD-L1 to PD-1 activates inhibitory signaling pathways in T cells, such as SHP-1 and SHP-2, resulting in impaired T cell activation.

该配体-受体互作最终导致对抗肿瘤T细胞活性的抑制。 This ligand-receptor interaction ultimately suppresses antitumor T cell activity.

5
课堂补充要点 Additional highlights from lecture

教师进一步指出，肿瘤细胞不仅通过下调抗原和MHC-I表达、上调PD-L1逃避免疫，还可通过表观遗传机制调控免疫原性。 The instructor further emphasized that tumor cells evade immunity not only by downregulating antigen and MHC-I expression and upregulating PD-L1, but also by modulating immunogenicity through epigenetic mechanisms.

平衡期肿瘤细胞基因组和表型高度不稳定，部分可逐步丧失免疫刺激能力，促进免疫抑制克隆的富集。 During equilibrium, tumor cell genomes and phenotypes are highly unstable, leading to stepwise loss of immune-stimulating features and enrichment of immunosuppressive clones.

癌症免疫编辑-逃逸阶段Cancer immunoediting - Escape

1
肿瘤免疫逃逸界定与表现 Definition and manifestations of tumor immune escape

逃逸阶段指低免疫原性的肿瘤克隆失控生长，形成临床可见肿瘤。 The escape phase refers to unrestrained growth of less immunogenic tumor clones, resulting in clinically apparent tumors.

2
免疫抑制细胞类型 Immunosuppressive cell types

肿瘤微环境中免疫抑制细胞显著增多，主要包括肿瘤相关巨噬细胞（TAM）、髓系抑制细胞（MDSC）和调节性T细胞（Treg）。 There is a marked increase in immunosuppressive cells in the tumor microenvironment, mainly including tumor-associated macrophages (TAMs), myeloid-derived suppressor cells (MDSCs), and regulatory T cells (Tregs)29.

3
肿瘤细胞表面分子异常表达 Abnormal expression of surface molecules in tumor cells

肿瘤细胞上调一系列分子，如HLA-G、HLA-E、CD155、CD112、CD47、PD-L1/PD-L2和CD39/CD73，有助于抑制免疫攻击和促进自身生存。 Tumor cells upregulate a series of molecules, such as HLA-G, HLA-E, CD155, CD112, CD47, PD-L1/PD-L2, and CD39/CD73, which help suppress immune attack and promote their own survival29.

4
分泌信号分子的异常 Dysregulation of secreted signaling molecules

微环境中免疫抑制因子升高（如TGFβ、CCL4、CCL2、IL-10），有利于逃逸；而促进免疫活性的细胞因子（如IL-2、IL-12、IL-15）下调。 Immunosuppressive factors are increased in the tumor microenvironment (such as TGFβ, CCL4, CCL2, IL-10), favoring escape; whereas immune-activating cytokines (such as IL-2, IL-12, IL-15) are downregulated29.

5
代谢紊乱 Metabolic dysregulation

腺苷上升和色氨酸下降，影响T细胞活性，是免疫逃逸的重要机制之一。 Elevated adenosine and decreased tryptophan impair T cell activity and represent important mechanisms of immune escape29.

6
多机制协同导致免疫逃逸 Multiple synergistic mechanisms in escape

肿瘤逃逸涉及多种机制协同，包括抗原丢失、MHC-I下调、免疫检查点分子（如PD-L1、CTLA-4、LAG-3）表达升高，以及分泌的抑制性因子的累积。 Tumor escape involves multiple synergistic mechanisms, including antigen loss, downregulation of MHC-I, elevated expression of immune checkpoint molecules (such as PD-L1, CTLA-4, LAG-3), and accumulation of suppressive factors30.

同时，相关分子（如Galectin-1/3/9、Stat3、Cox2、PGE2）促进免疫抑制与血管新生，进一步支持肿瘤进展。 Meanwhile, related molecules (such as Galectin-1/3/9, Stat3, Cox2, and PGE2) promote immunosuppression and angiogenesis, supporting tumor progression30.

7
肿瘤微环境变化及免疫细胞受损 Microenvironmental changes and impairment of immune cells

T细胞、NK细胞、γδT细胞、NKT等效应免疫细胞功能受损，凋亡或耗竭现象常见。 Effector immune cells such as T cells, NK cells, γδT cells, and NKT cells become dysfunctional, undergo apoptosis, or become exhausted30.

8
课堂补充要点 Additional highlights from lecture

老师强调，逃逸阶段关键特征是肿瘤细胞克隆免疫原性持续下降，免疫抑制微环境稳定形成，临床常见的实体瘤大多存在这一阶段。 The instructor emphasized that in the escape phase, a key feature is the sustained loss of tumor cell immunogenicity, establishment of a stable immunosuppressive microenvironment, and that most clinically evident solid tumors exist in this phase.

免疫检查点抑制剂的开发正是针对这些机制设计，为逆转T细胞抑制、重振免疫监视提供思路。 Development of immune checkpoint inhibitors specifically targets these mechanisms to reverse T cell suppression and restore immune surveillance.

癌症免疫编辑-逃逸阶段Cancer immunoediting - Escape

一、免疫细胞介导的抑制机制 Immune cell-mediated suppressive mechanisms

IFN-γ由CD8+ T细胞分泌，诱导肿瘤细胞表达PD-L1。 IFN-γ secreted by CD8+ T cells induces PD-L1 expression by tumor cells.

肿瘤细胞表面PD-L1与T细胞表面PD-1结合，抑制T细胞活化和抗肿瘤效应。 PD-L1 on tumor cells binds PD-1 on T cells, suppressing T cell activation and antitumor function.

肿瘤微环境中免疫抑制性细胞大量积聚，包括调节性T细胞（Treg）、髓系抑制性细胞（MDSCs）和肿瘤相关巨噬细胞（TAM）。 In the tumor microenvironment, immunosuppressive cells accumulate, including regulatory T cells (Tregs), myeloid-derived suppressive cells (MDSCs), and tumor-associated macrophages (TAMs)2334.

二、肿瘤细胞内在机制 Tumor cell-intrinsic mechanisms

免疫原性下降
Reduced immunogenicity:

肿瘤抗原丢失，MHC-I分子下调，钙网蛋白（Calreticulin，ICD相关）表达减少。 Tumor antigen loss, downregulation of MHC-I, decreased Calreticulin (associated with immunogenic cell death).

抗性与存活能力增强
Increased resistance/survival:

STAT3、BCL-2上调，FAS/TRAILR下调。 Upregulation of STAT3 and BCL-2, downregulation of FAS/TRAILR.

免疫抑制分子上调
Upregulation of immunosuppressive molecules:

PD-L1、CD39/CD73、腺苷受体和IDO表达上升。 Increased expression of PD-L1, CD39/CD73, adenosine receptor, and IDO.

抑制性细胞因子上调
Upregulation of immunosuppressive cytokines:

TGF-β和IL-10升高。 Increased TGF-β and IL-10.

三、IFN-γ刺激与PD-L1表达实验 IFN-γ-stimulation and PD-L1 expression experiment

实验证明，缺失IFN-γ或清除CD8+ T细胞的肿瘤中，PD-L1表达显著降低。 Experiments show that PD-L1 expression is significantly lower in tumors lacking IFN-γ or depleted of CD8+ T cells (the main producers of IFN-γ)23.

说明肿瘤微环境中T细胞分泌的IFN-γ是肿瘤细胞高PD-L1表达的关键驱动因素。 This demonstrates that IFN-γ produced by T cells in the tumor microenvironment is a key driver of high PD-L1 expression on tumor cells.

四、髓系抑制性细胞 MDSCs Myeloid-derived suppressive cells (MDSCs)

MDSCs是一类异质性高度的细胞群，兼具单核细胞和中性粒细胞特征，包括两种主要类型： MDSCs are a highly heterogeneous group with features of both monocytes and neutrophils, consisting of two major types:

粒细胞型/多形核型MDSCs（PMN-MDSCs，类似中性粒细胞） Granulocytic/polymorphonuclear MDSCs (PMN-MDSCs, neutrophil-like)

单核细胞型MDSCs（M-MDSCs，类似单核细胞） Monocytic MDSCs (M-MDSCs, monocyte-like)31.

五、免疫抑制细胞和分子的多重作用 Multiple effects of immunosuppressive cells and molecules

MDSCs与TAMs上调，抑制NK细胞、树突细胞的功能，同时促进Treg的归巢与诱导。 Increased MDSCs and TAMs inhibit NK cells and dendritic cells, while promoting Treg induction and recruitment.

抑制T细胞募集和活化，促使T细胞凋亡。 They suppress T cell recruitment and activation, and induce T cell apoptosis.

分泌IL-10、TGF-β等因子，促进免疫抑制微环境形成。 They secrete factors such as IL-10 and TGF-β, contributing to immunosuppressive microenvironment3134.

降低ATP、氨基酸（精氨酸、半胱氨酸、色氨酸）水平，加重氧化应激，干扰T细胞代谢和功能。 They decrease ATP and amino acid (arginine, cysteine, tryptophan) levels, increase oxidative stress, and disrupt T cell metabolism and functions.

下调B细胞功能，进一步削弱抗体介导免疫。 B cell inhibition further weakens antibody-mediated immunity.

六、肿瘤相关巨噬细胞（TAM）的主导作用 Dominant role of Tumor-Associated Macrophages (TAMs)

巨噬细胞是绝大多数肿瘤中最丰富的浸润性细胞。 Macrophages are the most abundant immune cells infiltrating most tumors36.

TAM促进肿瘤生长、转移、血管生成以及免疫抑制。 TAMs promote tumor growth, metastasis, angiogenesis, and immunosuppression.

机制包括分泌VEGF、PDGF、TGF-β、IL-10，诱导Treg，抑制树突细胞成熟和T细胞激活。 Their mechanisms include secretion of VEGF, PDGF, TGF-β, IL-10, induction of Tregs, and inhibition of dendritic cell maturation and T cell activation.

七、补充课堂讲解要点 Additional highlights from lecture

老师强调，肿瘤通过多途径协同抑制免疫，包括免疫细胞抑制、免疫原性降低和代谢异常，最终导致肿瘤不可控发展。 The instructor highlighted that tumors suppress immunity through multiple synergistic pathways, including immune cell suppression, reduced immunogenicity, and metabolic alterations, leading to uncontrolled tumor progression.

逃逸阶段出现的免疫抑制微环境不仅针对T细胞，还影响B细胞、NK细胞和树突细胞的抗肿瘤功能。 The immunosuppressive microenvironment in the escape phase affects not only T cells but also impairs antitumor roles of B cells, NK cells, and dendritic cells.

抑制性代谢酶（如CD39、CD73、IDO）和抑制性细胞因子的上调是重要研究和治疗靶点。 The upregulation of suppressive enzymes (CD39, CD73, IDO) and cytokines is a major research and therapeutic target.

炎症与肿瘤 Inflammation and cancer

1
肿瘤发展新兴标志与赋能特征 Emerging hallmarks and enabling characteristics of cancer

炎症是肿瘤的核心赋能特征之一，同时与避免免疫破坏、细胞能量异常、基因不稳定等多重机制相互作用。 Inflammation is one of the core enabling characteristics of cancer, interconnected with immune evasion, deregulated cellular energetics, and genome instability.

肿瘤促进性炎症既能驱动基因变异，也能为肿瘤生长提供有利环境。 Tumor-promoting inflammation can both drive genetic alterations and create a permissive environment for tumor growth.

2
炎症与免疫矛盾作用 Paradoxical effects of inflammation and immunity

促炎分子可招募和激活免疫细胞，理论上激发抗肿瘤免疫，但实际却助力肿瘤进展。 Pro-inflammatory molecules recruit and activate immune cells, theoretically enhancing antitumor immunity, but in practice they often support tumor progression.

这一现象看似矛盾，本质上反映炎症微环境的双刃剑作用。 This paradox reflects the dual role of the inflammatory microenvironment.

3
炎症驱动的肿瘤发生分阶段机制 Multi-stage mechanisms of inflammation-driven tumorigenesis

活性氧（ROS）和促炎因子（如TNF-α）能增加突变率，推动肿瘤起始。 Reactive oxygen species (ROS) and pro-inflammatory factors like TNF-α increase mutation rates, initiating tumorigenesis.

促炎因子（TNF-α，IL-6，IL-1β）激活转录因子（NF-κB，STAT3，AP-1），促进细胞"干性"、增殖、血管生成和侵袭。 Pro-inflammatory molecules (TNF-α, IL-6, IL-1β) activate transcription factors (NF-κB, STAT3, AP-1), enhancing cellular stemness, proliferation, angiogenesis, and invasion.

结果是肿瘤干细胞（CSCs）比例升高，肿瘤促进作用增强。 This results in increased cancer stem cell (CSC) content and promotes tumor progression.

4
炎症微环境的动态调控 Dynamic modulation of the inflammatory microenvironment

在正常/再生时，损伤后炎症有助修复，但持续炎症则驱动肿瘤进展、转移和耐药。 Inflammation following injury aids tissue repair in regeneration, but chronic inflammation drives progression, metastasis, and resistance.

控制促炎分子的释放与炎症强度，是既激活抗肿瘤免疫、又抑制肿瘤促进的关键。 Controlling the release and magnitude of pro-inflammatory molecules is crucial to stimulate antitumor immunity while limiting tumor promotion.

5
教师课上补充要点 Additional highlights from the lecture

老师强调，很多抗癌免疫反应与炎症机制重叠，因此杀伤细胞、促进性细胞因子等既可能抑瘤也可能促瘤，最终效果取决于炎症时间、部位和细胞类型。 The instructor stressed that many antitumor immune responses overlap with inflammatory processes, and that cytotoxic cells or cytokines may be tumor-suppressing or tumor-promoting depending on timing, location, and cell type.

维持"适度"炎症可提高免疫疗效，过强或持久炎症则促进基因不稳定和癌症演进。 Maintaining "moderate" inflammation can enhance immunotherapy efficacy, while excessive or chronic inflammation fosters genomic instability and cancer progression.

肿瘤免疫治疗Cancer immunotherapies

一、肿瘤微环境免疫成分的分类Classification of the immune component of the tumor microenvironment

"热肿瘤"（Hot tumors）：高突变负荷（TMB）、存在大量免疫细胞、免疫功能受抑但尚在、对免疫检查点抑制剂敏感。 "Hot tumors": High tumor mutational burden (TMB), abundant immune cells, immunity is suppressed but present, responsive to immune checkpoint inhibitors.

"冷肿瘤"（Cold tumors）：低TMB，免疫沙漠（几乎无免疫细胞），对免疫治疗效果差、临床挑战大。 "Cold tumors": Low TMB, immune desert (almost no immune cells), poor response to immunotherapy, very challenging clinically.

"突变型肿瘤"（Mutated tumors）：中至高TMB，免疫细胞被排除在外，需要寻找招募免疫细胞并促进其进入的新策略。 "Mutated tumors": Moderate to high TMB, immune cells are excluded, strategies needed to recruit immune cells and allow their entry.

"浸润型肿瘤"（Infiltrated tumors）：低TMB，存在大量免疫抑制细胞，重点可考虑靶向免疫抑制逆转。 "Infiltrated tumors": Low TMB, infiltrating suppressive immune cells are abundant, possible focus on targeting immunosuppression.

老师补充
上述分型反映肿瘤免疫微环境的异质性，不同类型决定免疫治疗的敏感性和潜在治疗策略。 Teacher’s note: These types reflect the heterogeneity of tumor immune microenvironment, which dictates sensitivity to immunotherapy and the choice of treatment strategies.
二、T细胞抑制
PD-L1/PD-1通路 T cell inhibition: the PD-L1/PD-1 axis, an immune checkpoint

肿瘤细胞高表达程序性死亡配体1（PD-L1），T细胞表面表达PD-1受体。 Cancer cells highly express programmed cell death ligand 1 (PD-L1); T cells express PD-1 receptor.

PD-L1与PD-1结合后阻断T细胞活化，抑制其抗肿瘤功能。 The binding of PD-L1 and PD-1 blocks T cell activation and suppresses antitumor function.

这一机制帮助肿瘤细胞逃避免疫清除，是免疫检查点抑制剂的主要靶点。 This mechanism helps tumor cells escape immune clearance and is the main target of immune checkpoint inhibitors.

老师强调
PD-L1/PD-1轴被称作免疫“刹车”，非特异性抑制广泛抗肿瘤T细胞反应。 Teacher’s note: The PD-L1/PD-1 axis acts as an immune “brake” that non-specifically suppresses broad antitumor T cell responses.

三、免疫检查点抑制剂的作用机制 Mechanism of immune checkpoint inhibitors

检查点抑制剂包括抗PD-1和抗PD-L1抗体，通过阻断PD-1/PD-L1结合解除T细胞抑制。 Checkpoint inhibitors include anti-PD-1 and anti-PD-L1 antibodies, which block their interaction and relieve T cell inhibition.

药物对应模型
Model:

抗PD-1/PD-L1抗体阻止肿瘤细胞释放抑制信号，使T细胞重新活化。 Anti-PD-1/PD-L1 antibodies prevent tumor cells from sending inhibitory signals, reactivating T cells.

效果
促进适应性免疫反应，抑制肿瘤生长。 Effect: Promotes adaptive immune response, suppresses tumor growth.

四、PD-1/PD-L1信号通路的分子细节与抗体机制 Molecular detail and antibody mechanism of the PD-1/PD-L1 pathway

肿瘤细胞及抗原提呈细胞（APCs）PD-L1表达↑，T细胞PD-1↑。 PD-L1 expression increases in cancer cells and APCs; PD-1 expression increases in T cells.

PD-1/PD-L1结合导致T细胞信号传导抑制，T细胞无力化/失活。 PD-1/PD-L1 binding suppresses T cell signaling, causing dysfunction/exhaustion.

抗PD-1/PD-L1抗体可解除这一抑制，恢复效应T细胞功能。 Anti-PD-1/PD-L1 antibodies reverse this inhibition and restore T cell effector functions.

这一机制适用于部分高表型肿瘤，尤其是“热肿瘤”。 This mechanism is especially effective in "hot tumors" with active but suppressed immunity.

五、重要历史突破及其临床意义Major breakthroughs and clinical significance

詹姆斯·艾利森与本庶佑因发现免疫抑制点（CTLA-4、PD-1）疗法获2018年诺贝尔奖。 James P. Allison and Tasuku Honjo were awarded the Nobel Prize in 2018 for the discovery of immune checkpoint therapy (CTLA-4, PD-1).

教师补充
免疫治疗革命性地改变了晚期癌症治疗，部分患者实现长期缓解甚至治愈。 Teacher’s note: Immunotherapy has revolutionized advanced cancer treatment, enabling durable remission and even cures in some cases.

肿瘤免疫治疗 Cancer immunotherapies

一、抗PD-L1抗体在黑色素瘤小鼠模型中的预临床研究 Preclinical models: anti-PD-L1 for melanoma treatment in mice

将B16F10肿瘤细胞接种于野生型小鼠，部分小鼠注射抗PD-L1抗体，作为药物处理组。 B16F10 tumor cells are inoculated into wild-type mice, with some mice receiving anti-PD-L1 antibody treatment as the experimental group.

后续监测肿瘤生长速度和生存情况。 Tumor growth and mouse survival are measured over time.

结果显示，抗PD-L1抗体能明显减缓肿瘤生长。 The results show that anti-PD-L1 antibody significantly reduces tumor growth.

同时，提高了小鼠的生存率和生存期。 At the same time, survival rates and lifespan of the mice are improved.

这证明了免疫检查点抑制剂在动物黑色素瘤模型中的抗肿瘤效率。 This demonstrates the antitumor efficacy of immune checkpoint inhibitors in a mouse melanoma model.

但老师补充
实际肿瘤治疗效果依赖肿瘤微环境、免疫细胞浸润和PD-L1表达水平，临床反应有个体差异。 However, the teacher noted that real tumor treatment efficacy depends on tumor microenvironment, immune cell infiltration, and PD-L1 expression; clinical outcomes vary between individuals.

二、CAR-T细胞治疗介绍 Adoptive Immunotherapy: CAR-T cells targeting tumor antigens

CAR-T细胞（嵌合抗原受体T细胞）是通过基因工程手段，将肿瘤特异性抗原结合结构（scFv）与T细胞激活信号结构连接，导入并表达于T细胞上的人工受体。 CAR-T cells (chimeric antigen receptor T cells) are engineered by fusing a tumor-specific antigen-binding structure (scFv) with T cell activation signaling domains, then expressing the resulting receptor on T cells.

制备流程包括
获得抗体序列，设计并工程化scFv结构，利用慢病毒载体构建CAR并转导T细胞，最后体外扩大培养与功能验证。 Process includes: obtaining antibody sequence, engineering scFv, constructing CAR using lentiviral vector, transducing T cells, and expanding/validating CAR-T in vitro.

CAR-T细胞能够直接识别并杀伤表达相应抗原的肿瘤细胞，且不依赖于MHC分子递呈抗原。 CAR-T cells recognize and kill antigen-expressing tumor cells directly, independent of MHC-mediated antigen presentation.

教师补充
CAR结构的抗原结合区通常来源于单链抗体（scFv），而细胞内信号区决定T细胞的激活和功能。 Teacher’s note: The antigen-binding region in CAR is usually derived from single-chain antibody fragments (scFv), while the intracellular signaling domain determines T cell activation and function.

三、免疫检查点和CAR-T疗法的临床转化 From clinical trials to cancer treatment

免疫检查点抑制剂（如PD-1/PD-L1抗体）2014/2015年起被FDA、EMA批准用于黑色素瘤，后续拓展到肺癌、膀胱癌等多种癌症。 Immune checkpoint inhibitors (like PD-1/PD-L1 antibodies) have been approved by FDA and EMA for melanoma since 2014/2015, and subsequently extended to lung, bladder, and other cancers.

CAR-T细胞治疗目前主要针对血液系统恶性肿瘤（如B细胞急性淋巴细胞白血病和淋巴瘤），部分品种已获批临床应用。 CAR-T therapy is currently used mainly for hematologic malignancies (such as B-cell ALL and NHL), with several products approved for clinical use.

四、CAR-T细胞制备流程及体外验证 CAR-T manufacturing and functional testing

典型流程包括六步
提供靶抗原抗体序列，工程化与验证scFv，制备慢病毒载体并初步验证，T细胞准备与转导，CAR-T功能验证，体外扩增和优化。 The typical process includes six steps: provide antigen-specific antibody sequence, engineer and validate scFv, produce and preliminarily validate lentiviral vector, prepare and transduce T cells, functionally validate CAR-T cells, and expand/optimize in vitro.

实验验证内容涵盖CAR的表达、干扰素γ（IFNγ）分泌能力以及杀伤肿瘤细胞的效果。 Validation includes CAR expression, interferon-γ (IFNγ) secretion, and cytotoxicity against tumor cells.

教师强调
功能验证阶段至关重要，需测试CAR-T细胞能否特异性识别并高效杀伤肿瘤靶细胞。 Teacher’s note: Functional validation is crucial, confirming that CAR-T can specifically recognize and efficiently kill target tumor cells.

五、CAR-T疗法的临床挑战与副作用 Clinical challenges and adverse effects of CAR-T therapy

CAR-T疗法已获批用于多种血液肿瘤，但面临两大典型不良反应：（1）细胞因子释放综合征（“On-target”效应，严重可致命）；（2）免疫效应细胞相关神经毒性综合征（ICANS）。 CAR-T therapy is approved for several hematological tumors, but faces two main adverse effects: (1) cytokine release syndrome ("on-target" effect, potentially fatal), (2) immune effector cell-associated neurotoxicity syndrome (ICANS).

相关管理措施包括使用糖皮质激素、抗IL-6等抑制炎症反应以及新一代CAR设计用于改善安全性。 Management includes glucocorticoids, anti-IL-6 to suppress inflammation, and development of new CAR designs to improve safety.

教师补充
高昂的治疗费用也是限制之一，每例常超10万欧元/年。 Teacher’s note: High cost is a major limiting factor, often exceeding €100,000 per patient per year.

六、CAR-T细胞进化与优化策略 CAR-T cells evolution and optimization strategies

提高肿瘤归巢与组织穿透力，可通过工程化趋化因子受体、肝素酶表达等方式。 Enhance tumor homing and penetration by engineering chemokine receptors and heparanase expression in CAR-T cells.

克服肿瘤微环境免疫抑制，如促进CAR-T分泌抗PD-1抗体、下调PD-1。 Overcome immunosuppression in the tumor microenvironment, for example by secreting anti-PD-1 antibodies or downregulating PD-1 in CAR-T cells.

控制CAR-T活性
开发可通过外源药物开关的CAR，以及蛋白酶体降解机制和自杀开关策略。 Control CAR-T cell activity: develop "on/off" switches using external drugs, proteasomal degradation systems, and suicide strategies.
优化特异性
组合使用多种CAR定位多个肿瘤抗原，或设定仅在遇到健康细胞时关闭CAR。 Improve specificity: use combinations of CARs to target multiple antigens, or program CAR deactivation upon encountering healthy cells.

七、未来发展方向和联合治疗前景 Future perspectives and combination therapies

新型疗效生物标志物
包括肿瘤及循环肿瘤来源囊泡中的PD-1/PD-L1表达，TMB（肿瘤突变负荷）临床应用等。 New biomarkers of efficacy: PD-1/PD-L1 expression in tumors and circulating tumor-derived vesicles, clinical implementation of TMB (tumor mutational burden), etc.

联合治疗趋势明显，包括化疗+检查点抑制剂、不同检查点抑制剂组合、模式识别受体激动剂+检查点抑制剂等。 Combination therapies become prominent, including chemotherapy + checkpoint inhibitors, combinations of different checkpoint inhibitors, pattern recognition receptor agonists + checkpoint inhibitors.

教师强调
未来肿瘤免疫治疗将越来越重视个体化和多手段联合，提升疗效，减少副作用。 Teacher’s note: Future cancer immunotherapies will increasingly emphasize personalized and combination approaches to enhance efficacy and reduce side effects.

10实验技巧

Cells in culture | 细胞体外培养

原代细胞
代表来源组织的细胞
Primary cells
represent the tissue of origin

原代细胞直接来源于特定组织，能够维持组织原有的特性。

Primary cells are derived directly from specific tissues and can maintain properties of the tissue of origin.

培养和维持原代细胞较困难，不同供体之间存在差异。

They are difficult to culture and maintain, with variability from donor to donor.

1917年实现了无菌操作和营养成分供给，促进了原代细胞培养的发展。

Aseptic conditions and sufficient nutrients were developed in 1917 to aid primary cell culture.

常见类型有角质形成细胞、小肠上皮细胞、内皮细胞、成纤维细胞、肌细胞和造血干细胞等。

Common types include keratinocytes, enterocytes, endothelial cells, myocytes, fibroblasts, hematopoietic stem cells, and others.

2
原代细胞向永生化继代细胞系的转化

Transformation of primary cells into immortalized secondary cell line

原代细胞可通过自发、化学或病毒方式转化为拥有无限增殖能力的永生化细胞系。

Primary cells can be transformed into immortalized cell lines either spontaneously, chemically, or via viral induction.

永生化细胞系便于培养，无供体差异。

These immortalized lines are easy to culture and show no variability between donors.

典型案例是HeLa细胞，1951年从Henrietta Lacks 的宫颈癌获得。

A typical example is the HeLa cell line, derived in 1951 from the cervix cancer of Henrietta Lacks.

3
干细胞

Stem cells

胚胎干细胞(ESCs)：全能或多能，来源于囊胚。

Embryonic stem cells (ESCs): totipotent or pluripotent, from blastocyst.

1981年首次获得小鼠ESCs，1998年获得人ESCs，两者均来自囊胚内细胞团。

Mouse ESCs were first isolated in 1981, human ESCs in 1998, both from the blastocyst inner cell mass.

诱导多能干细胞(iPSCs)

Induced pluripotent stem cells (iPSCs)

2006年首次通过重编程小鼠（及人类）成纤维细胞获得iPSCs。

In 2006, iPSCs were generated by reprogramming mouse (later human) fibroblasts.

约翰·戈登和山中伸弥因iPSC获得2012诺贝尔生理/医学奖。

John B. Gurdon and Shinya Yamanaka were awarded the 2012 Nobel Prize in Physiology or Medicine for iPSCs.

诱导多能干细胞的衍生类型，如诱导型多能神经干细胞（iNSCs）

Induced multipotent stem cells, e.g., induced neural stem cells (iNSCs)

2012年首次通过重编程成纤维细胞产生iNSCs，相较传统iPSCs癌变风险更低。

In 2012, iNSCs were generated from fibroblasts with reduced carcinogenic potential compared to iPSCs.

老师补充讲解要点

Key points from the lecture:

老师着重提到，原代细胞更接近体内状态但体外操作稳定性差，且批间差异大；永生化细胞系虽然便于实验，但可能逐步丧失某些组织特性。 The lecturer emphasized that primary cells are closer to in vivo status but less stable in vitro and have high batch-to-batch variation, while immortalized lines are easier for experiments but may gradually lose some tissue-specific features.

HeLa细胞是细胞生物学、癌症、药物筛选研究中最为著名的永生化人类细胞系。 HeLa cells are the most well-known human immortalized cell line, widely used in cell biology, cancer, and drug screening research.

关于iPSCs与ESCs，老师提到iPSC是通过特定基因重编程获得，可以从体细胞还原成多能状态。iPSC技术极大推动了疾病建模与再生医学领域。 For iPSCs and ESCs, the lecturer highlighted that iPSCs are generated by gene reprogramming of somatic cells into a pluripotent state, which has greatly advanced disease modeling and regenerative medicine.

诱导型多能/多能干细胞（如iNSCs）技术可以通过有限的重编程获得特定定向分化能力，减少致瘤风险。 Induced multipotent/pluripotent stem cell technology (such as iNSCs) enables targeted differentiation potential with a lower risk of tumorigenesis.

Cell line in 2D culture | 细胞二维培养生长曲线

1
细胞在传统二维培养中的生长曲线分为四个阶段

The cell growth curve in 2D culture has four main phases:

延滞期（Lag phase）：细胞刚刚接种，还未开始快速分裂。 Lag phase: cells are newly seeded and have not started to proliferate rapidly.

对数生长期（Log phase）：细胞增殖速率最大，细胞数量指数式增长 (N = 2^n）。 Log phase: cells proliferate at a maximum rate, cell number grows exponentially (N = 2^n).

稳定期（Stationary phase）：由于营养耗尽或空间饱和，细胞增殖速度趋于平衡，生死数目相近。 Stationary phase: growth slows as nutrients are depleted or space is saturated, numbers of dividing and dying cells balance.

衰亡期（Death phase）：细胞大量死亡，数量下降。 Death phase: cells die in large numbers and total cell count decreases.

老师补充说明，实验设计时通常选择对数生长期细胞进行各类实验，因其最健康、功能活跃。 The lecturer added that cells in the log phase are typically chosen for experiments due to their health and activity.

2D versus 3D cell culture | 二维与三维细胞培养的区别

二维培养
贴壁单层细胞，常用于原代细胞、永生化细胞系、干细胞
2D culture
monolayer adherent cell culture, commonly used for primary cells, immortalized cell lines, and stem cells.
三维培养
可通过球状体(spheroid)、类器官(organoid)或组织片段等，包含多种细胞类型
3D culture
can be achieved via spheroids, organoids, or tissue explants, involving multiple cell types.

球状体(spheroid)：至少一种细胞类型，细胞可形成三维结构。 Spheroid: at least one cell type, cells form a three-dimensional structure.

类器官(organoid)：至少包含一种上皮细胞和一种间充质细胞，可更好模拟组织微环境。 Organoid: contains at least one epithelial and one mesenchymal cell type, better mimicking tissue microenvironment.

组织片段(Tissue explant)：直接取材体内组织片段用于培养。 Tissue explant: directly cultured from tissue fragments.

“芯片上的模型”与微流控技术结合，可建立更复杂模拟体系。 "Organ-on-chip" models and microfluidic techniques allow for even more complex simulation of tissue environments.

老师强调，三维培养比二维更接近体内状态，能更好模拟肿瘤微环境、药物反应等。 The lecturer emphasized that 3D culture is closer to in vivo situations and thus better simulates the tumor microenvironment and drug responses.

Spheroid/organoid scaffold-free culture methods | 球状体/类器官无支架培养方法

1
依靠细胞自身的聚集和避免与器皿表面黏附实现自发三维结构形成

Self-assembly and prevention of cell adhesion to the flask enable spontaneous 3D structure formation.

2
常见方法：

Common methods:

沉淀离心管（pellet）：利用离心使细胞聚集沉淀后自发成团。 Pellet: centrifuge collects cells, which aggregate spontaneously.

非黏附性培养瓶（non adhesive flask）：防止细胞贴壁促使其聚集生长。 Non-adhesive flask: prevents cell adhesion, promoting aggregation.

悬滴法（hanging drop）：利用表面张力让小液滴中的细胞自聚成团。 Hanging drop: surface tension enables cells to aggregate in small droplets.

旋转容器（rotating vessel）：以低剪切力环境促进细胞三维聚集。 Rotating vessel: low shear force environment fosters 3D aggregation.

搅拌容器（stirring vessel）：维持细胞悬浮，利于聚集成团。 Stirring vessel: keeps cells in suspension for spheroid formation.

微流控芯片（microfluidic organ-on-chip）：利用可控微环境促使细胞形成结构。 Microfluidic (organ-on-chip): uses a controlled microenvironment to organize cells.

磁悬浮（magnetic levitation）：磁性技术诱导细胞三维聚集。 Magnetic levitation: magnetic forces encourage 3D cell aggregation.

老师指出，这些方法避免了外源基质成分引入，利于机制研究。 The lecturer noted these methods avoid adding exogenous matrix, aiding research on intrinsic mechanisms.

Scaffolds for 3D culture: natural or synthetic hydrogel or porous | 三维培养支架：天然/合成水凝胶或多孔材料

1
天然或合成支架材料用于支持三维培养细胞的空间结构

Natural or synthetic scaffolds support the spatial structure of 3D cultured cells.

2
常见三维培养支架类型：

Common types of 3D scaffolds:

极化上皮细胞培养
多孔膜支持单层上皮细胞极化。 Polarized epithelial cell culture: porous membrane supports a polarized epithelial cell monolayer.
人工皮肤
角质形成细胞覆盖生物可降解纤维网，与下方成纤维细胞共同形成结构。 Artificial skin: keratinocytes cover a biodegradable fiber mesh alongside fibroblasts.
微载体培养
多孔微载体提升细胞贴附与三维生长。 Microcarrier culture: porous polymer microcarriers enhance cell attachment and 3D growth.

胶原水凝胶包埋上皮细胞，形成三维囊泡状结构（如MDcK细胞小囊泡）。 Collagen hydrogel: supports epithelial cells in forming vesicular 3D structures (e.g., MDcK cysts).

老师补充，选择何种支架应结合研究对象、细胞类型、实验需求综合考虑。 The lecturer added that scaffold selection should be based on study objectives, cell type, and experimental requirements.

基因表达 Gene expression

1
概念概要

基因表达是DNA信息通过转录和翻译合成蛋白质的过程。

Gene expression is the process where DNA information is used to synthesize proteins through transcription and translation.

2
主要步骤

首先，DNA在细胞核内被转录为信使RNA（mRNA）。 First, DNA is transcribed into messenger RNA (mRNA) in the cell nucleus.

转录需要启动子区、编码区，前体mRNA含有外显子和内含子。 Transcription requires a promoter region and coding region; pre-mRNA contains both exons and introns.

随后，前体mRNA经过剪接去除内含子，加上5’帽和3’多聚腺苷尾，形成成熟mRNA。 Then, pre-mRNA undergoes splicing to remove introns, a 5’ cap and 3’ polyA tail are added to produce mature mRNA.

成熟mRNA离开细胞核进入细胞质，与核糖体结合进行翻译。 Mature mRNA exits the nucleus into the cytoplasm and binds to ribosomes for translation.

核糖体根据mRNA上的密码子序列合成蛋白质。 Ribosomes synthesize proteins according to the codon sequence in mRNA.

3
结构要点

DNA、mRNA和蛋白质分别位于细胞不同位置（DNA在核内，mRNA在核与胞质，蛋白质在胞质生成）。 DNA, mRNA, and proteins are located in different cell compartments (DNA in the nucleus, mRNA in nucleus and cytoplasm, protein in cytoplasm).

ATG代表起始密码子，STOP代表终止密码子，决定蛋白编码区域。 ATG signals the start codon, STOP is the termination codon; they define the protein-coding region.

4
课上补充说明

老师强调基因表达的每个环节都有精细调控，对细胞功能和疾病密切相关。 The lecturer emphasized that each step of gene expression is finely regulated, which is closely related to cellular function and disease.

转录调控、RNA剪接以及翻译效率都直接影响蛋白表达水平。 Regulation of transcription, RNA splicing, and translation efficiency directly affect protein expression levels.

克隆 Cloning

1
核心流程

克隆是将目标DNA片段连接到载体，并在细菌中扩增的过程。

Cloning is the process of inserting a target DNA fragment into a vector and amplifying it in bacteria.

2
步骤分解

目标DNA通过PCR扩增获得。 The DNA sequence of interest is first amplified by PCR.

限制酶切割DNA和载体（质粒），产生兼容末端。 Restriction enzymes cut both the DNA and the vector (plasmid), generating compatible ends.

连接酶将目标DNA与载体连接成重组质粒。 DNA ligase joins the target DNA and the vector to form recombinant plasmid.

重组质粒转化到细菌中扩增。 The recombinant plasmid is introduced (transformed) into bacteria for amplification.

3
技术要点与历史

限制酶的发现使分子克隆成为可能，该领域获1978年诺贝尔奖。 Discovery of restriction enzymes made molecular cloning possible; this achievement was awarded the Nobel Prize in 1978.

载体常见类型为环状质粒，可在细菌中稳定扩增。 The most common vectors are circular plasmids, which can be amplified stably in bacteria.

4
课堂补充

老师提出，分子克隆是现代生物技术基础，广泛用于基因功能、蛋白表达和基因编辑研究。 The lecturer pointed out that molecular cloning is a foundation of modern biotechnology, widely used for gene function studies, protein production, and gene editing research.

RT-qPCR

1
技术原理

RT-qPCR通过逆转录-实时定量PCR检测分析RNA的丰度。

RT-qPCR quantifies RNA abundance by combining reverse transcription and real-time quantitative PCR.

2
实验步骤

首先由逆转录酶将RNA逆转录成cDNA。 First, reverse transcriptase synthesizes cDNA from RNA.

以cDNA为模板，利用DNA聚合酶和特异性引物进行PCR扩增。 cDNA serves as template for PCR amplification using DNA polymerase and specific primers.

荧光染料（如SYBR Green）或探针（如TaqMan）用于实时监测扩增产物。 Fluorescent dyes (SYBR Green) or probes (TaqMan) are used to detect products in real time.

信号与目标基因初始丰度成正比，实现定量分析。 Detected signal is proportional to the initial abundance of the target gene, enabling quantitative analysis.

3
技术特点

qPCR实现高灵敏度、相对定量甚至绝对定量。 qPCR enables highly sensitive, relative or even absolute quantification.

本实验获1993年诺贝尔化学奖（Mullis & Smith）。 This technology was awarded the 1993 Nobel Prize in Chemistry (Mullis & Smith).

4
课堂补充

老师强调qPCR适用于转录水平（RNA）的检测分析，是分子生物学最常用的定量方法之一。 The lecturer stressed that qPCR is one of the most commonly used methods for evaluating gene expression at the transcriptional (RNA) level.

第三代PCR
数字PCR Third generation PCR: digital PCR
1
数字PCR基本原理

数字PCR通过将反应分割为大量小单元，实现高精度绝对定量。

Digital PCR divides the reaction into numerous small partitions, allowing precise absolute quantification.

2
实验流程

首先将样品分散为微反应体系。 First, the sample is partitioned into many miniature reaction units.

每个单元独立扩增目标序列，靠阳性反应数推算DNA绝对含量。 Each partition independently amplifies the target sequence; the number of positive reactions is used to calculate the absolute quantity of DNA.

包括准备、分割、扩增和定量四个主要步骤。 The steps include preparation, digitization (partitioning), amplification, and quantitation.

3
优点

可以检测极低丰度的DNA/RNA，无需标准曲线。 Capable of detecting very low abundance DNA/RNA without the need for a standard curve.

提高对基因变异、拷贝数变异的检测灵敏度。 Offers increased sensitivity in detecting genetic variations and copy number changes.

4
课堂补充

老师说明，数字PCR适合对极低丰度或微小差异的目标进行精准检测。 The lecturer explained that digital PCR is suitable for highly accurate detection of low-abundance or slight differences in target DNA or RNA.

细胞转染与荧光标记蛋白 Cell transfection, fluorescent-tagged protein

1
DNA转染方法

通过多种方式将外源DNA导入细胞，包括脂质体、聚合物、电穿孔、病毒和显微注射。

Exogenous DNA is introduced into cells by various methods, including liposome-mediated transfection, polymers, electroporation, viral vectors, and microinjection.

2
转录/翻译表达

外源DNA表达为mRNA和蛋白质，也可直接转染mRNA。

Exogenous DNA is transcribed to mRNA and translated to protein, or mRNA can be directly transfected.

3
荧光蛋白标记

绿色荧光蛋白（GFP）等作为融合标签追踪蛋白定位或表达，用于基因功能研究。

Fluorescent proteins such as GFP serve as fusion tags for tracking protein localization or expression in gene function studies.

4
作用与应用

用于基因功能、蛋白定位、过表达或功能救援实验。 Used for gene function studies, protein localization, over-expression, or rescue experiments.

GFP因其被发现与开发荣获2008年诺贝尔化学奖。 GFP discovery and development won the Nobel Prize in Chemistry in 2008.

5
课堂补充

老师特别提到，荧光标记蛋白的表达能实时、无损地监测细胞内动态，是活细胞成像的强大工具。 The lecturer highlighted that fluorescent protein tagging allows real-time, non-destructive tracking of cellular dynamics, making it a powerful tool for live-cell imaging.
